# bakeoff-specs Plan 2 — the runner — Implementation Plan (DRAFT 2)

> **Status: DRAFT 2 for owner review.** Written 2026-09-29 overnight, after the independent review
> ([`2026-09-29-runner.review.md`](2026-09-29-runner.review.md)) of draft 1. Nothing in it has been
> implemented, no candidate has been run, and GitHub has not been touched. Every review finding is
> accounted for in **Changes from draft 1** below. Every point where the design is silent, or where the
> reviewer disagreed with draft 1's default, is under **Owner decisions needed**; where the reviewer
> disagreed, both options are shown side by side and the affected code takes the answer as a parameter.

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or
> superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** A deterministic, scriptable runner that takes each arm through setup, the seven-step evolution
(pass 1 and pass 2), the product-owner loop and the owner's gates; that runs the hidden tests and quality
tools against every step's result; and that records every piece of evidence design §7 asks for, applying the
§9 stop rules. The LLM sessions (the arm, the product-owner agent, the step-2 "teammate") are the only
non-deterministic parts.

**Architecture:** A Python package `runner/` in this repo, driven from the owner's terminal
(`uv run python -m runner …`). Every Claude session runs as `claude -p` inside a throwaway container built
from spike B's stage: pinned Claude Code 2.1.281, read-only root, no capabilities, `--init`, an
`--internal` network whose only way out is an allowlisting CONNECT proxy, and the owner's OAuth token handed
over stdin → fd 3 (never env, argv, disk or `docker inspect`). Each arm has a persistent per-pass home
directory (its `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`, plugins, caches) and a work directory holding its repo; both are mounted
into every turn's container, so sessions continue with `--resume`. **The host never runs git (or anything
else that reads repo-local configuration) against an arm repo:** every git command on an arm repo runs in a
network-less container, and publishing pushes from a clean host clone of a bundle. After each step the
runner exports the arm's committed `refs/heads/main` into a scratch copy and runs `pytest tests/hidden`
**inside a separate tester container** (the harness mounted read-only only there), which resolves every
`PLAN-2 NOTE` from Plan 1 by putting pytest, the arm's CLI, the HTTP fixture and the theatre directories in
one process tree, one user and one filesystem. Arm behaviour (install, egress, entry commands, tracker,
checks) is data in `runner/config/arms/<arm>.toml`; the runner code is arm-agnostic.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.12 stdlib (`asyncio`, `subprocess`, `tomllib`, `json`, `dataclasses`, `tarfile`),
pytest (runner unit tests + `docker`-marked and `network`-marked integration tests), Docker 29 (Docker
Desktop, macOS), tinyproxy (egress), Claude Code 2.1.281 native binary, uv 0.12.19, gh 2.101.0, mypy / ruff /
mutmut pinned with hashes in the tester image.

**Spec:** [`docs/design.md`](../../design.md) (approved 2026-09-28). Inputs: Plan 1
[`2026-09-28-measuring-instrument.md`](2026-09-28-measuring-instrument.md) and its
[ledger](2026-09-28-measuring-instrument.ledger.md) (every `PLAN-2 NOTE` is a requirement here),
[`research/CANDIDATES.md`](../../../research/CANDIDATES.md), `../spike-claude-docker/FINDINGS.md`, and the
draft-1 review.

**Scope:** Plan 2 of 3. Scoring and judging (anonymisation, LLM judges, weights) are Plan 3. This plan
produces the evidence Plan 3 scores; where it measures something only Plan 3 interprets (e.g. hand-edit
detection), it records raw facts, not scores.

---

## Changes from draft 1

Every finding in the review, by its review ID, and what draft 2 does with it. "Fixed" means the fix is in
the named task(s) with code and tests; "OD" means the finding's substance is an owner choice, presented with
both options; "Rejected" means not done, with the reason. Nothing was dropped.

**Totals:** 58 findings (1 critical, 28 important, 29 minor). **Fixed: 51. Turned into an owner choice: 6
(F-3, F-4, F-10, G4, C-7, and G6's plugin-route half — G6's other half is fixed). Rejected: 1 (F-9, no
separate action; see row).** Plus: the reviewer's canary recommendation is Task 26; the dry-run coverage gaps
(review §3 table) are closed by Tasks 14, 24, 25 and 26; the two PLAN-2 NOTE follow-ups in the review's §3
table (NOTE 1 lock-in test, NOTE 8 VM load) are fixed in Task 18.

| ID | Sev | Finding | Handling |
|---|---|---|---|
| S-1 | C | Host git runs on arm-writable repos (`status`, `diff`, `archive`, `grep`, teammate commit, `push` with the owner's credentials) | **Fixed, Task 8.** New `runner/gitops.py`: every git command on an arm repo runs inside a `--network none`, read-only-root container of the arm's own image, with `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1`, `GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null`, and (for reads) `-c core.hooksPath=/dev/null -c core.fsmonitor=false --no-textconv --no-ext-diff`. `publish` bundles `refs/heads/main` inside a container, clones that bundle on the host into a fresh scratch directory (a bundle carries no config or hooks), secret-scans it (Task 21), and pushes from there with hooks disabled. A unit test fails if any `runner/` module other than `gitops.py` and the harness-repo helpers builds a `git` argv. Extension found while fixing: host-side *reads* of arm trees (transcripts, snapshots) now refuse symlinks and restore tars with `filter="data"` (Tasks 7, 8), so an arm cannot plant a symlink that makes the host copy a host file into evidence. |
| G1 | I | Spec Kitty's beads tracker binding never performed | **Fixed, Task 11.** Setup step `spec-kitty tracker bind beads --egress local` (exact syntax confirmed by probe P9; a wrong flag fails setup loudly), machine check `speckitty.tracker-binding`, OV item now checks the recorded binding rather than an action nobody ran. |
| G2 | I | beads' `bd setup claude` not run | **Fixed, Task 11.** Setup step `bd setup claude`; machine check `beads.claude-hook` (a SessionStart hook running `bd prime` is present in the arm's settings). |
| G3 | M | OpenSpec's project-context slot left empty while others get principles | **Fixed, Task 10** (setup writes the principles into `openspec/config.yaml` `context`). Flagged under OD22 because it changes what OpenSpec is given; reverting is one line. |

## Owner decisions needed

> **Owner answers, 2026-09-29.**
> - Plan 1's two [OWNER TO CONFIRM] write-back rulings are **confirmed** (comment column held for grown values and `|`
>   headers; scalar → block collection keeps the key line's bytes).
> - **Every arm repo gets an `origin`.** At least one framework the owner has used fails without a remote
>   defined. `init_repo` must always add one: a local bare repository created by the runner (e.g.
>   `<arm-home>/origin.git`, inside the same container mounts as the repo — never a network remote), even when
>   no GitHub URL is given. Publishing to GitHub stays a separate, owner-approved step from a clean clone.
>   Setup verification checks `git remote get-url origin` resolves and `git push origin main` works.
> - **Step 6 fallback bug (owner, 2026-09-29).** The planted alias bug is plan A. Before delivering step 6 to an
>   arm, the runner runs step 6's hidden tests against the arm's step-5 commit. If they already pass (the arm
>   handled aliases correctly unprompted), the step pauses for the owner, who picks a substitute bug on the fly
>   from that arm's real defects — comparing notes across arms to choose the most consistent substitute. The
>   substitute issue text, and why, are recorded in the arm's evidence.
> - **Include marker renamed `$include` → `$ref`** (owner, 2026-09-29) — applied to the contract, hidden tests and
>   reference in Plan 1; runner handoffs pick it up from `scenario/`.
> - **Approved by owner 2026-09-29 (spec changes accepted as recommended):** OD2, OD3, OD4, OD5, OD6, OD8, OD10,
>   OD14, OD17, OD20, OD23; OD24 = B; OD25 approved (canary); OD11 accepted ("probably acceptable"). All ODs now decided — the rest stand at their recommended options.
> - No ODs remain open.

Each item is a point where the design is silent or ambiguous, or where the reviewer disagreed with draft 1.
Where the reviewer disagreed, the entry shows **A (draft-1 default)** and **B (reviewer's alternative)** side by
side with a one-line trade-off and a recommendation, and the tasks it affects read the answer from one
parameter so that either answer is a small edit. **Draft 2 is written with the recommended option selected**
in the parameter's default; the "switch" line says exactly what to edit for the other one.

Items marked **SPEC CHANGE — needs explicit owner approval** change or extend what `docs/design.md` says;
ideally the approved answer is also recorded in design.md.

| # | Decision | Default / options | Tasks |
|---|---|---|---|
| OD1 | **What the step-2 hand edit is** (design §4 says only "a hand edit made outside the method's workflow"). *Fills a gap; reviewer: agree, with fixes (all applied).* | A "teammate" commit adding an undocumented, contract-compatible feature: `TROUPE_THEATRE` env var becomes the default for `--theatre`. Made by a scripted plain-Claude session with no method loaded (code + at most one test, no docs/specs), verified by a probe, committed in a container as `Sam Teammate`. Invisible to hidden tests. Detection recorded as facts. | 16 |
| OD2 | **Model pin vs methods that choose cheaper subagent models.** **SPEC CHANGE — needs explicit owner approval** (reinterprets design §11). *Reviewer: agree, conditionally (conditions applied: per-arm report, full-ID case caught at setup, P3 full-ID variant).* | Every arm session gets `ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_{OPUS,SONNET,HAIKU}_MODEL` and `CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL` = `claude-opus-5-5`. The method's own model choices are recorded per arm (`model-overrides.json`) and reported as "overridden". A method whose installed files name a full non-Opus model ID fails setup check `common.no-foreign-model-ids`; the owner decides at setup (e.g. a documented config override), never as a recurring halt. | 5, 9, 20, 25 |
| OD3 | **What an "attempt" is; the no-progress nudge.** **SPEC CHANGE — needs explicit owner approval** (the nudge is feedback design §9 doesn't mention; review C-7). *Reviewer: agree.* | One attempt = one delivery of the step (ends when the arm reports done, stalls or times out). No progress = zero *discriminating* tests first valid at this step pass. After such an attempt the arm gets the fixed, counts-only message "Acceptance testing found that none of this step's new behaviour works yet. Please continue with the request." After 3 no-progress attempts the arm stops at that step. Partial progress is final. | 22 |
| OD4 | **What is tested, and how the arm is told.** **SPEC CHANGE — needs explicit owner approval** (handoff text). *Reviewer: disagree as drafted.* Fixed regardless (not part of the choice): every handoff ends with one identical delivery sentence (Task 12), and `refs/heads/main` is what is tested. Remaining choice: **A (draft 1):** also keep the runner's one-time nudge "Please commit this step's work on the main branch." when the arm reports done with its work not on `main`, and add the PO-brief standing answer. **B (reviewer):** delivery sentence only; no nudge; record the branch state as evidence. *Trade-off:* A rescues a method that ignores the sentence once, at the cost of one harness message some arms get and others don't; B is strictly identical input for every arm, but a single lapse scores zero for the step. **Recommendation: A** — the nudge is identical text, logged as an intervention, and now fires only when an arm ignored an explicit instruction. **Switch to B:** set `commit_on_main = ""` in `runner/config/scenario.toml`. **Also approve the wording** (`[messages] delivery`): *"Delivery: this request is done when its work is committed on the `main` branch of this repository — the `main` branch is what gets tested. Do not push and do not open pull requests."* | 12, 15 |
| OD5 | **How each method is driven.** **SPEC CHANGE — needs explicit owner approval** (defines "in the arm's own workflow"). *Reviewer: agree in principle; fixes F-2 and F-5 applied, F-3 is OD19.* | Per-arm entry command (Task 12 table). After it the runner follows the method's own guidance: a recommended next command **or named skill** is sent in the method's own invocation form. `must_run` = the documented closing command(s) of the method's per-change flow, one rule for all arms, cited per arm. **Please confirm the resulting lists:** openspec `/opsx:archive`; speckit `/speckit-converge`; speckitty `/spec-kitty.accept`, `/spec-kitty.merge`; ccsdd `/kiro-validate-impl`; superpowers `/superpowers:finishing-a-development-branch` (new in draft 2); bmad none (its one-session path documents no closing command; `bmad-retrospective` closes epics only); control none. | 9, 12, 15 |
| OD6 | **Step 5's "two concurrent change requests".** **SPEC CHANGE — needs explicit owner approval** (interpretation of design §4). *Reviewer: disagree.* **A (draft 1):** one message with CR-A and CR-B as the brief has them. **B (reviewer, made deterministic):** CR-A as the step's request; CR-B as a second request, via the entry command, while CR-A is in flight. The reviewer suggested "after CR-A's first approval gate"; since gates fall at different points per method, draft 2 sends CR-B at a **fixed point for every arm: at the end of the arm's second turn on CR-A** (it preempts whatever that turn asked; the text is logged as `preempted`), or at the end of the first turn if the arm reports CR-A done in one turn (`[step5] cr_b_after_turn = 2`). *Trade-off:* A is the simplest identical input but lets every method treat the pair as one change, so "concurrent" is probably never exercised; B exercises concurrency (OpenSpec's sharpest case) at the same conversational point for every arm, but CR-B can preempt an unanswered question (the arm may re-ask), and the split needs harness text (below) the owner approves. **Recommendation: B.** Harness text for B (`scenario.toml [step5]`): message 1 = the harness title "# Step 5 — a change request" (the brief's own title announces two) + CR-A section + the brief's contract line + contract section + delivery sentence; message 2 = "A second change request has arrived while you work on the first one:" + CR-B section + delivery sentence. **Switch to A:** set `delivery = "one-message"` in `[step5]`. | 12, 15 |
| OD7 | **Which approvals the owner sees live.** *Reviewer: agree; C-8 list added.* | Every PO-classified approval request pauses for the owner (PO draft verdict shown; accept or override). Every escalation pauses. Questions answered from the brief do not pause; significant ones and "possible missed approvals" are listed in the step summary. | 13, 14, 15, 20 |
| OD8 | **GitHub repos across two passes, and visibility.** **SPEC CHANGE — needs explicit owner approval** (design §10 naming, §6 "public" timing). *Reviewer: agree.* | Pass 1: `troupe-run/bakeoff-specs-<arm>-pass1`; pass 2 (scored): `troupe-run/bakeoff-specs-<arm>`. All created **private** by the owner (runner prints the commands) and made public together when the bake-off concludes. | 8, 17 |
| OD9 | **Version pins and plugin install route.** *Reviewer: agree on versions, disagree on route.* Versions (agreed): OpenSpec 1.13.2, BMAD 6.12.0, Spec Kit 1.0.12, Spec Kitty 3.2.7, superpowers 6.4.2, cc-sdd 3.1.0, beads 1.3.0. **A (draft 1):** documented marketplace route (`claude plugin marketplace add` + `install`); `--plugin-dir` at the tag only if the marketplace no longer serves the pin. **B (reviewer):** `--plugin-dir` at the tag's **verified commit SHA** as the primary route for both passes, vendored into the arm image at build time. *Trade-off:* A is exactly what the tools document (setup ergonomics is scored) but a marketplace follows its head, so pass 2's re-setup can install a different version than pass 1; B freezes the code both passes run, at the cost of not exercising the documented install. **Recommendation: B**, with the documented marketplace install still run once in pass 1's setup as a *recorded* ergonomics check (not the code the sessions load). **Switch to A:** set `route = "marketplace"` in `[plugins]` in `pins.toml`. | 3, 9, 10 |
| OD10 | **Web tools.** **SPEC CHANGE — needs explicit owner approval** (design is silent; affects methods with research steps). *Reviewer: agree; F-8 recording added.* | `WebSearch` and `WebFetch` disallowed for every arm, control included; attempted research is recorded per arm so Plan 3 doesn't score it as a weakness. | 5, 6, 20 |
| OD11 | **Permissions and `AskUserQuestion`.** *Reviewer: agree (sandbox is the boundary once S-1 is fixed — it is).* | `--permission-mode bypassPermissions` inside the sandbox; `AskUserQuestion` disallowed, so questions arrive as text. Probe P4 records what 2.1.281 does. | 6, 25 |
| OD12 | **An escalation first arising in pass 2.** *Reviewer: agree.* | Owner answers it; appended to the brief; flagged as a pass-2 addendum. Arms already run in pass 2 are not re-run. | 13, 23 |
| OD13 | **Arm order.** *Reviewer: partly disagree.* Pass 1 (agreed): random order from a recorded seed; the control does not go first. **A (draft 1):** pass 2 uses the same order. **B (reviewer):** pass 2 uses the **reverse** order. *Trade-off:* A keeps each arm's position (and owner fatigue level) the same in both passes; B counterbalances owner drift over weeks and the pass-2 addenda that favour later arms. **Recommendation: B.** **Switch to A:** set `pass2_order = "same"` in `[order]` in `pins.toml`. | 23 |
| OD14 | **Runaway limits.** **SPEC CHANGE — needs explicit owner approval** ("stalled" as an arm result is a new rule). *Reviewer: disagree on the numbers.* **A (draft 1):** 4 h wall-clock per arm turn; 80 PO exchanges per attempt; a breach ends the attempt as `stalled`/`timeout`, an arm outcome. **B (reviewer):** 8 h and 250 exchanges, and the owner may reclassify the *first* breach per arm as a harness fault. *Trade-off:* A bounds a runaway sooner and costs less; B stops the cap from penalising documented question-heavy flows (BMAD elicitation, Spec Kit clarify) and long autonomous implement phases. **Recommendation: B** (quality of conclusion over cost, design §1). **Switch to A:** in `pins.toml [limits]` set `turn_seconds = 14400`, `exchanges_per_attempt = 80`, `owner_reclassifies_first_breach = false`. | 15, 22 |
| OD15 | **Quality tools.** *Reviewer: agree with fixes — C-1 applied; the sampling preference is a choice.* `mypy --strict`, `ruff --isolated` (+ the arm's own ruff config), `mutmut` over the arm's own tests in a separate copy. **A (draft 1):** mutmut per step capped at 60 min, a partial score marked partial. **B (reviewer):** a fixed seeded sample of 200 mutants per step, so scores compare across arms regardless of code size. *Trade-off:* A measures the whole suite when it fits and is size-dependent when it doesn't; B always compares like with like but depends on listing mutants without running them, which Task 19's docker test verifies on mutmut's pinned version (if that test fails, only A is available). **Recommendation: B**, falling back to A automatically if the listing test fails. **Switch to A:** set `mutation_sample = 0` in `pins.toml [limits]`. | 19 |
| OD16 | **Credentials.** *Reviewer: agree with additions (S-2, S-3, PAT expiry applied).* | `claude setup-token` OAuth token in the keychain (`bakeoff-claude-oauth`), stdin → fd 3. The owner confirms the subscription terms allow this use. GitHub: one fine-grained PAT per GitHub-tracker arm per pass, that repo only, Issues RW + Metadata R, **expiry ≤ 45 days**, pass-1 PATs revoked at pass-1 close-out. | 5, 9, 23, 27 |
| OD17 | **Results path.** **SPEC CHANGE — needs explicit owner approval** (trivial: design §7 predates two passes). *Reviewer: agree.* | `results/<pass>/<arm>/<NN>/`; dry runs in `results/dry*/` (gitignored), probe in `results/probe/`, canary in `results/canary/`. | 1, 20 |
| OD18 | **Cost unit and source.** *Reviewer: agree.* | USD from `result.total_cost_usd`, labelled "client-side estimate (OAuth subscription; not a bill)"; tokens alongside; arm, PO and harness (teammate) kept apart. | 20 |
| OD19 | **Session granularity.** *Reviewer: agree, with an exception.* **A (draft 1):** one Claude session per attempt, resumed for every turn; each step starts a fresh session. **B (reviewer):** as A, **plus** a fresh session before any command the method's docs say to run in a new context (per-arm `fresh_session_before`, each entry cited). *Trade-off:* A is simplest and identical; B runs context-hygiene methods as documented but needs per-tool citations that are not yet researched (review marks them UNVERIFIED). **Recommendation: B with empty lists until P9/research finds citations** — i.e. code ready, behaviour identical to A until a citation is added. **Switch:** nothing to switch for A; for B add cited entries to `[drive] fresh_session_before` per arm. | 12, 15 |
| OD20 | **What pass 2 resets.** **SPEC CHANGE — needs explicit owner approval** (design §5 says only "repo"). *Reviewer: agree (requires S-5 pinning — applied, plus OD9).* | Fresh per-arm home and work directories; setup and checklist re-run with the same pins; the setup manifest must equal pass 1's (or the owner decides); pass 1's repos kept as bundles. | 8, 9, 23 |
| OD21 | **What the PO sees.** *Reviewer: agree; mount `/work` read-only (applied).* | PO brief, contract sections in force, current step brief, this step's Q&A, and the arm's whole `/work` **read-only** (so worktree specs/plans can be reviewed). Never hidden tests, reference or other arms. | 13 |
| OD22 | **Constitution / charter / steering / context text.** *Reviewer: agree; G3 and F-7 applied.* | One fixed text (design §3.1's constraints, already in step 1's brief) given through each method's documented slot: Spec Kit constitution, Spec Kitty charter, cc-sdd steering, **OpenSpec `config.yaml` context (new)**, **BMAD setup session with an instruction (changed)**. superpowers and control have no slot and get nothing. | 10, 11 |
| OD23 | **Where hidden tests run** (draft 1 self-review item 10). **SPEC CHANGE — needs explicit owner approval** (design §7 says "from outside against the arm's CLI"). *Reviewer: agree.* | A separate tester container runs the hidden tests on the arm's committed `main` — outside every arm session, inside the stage. It is the only design that resolves every PLAN-2 NOTE. | 18 |
| OD24 | **Spec Kit's GitHub export** (review G4). Design §2 says Spec Kit's tracker *is* the export to GitHub Issues. **A:** leave `/speckit-taskstoissues` to the method's own choice (the speckit tracker column may stay empty). **B:** add `/speckit-taskstoissues` to speckit's `must_run` (sent once after `/speckit-tasks` has run in a step) and, **only if probe P11 finds the skill requires the GitHub MCP server**, give the speckit arm the official GitHub MCP server configured with its Issues-only PAT (the one arm with an MCP server). *Trade-off:* A keeps every arm's tool surface identical but may leave design §2's tracker for Spec Kit empty by construction; B delivers what design §2 names at the cost of an arm-specific MCP server. **Recommendation: B** (design §2 is explicit). A is a **SPEC CHANGE — needs explicit owner approval**. **Switch to A:** set `export_after = ""` in `arms/speckit.toml [drive]`. | 12, 15, 25 |
| OD25 | **Approve the canary run** (Task 26): the control arm, steps 1–2, real model and real GitHub tracker, owner present, before go/no-go. Spends real quota (estimated as one arm-step pair). Not a spec change. **Recommendation: approve.** | 26 |

## Global Constraints

- **Pins (design §2, §11):** Claude Code `2.1.281`; model `claude-opus-5-5`, passed as `--model` on every
  session (arm, PO, teammate); tool versions per OD9. Every pin is in `runner/config/pins.toml`, the single
  source of truth, copied into every `run.json`. No upgrade mid-bake-off: images are built **once**, saved
  with `docker save`, and the runner refuses to start a step if an image ID differs from the lock or from the
  arm's setup record.
- **Model pin is verified, not assumed (design §11):** every `assistant` line in every transcript file
  (arm, subagents, PO, teammate), every stream `assistant` event, every `system/init` event and every
  `result.modelUsage` key is checked against `accepted_models`; the stream's `claude_code_version` is checked
  against the pin. A step with no arm transcript is a fault (`model-pin:no-evidence`). Anything else outside
  the pins is a harness fault and the step is re-run from its snapshot.
- **Isolation (design §6):** own `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` per arm; `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_AUTO_MEMORY=1`,
  `DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER=1`, `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC=1`, `OPENSPEC_TELEMETRY=0` in every arm
  container; `--strict-mcp-config` with no MCP servers (OD24 B is the only possible exception, speckit only);
  the owner's `~/.claude` is never mounted.
- **Container stage (design §11, spike B):** `--rm --init --read-only --cap-drop ALL --security-opt
  no-new-privileges --user 1000:1000 --pids-limit 2048 --cpus 4 --memory 8g`, per-arm `--internal` network
  plus allowlisting proxy, `--label bakeoff.run=<pass>/<arm>`, supervisor-chosen `--name`. Cancellation is
  `docker stop -t 30`, then `docker rm -f`. A startup sweep removes labelled containers **and networks**.
- **The host never touches an arm repo with git (review S-1).** All git on arm repos goes through
  `runner/gitops.py`, which runs it in a `--network none` container. Host Python reads of arm-controlled trees
  never follow symlinks; tar restores use `filter="data"`. Publishing pushes from a host clone of a bundle.
- **Accepted boundary (review S-4):** GitHub-tracker arms can reach `api.github.com`, so any *public* GitHub
  content is reachable (CONNECT filtering cannot restrict paths). That is accepted; the runner verifies that
  no `troupe-run` repo is public during the bake-off (G7).
- **Credential (spike B, OD16):** never in env, argv, a file inside the container, a log, `docker inspect` or
  committed evidence. Stdin line 1 → wrapper → fd 3 → `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN_FILE_DESCRIPTOR=3`. It *is*
  reachable from the Claude process's memory by a same-uid tool (spike B FINDINGS L316, L366) — by design,
  acceptable for trusted prompts. Every results commit and every publish is secret-scanned (Task 21).
- **Outcome is derived, not trusted (spike B Q8):** `result.is_error` is checked; no `result` event = cancelled
  or crashed; `api_retry` with `authentication_failed` → abort at once; transient API errors are retried in
  place before they become harness faults; `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_RETRIES=4`.
- **Hidden tests never enter an arm repo or an arm session container (design §6).**
- **The PO brief the arm experiences is recorded** (SHA-256 of brief, contract, step brief per step).
- **One thing at a time:** `state/runner.lock`; preflight refuses to test if another `bakeoff.*` container runs
  or if the host **or the Docker VM** 1-minute load exceeds `load_factor × cpus`.
- **GitHub writes are owner-confirmed, one at a time;** repo creation is never done by the runner.
- **Deterministic runner:** all choices derive from config and recorded inputs; every session's full input and
  output is saved.
- **Test markers:** unmarked = pure unit tests (always run); `docker` = needs Docker and built images;
  `network` = also needs internet (real arm setup); `live` = spends real model quota (probe and canary only,
  never run by `pytest` without `--run-live`).

## Review Focus

1. **Credential leakage.** The token must not be in argv, env, `docker inspect`, logs, results, transcripts
   or on disk in the arm's home. (It is reachable from the Claude process's memory by design — spike B.)
   Tasks 5, 6, 21, 24 and probe P10 test this.
2. **Unequal treatment of arms.** Anything that differs per arm is data in `arms/<arm>.toml` with a
   CANDIDATES citation; harness-authored text is identical across arms. Reviewers: diff the seven toml files.
3. **Leaks of hidden information into arm sessions.** The PO never sees tests; no arm mount contains harness
   paths or another role's messages; pass 2 starts from nothing (OD20).
4. **Harness faults misclassified as arm faults (and vice versa)** — Task 22's table, incl. `flaky-arm`.
5. **PLAN-2 NOTEs** — resolved by construction in Task 18, exercised by Task 24.
6. **Tests whose names overclaim** (owner's rule) — see the test-name audit at the end.
7. **Host exposure to arm-controlled files** (new): no host git, no symlink-following reads, safe tar restore.

## File Structure

```
bakeoff-specs/
  .gitignore                          # + state/, results/dry*/, results/**/scratch/
  .githooks/pre-commit                # runs `runner scan --staged` (Task 21)
  runner/
    __init__.py
    __main__.py                       # `uv run python -m runner <subcommand>`
    cli.py                            # argparse front end; one function per subcommand
    config.py                         # load/validate pins.toml + arms/*.toml → frozen dataclasses
    paths.py                          # every state/ and results/ path (single source)
    docker.py                         # pure argv builders + executor + label sweep (containers and networks)
    egress.py                         # allowlist files, per-arm internal network + proxy lifecycle
    credentials.py                    # keychain read (owner), dummy (dry run); stdin payload; per-turn guard
    session.py                        # one claude turn in a container: stream + stderr capture, outcome
    transcripts.py                    # symlink-safe collection; model-pin + version verification
    gitops.py                         # EVERY git command on an arm repo, run in a container (S-1)
    repo.py                           # arm repo init, snapshots, restore, export, bundle, GitHub plan/verify, publish
    commands.py                       # installed commands/skills; invocation form (F-2)
    checklist.py                      # setup flow, verify scripts, owner items, manifest; gate for step 1
    handoff.py                        # step messages (brief + contract + delivery sentence), step-5 split
    po.py                             # PO session, protocol parse, escalation, brief append
    owner.py                          # terminal owner + scripted owner (dry only); timing
    drive.py                          # per-attempt turn loop
    handedit.py                       # step-2 teammate edit, probe, detection facts
    tracker.py                        # step-6 issue: github | beads | dry; snapshots; round-trip facts
    hidden.py                         # tester container: prep (copy A/B), pytest, junit, flake vs reference
    quality.py                        # mypy, ruff, mutmut (copy B)
    evidence.py                       # run.json, cost.json, git.json, summary.md …
    secrets.py                        # secret scan of results/ and publish clones (S-2)
    faults.py                         # HarnessFault, ledger (namespaced), halt rule
    stepflow.py                       # one step: attempts, harness re-runs, tests, quality, evidence
    preflight.py                      # lock, idle (host + VM), image IDs
    orchestrate.py                    # order / setup / run-arm / reset-pass2 / dry-run / canary
    images.py                         # pin digests, build once, save, lock
    probe.py                          # Task 25 live probe (owner-run)
    config/
      pins.toml
      arms/{control,superpowers,openspec,speckit,speckitty,bmad,ccsdd}.toml
      arms/{dryrun,dryrun-broken,dryrun-pinfault,dryrun-edge}.toml     # dry-run arms (dry = true)
      images.lock.json                # generated by `runner images build`
      dryrun-owner.toml               # scripted owner answers, dry arms only
      po-protocol.md                  # harness-authored PO instructions (identical for all arms)
      teammate-prompt.md              # OD1 hand edit instructions
      project-principles.md           # OD22 text
      scenario.toml                   # step-6 title; fixed harness messages; step-5 delivery (OD6)
    images/
      base/Dockerfile, base/bk-claude-turn
      proxy/Dockerfile, proxy/tinyproxy.conf
      tester/Dockerfile, tester/requirements.in, tester/requirements.txt, tester/mutmut-requirements.txt
      arm/openspec/{Dockerfile,package.json,package-lock.json}
      arm/ccsdd/{Dockerfile,package.json,package-lock.json}
      arm/speckit/{Dockerfile,requirements.txt}
      arm/speckitty/{Dockerfile,requirements.txt,package.json,package-lock.json}
      arm/superpowers/Dockerfile, arm/bmad/Dockerfile            # vendored plugins (OD9 B)
      dryrun/Dockerfile, dryrun/fake_claude.py
  checklists/
    README.md                         # owner one-time steps
    <arm>.md                          # machine part + owner part + known traps
    verify/lib.sh, verify/<arm>.sh    # machine part, runs inside the arm container, prints JSON lines
  tests/runner/                       # unit + docker/network/live-marked tests; conftest.py holds test helpers
  state/                              # gitignored
  results/<pass>/<arm>/<NN>/          # evidence (committed)
```

`state/<pass>/<arm>/` (all via `paths.py`): `home/` (mounted at `/home/actor`; `.claude/` =
`CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`, `.config/gh`, caches), `work/` (mounted at `/work`; holds `bakeoff-specs-<arm>/` and any
sibling worktrees), `inbox/<role>/` (one file per message; **only the current file** is mounted, read-only, at
`/inbox/msg.md`), `snapshots/step-NN.tar`, `scratch/`, `progress.json`.

`results/<pass>/<arm>/<NN>/`: `run.json`, `handoff.md`, `turns/<stem>.{prompt.md,stream.jsonl.gz,stderr.gz}`,
`transcripts/a<k>/…jsonl.gz`, `po/a<k>-NNN/{inputs, turns/, transcripts/, decision.json, model-pin.json}`, `questions.jsonl`,
`owner-gates.jsonl`, `model-pin.json`, `cost.json`, `egress.log`, `git.json`,
`hidden/{junit.xml,summary.json,pytest.log,flake.json}`, `quality/{mypy.txt,ruff.json,ruff-arm.json,mutation.json,summary.json}`,
`summary.md`, `hand-edit/` (step 02), `tracker.json`, `attempts/<k>-<label>/`.

---

## Tasks

Task order: 1–8 container stage, one-turn sessions, safe git; 9–11 arm setup and checklists; 12–17 driving a
step; 18–21 measuring, recording, secret scan; 22–23 rules and orchestration; 24 dry run; 25 live probe
(owner-run, spends a little); 26 canary (owner-approved, spends quota); 27 go/no-go gate. **No real arm runs
until Task 27 is signed off.**

### Task 1: Runner package, pins, arm config schema, paths

**Files:**
- Create: `runner/__init__.py` (empty), `runner/__main__.py`, `runner/cli.py`, `runner/config.py`, `runner/paths.py`
- Create: `runner/config/pins.toml`, `runner/config/arms/control.toml` (other arms: Tasks 3, 10–12)
- Create: `tests/runner/__init__.py` (empty), `tests/runner/conftest.py`, `tests/runner/test_config.py`, `tests/runner/test_paths.py`
- Modify: `tests/conftest.py` (markers `docker`, `network`, `live`; option `--run-live`), `.gitignore`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `config.load_pins() -> Pins`; `config.load_arm(name) -> Arm`; `config.all_arms() -> list[str]` (the
  seven real arms, alphabetical); `config.ConfigError`; `paths.Paths(pass_, arm)`; `paths.brief_path(pass_)`;
  module constants `paths.REPO_ROOT, STATE, RESULTS, LOCK, FAULTS, HALTED, IMAGES_SAVE`.
- `Pins` fields (complete): `claude_version, model, accepted_models, synthetic_models, max_retries,
  permission_mode, disallowed_tools, binary_sha256, images (dict), digests (dict), limits (Limits),
  pass2_order, plugin_route, egress_setup_common, egress_run_common, egress_po, egress_tester_prep`.
- `Limits` fields: `turn_seconds, exchanges_per_attempt, owner_reclassifies_first_breach,
  attempts_no_progress, mutation_seconds, mutation_sample, load_factor, cpus, memory, pids, transient_backoff`.
- `Arm` fields (complete — every later task's keys exist from the start, review Q-5): `name, tool, version,
  citation, image, tracker, repo_name, env, egress_setup, egress_run, setup_shell, setup_agent,
  setup_post_shell, plugins (list[Plugin]), smoke (Smoke | None), manifest_paths, entry, bug_entry, must_run, must_run_source,
  fresh_session_before, export_after, dry`.
- `Plugin(name, marketplace, install, version, repo, tag, sha, subdir)`; `Smoke(prompt, expect_skill)`.

```python
# tests/runner/test_config.py
import pytest
from runner import config

def test_pins_pin_claude_code_and_the_model():
    p = config.load_pins()
    assert p.claude_version == "2.1.281"
    assert p.model == "claude-opus-5-5"
    assert p.accepted_models == ["claude-opus-5-5"]

def test_web_tools_and_ask_user_question_are_disallowed_for_every_session():
    assert set(config.load_pins().disallowed_tools) >= {"WebSearch", "WebFetch", "AskUserQuestion"}

def test_od_parameters_have_their_recommended_defaults():
    p = config.load_pins()
    assert p.pass2_order == "reverse"                      # OD13 B
    assert p.plugin_route == "plugin-dir"                  # OD9 B
    assert (p.limits.turn_seconds, p.limits.exchanges_per_attempt) == (28800, 250)   # OD14 B
    assert p.limits.owner_reclassifies_first_breach is True
    assert p.limits.mutation_sample == 200                 # OD15 B

def test_control_arm_loads_with_plain_entry():
    a = config.load_arm("control")
    assert a.entry == "{handoff}" and a.tracker == "github" and a.must_run == [] and a.smoke is None

def test_unknown_key_in_arm_file_is_rejected(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    (tmp_path / "x.toml").write_text('name="x"\nsurprise=1\n')
    monkeypatch.setattr(config, "ARMS_DIR", tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(config.ConfigError, match="surprise"):
        config.load_arm("x")

def test_smoke_table_is_accepted_from_the_start(tmp_path, monkeypatch):     # review Q-5
    text = (config.ARMS_DIR / "control.toml").read_text().replace(
        'manifest_paths = [".claude"]', 'manifest_paths = [".claude"]\nsmoke = { prompt = "p", expect_skill = "s" }')
    (tmp_path / "control.toml").write_text(text)
    monkeypatch.setattr(config, "ARMS_DIR", tmp_path)
    assert config.load_arm("control").smoke == config.Smoke("p", "s")

def test_arm_cannot_widen_the_run_allowlist_with_an_unanchored_pattern(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    text = (config.ARMS_DIR / "control.toml").read_text().replace(
        "run = ['^api\\.github\\.com$']", "run = ['api.github.com']")
    assert "run = ['api.github.com']" in text
    (tmp_path / "control.toml").write_text(text)
    monkeypatch.setattr(config, "ARMS_DIR", tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(config.ConfigError, match="anchored"):
        config.load_arm("control")

def test_must_run_needs_a_citation(tmp_path, monkeypatch):                  # review F-5
    text = (config.ARMS_DIR / "control.toml").read_text().replace('must_run = []', 'must_run = ["/x"]').replace(
        'must_run_source = "no methodology: no closing command"', 'must_run_source = ""')
    (tmp_path / "control.toml").write_text(text)
    monkeypatch.setattr(config, "ARMS_DIR", tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(config.ConfigError, match="must_run_source"):
        config.load_arm("control")
```

```python
# tests/runner/test_paths.py
from runner.paths import Paths, RESULTS, STATE, brief_path, REPO_ROOT

def test_state_and_results_are_per_pass_and_arm():
    p = Paths("pass1", "openspec")
    assert p.home == STATE / "pass1" / "openspec" / "home"
    assert p.repo == STATE / "pass1" / "openspec" / "work" / "bakeoff-specs-openspec"
    assert p.step(2) == RESULTS / "pass1" / "openspec" / "02"

def test_container_paths_are_constants():                                    # renamed (review Q-11)
    p = Paths("pass2", "speckit")
    assert p.container_repo == "/work/bakeoff-specs-speckit" and p.container_home == "/home/actor"

def test_every_role_has_its_own_inbox():                                     # review C-12
    p = Paths("pass1", "control")
    assert len({p.inbox("arm"), p.inbox("po"), p.inbox("teammate")}) == 3

def test_real_passes_use_the_real_brief_and_other_passes_a_copy():
    assert brief_path("pass1") == REPO_ROOT / "scenario" / "po-brief.md"
    assert brief_path("dry1") == STATE / "dry1" / "po-brief.md"
    assert brief_path("canary") == STATE / "canary" / "po-brief.md"
```

```toml
# runner/config/pins.toml — single source of truth for every pinned value (design §2, §11)
[claude]
version = "2.1.281"
model = "claude-opus-5-5"
accepted_models = ["claude-opus-5-5"]        # probe P1 confirms the exact transcript string
synthetic_models = ["<synthetic>"]           # Claude Code's own non-model messages; counted, not faults
max_retries = 4
permission_mode = "bypassPermissions"        # OD11
disallowed_tools = ["WebSearch", "WebFetch", "AskUserQuestion"]   # OD10, OD11
binary_sha256 = ""                           # written by `runner images pin` (Task 2); build refuses while empty

[images]
base = "bakeoff-base:cc2.1.281"
tester = "bakeoff-tester:1"
proxy = "bakeoff-egress:1"
dryrun = "bakeoff-arm-dryrun:1"
uv = "0.12.19"
gh = "2.101.0"
node_major = "22"
python = "3.12"

[images.digests]                             # written by `runner images pin` (Task 2); build refuses while empty
debian = ""
node = ""
uv = ""

[limits]
turn_seconds = 28800                         # OD14 B (A: 14400)
exchanges_per_attempt = 250                  # OD14 B (A: 80)
owner_reclassifies_first_breach = true       # OD14 B (A: false)
attempts_no_progress = 3                     # design §9
mutation_seconds = 3600                      # OD15 A cap; also the B safety cap
mutation_sample = 200                        # OD15 B (A: 0 = no sampling)
load_factor = 0.5                            # idle-machine preflight (host and Docker VM)
cpus = "4"
memory = "8g"
pids = 2048
transient_backoff = [60, 300, 900]           # review C-4: in-place retries of transient API errors

[order]
pass2_order = "reverse"                      # OD13 B (A: "same")

[plugins]
route = "plugin-dir"                         # OD9 B (A: "marketplace")

[egress]
# Anchored ERE host patterns for tinyproxy (spike B Q3). Setup = install/init time; run = step sessions.
setup_common = ['^api\.anthropic\.com$', '^registry\.npmjs\.org$', '^pypi\.org$', '^files\.pythonhosted\.org$',
                '^github\.com$', '^api\.github\.com$', '^codeload\.github\.com$', '^objects\.githubusercontent\.com$',
                '^raw\.githubusercontent\.com$', '^release-assets\.githubusercontent\.com$']
run_common = ['^api\.anthropic\.com$', '^pypi\.org$', '^files\.pythonhosted\.org$']
po = ['^api\.anthropic\.com$']
tester_prep = ['^pypi\.org$', '^files\.pythonhosted\.org$']
```

```python
# runner/config.py
"""Pins and per-arm configuration. Everything that differs between arms lives in config/arms/<arm>.toml."""
from __future__ import annotations
import re, tomllib
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

CONFIG_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "config"
ARMS_DIR = CONFIG_DIR / "arms"
REAL_ARMS = ("bmad", "ccsdd", "control", "openspec", "speckit", "speckitty", "superpowers")

class ConfigError(ValueError):
    pass

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Limits:
    turn_seconds: int
    exchanges_per_attempt: int
    owner_reclassifies_first_breach: bool
    attempts_no_progress: int
    mutation_seconds: int
    mutation_sample: int
    load_factor: float
    cpus: str
    memory: str
    pids: int
    transient_backoff: list[int]

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Pins:
    claude_version: str
    model: str
    accepted_models: list[str]
    synthetic_models: list[str]
    max_retries: int
    permission_mode: str
    disallowed_tools: list[str]
    binary_sha256: str
    images: dict[str, str]
    digests: dict[str, str]
    limits: Limits
    pass2_order: str
    plugin_route: str
    egress_setup_common: list[str]
    egress_run_common: list[str]
    egress_po: list[str]
    egress_tester_prep: list[str]

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Plugin:
    name: str             # plugin name as installed, e.g. "superpowers"
    marketplace: str      # e.g. "anthropics/claude-plugins-official" ("" = already added by an earlier entry)
    install: str          # e.g. "superpowers@claude-plugins-official"
    version: str          # pinned; verified by the checklist
    repo: str             # GitHub repo holding the plugin source, e.g. "obra/superpowers" (OD9 B)
    tag: str              # e.g. "v6.4.2"
    sha: str = ""         # the tag's commit, written by `runner images pin`
    subdir: str = ""      # plugin root inside the repo ("" = repo root)

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Smoke:
    prompt: str
    expect_skill: str

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Arm:
    name: str
    tool: str
    version: str
    citation: str
    image: str
    tracker: str
    repo_name: str
    env: dict[str, str]
    egress_setup: list[str]
    egress_run: list[str]
    setup_shell: list[str]
    setup_agent: list[str]
    setup_post_shell: list[str]                  # shell steps after the agent turns (e.g. BMAD script locks)
    plugins: list[Plugin]
    smoke: Smoke | None
    manifest_paths: list[str]
    entry: str
    bug_entry: str
    must_run: list[str]
    must_run_source: str
    fresh_session_before: list[dict[str, str]]    # [{command, source}] (OD19 B)
    export_after: str                              # speckit only (OD24 B): the command after which export runs
    dry: bool = False

_ANCHORED = re.compile(r"^\^.+\$$")
TOP = {"name", "tool", "version", "citation", "image", "tracker", "repo_name", "env", "egress", "setup", "drive", "dry"}
SETUP = {"shell", "agent", "post_shell", "plugins", "smoke", "manifest_paths"}
DRIVE = {"entry", "bug_entry", "must_run", "must_run_source", "fresh_session_before", "export_after"}

def _check_hosts(patterns: list[str], where: str) -> list[str]:
    for p in patterns:
        if not _ANCHORED.match(p):
            raise ConfigError(f"{where}: host pattern {p!r} must be anchored (^…$), spike B Q3")
    return list(patterns)

def _only(d: dict, allowed: set[str], where: str) -> dict:
    extra = set(d) - allowed
    if extra:
        raise ConfigError(f"{where}: unknown key(s) {sorted(extra)}")
    return d

def load_pins() -> Pins:
    raw = tomllib.loads((CONFIG_DIR / "pins.toml").read_text())
    c, e, im = raw["claude"], raw["egress"], dict(raw["images"])
    digests = im.pop("digests")
    if raw["order"]["pass2_order"] not in ("same", "reverse"):
        raise ConfigError("pins.order.pass2_order must be same|reverse")
    if raw["plugins"]["route"] not in ("marketplace", "plugin-dir"):
        raise ConfigError("pins.plugins.route must be marketplace|plugin-dir")
    return Pins(
        claude_version=c["version"], model=c["model"], accepted_models=list(c["accepted_models"]),
        synthetic_models=list(c["synthetic_models"]), max_retries=int(c["max_retries"]),
        permission_mode=c["permission_mode"], disallowed_tools=list(c["disallowed_tools"]),
        binary_sha256=c["binary_sha256"], images=im, digests=dict(digests),
        limits=Limits(**raw["limits"]), pass2_order=raw["order"]["pass2_order"],
        plugin_route=raw["plugins"]["route"],
        egress_setup_common=_check_hosts(e["setup_common"], "pins.egress.setup_common"),
        egress_run_common=_check_hosts(e["run_common"], "pins.egress.run_common"),
        egress_po=_check_hosts(e["po"], "pins.egress.po"),
        egress_tester_prep=_check_hosts(e["tester_prep"], "pins.egress.tester_prep"),
    )

def load_arm(name: str) -> Arm:
    where = f"arms/{name}.toml"
    raw = _only(tomllib.loads((ARMS_DIR / f"{name}.toml").read_text()), TOP, where)
    eg = _only(raw.get("egress", {}), {"setup", "run"}, where + " [egress]")
    su = _only(raw.get("setup", {}), SETUP, where + " [setup]")
    dr = _only(raw.get("drive", {}), DRIVE, where + " [drive]")
    if raw.get("tracker") not in ("github", "beads", "dry"):
        raise ConfigError(f"{where}: tracker must be github|beads|dry")
    if "{handoff}" not in dr.get("entry", "") or "{handoff}" not in dr.get("bug_entry", ""):
        raise ConfigError(f"{where}: entry and bug_entry must contain {{handoff}}")
    must_run = list(dr.get("must_run", []))
    if must_run and not dr.get("must_run_source"):
        raise ConfigError(f"{where}: must_run needs must_run_source (a CANDIDATES citation, review F-5)")
    fresh = [dict(x) for x in dr.get("fresh_session_before", [])]
    if any(not x.get("source") for x in fresh):
        raise ConfigError(f"{where}: every fresh_session_before entry needs a source (OD19)")
    sm = su.get("smoke")
    return Arm(
        name=raw["name"], tool=raw["tool"], version=raw["version"], citation=raw.get("citation", ""),
        image=raw["image"], tracker=raw["tracker"], repo_name=raw["repo_name"], env=dict(raw.get("env", {})),
        egress_setup=_check_hosts(eg.get("setup", []), where), egress_run=_check_hosts(eg.get("run", []), where),
        setup_shell=list(su.get("shell", [])), setup_agent=list(su.get("agent", [])),
        setup_post_shell=list(su.get("post_shell", [])),
        plugins=[Plugin(**p) for p in su.get("plugins", [])],
        smoke=Smoke(sm["prompt"], sm["expect_skill"]) if sm else None,
        manifest_paths=list(su.get("manifest_paths", [".claude"])),
        entry=dr["entry"], bug_entry=dr["bug_entry"], must_run=must_run,
        must_run_source=dr.get("must_run_source", ""), fresh_session_before=fresh,
        export_after=dr.get("export_after", ""), dry=bool(raw.get("dry", False)),
    )

def all_arms() -> list[str]:
    return list(REAL_ARMS)
```

```python
# runner/paths.py
"""Every on-disk location the runner uses. Nothing else in runner/ builds a state/ or results/ path."""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
STATE = REPO_ROOT / "state"
RESULTS = REPO_ROOT / "results"
LOCK = STATE / "runner.lock"
FAULTS = RESULTS / "faults.jsonl"          # real passes; dry passes are namespaced (Task 22)
HALTED = RESULTS / "HALTED"
IMAGES_SAVE = STATE / "images"             # `docker save` tars (Task 2)
REAL_PASSES = ("pass1", "pass2")

def brief_path(pass_: str) -> Path:
    """The real brief for real passes; a copy for dry runs, the probe and the canary (never edits the real one)."""
    return REPO_ROOT / "scenario" / "po-brief.md" if pass_ in REAL_PASSES else STATE / pass_ / "po-brief.md"

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Paths:
    pass_: str
    arm: str

    @property
    def base(self) -> Path: return STATE / self.pass_ / self.arm
    @property
    def home(self) -> Path: return self.base / "home"
    @property
    def config_dir(self) -> Path: return self.home / ".claude"
    @property
    def work(self) -> Path: return self.base / "work"
    @property
    def repo(self) -> Path: return self.work / f"bakeoff-specs-{self.arm}"
    def inbox(self, role: str) -> Path: return self.base / "inbox" / role
    @property
    def snapshots(self) -> Path: return self.base / "snapshots"
    @property
    def progress(self) -> Path: return self.base / "progress.json"
    @property
    def scratch(self) -> Path: return self.base / "scratch"
    @property
    def po_home(self) -> Path: return STATE / self.pass_ / "po-home" / self.arm
    def teammate_home(self, attempt: int) -> Path: return self.base / f"teammate-home-{attempt}"
    @property
    def results(self) -> Path: return RESULTS / self.pass_ / self.arm
    def step(self, n: int) -> Path: return self.results / f"{n:02d}"
    @property
    def setup_results(self) -> Path: return self.results / "00-setup"
    @property
    def container_repo(self) -> str: return f"/work/bakeoff-specs-{self.arm}"
    @property
    def container_home(self) -> str: return "/home/actor"
```

```python
# runner/cli.py — each later task adds one `register_<x>(sub)` function to REGISTRARS.
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
from typing import Callable

REGISTRARS: list[Callable[[argparse._SubParsersAction], None]] = []

def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="runner", description="bakeoff-specs runner")
    sub = p.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)
    for register in REGISTRARS:
        register(sub)
    return p

def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    ns = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
    return int(ns.func(ns) or 0)
```

```python
import pytest

def pytest_collection_modifyitems(config, items):
    if config.getoption("--run-live"):
        return
    skip = pytest.mark.skip(reason="live test: pass --run-live")
    for item in items:
        if "live" in item.keywords:
            item.add_marker(skip)
```

### Task 2: Base, proxy and tester images pinned by digest; build once; image lock

**Files:**
- Create: `runner/images/base/Dockerfile`, `runner/images/base/bk-claude-turn`
- Create: `runner/images/proxy/Dockerfile`, `runner/images/proxy/tinyproxy.conf` (copied from
  `../spike-claude-docker/proxy/`, unchanged)
- Create: `runner/images/tester/Dockerfile`, `runner/images/tester/requirements.in`,
  `runner/images/tester/requirements.txt` (generated), `runner/images/tester/mutmut-requirements.txt` (generated)
- Create: `runner/images.py`, `runner/config/images.lock.json` (generated), `tests/runner/test_images.py`,
  `tests/runner/test_images_docker.py`
- Modify: `runner/cli.py` (`images pin`, `images build [--rebuild]`, `images load`), `runner/config/pins.toml`
  (digests and `binary_sha256`, written by `images pin`)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `images.pin() -> dict` (resolves the three base-image digests, the Claude binary's SHA-256 and every
  plugin tag's commit SHA; writes them into `pins.toml` / `arms/*.toml`; prints a diff for the owner to commit);
  `images.build_all(arms, rebuild=False) -> dict` (refuses while any digest or `binary_sha256` is empty; refuses
  to overwrite an existing lock unless `rebuild=True`; builds, `docker save`s every image to
  `state/images/<tag>.tar`, writes the lock); `images.load_saved()`; `images.lock() -> dict`;
  `images.current_id(tag) -> str`.
- Consumed by: `preflight.check_images()` (Task 23).
- **Build once (review S-5):** the lock records every image's ID, its saved tar's SHA-256, the resolved
  digests and each tool's `--version`. `images build` after a lock exists needs `--rebuild` and the owner
  typing `rebuild`; Task 27's go/no-go build is the last one before pass 1, and pass 2 uses the same images.
- **Trust on first use:** `images pin` records what the Claude installer and the registries serve *today*;
  every later build verifies against those records (the Dockerfile checks the binary's SHA-256; `FROM` uses
  digests). That is the strongest pin available for a curl-installed binary.

```python
# tests/runner/test_images.py
import dataclasses
import pytest
from runner import config, images

def test_build_refuses_while_digests_are_unpinned(monkeypatch):
    p = dataclasses.replace(config.load_pins(), digests={"debian": "", "node": "x", "uv": "y"}, binary_sha256="z")
    monkeypatch.setattr(images.config, "load_pins", lambda: p)
    with pytest.raises(images.NotPinned, match="debian"):
        images.check_pinned()

def test_base_build_args_use_digests_not_tags():
    args = images.base_build_args(dataclasses.replace(
        config.load_pins(), digests={"debian": "sha256:a", "node": "sha256:b", "uv": "sha256:c"}, binary_sha256="d"))
    assert args["DEBIAN_IMAGE"] == "debian:bookworm-slim@sha256:a"
    assert args["NODE_IMAGE"] == "node:22-bookworm-slim@sha256:b"
    assert args["UV_IMAGE"] == "ghcr.io/astral-sh/uv:0.12.19@sha256:c"
    assert args["CLAUDE_SHA256"] == "d"

def test_cc_sdd_version_probe_reads_the_version_not_the_prefix():          # review C-15
    out = '{"dependencies": {"cc-sdd": {"version": "3.1.0"}}}'
    assert images.npm_version(out, "cc-sdd") == "3.1.0"
```

```python
# tests/runner/test_images_docker.py
import subprocess
import pytest
from runner import config

pytestmark = pytest.mark.docker

def run(image, *cmd, user="1000:1000"):
    return subprocess.run(["docker", "run", "--rm", "--user", user, "--network", "none", image, *cmd],
                          capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120)

def all_arm_images():
    from runner import images
    return sorted({config.load_arm(a).image for a in images._existing(config.all_arms())})

def test_base_image_has_the_pinned_claude_code():
    assert run(config.load_pins().images["base"], "claude", "--version").stdout.strip() == "2.1.281 (Claude Code)"

@pytest.mark.parametrize("image", all_arm_images())                            # review Q-11: really every arm image
@pytest.mark.parametrize("cmd,expect", [
    (["uv", "--version"], "uv 0.12.19"), (["python3", "--version"], "Python 3.12."), (["node", "--version"], "v22."),
    (["gh", "--version"], "gh version 2.101.0"), (["git", "--version"], "git version"),
    (["claude", "--version"], "2.1.281 (Claude Code)"),
])
def test_every_arm_image_has_the_identical_base_toolchain(image, cmd, expect):
    assert run(image, *cmd).stdout.startswith(expect)

def test_base_image_bakes_the_isolation_env():
    out = run(config.load_pins().images["base"], "env").stdout.splitlines()
    for line in ["CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_AUTO_MEMORY=1", "DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER=1",
                 "CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC=1", "OPENSPEC_TELEMETRY=0",
                 "CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=/home/actor/.claude", "UV_PYTHON_DOWNLOADS=never"]:
        assert line in out

def test_claude_binary_matches_the_pinned_sha256():
    r = run(config.load_pins().images["base"], "sha256sum", "/usr/local/bin/claude")
    assert r.stdout.split()[0] == config.load_pins().binary_sha256

def test_tester_image_has_harness_venv_quality_tools_and_the_reference():
    img = config.load_pins().images["tester"]
    for tool in ["pytest", "mypy", "ruff"]:
        assert run(img, f"/opt/harness/bin/{tool}", "--version").returncode == 0
    assert run(img, "/opt/harness/bin/python", "-c", "import ruamel.yaml, yaml").returncode == 0
    assert run(img, "/opt/reference/.venv/bin/troupe", "--help").returncode in (0, 2)   # reference for flake checks (C-2)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the Dockerfiles and the wrapper**

```dockerfile
# runner/images/base/Dockerfile — the one environment every arm shares (design §6, §11; spike B Q1).
# Every FROM is by digest (review S-5); the values come from pins.toml via `runner images build`.
ARG DEBIAN_IMAGE
ARG NODE_IMAGE
ARG UV_IMAGE
FROM ${NODE_IMAGE} AS node
FROM ${UV_IMAGE} AS uv

FROM ${DEBIAN_IMAGE} AS os
RUN apt-get update \
 && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends ca-certificates curl git procps jq \
 && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
RUN useradd --create-home --uid 1000 --shell /bin/bash actor

FROM os AS claude
ARG CLAUDE_VERSION
ARG CLAUDE_SHA256
USER actor
RUN curl -fsSL https://claude.ai/install.sh | bash -s ${CLAUDE_VERSION} \
 && cp -L /home/actor/.local/share/claude/versions/${CLAUDE_VERSION} /home/actor/claude-bin \
 && if [ -n "${CLAUDE_SHA256}" ]; then echo "${CLAUDE_SHA256}  /home/actor/claude-bin" | sha256sum -c -; fi

FROM os AS gh
ARG GH_VERSION
ARG TARGETARCH
RUN curl -fsSLo /tmp/gh.tgz https://github.com/cli/cli/releases/download/v${GH_VERSION}/gh_${GH_VERSION}_linux_${TARGETARCH}.tar.gz \
 && curl -fsSLo /tmp/sums https://github.com/cli/cli/releases/download/v${GH_VERSION}/gh_${GH_VERSION}_checksums.txt \
 && (cd /tmp && grep " gh_${GH_VERSION}_linux_${TARGETARCH}.tar.gz$" sums | awk '{print $1"  gh.tgz"}' | sha256sum -c -) \
 && tar -xzf /tmp/gh.tgz -C /tmp && cp /tmp/gh_${GH_VERSION}_linux_${TARGETARCH}/bin/gh /gh

FROM os
COPY --from=claude --chmod=0755 /home/actor/claude-bin /usr/local/bin/claude
COPY --from=uv /uv /uvx /usr/local/bin/
COPY --from=node /usr/local/bin/node /usr/local/bin/node
COPY --from=node /usr/local/lib/node_modules /usr/local/lib/node_modules
RUN ln -s ../lib/node_modules/npm/bin/npm-cli.js /usr/local/bin/npm \
 && ln -s ../lib/node_modules/npm/bin/npx-cli.js /usr/local/bin/npx
COPY --from=gh --chmod=0755 /gh /usr/local/bin/gh
ENV UV_PYTHON_INSTALL_DIR=/opt/python UV_PYTHON_PREFERENCE=only-managed
RUN uv python install 3.12 && chmod -R a+rX /opt/python \
 && ln -s "$(uv python find 3.12)" /usr/local/bin/python3
COPY --chmod=0755 bk-claude-turn /usr/local/bin/bk-claude-turn
USER actor
ENV CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_AUTO_MEMORY=1 \
    DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER=1 \
    CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC=1 \
    OPENSPEC_TELEMETRY=0 \
    CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=/home/actor/.claude \
    UV_PYTHON_DOWNLOADS=never \
    HOME=/home/actor
WORKDIR /work
CMD ["claude", "--version"]
```

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# runner/images/base/bk-claude-turn MESSAGE_FILE
# Runs one `claude -p` turn. Stdin line 1 is the OAuth token (spike B option a′): it becomes fd 3 and is
# never placed in env, argv or a file. Every other setting arrives as a non-secret BK_* env var.
set -euo pipefail
IFS= read -r T
exec 3< <(printf %s "$T"); unset T
args=(-p "$(cat "$1")" --model "$BK_MODEL" --output-format stream-json --verbose
      --permission-mode "$BK_PERMISSION_MODE" --strict-mcp-config)
if [ -n "${BK_MCP_CONFIG:-}" ]; then args+=(--mcp-config "$BK_MCP_CONFIG"); fi     # OD24 B only (speckit)
if [ -n "${BK_RESUME:-}" ]; then args+=(--resume "$BK_RESUME"); fi
if [ "${BK_ISOLATED:-0}" = 1 ]; then args+=(--setting-sources ""); fi
if [ -n "${BK_TOOLS:-}" ]; then args+=(--tools "$BK_TOOLS"); fi
for d in ${BK_PLUGIN_DIRS:-}; do args+=(--plugin-dir "$d"); done                   # OD9 B
# Variadic flag last so it cannot swallow the prompt (spike B Q8 surprise 9).
if [ -n "${BK_DISALLOWED:-}" ]; then args+=(--disallowedTools "$BK_DISALLOWED"); fi
unset BK_MODEL BK_PERMISSION_MODE BK_RESUME BK_ISOLATED BK_TOOLS BK_DISALLOWED BK_PLUGIN_DIRS BK_MCP_CONFIG
exec env CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN_FILE_DESCRIPTOR=3 claude "${args[@]}" </dev/null
```

```dockerfile
# runner/images/tester/Dockerfile — runs hidden tests and quality tools; never runs Claude.
# Built with the REPO ROOT as context (it copies reference/ for the flake-vs-reference check, review C-2).
ARG BASE_IMAGE
FROM ${BASE_IMAGE}
USER root
COPY runner/images/tester/requirements.txt runner/images/tester/mutmut-requirements.txt /opt/
RUN UV_PYTHON_DOWNLOADS=never uv venv --python 3.12 /opt/harness \
 && uv pip install --python /opt/harness/bin/python --require-hashes -r /opt/requirements.txt \
 && chmod -R a+rX /opt/harness /opt/mutmut-requirements.txt
COPY reference /opt/reference
RUN cd /opt/reference && rm -rf .venv && UV_PYTHON_DOWNLOADS=never uv sync --frozen --no-dev \
 && chmod -R a+rX /opt/reference
USER actor
ENV PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 UV_NO_SYNC=1
```

`runner/images/tester/requirements.in`:
```
pytest
pyyaml
ruamel.yaml>=0.19.1,<0.20
mypy
ruff
```
Generate the hash-locked files (commit both):
```bash
uv pip compile runner/images/tester/requirements.in --python-version 3.12 --generate-hashes \
  -o runner/images/tester/requirements.txt
echo mutmut | uv pip compile - --python-version 3.12 --generate-hashes -o runner/images/tester/mutmut-requirements.txt
```
The pytest / pyyaml / ruamel.yaml versions must equal the harness `uv.lock` (`uv tree --depth 1`); add `==`
pins to `requirements.in` if they differ, then regenerate.

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `runner/images.py` and the subcommands**

```python
# runner/images.py
from __future__ import annotations
import hashlib, json, re, subprocess, tomllib
from pathlib import Path
from runner import config, paths

IMAGES = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "images"
LOCK = config.CONFIG_DIR / "images.lock.json"
VERSION_PROBES = {"claude": ["claude", "--version"], "uv": ["uv", "--version"], "python3": ["python3", "--version"],
                  "node": ["node", "--version"], "gh": ["gh", "--version"], "git": ["git", "--version"]}
ARM_VERSION_PROBES = {
    "openspec": {"openspec": ["openspec", "--version"]},
    "speckit": {"specify": ["specify", "version"]},
    "speckitty": {"spec-kitty": ["spec-kitty", "--version"], "bd": ["bd", "version"],
                  "bd-sha256": ["sh", "-c", "sha256sum \"$(readlink -f \"$(command -v bd)\")\""]},   # S-5
    "ccsdd": {"cc-sdd": ["sh", "-c", "npm ls -g cc-sdd --depth=0 --json"]},       # parsed by npm_version (C-15)
}

class NotPinned(RuntimeError):
    pass

def _sh(*argv: str) -> str:
    return subprocess.run(argv, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()

def current_id(tag: str) -> str:
    return _sh("docker", "image", "inspect", "--format", "{{.Id}}", tag)

def _existing(arms: list[str]) -> list[str]:
    """Arms whose toml exists yet (Tasks 3 and 6 add them); lets Task 2 build and pin with only control present."""
    return [a for a in arms if (config.ARMS_DIR / f"{a}.toml").exists()]

def npm_version(json_text: str, pkg: str) -> str:
    return json.loads(json_text)["dependencies"][pkg]["version"]

def check_pinned() -> None:
    p = config.load_pins()
    missing = [k for k, v in p.digests.items() if not v] + (["binary_sha256"] if not p.binary_sha256 else [])
    if missing:
        raise NotPinned(f"unpinned: {missing}; run `uv run python -m runner images pin` and commit the result")

def base_build_args(p: config.Pins) -> dict[str, str]:
    im = p.images
    return {"DEBIAN_IMAGE": f"debian:bookworm-slim@{p.digests['debian']}",
            "NODE_IMAGE": f"node:{im['node_major']}-bookworm-slim@{p.digests['node']}",
            "UV_IMAGE": f"ghcr.io/astral-sh/uv:{im['uv']}@{p.digests['uv']}",
            "CLAUDE_VERSION": p.claude_version, "CLAUDE_SHA256": p.binary_sha256, "GH_VERSION": im["gh"]}

def _build(tag: str, context: Path, dockerfile: Path | None = None, target: str | None = None, **args: str) -> None:
    argv = ["docker", "build", "-q", "-t", tag]
    if dockerfile:
        argv += ["-f", str(dockerfile)]
    if target:
        argv += ["--target", target]
    for k, v in args.items():
        argv += ["--build-arg", f"{k}={v}"]
    subprocess.run([*argv, str(context)], check=True)

def _digest(ref: str) -> str:
    out = _sh("docker", "buildx", "imagetools", "inspect", ref, "--format", "{{json .Manifest.Digest}}")
    return json.loads(out)

def _set_toml_value(path: Path, key: str, value: str) -> None:
    """Replace `key = "..."` (first occurrence) in a toml file, keeping every other byte."""
    text = path.read_text()
    new, n = re.subn(rf'(?m)^({re.escape(key)}\s*=\s*)"[^"]*"', rf'\g<1>"{value}"', text, count=1)
    if n != 1:
        raise RuntimeError(f"{path}: key {key!r} not found")
    path.write_text(new)

def pin() -> dict:
    p = config.load_pins()
    im, pins_file = p.images, config.CONFIG_DIR / "pins.toml"
    got = {"debian": _digest("debian:bookworm-slim"), "node": _digest(f"node:{im['node_major']}-bookworm-slim"),
           "uv": _digest(f"ghcr.io/astral-sh/uv:{im['uv']}")}
    text = pins_file.read_text()
    for k, v in got.items():                                   # the [images.digests] table
        text = re.sub(rf'(?m)^({k}\s*=\s*)"[^"]*"', rf'\g<1>"{v}"', text, count=1)
    pins_file.write_text(text)
    args = base_build_args(config.load_pins()) | {"CLAUDE_SHA256": ""}
    _build("bakeoff-claude-stage:pin", IMAGES / "base", target="claude", **args)
    sha = _sh("docker", "run", "--rm", "--network", "none", "bakeoff-claude-stage:pin",
              "sha256sum", "/home/actor/claude-bin").split()[0]
    _set_toml_value(pins_file, "binary_sha256", sha)
    got["binary_sha256"] = sha
    for arm_name in _existing(config.all_arms()):              # OD9 B: tag -> commit SHA, recorded per plugin
        arm_file = config.ARMS_DIR / f"{arm_name}.toml"
        for pl in config.load_arm(arm_name).plugins:
            from runner import gitops                          # Task 8; imported lazily so Task 2 stands alone
            commit = gitops.ls_remote_tag(pl.repo, pl.tag)
            text = arm_file.read_text()
            text = text.replace(f'tag = "{pl.tag}", sha = "{pl.sha}"', f'tag = "{pl.tag}", sha = "{commit}"', 1)
            arm_file.write_text(text)
            got[f"{arm_name}:{pl.name}"] = commit
    print("Pinned. Review `git diff runner/config` and commit it.")
    return got

def _versions(tag: str, extra: dict[str, list[str]]) -> dict[str, str]:
    out = {}
    for name, cmd in {**VERSION_PROBES, **extra}.items():
        r = subprocess.run(["docker", "run", "--rm", "--network", "none", tag, *cmd], capture_output=True, text=True)
        text = (r.stdout or r.stderr).strip()
        if name == "cc-sdd" and r.returncode == 0:
            text = npm_version(r.stdout, "cc-sdd")
        out[name] = text.splitlines()[0] if text else f"exit {r.returncode}"
    return out

def _save(tag: str) -> str:
    paths.IMAGES_SAVE.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out = paths.IMAGES_SAVE / (tag.replace(":", "_").replace("/", "_") + ".tar")
    subprocess.run(["docker", "save", "-o", str(out), tag], check=True)
    return hashlib.sha256(out.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def build_all(arms: list[str], rebuild: bool = False) -> dict:
    check_pinned()
    if LOCK.exists() and not rebuild:
        raise RuntimeError("images.lock.json exists: images are built once (review S-5); use --rebuild deliberately")
    p = config.load_pins()
    im = p.images
    _build(im["base"], IMAGES / "base", **base_build_args(p))
    _build(im["proxy"], IMAGES / "proxy")
    _build(im["tester"], paths.REPO_ROOT, dockerfile=IMAGES / "tester" / "Dockerfile", BASE_IMAGE=im["base"])
    if (IMAGES / "dryrun" / "Dockerfile").exists():
        _build(im["dryrun"], paths.REPO_ROOT, dockerfile=IMAGES / "dryrun" / "Dockerfile", BASE_IMAGE=im["base"])
    lock: dict = {"digests": p.digests, "binary_sha256": p.binary_sha256, "images": {}, "arms": {}}
    for tag in [im["base"], im["proxy"], im["tester"]] + ([im["dryrun"]] if (IMAGES / "dryrun").exists() else []):
        lock["images"][tag] = {"id": current_id(tag), "tar_sha256": _save(tag)}
    lock["images"][im["base"]]["versions"] = _versions(im["base"], {})
    for name in _existing(arms):
        arm = config.load_arm(name)
        df = IMAGES / "arm" / name / "Dockerfile"
        if df.exists():
            _build(arm.image, IMAGES / "arm" / name, BASE_IMAGE=im["base"],
                   **{f"PLUGIN_{i}_{k.upper()}": getattr(pl, k) for i, pl in enumerate(arm.plugins)
                      for k in ("name", "repo", "tag", "sha", "subdir")})
        lock["arms"][name] = {"image": arm.image, "id": current_id(arm.image),
                              "tar_sha256": _save(arm.image) if df.exists() else lock["images"][im["base"]]["tar_sha256"],
                              "versions": _versions(arm.image, ARM_VERSION_PROBES.get(name, {}))}
    LOCK.write_text(json.dumps(lock, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n")
    return lock

def load_saved() -> None:
    for tar in sorted(paths.IMAGES_SAVE.glob("*.tar")):
        subprocess.run(["docker", "load", "-i", str(tar)], check=True)

def lock() -> dict:
    return json.loads(LOCK.read_text())
```
(`gitops.ls_remote_tag` is defined in Task 8 and imported inside `pin()`; in Task 2 no arm config exists beyond
`control`, so the plugin loop has nothing to do. Task 8 Step 6 re-runs `images pin` to record the plugin SHAs.)

Add to `cli.py`:
```python
def register_images(sub):
    from runner import images
    p = sub.add_parser("images", help="pin, build (once) and load the container images")
    s = p.add_subparsers(dest="images_cmd", required=True)
    s.add_parser("pin").set_defaults(func=lambda ns: print(json.dumps(images.pin(), indent=2)))
    b = s.add_parser("build"); b.add_argument("--rebuild", action="store_true")
    def build(ns):
        # During development (before any real pass) --rebuild is routine; once pass 1 has results it needs the owner.
        if ns.rebuild and (paths.RESULTS / "pass1").exists() and \
                input("Images are built once (review S-5). Type 'rebuild' to rebuild: ").strip() != "rebuild":
            return 1
        lock = images.build_all(config.all_arms(), rebuild=ns.rebuild)
        print(json.dumps(lock["arms"], indent=2))
    b.set_defaults(func=build)
    s.add_parser("load").set_defaults(func=lambda ns: images.load_saved())
REGISTRARS.append(register_images)
```
(with `import json` and `from runner import config, paths` at the top of `cli.py`). Every task below that
adds an image runs `uv run python -m runner images build --rebuild`; Task 27's build is the last one.

- [ ] **Step 5: Pin, build and test**

Run: `uv run pytest tests/runner/test_images.py -q` → pass.
Run: `uv run python -m runner images pin` (network needed); review `git diff runner/config/pins.toml`.
Run: `uv run python -m runner images build` (several minutes).
Run: `uv run pytest tests/runner/test_images_docker.py -q -m docker` → all pass.

- [ ] **Step 6: Commit**

```bash
git add runner/images runner/images.py runner/config/pins.toml runner/config/images.lock.json runner/cli.py tests/runner/test_images*.py
git commit -m "Runner: base, proxy and tester images pinned by digest and binary hash; built once, saved, locked"
```

### Task 3: Per-arm images (locked installs, vendored plugins) and the egress model

**Files:**
- Create: `runner/images/arm/openspec/{Dockerfile,package.json,package-lock.json}`,
  `runner/images/arm/ccsdd/{Dockerfile,package.json,package-lock.json}`,
  `runner/images/arm/speckit/{Dockerfile,requirements.txt}`,
  `runner/images/arm/speckitty/{Dockerfile,requirements.txt,package.json,package-lock.json}`,
  `runner/images/arm/superpowers/Dockerfile`, `runner/images/arm/bmad/Dockerfile`
- Create: `runner/config/arms/{superpowers,openspec,speckit,speckitty,bmad,ccsdd}.toml` (identity, image,
  tracker, env, egress, plugins; `[setup]` shell/agent are filled in Tasks 10–11 and `[drive]` in Task 12 —
  until then they hold the neutral values shown, which load)
- Create: `tests/runner/test_arm_configs.py`

**The egress model.** Each phase has its own proxy allowlist (anchored host regexes, spike B Q3). The
`--internal` network enforces it: no route out and no external DNS; the proxy is the only allow path and the log.

| Phase | When | Allowlist | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| Image build | `runner images build` (once) | unrestricted, but every input is pinned by digest, lockfile, hash or commit SHA | OS packages, Claude binary, uv, Python, Node, gh, method CLIs, vendored plugins |
| Setup | `runner setup <pass> <arm>` | `pins.egress.setup_common` + `arm.egress.setup` | marketplace ergonomics check, `specify init` templates, `bd init`, setup agent turns |
| Run | every step session | `pins.egress.run_common` + `arm.egress.run` | Anthropic API; PyPI for the arm's own `uv add`; `api.github.com` only for GitHub-tracker arms |
| Tester | hidden tests / quality | prep: PyPI only; run: `--network none` | `uv sync` of the arm's lockfile, then offline |
| PO / teammate | PO decisions, step-2 hand edit | Anthropic only | — |
| git | every `gitops` call | `--network none` | reads and commits on arm repos (review S-1) |

Every proxy decision is saved as `egress.log` in the step's results; refusals are listed in `summary.md`. A
refusal is not a fault by itself; the owner may declare one a harness fault if the method's documented
operation needed that host (Task 22, `faults declare`).

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the per-arm Dockerfiles** (control uses the base image unchanged)

```dockerfile
# runner/images/arm/openspec/Dockerfile — transitive deps pinned by package-lock.json (review S-5)
ARG BASE_IMAGE
FROM ${BASE_IMAGE}
USER root
COPY package.json package-lock.json /opt/openspec/
RUN cd /opt/openspec && npm ci --omit=dev --no-audit --no-fund && npm cache clean --force \
 && ln -s /opt/openspec/node_modules/.bin/openspec /usr/local/bin/openspec && chmod -R a+rX /opt/openspec
USER actor
```

```dockerfile
# runner/images/arm/ccsdd/Dockerfile — `npx cc-sdd@3.1.0` runs this same bin; PATH makes `cc-sdd` resolve to it
ARG BASE_IMAGE
FROM ${BASE_IMAGE}
USER root
COPY package.json package-lock.json /opt/ccsdd/
RUN cd /opt/ccsdd && npm ci --omit=dev --no-audit --no-fund && npm cache clean --force && chmod -R a+rX /opt/ccsdd \
 && npm install -g --offline --no-audit --no-fund /opt/ccsdd/node_modules/cc-sdd
USER actor
```

```dockerfile
# runner/images/arm/speckit/Dockerfile — hash-locked (review S-5)
ARG BASE_IMAGE
FROM ${BASE_IMAGE}
USER root
COPY requirements.txt /opt/speckit-requirements.txt
RUN UV_PYTHON_DOWNLOADS=never uv venv --python 3.12 /opt/venvs/speckit \
 && uv pip install --python /opt/venvs/speckit/bin/python --require-hashes -r /opt/speckit-requirements.txt \
 && ln -s /opt/venvs/speckit/bin/specify /usr/local/bin/specify && chmod -R a+rX /opt/venvs
USER actor
```

```dockerfile
# runner/images/arm/speckitty/Dockerfile — 3.2.7 stable, never the 4.0 RC line (design §2); beads from its lockfile.
# @beads/bd downloads its binary in postinstall; the binary's SHA-256 is recorded in images.lock.json (bd-sha256).
ARG BASE_IMAGE
FROM ${BASE_IMAGE}
USER root
COPY requirements.txt /opt/speckitty-requirements.txt
COPY package.json package-lock.json /opt/beads/
RUN UV_PYTHON_DOWNLOADS=never uv venv --python 3.12 /opt/venvs/speckitty \
 && uv pip install --python /opt/venvs/speckitty/bin/python --require-hashes -r /opt/speckitty-requirements.txt \
 && ln -s /opt/venvs/speckitty/bin/spec-kitty /usr/local/bin/spec-kitty \
 && cd /opt/beads && npm ci --omit=dev --no-audit --no-fund && npm cache clean --force \
 && ln -s /opt/beads/node_modules/.bin/bd /usr/local/bin/bd && chmod -R a+rX /opt/venvs /opt/beads
USER actor
```

```dockerfile
# runner/images/arm/superpowers/Dockerfile — OD9 B: the plugin at its tag's verified commit, vendored read-only.
ARG BASE_IMAGE
FROM ${BASE_IMAGE}
ARG PLUGIN_0_NAME
ARG PLUGIN_0_REPO
ARG PLUGIN_0_TAG
ARG PLUGIN_0_SHA
ARG PLUGIN_0_SUBDIR
USER root
RUN git clone --quiet --depth 1 --branch "${PLUGIN_0_TAG}" "https://github.com/${PLUGIN_0_REPO}" /tmp/p0 \
 && test "$(git -C /tmp/p0 rev-parse HEAD)" = "${PLUGIN_0_SHA}" \
 && mkdir -p /opt/plugins && cp -r "/tmp/p0/${PLUGIN_0_SUBDIR}" "/opt/plugins/${PLUGIN_0_NAME}" \
 && test -f "/opt/plugins/${PLUGIN_0_NAME}/.claude-plugin/plugin.json" \
 && rm -rf /tmp/p0 && chmod -R a+rX /opt/plugins
USER actor
```

```dockerfile
# runner/images/arm/bmad/Dockerfile — OD9 B: both BMAD plugins at the verified commit. The plugin roots inside the
# repo are recorded in arms/bmad.toml `subdir` and confirmed by probe P5; a wrong subdir fails the build here.
ARG BASE_IMAGE
FROM ${BASE_IMAGE}
ARG PLUGIN_0_NAME
ARG PLUGIN_0_REPO
ARG PLUGIN_0_TAG
ARG PLUGIN_0_SHA
ARG PLUGIN_0_SUBDIR
ARG PLUGIN_1_NAME
ARG PLUGIN_1_SUBDIR
USER root
RUN git clone --quiet --depth 1 --branch "${PLUGIN_0_TAG}" "https://github.com/${PLUGIN_0_REPO}" /tmp/p0 \
 && test "$(git -C /tmp/p0 rev-parse HEAD)" = "${PLUGIN_0_SHA}" \
 && mkdir -p /opt/plugins \
 && cp -r "/tmp/p0/${PLUGIN_0_SUBDIR}" "/opt/plugins/${PLUGIN_0_NAME}" \
 && cp -r "/tmp/p0/${PLUGIN_1_SUBDIR}" "/opt/plugins/${PLUGIN_1_NAME}" \
 && test -f "/opt/plugins/${PLUGIN_0_NAME}/.claude-plugin/plugin.json" \
 && test -f "/opt/plugins/${PLUGIN_1_NAME}/.claude-plugin/plugin.json" \
 && rm -rf /tmp/p0 && chmod -R a+rX /opt/plugins
USER actor
```
