### Task 4: Container argv builders, egress network lifecycle, label sweep (containers and networks)

**Files:**
- Create: `runner/docker.py`, `runner/egress.py`, `tests/runner/test_docker_argv.py`, `tests/runner/test_egress.py`,
  `tests/runner/test_egress_docker.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (pure; exact argv unit-tested): `docker.Mount(host: Path, ctr: str, ro: bool)` (host may be a file);
  `docker.RunSpec(name, image, label, network, mounts, env, workdir, cmd, stdin, limits, proxy)`;
  `docker.run_argv(spec) -> list[str]`; `docker.stop_argv(name, grace=30)`; `docker.rm_argv(name)`;
  `docker.recording() -> ContextManager[list[RunSpec]]` (collects every `RunSpec` passed to `run_argv` while
  active — used by the credential-absence test over a whole dry run, review Q-11).
- Produces (effects): `docker.stop(name)`; `docker.sweep(label="bakeoff.run") -> dict` (removes labelled
  containers **and networks**, review C-13); `docker.running(label) -> list[str]`; `docker.run(spec, input=None,
  timeout=None) -> CompletedProcess` (synchronous, for short containers: git, verify scripts, tester).
- `egress.Stage(pass_, arm, phase, hosts, log_to)` context manager: creates `bk-net-<pass>-<arm>-<phase>`
  (`--internal`, labelled) and proxy `bk-proxy-<pass>-<arm>-<phase>` (alias `egress`); on exit appends the
  proxy log to `log_to` and removes both. `egress.decisions(log_text) -> list[tuple[str, str]]`.

```python
                network="bk-net-pass1-control-run", mounts=[Mount(Path("/h/home"), "/home/actor", False)],

                 "--user", "1000:1000", "--pids-limit", "2048", "--cpus", "4", "--memory", "8g", "-i"]:

    assert "/tmp:rw,exec,nosuid,nodev,size=4g" in run_argv(spec())

def test_stop_targets_the_container_not_the_cli():
    assert stop_argv("x") == ["docker", "stop", "-t", "30", "x"]

def test_recording_collects_every_spec_built():
    with docker.recording() as got:
        run_argv(spec()); run_argv(spec(name="b"))
    assert [s.name for s in got] == ["bk-pass1-control-01-arm-a1-001", "b"]
    run_argv(spec(name="c"))

def test_sweep_removes_labelled_networks_too(monkeypatch):                    # review C-13
    calls = []
    def fake_run(argv, **kw):
        calls.append(argv)
        out = "n1\n" if argv[:3] == ["docker", "network", "ls"] else ("c1\n" if argv[:2] == ["docker", "ps"] else "")
        return type("R", (), {"stdout": out, "returncode": 0})()
    monkeypatch.setattr(docker.subprocess, "run", fake_run)
    assert docker.sweep() == {"containers": ["c1"], "networks": ["n1"]}
    assert ["docker", "network", "rm", "n1"] in calls
```

```python
# tests/runner/test_egress_docker.py — port of spike B demos/q3_egress.sh
import subprocess, pytest
from pathlib import Path
from runner import config, egress

pytestmark = pytest.mark.docker

def curl(net, *args):
    return subprocess.run(["docker", "run", "--rm", "--network", net, "-e", "HTTPS_PROXY=http://egress:3128",
                           config.load_pins().images["base"], "curl", "-sS", "-o", "/dev/null", "-w", "%{http_code}",
                           "--max-time", "20", *args], capture_output=True, text=True)

def test_stage_allows_listed_host_and_refuses_others(tmp_path):
    log = tmp_path / "egress.log"
    with egress.Stage("t", "probe", "run", [r"^api\.anthropic\.com$"], log) as st:
        assert curl(st.net, "https://api.anthropic.com/").stdout.strip() not in ("", "000")
        assert curl(st.net, "https://example.com/").returncode != 0
        assert curl(st.net, "--noproxy", "*", "https://1.1.1.1/").returncode != 0
    assert egress.decisions(log.read_text()) == [("api.anthropic.com", "allowed"), ("example.com", "refused")]
    assert subprocess.run(["docker", "network", "inspect", st.net], capture_output=True).returncode != 0
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement**

```python
# runner/docker.py
"""docker argv builders (pure) and the few effectful calls. The argv is built only from declared inputs:
no env pass-through, no implicit mounts (spike B Q2)."""
from __future__ import annotations
import contextlib, subprocess
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Iterator
from runner.config import Limits

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Mount:
    host: Path
    ctr: str
    ro: bool

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunSpec:
    name: str
    image: str
    label: str
    network: str                 # a network name, or "none"
    mounts: list[Mount]
    env: dict[str, str]
    workdir: str
    cmd: list[str]
    stdin: bool
    limits: Limits
    proxy: bool

_recorder: list[RunSpec] | None = None

@contextlib.contextmanager
def recording() -> Iterator[list[RunSpec]]:
    global _recorder
    got: list[RunSpec] = []
    prev, _recorder = _recorder, got
    try:
        yield got
    finally:
        _recorder = prev

def run_argv(s: RunSpec) -> list[str]:
    if _recorder is not None:
        _recorder.append(s)
    a = ["docker", "run", "--rm", "--init", "--name", s.name, "--label", s.label,
         "--read-only", "--tmpfs", "/tmp:rw,exec,nosuid,nodev,size=4g",
         "--user", "1000:1000", "--cap-drop", "ALL", "--security-opt", "no-new-privileges",
         "--pids-limit", str(s.limits.pids), "--cpus", s.limits.cpus, "--memory", s.limits.memory,
         "--network", s.network, "-w", s.workdir]
    if s.stdin:
        a.append("-i")
    env = dict(s.env)
    if s.proxy:
        env |= {"HTTPS_PROXY": "http://egress:3128", "HTTP_PROXY": "http://egress:3128",
                "NO_PROXY": "localhost,127.0.0.1"}
    for k in sorted(env):
        a += ["-e", f"{k}={env[k]}"]
    for m in s.mounts:
        a += ["-v", f"{m.host}:{m.ctr}:{'ro' if m.ro else 'rw'}"]
    return [*a, s.image, *s.cmd]

def stop_argv(name: str, grace: int = 30) -> list[str]:
    return ["docker", "stop", "-t", str(grace), name]

def rm_argv(name: str) -> list[str]:
    return ["docker", "rm", "-f", name]

def stop(name: str) -> None:
    """Graceful then hard; never relies on killing the local docker CLI (spike B Q5)."""
    subprocess.run(stop_argv(name), capture_output=True)
    subprocess.run(rm_argv(name), capture_output=True)

def run(spec: RunSpec, input: bytes | None = None, timeout: float | None = None) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    """A short container (git, verify, tester): stdout/stderr captured, container removed on timeout."""
    try:
        return subprocess.run(run_argv(spec), input=input, capture_output=True, timeout=timeout)
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        stop(spec.name)
        raise

def sweep(label: str = "bakeoff.run") -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    ids = subprocess.run(["docker", "ps", "-aq", "--filter", f"label={label}"],
                         capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.split()
    for i in ids:
        subprocess.run(rm_argv(i), capture_output=True)
    nets = subprocess.run(["docker", "network", "ls", "-q", "--filter", f"label={label}"],
                          capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.split()
    for n in nets:
        subprocess.run(["docker", "network", "rm", n], capture_output=True)
    return {"containers": ids, "networks": nets}

def running(label: str = "bakeoff.run") -> list[str]:
    return subprocess.run(["docker", "ps", "-q", "--filter", f"label={label}"],
                          capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.split()
```

```python
# runner/egress.py
"""Per-arm, per-phase egress stage: an --internal network plus an allowlisting tinyproxy (spike B Q3)."""
from __future__ import annotations
import re, subprocess, tempfile
from pathlib import Path
from runner import config

_CONNECT = re.compile(r"CONNECT ([^:\s]+):\d+")
_REFUSED = re.compile(r'refused on filtered domain "([^"]+)"')

def allowlist_text(hosts: list[str]) -> str:
    return "".join(h + "\n" for h in hosts)

def decisions(log: str) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    out: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    for line in log.splitlines():
        if m := _CONNECT.search(line):
            out.append((m.group(1), "allowed"))
        elif (m := _REFUSED.search(line)) and out and out[-1][0] == m.group(1):
            out[-1] = (m.group(1), "refused")
    return out

class Stage:
    def __init__(self, pass_: str, arm: str, phase: str, hosts: list[str], log_to: Path):
        self.net = f"bk-net-{pass_}-{arm}-{phase}"
        self.proxy = f"bk-proxy-{pass_}-{arm}-{phase}"
        self.label = f"bakeoff.run={pass_}/{arm}"
        self.hosts, self.log_to = hosts, log_to

    def __enter__(self) -> "Stage":
        self._allow = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile("w", suffix=".txt", delete=False)
        self._allow.write(allowlist_text(self.hosts)); self._allow.close()
        Path(self._allow.name).chmod(0o644)
        subprocess.run(["docker", "network", "create", "--internal", "--label", self.label, self.net],
                       check=True, capture_output=True)
        subprocess.run(["docker", "run", "-d", "--name", self.proxy, "--label", self.label, "--network", "bridge",
                        "-v", f"{self._allow.name}:/etc/tinyproxy/allow.txt:ro",
                        config.load_pins().images["proxy"]], check=True, capture_output=True)
        subprocess.run(["docker", "network", "connect", "--alias", "egress", self.net, self.proxy],
                       check=True, capture_output=True)
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *exc) -> None:
        log = subprocess.run(["docker", "logs", self.proxy], capture_output=True, text=True)
        self.log_to.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with open(self.log_to, "a") as fh:
            fh.write(log.stdout + log.stderr)
        subprocess.run(["docker", "rm", "-f", self.proxy], capture_output=True)
        subprocess.run(["docker", "network", "rm", self.net], capture_output=True)
        Path(self._allow.name).unlink(missing_ok=True)
```

### Task 5: Credentials and the session environment

**Files:**
- Create: `runner/credentials.py`, `tests/runner/test_credentials.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `credentials.Credential` (opaque; `.payload() -> bytes` = token + `\n`; `repr`/`str`/`format` all
  `"<credential>"`; `.check_turn(dry: bool)` raises `PermissionError` if a dummy credential would drive a real
  arm **or** the real credential would drive a dry arm — called by `session.run_turn` on every turn, review
  C-14); `.fingerprint() -> str` (SHA-256 of the token, for the secret scanner, Task 21);
  `credentials.from_keychain(service) -> Credential`; `credentials.dummy() -> Credential`;
  `credentials.session_env(pins, arm, *, role, isolated, tools="", resume="", mcp=False) -> dict[str, str]`.
- Owner one-time action (in `checklists/README.md`, Task 9): `claude setup-token` in their own terminal, then
  `security add-generic-password -s bakeoff-claude-oauth -a "$USER" -w` and paste the token at the prompt.

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement**

```python
# runner/credentials.py
"""The OAuth token lives only in this process's memory and on one container's stdin (spike B option a′)."""
from __future__ import annotations
import hashlib, subprocess
from runner.config import Arm, Pins

class Credential:
    __slots__ = ("_t", "_dummy")
    def __init__(self, token: str, dummy: bool = False):
        self._t, self._dummy = token.strip(), dummy
    def payload(self) -> bytes:
        return (self._t + "\n").encode()
    def fingerprint(self) -> str:
        return hashlib.sha256(self._t.encode()).hexdigest()
    def raw_for_scanner(self) -> str:
        """Only runner.secrets calls this, to search evidence for the literal token; never logged."""
        return self._t
    def check_turn(self, dry: bool) -> None:
        if self._dummy and not dry:
            raise PermissionError("the dummy credential may only drive dry-run arms")
        if dry and not self._dummy:
            raise PermissionError("the real credential never drives a dry-run arm")
    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        return "<credential>"
    __str__ = __repr__
    def __format__(self, spec: str) -> str:
        return "<credential>"

def from_keychain(service: str = "bakeoff-claude-oauth") -> Credential:
    r = subprocess.run(["security", "find-generic-password", "-s", service, "-w"], capture_output=True, text=True)
    if r.returncode != 0 or not r.stdout.strip():
        raise RuntimeError(f"no usable keychain item {service!r} (exit {r.returncode}); "
                           "see checklists/README.md (owner one-time step)")
    return Credential(r.stdout)

def dummy() -> Credential:
    return Credential("dry-run-dummy-token", dummy=True)

def session_env(pins: Pins, arm: Arm, *, role: str, isolated: bool, tools: str = "", resume: str = "",
                mcp: bool = False) -> dict[str, str]:
    m = pins.model
    env = {
        "BK_MODEL": m, "BK_PERMISSION_MODE": pins.permission_mode,
        "BK_DISALLOWED": ",".join(pins.disallowed_tools),
        "ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_OPUS_MODEL": m, "ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_SONNET_MODEL": m,
        "ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_HAIKU_MODEL": m, "CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL": m,
        "CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_AUTO_MEMORY": "1", "DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER": "1",
        "CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC": "1", "CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_RETRIES": str(pins.max_retries),
        "CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR": "/home/actor/.claude", "OPENSPEC_TELEMETRY": "0",
        **(arm.env if role == "arm" else {}),
    }
    if role == "arm" and pins.plugin_route == "plugin-dir" and arm.plugins:
        env["BK_PLUGIN_DIRS"] = " ".join(f"/opt/plugins/{p.name}" for p in arm.plugins)
    if role == "arm" and mcp:
        env["BK_MCP_CONFIG"] = "/home/actor/.bk-mcp.json"        # OD24 B with MCP only (speckit)
    if isolated:
        env["BK_ISOLATED"] = "1"
    if tools:
        env["BK_TOOLS"] = tools
    if resume:
        env["BK_RESUME"] = resume
    return env
```

```bash
git commit -m "Runner: credential (stdin→fd only, per-turn guard both ways) and pinned session env"
```

### Task 6: One Claude turn in a container (supervision, stream + stderr capture, outcome)

**Files:**
- Create: `runner/session.py`, `tests/runner/test_session_parse.py`, `tests/runner/test_session_docker.py`
- Create: `runner/images/dryrun/Dockerfile`, `runner/images/dryrun/fake_claude.py` (minimal here; Task 24 adds
  the scripted arm/PO/teammate roles), `runner/config/arms/dryrun.toml`

**Interfaces (complete field lists, review Q-6):**
- `session.Turn(pass_, arm, step, attempt, n, image, message, env, mounts, network, proxy, workdir, inbox_dir,
  role, dry, config_dir)` — `inbox_dir` is the **role's own** host inbox (`Paths.inbox(role)`); `config_dir` is the
  host path of the session's `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` (for the post-turn credential check); `role` ∈ `arm | po |
  teammate | setup | probe`.
- `session.run_turn(turn, cred, out_dir, timeout_s) -> TurnResult`. Writes `out_dir/turns/<stem>.prompt.md`,
  `<stem>.stream.jsonl.gz` and `<stem>.stderr.gz`. The message is written to `inbox_dir/<stem>.md` and **only
  that file** is mounted, read-only, at `/inbox/msg.md` (review C-12). Calls `cred.check_turn(turn.dry)` first
  (review C-14).
- `TurnResult(session_id, outcome, final_text, init, result, cost_usd, usage, model_usage, wall_seconds, retries,
  stream_path, stderr_path, permission_denials, credential_files)`; `outcome ∈ ok | is_error | no_result |
  timeout | auth_failed`.
- `session.parse_stream(lines) -> Parsed` (pure).

**Outcome rules (spike B Q5/Q8):** `ok` needs a `result` event with `is_error == false`; `is_error` = a `result`
with `is_error == true` whatever its subtype; `no_result` = the process ended without a `result`; `auth_failed`
= a `system/api_retry` with `error == "authentication_failed"` (container stopped at once); `timeout` = over
`limits.turn_seconds` (`docker.stop`). **stderr is drained concurrently** into `<stem>.stderr.gz`, so a chatty
stderr can never block the container until the timeout (review C-9). **After every turn** the runner lists
files under `config_dir` changed during the turn that contain the token bytes, plus any `.credentials.json`;
a non-empty list is returned as `credential_files`, which `drive` turns into the harness fault
`credential-persisted` (review S-3).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the pure parser tests**

```python
# tests/runner/test_session_parse.py
import json
from runner.session import parse_stream

def j(**o): return json.dumps(o)

INIT = j(type="system", subtype="init", session_id="s1", model="claude-opus-5-5", claude_code_version="2.1.281")

def test_ok_needs_a_result_that_is_not_an_error():
    p = parse_stream([INIT, j(type="result", subtype="success", is_error=False, result="done",
                              total_cost_usd=0.5, usage={"output_tokens": 3}, modelUsage={"claude-opus-5-5": {}})])
    assert p.outcome == "ok" and p.final_text == "done" and p.session_id == "s1" and p.cost_usd == 0.5

def test_success_subtype_with_is_error_is_an_error():
    assert parse_stream([INIT, j(type="result", subtype="success", is_error=True, result="API Error: 401")]).outcome == "is_error"

def test_missing_result_is_no_result():
    assert parse_stream([INIT]).outcome == "no_result"

def test_authentication_failed_retry_is_detected():
    p = parse_stream([INIT, j(type="system", subtype="api_retry", attempt=1, error="authentication_failed")])
    assert p.outcome == "auth_failed"

def test_parser_handles_a_200k_line():                              # renamed (review Q-11); the docker test covers the pipe
    big = j(type="assistant", message={"model": "claude-opus-5-5", "content": [{"type": "text", "text": "x" * 200_000}]})
    assert parse_stream([INIT, big]).outcome == "no_result"

def test_truncated_last_line_is_ignored_not_fatal():
    assert parse_stream([INIT, '{"type": "resu']).outcome == "no_result"

def test_permission_denials_are_kept_for_the_research_record():     # review F-8
    p = parse_stream([INIT, j(type="result", is_error=False, result="x",
                              permission_denials=[{"tool_name": "WebSearch"}])])
    assert p.result["permission_denials"] == [{"tool_name": "WebSearch"}]
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the minimal fake claude and its image**

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# runner/images/dryrun/fake_claude.py — installed as /usr/local/bin/claude in bakeoff-arm-dryrun.
# Speaks just enough of Claude Code 2.1.281's CLI: -p PROMPT, --model, --resume, --output-format stream-json.
# Directives in the prompt (lines starting "FAKE:") drive failure modes for the runner's own tests.
import json, os, sys, time, uuid
from pathlib import Path

def arg(name, default=None):
    a = sys.argv
    return a[a.index(name) + 1] if name in a else default

def emit(**o):
    print(json.dumps(o), flush=True)

def main():
    if "--version" in sys.argv:
        print("2.1.281 (Claude Code)"); return 0
    prompt = arg("-p", "")
    fd = os.environ.get("CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN_FILE_DESCRIPTOR")
    token = os.read(int(fd), 4096).decode() if fd else ""
    model = arg("--model", "claude-opus-5-5")
    sid = arg("--resume") or str(uuid.uuid4())
    directives = dict(l[5:].split("=", 1) if "=" in l else (l[5:], "1") for l in prompt.splitlines() if l.startswith("FAKE:"))
    emit(type="system", subtype="init", session_id=sid, model=directives.get("init_model", model),
         claude_code_version=directives.get("cc_version", "2.1.281"),
         tools=["Bash", "Read", "Edit", "Write", "Task"], cwd=os.getcwd())
    if "stderr" in directives:
        sys.stderr.write("e" * int(directives["stderr"])); sys.stderr.flush()
    if "auth_fail" in directives:
        emit(type="system", subtype="api_retry", attempt=1, max_retries=4, error="authentication_failed")
        time.sleep(3600)
    text = directives.get("reply", "OK")
    if "ls_inbox" in directives:
        text = " ".join(sorted(os.listdir("/inbox")))
    if "bigline" in directives:
        emit(type="assistant", session_id=sid, message={"model": model, "content": [{"type": "text", "text": "x" * int(directives["bigline"])}]})
    # Transcript, where Claude Code keeps it (probe P1 verifies): $CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR/projects/<cwd slug>/<session>.jsonl
    proj = Path(os.environ["CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR"]) / "projects" / os.getcwd().replace("/", "-")
    proj.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    msg = {"type": "assistant", "sessionId": sid,
           "message": {"model": directives.get("model", model), "content": [{"type": "text", "text": text}]}}
    with open(proj / f"{sid}.jsonl", "a") as fh:
        fh.write(json.dumps({"type": "user", "sessionId": sid, "message": {"content": prompt}}) + "\n")
        fh.write(json.dumps(msg) + "\n")
    sub_model = directives.get("subagent_model") or os.environ.get("FAKE_SUBAGENT_MODEL")
    if sub_model:
        sub = proj / sid / "subagents"; sub.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with open(sub / "agent-1.jsonl", "a") as fh:
            fh.write(json.dumps({"type": "assistant", "message": {"model": sub_model, "content": []}}) + "\n")
    if "sleep" in directives:          # after the transcript is written, as Claude Code writes as it goes
        time.sleep(float(directives["sleep"]))
    if "persist_token" in directives:
        (Path(os.environ["CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR"]) / ".credentials.json").write_text(json.dumps({"t": token.strip()}))
    emit(type="assistant", session_id=sid, message=msg["message"])
    if "no_result" in directives:
        return 0
    if "api_error" in directives:
        emit(type="result", subtype="success", is_error=True, result=directives["api_error"], session_id=sid)
        return 1
    emit(type="result", subtype="success", is_error="is_error" in directives, result=text, session_id=sid,
         total_cost_usd=0.0, usage={"input_tokens": 1, "output_tokens": 1},
         modelUsage={directives.get("model", model): {"inputTokens": 1, "outputTokens": 1}},
         permission_denials=[], token_seen=bool(token.strip()))
    return 0

sys.exit(main())
```
(`token_seen` proves the fd hand-off works without ever printing the token.)

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `runner/session.py`**

```python
# runner/session.py
from __future__ import annotations
import asyncio, gzip, json, os, time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Iterable, Literal
from runner import docker
from runner.credentials import Credential
from runner.docker import Mount, RunSpec
from runner.config import load_pins

Outcome = Literal["ok", "is_error", "no_result", "timeout", "auth_failed"]

@dataclass
class Parsed:
    session_id: str | None = None
    init: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    result: dict | None = None
    retries: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    auth_failed: bool = False

    @property
    def outcome(self) -> Outcome:
        if self.auth_failed:
            return "auth_failed"
        if self.result is None:
            return "no_result"
        return "is_error" if self.result.get("is_error") else "ok"
    @property
    def final_text(self) -> str:
        return (self.result or {}).get("result") or ""
    @property
    def cost_usd(self) -> float:
        return float((self.result or {}).get("total_cost_usd") or 0.0)

def feed(p: Parsed, line: str) -> None:
    try:
        ev = json.loads(line)
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return                                   # a truncated last line (timed-out turn) is not fatal
    t, st = ev.get("type"), ev.get("subtype")
    if t == "system" and st == "init":
        p.init, p.session_id = ev, ev.get("session_id")
    elif t == "system" and st == "api_retry":
        p.retries.append(ev)
        if ev.get("error") == "authentication_failed":
            p.auth_failed = True
    elif t == "result":
        p.result = ev

def parse_stream(lines: Iterable[str]) -> Parsed:
    p = Parsed()
    for line in lines:
        feed(p, line)
    return p

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Turn:
    pass_: str
    arm: str
    step: int
    attempt: int
    n: int
    image: str
    message: str
    env: dict[str, str]
    mounts: list[Mount]
    network: str
    proxy: bool
    workdir: str
    inbox_dir: Path
    role: str = "arm"
    dry: bool = False
    config_dir: Path | None = None

@dataclass
class TurnResult:
    session_id: str | None
    outcome: Outcome
    final_text: str
    init: dict
    result: dict | None
    cost_usd: float
    usage: dict
    model_usage: dict
    wall_seconds: float
    retries: list[dict]
    stream_path: Path | None
    stderr_path: Path | None = None
    permission_denials: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    credential_files: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

def stem(t: Turn) -> str:
    """File/container stem; includes role and attempt so nothing ever overwrites another turn's files."""
    return f"{t.role}-a{t.attempt}-{t.n:03d}"

def container_name(t: Turn) -> str:
    return f"bk-{t.pass_}-{t.arm}-{t.step:02d}-{stem(t)}"

def credential_files(config_dir: Path | None, cred: Credential, since: float) -> list[str]:
    """Files under config_dir changed during the turn that contain the token, and any .credentials.json (S-3).
    Walks without following symlinks (review S-1 extension)."""
    if config_dir is None or not config_dir.exists():
        return []
    needle, hits = cred.raw_for_scanner().encode(), []
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(config_dir, followlinks=False):
        for f in files:
            p = Path(root) / f
            if p.is_symlink():
                continue
            if f == ".credentials.json":
                hits.append(str(p.relative_to(config_dir))); continue
            try:
                if p.stat().st_mtime >= since and needle in p.read_bytes():
                    hits.append(str(p.relative_to(config_dir)))
            except OSError:
                continue
    return sorted(set(hits))

async def _run(t: Turn, cred: Credential, stream_path: Path, stderr_path: Path, timeout_s: int) -> tuple[Parsed, str]:
    name = container_name(t)
    msg_file = t.inbox_dir / f"{stem(t)}.md"
    spec = RunSpec(name=name, image=t.image, label=f"bakeoff.run={t.pass_}/{t.arm}", network=t.network,
                   mounts=[*t.mounts, Mount(msg_file, "/inbox/msg.md", True)], env=t.env, workdir=t.workdir,
                   cmd=["bk-claude-turn", "/inbox/msg.md"], stdin=True, limits=load_pins().limits, proxy=t.proxy)
    proc = await asyncio.create_subprocess_exec(*docker.run_argv(spec), stdin=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
                                                stdout=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE, stderr=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
                                                start_new_session=True, limit=64 * 1024 * 1024)
    proc.stdin.write(cred.payload()); await proc.stdin.drain(); proc.stdin.close()
    parsed, how = Parsed(), "exited"
    with gzip.open(stream_path, "wt") as out, gzip.open(stderr_path, "wb") as err:
        async def drain_stderr():                              # review C-9
            while chunk := await proc.stderr.read(65536):
                err.write(chunk)
        async def pump():
            async for raw in proc.stdout:
                line = raw.decode(errors="replace")
                out.write(line); feed(parsed, line)
                if parsed.auth_failed:
                    return
        err_task = asyncio.create_task(drain_stderr())
        try:
            await asyncio.wait_for(pump(), timeout=timeout_s)
            if parsed.auth_failed:
                how = "auth_failed"
        except asyncio.TimeoutError:
            how = "timeout"
        if how != "exited":
            docker.stop(name)
        await proc.wait()
        await err_task
    return parsed, how

def run_turn(t: Turn, cred: Credential, out_dir: Path, timeout_s: int) -> TurnResult:
    cred.check_turn(t.dry)                                     # review C-14
    (out_dir / "turns").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    t.inbox_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (t.inbox_dir / f"{stem(t)}.md").write_text(t.message)
    (out_dir / "turns" / f"{stem(t)}.prompt.md").write_text(t.message)
    stream = out_dir / "turns" / f"{stem(t)}.stream.jsonl.gz"
    stderr = out_dir / "turns" / f"{stem(t)}.stderr.gz"
    wall0, start = time.time(), time.monotonic()
    parsed, how = asyncio.run(_run(t, cred, stream, stderr, timeout_s))
    outcome: Outcome = "timeout" if how == "timeout" else parsed.outcome
    r = parsed.result or {}
    return TurnResult(parsed.session_id, outcome, parsed.final_text, parsed.init, parsed.result, parsed.cost_usd,
                      r.get("usage", {}), r.get("modelUsage", {}), time.monotonic() - start, parsed.retries,
                      stream, stderr, list(r.get("permission_denials") or []),
                      credential_files(t.config_dir, cred, wall0 - 1))
```

```python
# tests/runner/test_session_docker.py
import gzip, pytest
from runner import config, credentials, docker
from runner.session import Turn, run_turn
from runner.docker import Mount

pytestmark = pytest.mark.docker

def turn(tmp_path, message, n=1, role="arm"):
    home = tmp_path / "home"; (home / ".claude").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    work = tmp_path / "work" / "bakeoff-specs-dryrun"; work.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    env = credentials.session_env(config.load_pins(), config.load_arm("dryrun"), role="arm", isolated=False)
    return Turn("t", "dryrun", 1, 1, n, config.load_pins().images["dryrun"], message, env,
                [Mount(home, "/home/actor", False), Mount(tmp_path / "work", "/work", False)],
                "none", False, "/work/bakeoff-specs-dryrun", tmp_path / "inbox" / role, role, True, home / ".claude")

def test_ok_turn_records_stream_and_session(tmp_path):
    r = run_turn(turn(tmp_path, "hello\nFAKE:reply=done"), credentials.dummy(), tmp_path / "out", 60)
    assert r.outcome == "ok" and r.final_text == "done" and r.session_id
    assert r.result["token_seen"] is True                      # fd 3 hand-off works
    assert "dry-run-dummy-token" not in gzip.open(r.stream_path, "rt").read()

def test_timeout_stops_the_container(tmp_path):
    r = run_turn(turn(tmp_path, "FAKE:sleep=120"), credentials.dummy(), tmp_path / "out", 5)
    assert r.outcome == "timeout" and not docker.running("bakeoff.run=t/dryrun")

def test_auth_failure_aborts_without_waiting_for_retries(tmp_path):
    r = run_turn(turn(tmp_path, "FAKE:auth_fail"), credentials.dummy(), tmp_path / "out", 600)
    assert r.outcome == "auth_failed" and r.wall_seconds < 60

def test_a_200k_stream_line_passes_through_the_pipe(tmp_path):                 # review Q-11 (the real 64 KiB limit)
    r = run_turn(turn(tmp_path, "FAKE:bigline=200000\nFAKE:reply=ok"), credentials.dummy(), tmp_path / "out", 60)
    assert r.outcome == "ok"

def test_a_chatty_stderr_does_not_block_the_turn(tmp_path):                    # review C-9
    r = run_turn(turn(tmp_path, "FAKE:stderr=500000\nFAKE:reply=ok"), credentials.dummy(), tmp_path / "out", 60)
    assert r.outcome == "ok" and r.wall_seconds < 30 and len(gzip.open(r.stderr_path).read()) == 500000

def test_only_the_current_message_is_visible_in_the_container(tmp_path):      # review C-12
    (tmp_path / "inbox" / "teammate").mkdir(parents=True)
    (tmp_path / "inbox" / "teammate" / "secret.md").write_text("teammate prompt")
    run_turn(turn(tmp_path, "first", n=1), credentials.dummy(), tmp_path / "out", 60)
    r = run_turn(turn(tmp_path, "FAKE:ls_inbox", n=2), credentials.dummy(), tmp_path / "out", 60)
    assert r.final_text == "msg.md"            # not the earlier message, not another role's inbox

def test_a_persisted_credential_file_is_reported(tmp_path):                    # review S-3
    r = run_turn(turn(tmp_path, "FAKE:persist_token\nFAKE:reply=ok"), credentials.dummy(), tmp_path / "out", 60)
    assert r.credential_files == [".credentials.json"]

def test_the_real_credential_is_refused_for_a_dry_arm(tmp_path):              # review C-14
    with pytest.raises(PermissionError):
        run_turn(turn(tmp_path, "x"), credentials.Credential("real"), tmp_path / "out", 60)
```

### Task 7: Transcript collection (symlink-safe) and model-pin verification

**Files:**
- Create: `runner/transcripts.py`, `tests/runner/test_transcripts.py`

**Interfaces:**
- `transcripts.safe_files(root: Path, pattern="*.jsonl") -> list[Path]`: regular files under `root`, walked with
  `followlinks=False`; symlinks and anything whose real path leaves `root` are skipped (review S-1 extension:
  the arm controls this tree).
- `transcripts.snapshot(config_dir) -> dict[str, str]` (relative path under `projects/` → sha256);
  `transcripts.collect(config_dir, before, dest) -> list[Path]` (gzips every new or changed file, recursively,
  so `…/<session>/subagents/*.jsonl` are included).
- `transcripts.verify_pin(transcript_files, stream_files, pins, *, require_transcripts=True) -> PinReport`;
  `PinReport(ok, assistant_messages, stream_assistant_messages, synthetic, truncated_lines, claude_code_versions,
  no_evidence, violations)` → `model-pin.json`.
- Checked (review C-6, C-16): every transcript `assistant` line's `message.model`; every stream `system/init`
  `model` **and `claude_code_version`**; every stream `assistant` event's `message.model`; every `result.modelUsage`
  key. A model in `synthetic_models` is counted, not a violation. `no_evidence` = `require_transcripts` and zero
  transcript assistant messages (the transcript path assumption failing must fault, not pass on streams). An
  undecodable line (a timed-out turn's truncated last line) is counted in `truncated_lines`, never fatal.
- `faults.classify_pin(report)` (Task 22) turns a report into `model-pin:no-evidence`, `model-pin:<model>` or
  `pin-claude-version:<version>`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write failing tests**

```python
# tests/runner/test_transcripts.py
import gzip, json, os
from runner import config
from runner.transcripts import snapshot, collect, verify_pin, safe_files

def w(p, *objs):
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    p.write_text("".join(json.dumps(o) + "\n" for o in objs))

def asst(m): return {"type": "assistant", "message": {"model": m, "content": []}}

def gz(p, *objs):
    with gzip.open(p, "wt") as fh:
        fh.write("".join(json.dumps(o) + "\n" for o in objs))
    return p

INIT_OK = {"type": "system", "subtype": "init", "model": "claude-opus-5-5", "claude_code_version": "2.1.281"}

def test_collect_includes_new_subagent_transcripts_only(tmp_path):
    cfg = tmp_path / ".claude"
    w(cfg / "projects/p/old.jsonl", asst("claude-opus-5-5"))
    before = snapshot(cfg)
    w(cfg / "projects/p/s1.jsonl", asst("claude-opus-5-5"))
    w(cfg / "projects/p/s1/subagents/agent-1.jsonl", asst("claude-opus-5-5"))
    assert sorted(p.name for p in collect(cfg, before, tmp_path / "out")) == ["agent-1.jsonl.gz", "s1.jsonl.gz"]

def test_symlinks_in_the_arm_tree_are_never_followed(tmp_path):            # review S-1 extension
    secret = tmp_path / "host-secret.jsonl"; secret.write_text("private\n")
    root = tmp_path / ".claude" / "projects" / "p"; root.mkdir(parents=True)
    os.symlink(secret, root / "evil.jsonl")
    assert safe_files(tmp_path / ".claude" / "projects") == []

def test_subagent_on_another_model_is_a_violation(tmp_path):
    f = tmp_path / "agent-1.jsonl"; w(f, asst("claude-opus-5-5"), asst("claude-haiku-4-5"))
    r = verify_pin([f], [], config.load_pins())
    assert not r.ok and r.violations == [{"file": str(f), "line": 2, "where": "assistant", "model": "claude-haiku-4-5"}]

def test_synthetic_messages_are_counted_not_faulted(tmp_path):
    f = tmp_path / "s.jsonl"; w(f, asst("<synthetic>"), asst("claude-opus-5-5"))
    r = verify_pin([f], [], config.load_pins())
    assert r.ok and r.synthetic == 1 and r.assistant_messages == 2

def test_stream_assistant_events_and_model_usage_are_checked(tmp_path):     # review C-6
    t = tmp_path / "s.jsonl"; w(t, asst("claude-opus-5-5"))
    s = gz(tmp_path / "t.stream.jsonl.gz", INIT_OK, asst("claude-sonnet-5"),
           {"type": "result", "modelUsage": {"claude-opus-5-5": {}, "claude-haiku-4-5": {}}})
    r = verify_pin([t], [s], config.load_pins())
    assert [v["where"] for v in r.violations] == ["stream.assistant", "result.modelUsage"]

def test_streams_alone_are_not_evidence_for_an_arm_step(tmp_path):         # review C-6
    s = gz(tmp_path / "t.stream.jsonl.gz", INIT_OK, asst("claude-opus-5-5"))
    r = verify_pin([], [s], config.load_pins())
    assert not r.ok and r.no_evidence

def test_a_wrong_claude_code_version_is_a_violation(tmp_path):             # review C-16
    t = tmp_path / "s.jsonl"; w(t, asst("claude-opus-5-5"))
    s = gz(tmp_path / "t.stream.jsonl.gz", {**INIT_OK, "claude_code_version": "2.1.290"})
    r = verify_pin([t], [s], config.load_pins())
    assert r.violations == [{"file": str(s), "line": 1, "where": "claude_code_version", "model": "2.1.290"}]

def test_a_truncated_last_line_is_counted_not_fatal(tmp_path):             # review C-6
    f = tmp_path / "s.jsonl"; f.write_text(json.dumps(asst("claude-opus-5-5")) + "\n" + '{"type": "assi')
    r = verify_pin([f], [], config.load_pins())
    assert r.ok and r.truncated_lines == 1

def test_nothing_at_all_is_not_evidence(tmp_path):
    r = verify_pin([], [], config.load_pins())
    assert not r.ok and r.assistant_messages == 0
```

```python
# runner/transcripts.py
from __future__ import annotations
import gzip, hashlib, json, os, shutil
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict, field
from pathlib import Path
from runner.config import Pins

def _open(p: Path):
    return gzip.open(p, "rt", errors="replace") if p.suffix == ".gz" else open(p, errors="replace")

def safe_files(root: Path, pattern: str = "*.jsonl") -> list[Path]:
    if not root.exists():
        return []
    real_root, out = root.resolve(), []
    for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(root, followlinks=False):
        dirnames[:] = [d for d in dirnames if not (Path(dirpath) / d).is_symlink()]
        for f in filenames:
            p = Path(dirpath) / f
            if p.is_symlink() or not p.match(pattern) or not p.is_file():
                continue
            if not str(p.resolve()).startswith(str(real_root) + os.sep):
                continue
            out.append(p)
    return sorted(out)

def snapshot(config_dir: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
    root = config_dir / "projects"
    return {str(f.relative_to(root)): hashlib.sha256(f.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for f in safe_files(root)}

def collect(config_dir: Path, before: dict[str, str], dest: Path) -> list[Path]:
    root, out = config_dir / "projects", []
    for rel, digest in snapshot(config_dir).items():
        if before.get(rel) == digest:
            continue
        target = dest / (rel + ".gz"); target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with open(root / rel, "rb") as src, gzip.open(target, "wb") as dst:
            shutil.copyfileobj(src, dst)
        out.append(target)
    return out

@dataclass
class PinReport:
    ok: bool
    assistant_messages: int
    stream_assistant_messages: int
    synthetic: int
    truncated_lines: int
    claude_code_versions: list[str]
    no_evidence: bool
    violations: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    def to_json(self) -> str:
        return json.dumps(asdict(self), indent=2)

def _lines(f: Path):
    with _open(f) as fh:
        for i, line in enumerate(fh, 1):
            if not line.strip():
                continue
            try:
                yield i, json.loads(line)
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                yield i, None

def verify_pin(transcript_files: list[Path], stream_files: list[Path], pins: Pins, *,
               require_transcripts: bool = True) -> PinReport:
    ok_models, synth = set(pins.accepted_models), set(pins.synthetic_models)
    n = sn = s = trunc = 0; bad: list[dict] = []; versions: set[str] = set()
    def judge(f, i, where, model):
        nonlocal s
        if model in synth:
            s += 1
        elif model not in ok_models:
            bad.append({"file": str(f), "line": i, "where": where, "model": model})
    for f in transcript_files:
        for i, ev in _lines(f):
            if ev is None:
                trunc += 1
            elif ev.get("type") == "assistant":
                n += 1; judge(f, i, "assistant", (ev.get("message") or {}).get("model"))
    for f in stream_files:
        for i, ev in _lines(f):
            if ev is None:
                trunc += 1; continue
            t = ev.get("type")
            if t == "system" and ev.get("subtype") == "init":
                judge(f, i, "init", ev.get("model"))
                v = ev.get("claude_code_version")
                versions.add(str(v))
                if v != pins.claude_version:
                    bad.append({"file": str(f), "line": i, "where": "claude_code_version", "model": v})
            elif t == "assistant":
                sn += 1; judge(f, i, "stream.assistant", (ev.get("message") or {}).get("model"))
            elif t == "result":
                for m in (ev.get("modelUsage") or {}):
                    judge(f, i, "result.modelUsage", m)
    no_evidence = require_transcripts and n == 0
    any_input = bool(transcript_files or stream_files)
    return PinReport(ok=not bad and not no_evidence and any_input, assistant_messages=n,
                     stream_assistant_messages=sn, synthetic=s, truncated_lines=trunc,
                     claude_code_versions=sorted(versions), no_evidence=no_evidence or not any_input, violations=bad)
```

### Task 8: Containerised git (review S-1) and the arm repo lifecycle

**Why this task exists (review S-1, critical).** An arm runs with `bypassPermissions` and read-write access to
its repo, including `.git/config` and `.git/hooks`. Any host-side git command on that repo can execute host
commands as the owner (`core.fsmonitor`, `diff.external`, textconv and filter drivers, hooks,
`credential.helper`/`core.sshCommand` on push). No malice is needed to break things either: beads' `bd init`
installs hooks that call `bd`, which does not exist on the host. So: **every git command that touches an arm
repo runs inside a `--network none`, read-only-root container of the arm's own image**, and publishing pushes
from a host-side *bare clone of a bundle* (a bundle carries objects and refs only — no config, no hooks).

**Files:**
- Create: `runner/gitops.py`, `runner/repo.py`, `tests/runner/test_gitops.py`, `tests/runner/test_repo_docker.py`
- Modify: `runner/cli.py` (`github-plan <pass>`, `github-verify <pass>`, `publish <pass> <arm>`)

**Interfaces:**
- `gitops.IDENTITY = ("Developer", "developer@troupe.run")` — one neutral identity for every arm (review G9);
  `gitops.TEAMMATE = ("Sam Teammate", "sam.teammate@troupe.run")`.
- `gitops.spec(p: Paths, argv: list[str], *, write=False, env=None, out_dir=None) -> RunSpec` (pure);
  `gitops.git(p, *args, write=False, env=None, input=None, out_dir=None, check=True) -> CompletedProcess`.
- Reads: `head(p)`, `main_sha(p) -> str | None` (`refs/heads/main`), `state(p) -> RepoState(branch, dirty,
  head, main, head_in_main)`, `archive(p, sha) -> bytes`, `grep_files(p, sha, pattern, pathspec) -> list[str]`,
  `log(p, a, b) -> list[dict]`, `diffstat(p, a, b) -> str`, `numstat(p, a, b)`, `worktree_diffstat(p) -> str`,
  `show(p, sha) -> str`, `porcelain(p) -> str`, `ls_files(p, sha) -> list[str]`, `commits_between(p, a, b) -> str`
  (records `sha\x1fmessage\x1e`).
- Writes (in the container, hooks enabled because a real developer's commit runs them — the sandbox contains
  them): `init_repo(p, remote_url)`, `commit_all(p, who, message) -> str`, `checkout_paths(p, paths)`,
  `remove_untracked(p, paths)`, `bundle(p, host_dest, refs=("--all",))`.
- Harness-repo and remote helpers (the host's own repo, never an arm's): `ls_remote_tag(repo, tag) -> str`,
  `commit_harness_file(path, message)`, `harness_is_clean(path) -> bool`.
- `repo.github_slug(pass_, arm)`; `repo.init(p, arm, pass_) -> str`; `repo.snapshot(p, step)`;
  `repo.restore(p, step)` (tar `filter="data"`); `repo.export(p, sha, dest)`; `repo.bundle(p, dest)`;
  `repo.github_plan(pass_) -> list[str]`; `repo.github_verify(pass_) -> dict`; `repo.publish(p, pass_, ask,
  scan) -> bool`.

```python
# tests/runner/test_gitops.py
import re
from pathlib import Path
from runner import gitops, repo
from runner.paths import Paths

def test_git_runs_in_a_networkless_container_of_the_arms_image():
    s = gitops.spec(Paths("pass1", "speckitty"), ["status", "--porcelain"])
    assert s.network == "none" and s.image == "bakeoff-arm-speckitty:1" and not s.proxy
    assert s.cmd[:3] == ["git", "-C", "/work/bakeoff-specs-speckitty"]

def test_reads_mount_the_work_tree_read_only_and_ignore_global_config():
    s = gitops.spec(Paths("pass1", "control"), ["status"])
    assert [(m.ctr, m.ro) for m in s.mounts] == [("/work", True)]
    assert s.env["GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM"] == "1" and s.env["GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL"] == "/dev/null"
    for flag in ["core.hooksPath=/dev/null", "core.fsmonitor=false", "safe.directory=*"]:
        assert flag in s.cmd

def test_writes_mount_work_read_write_and_keep_hooks():
    s = gitops.spec(Paths("pass1", "control"), ["commit", "-m", "x"], write=True)
    assert any(m.ctr == "/work" and not m.ro for m in s.mounts)
    assert "core.hooksPath=/dev/null" not in s.cmd

def test_one_neutral_identity_for_every_arm():                                # review G9
    assert gitops.IDENTITY == ("Developer", "developer@troupe.run")

def test_no_runner_module_but_gitops_builds_a_git_argv():                     # review S-1
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "runner"
    offenders = [f.name for f in root.glob("*.py") if f.name != "gitops.py"
                 and re.search(r"""\[\s*["']git["']\s*,""", f.read_text())]
    assert offenders == []

def test_github_plan_prints_private_repos_and_runs_nothing(monkeypatch):     # strengthened (review Q-11)
    def boom(*a, **k): raise AssertionError("github_plan ran a command")
    monkeypatch.setattr(repo.subprocess, "run", boom)
    monkeypatch.setattr(gitops.subprocess, "run", boom)
    cmds = repo.github_plan("pass1")
    assert len(cmds) == 7 and all(c.startswith("gh repo create troupe-run/bakeoff-specs-") and "--private" in c for c in cmds)

def test_pass2_uses_the_scored_repo_name_and_other_passes_have_none():
    assert repo.github_slug("pass2", "openspec") == "troupe-run/bakeoff-specs-openspec"
    assert repo.github_slug("pass1", "openspec") == "troupe-run/bakeoff-specs-openspec-pass1"
    assert repo.github_slug("dry1", "dryrun") is None
    assert repo.github_slug("canary", "control") == "troupe-run/bakeoff-specs-control-canary"

def test_verify_fails_when_any_troupe_run_repo_is_public(monkeypatch):     # review G7
    def fake(argv, **k):
        if argv[:3] == ["gh", "repo", "list"]:
            return type("R", (), {"returncode": 0, "stdout": '[{"name": "leaky"}]', "stderr": ""})()
        return type("R", (), {"returncode": 0, "stderr": "",
                              "stdout": '{"visibility": "PRIVATE", "hasIssuesEnabled": true, "isEmpty": true}'})()
    monkeypatch.setattr(repo.subprocess, "run", fake)
    res = repo.github_verify("pass1")
    assert res["_public_repos"] == ["leaky"] and not repo.verify_ok(res)
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement `runner/gitops.py`**

```python
# runner/gitops.py — the ONLY module that builds git argv (review S-1; enforced by a test).
from __future__ import annotations
import json, os, subprocess, tempfile, uuid
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from runner import config, docker
from runner.docker import Mount, RunSpec
from runner.paths import Paths, REPO_ROOT

IDENTITY = ("Developer", "developer@troupe.run")
TEAMMATE = ("Sam Teammate", "sam.teammate@troupe.run")
READ_CFG = ["-c", "core.hooksPath=/dev/null", "-c", "core.fsmonitor=false", "-c", "safe.directory=*",
            "-c", "diff.external=", "-c", "core.pager=cat"]
WRITE_CFG = ["-c", "safe.directory=*", "-c", "core.pager=cat"]

def image_for(arm: str) -> str:
    a = config.load_arm(arm)
    return config.load_pins().images["dryrun"] if a.dry else a.image

def spec(p: Paths, argv: list[str], *, write: bool = False, env: dict | None = None,
         out_dir: Path | None = None) -> RunSpec:
    mounts = [Mount(p.work, "/work", not write)]
    if write:                       # a real commit runs the repo's hooks (e.g. beads' `bd`), which may use $HOME
        mounts.append(Mount(p.home, "/home/actor", False))
    if out_dir is not None:
        mounts.append(Mount(out_dir, "/out", False))
    e = {"GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM": "1", "GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL": "/dev/null", "HOME": "/home/actor" if write else "/tmp",
         "GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT": "0", **(env or {})}
    return RunSpec(name=f"bk-git-{p.pass_}-{p.arm}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}", image=image_for(p.arm),
                   label=f"bakeoff.run={p.pass_}/{p.arm}", network="none", mounts=mounts, env=e,
                   workdir="/work", cmd=["git", "-C", p.container_repo, *(WRITE_CFG if write else READ_CFG), *argv],
                   stdin=False, limits=config.load_pins().limits, proxy=False)

def git(p: Paths, *args: str, write: bool = False, env: dict | None = None, input: bytes | None = None,
        out_dir: Path | None = None, check: bool = True) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    s = spec(p, list(args), write=write, env=env, out_dir=out_dir)
    if input is not None:
        s = RunSpec(**{**s.__dict__, "stdin": True})
    r = docker.run(s, input=input, timeout=600)
    if check and r.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f"git {args[0]} failed in container ({r.returncode}): {r.stderr.decode(errors='replace')[:400]}")
    return r

def _out(r: subprocess.CompletedProcess) -> str:
    return r.stdout.decode(errors="replace").strip()

def head(p: Paths) -> str:
    return _out(git(p, "rev-parse", "HEAD"))

def main_sha(p: Paths) -> str | None:
    r = git(p, "rev-parse", "--verify", "-q", "refs/heads/main", check=False)
    return _out(r) or None

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RepoState:
    branch: str
    dirty: bool
    head: str
    main: str | None
    head_in_main: bool

_STATE_SH = r"""cd "$1"; g() { git -c safe.directory='*' -c core.hooksPath=/dev/null -c core.fsmonitor=false "$@"; }
b=$(g branch --show-current); d=$(g status --porcelain | head -c 1); h=$(g rev-parse HEAD)
m=$(g rev-parse --verify -q refs/heads/main || true)
if [ -n "$m" ] && g merge-base --is-ancestor "$h" "$m"; then a=true; else a=false; fi
jq -cn --arg b "$b" --arg d "$d" --arg h "$h" --arg m "$m" --argjson a $a \
  '{branch:$b, dirty:($d!=""), head:$h, main:(if $m=="" then null else $m end), head_in_main:$a}'"""

def state(p: Paths) -> RepoState:
    s = spec(p, [])
    s = RunSpec(**{**s.__dict__, "cmd": ["bash", "-c", _STATE_SH, "state", p.container_repo]})
    r = docker.run(s, timeout=120)
    return RepoState(**json.loads(r.stdout))

def archive(p: Paths, sha: str) -> bytes:
    return git(p, "archive", "--format=tar", sha).stdout

def grep_files(p: Paths, sha: str, pattern: str, pathspec: str = ":!*.py") -> list[str]:
    r = git(p, "grep", "-l", "-F", pattern, sha, "--", pathspec, check=False)
    return sorted(l.split(":", 1)[1] for l in _out(r).splitlines() if ":" in l)

def log(p: Paths, a: str, b: str) -> list[dict]:
    r = git(p, "log", "--format=%H%x1f%an%x1f%s", f"{a}..{b}")
    return [dict(zip(("sha", "author", "subject"), l.split("\x1f"))) for l in _out(r).splitlines() if l]

def commits_between(p: Paths, a: str, b: str) -> str:
    """Records separated by \x1e; sha and message separated by \x1f (unambiguous for any message text)."""
    return _out(git(p, "log", "--format=%H%x1f%s%n%b%x1e", f"{a}..{b}"))

def diffstat(p: Paths, a: str, b: str) -> str:
    return _out(git(p, "diff", "--no-textconv", "--no-ext-diff", "--stat", a, b))

def numstat(p: Paths, a: str, b: str) -> list[tuple[int, int, str]]:
    out = []
    for l in _out(git(p, "diff", "--no-textconv", "--no-ext-diff", "--numstat", a, b)).splitlines():
        ad, de, f = l.split("\t", 2)
        out.append((int(ad) if ad.isdigit() else 0, int(de) if de.isdigit() else 0, f))
    return out

def worktree_diffstat(p: Paths) -> str:
    return _out(git(p, "diff", "--no-textconv", "--no-ext-diff", "--stat", "HEAD"))

def show(p: Paths, sha: str) -> str:
    return git(p, "show", "--no-textconv", "--no-ext-diff", "--format=fuller", sha).stdout.decode(errors="replace")

def porcelain(p: Paths) -> str:
    return git(p, "status", "--porcelain", "-uall").stdout.decode(errors="replace")

def ls_files(p: Paths, sha: str) -> list[str]:
    return _out(git(p, "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", sha)).splitlines()

def init_repo(p: Paths, remote_url: str | None) -> str:
    name, email = IDENTITY
    env = {"GIT_AUTHOR_NAME": name, "GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL": email, "GIT_COMMITTER_NAME": name, "GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL": email}
    git(p, "init", "-q", "-b", "main", write=True)
    git(p, "commit", "-q", "--allow-empty", "-m", "Initial commit", write=True, env=env)
    if remote_url:
        git(p, "remote", "add", "origin", remote_url, write=True)
    return head(p)

def commit_all(p: Paths, who: tuple[str, str], message: str) -> str:
    env = {"GIT_AUTHOR_NAME": who[0], "GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL": who[1], "GIT_COMMITTER_NAME": who[0], "GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL": who[1]}
    git(p, "add", "-A", write=True, env=env)
    git(p, "commit", "-q", "-m", message, write=True, env=env)
    return head(p)

def checkout_paths(p: Paths, paths_: list[str]) -> None:
    if paths_:
        git(p, "checkout", "--", *paths_, write=True)

def remove_untracked(p: Paths, paths_: list[str]) -> None:
    if paths_:
        git(p, "clean", "-f", "-q", "--", *paths_, write=True)

def bundle(p: Paths, host_dest: Path, refs: tuple[str, ...] = ("--all",)) -> Path:
    host_dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    git(p, "bundle", "create", f"/out/{host_dest.name}", *refs, out_dir=host_dest.parent)
    return host_dest

# ---- host-side helpers that never touch an arm repo -----------------------------------------------------
def _host_env() -> dict:
    return {**os.environ, "GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM": "1", "GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT": "0"}

def ls_remote_tag(repo_slug: str, tag: str) -> str:
    """Commit SHA of a tag on a public GitHub repo (peeled for annotated tags). Runs outside any repo."""
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
        r = subprocess.run(["git", "ls-remote", f"https://github.com/{repo_slug}", f"refs/tags/{tag}",
                            f"refs/tags/{tag}^{{}}"], cwd=d, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True, env=_host_env())
    refs = dict(reversed(l.split("\t")) for l in r.stdout.splitlines())
    return refs.get(f"refs/tags/{tag}^{{}}") or refs[f"refs/tags/{tag}"]

def commit_harness_file(path: Path, message: str) -> None:
    """The harness repo (this repo) only. Commits exactly one path, never whatever else is staged (review S-8)."""
    subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(REPO_ROOT), "add", "--", str(path)], check=True)
    subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(REPO_ROOT), "commit", "-q", "-m", message, "--", str(path)], check=True)

def harness_is_clean(path: Path) -> bool:
    r = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(REPO_ROOT), "diff", "--quiet", "--", str(path)])
    return r.returncode == 0

def clone_bundle_bare(bundle_path: Path, dest: Path) -> None:
    """Host: bare clone of a bundle made in a container — objects and refs only, no config or hooks."""
    subprocess.run(["git", "-c", "core.hooksPath=/dev/null", "clone", "-q", "--bare", str(bundle_path), str(dest)],
                   check=True, env=_host_env())

def archive_from_bare(bare: Path, ref: str) -> bytes:
    return subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(bare), "archive", "--format=tar", ref], capture_output=True,
                          check=True, env=_host_env()).stdout

def push_from_bare(bare: Path, url: str, ref: str = "main") -> None:
    """Uses the owner's own global git credentials (global config is read; the bare clone's config is git's default)."""
    subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(bare), "-c", "core.hooksPath=/dev/null", "push", url, f"{ref}:{ref}"], check=True)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `runner/repo.py`**

```python
# runner/repo.py
from __future__ import annotations
import io, json, shutil, subprocess, tarfile
from pathlib import Path
from runner import config, gitops
from runner.paths import Paths

ORG = "troupe-run"

def github_slug(pass_: str, arm: str) -> str | None:
    if pass_ == "pass1":
        return f"{ORG}/bakeoff-specs-{arm}-pass1"
    if pass_ == "pass2":
        return f"{ORG}/bakeoff-specs-{arm}"
    if pass_ == "canary":
        return f"{ORG}/bakeoff-specs-{arm}-canary"
    return None

def init(p: Paths, arm: str, pass_: str) -> str:
    if p.base.exists():
        raise FileExistsError(f"{p.base} exists; a pass starts from nothing (OD20)")
    for d in (p.config_dir, p.repo, p.snapshots, p.scratch):
        d.mkdir(parents=True)
    name, email = gitops.IDENTITY
    (p.home / ".gitconfig").write_text(f"[user]\n\tname = {name}\n\temail = {email}\n"
                                       f"[init]\n\tdefaultBranch = main\n[safe]\n\tdirectory = *\n")
    slug = github_slug(pass_, arm)
    return gitops.init_repo(p, f"https://github.com/{slug}.git" if slug else None)

def head(p: Paths) -> str:
    return gitops.head(p)

def _snap_name(step: int | str) -> str:
    return f"step-{step:02d}.tar" if isinstance(step, int) else f"step-{step}.tar"

def snapshot(p: Paths, step: int | str) -> Path:
    """Host tar of arm-controlled trees: symlinks are stored as links, never followed (tarfile default)."""
    p.snapshots.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out = p.snapshots / _snap_name(step)
    with tarfile.open(out, "w") as tf:
        tf.add(p.home, arcname="home"); tf.add(p.work, arcname="work")
    return out

def restore(p: Paths, step: int | str) -> None:
    """Extract into a temp dir first (filter="data" refuses links pointing outside, absolute paths, devices),
    so a refused snapshot leaves the current state untouched; then swap."""
    src, tmp = p.snapshots / _snap_name(step), p.base / ".restore-tmp"
    shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)
    with tarfile.open(src) as tf:
        tf.extractall(tmp, filter="data")
    shutil.rmtree(p.home); shutil.rmtree(p.work)
    (tmp / "home").rename(p.home); (tmp / "work").rename(p.work)
    tmp.rmdir()

def export(p: Paths, sha: str, dest: Path) -> None:
    dest.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with tarfile.open(fileobj=io.BytesIO(gitops.archive(p, sha))) as tf:
        tf.extractall(dest, filter="data")

def bundle(p: Paths, dest: Path) -> Path:
    return gitops.bundle(p, dest)

def github_plan(pass_: str) -> list[str]:
    out = []
    for arm in config.all_arms():
        a = config.load_arm(arm)
        out.append(f'gh repo create {github_slug(pass_, arm)} --private '
                   f'--description "bakeoff-specs arm: {a.tool} {a.version} ({pass_})"')
    return out

def github_verify(pass_: str) -> dict:
    res: dict = {}
    for arm in config.all_arms():
        r = subprocess.run(["gh", "repo", "view", github_slug(pass_, arm), "--json",
                            "visibility,hasIssuesEnabled,isEmpty"], capture_output=True, text=True)
        res[arm] = json.loads(r.stdout) if r.returncode == 0 else {"error": r.stderr.strip()}
    r = subprocess.run(["gh", "repo", "list", ORG, "--visibility", "public", "--json", "name", "--limit", "500"],
                       capture_output=True, text=True)
    res["_public_repos"] = [x["name"] for x in json.loads(r.stdout)] if r.returncode == 0 else ["<gh error>"]
    return res

def verify_ok(res: dict) -> bool:
    arms = [v for k, v in res.items() if not k.startswith("_")]
    return not res["_public_repos"] and all(
        v.get("visibility") == "PRIVATE" and v.get("hasIssuesEnabled") and v.get("isEmpty") for v in arms)

def publish(p: Paths, pass_: str, ask, scan) -> bool:
    """Push refs/heads/main via a host bare clone of a bundle made in a container (review S-1).
    `scan(dir) -> list[findings]` is secrets.scan_tree (Task 21); any finding refuses the push."""
    slug = github_slug(pass_, p.arm)
    if not slug:
        return False
    work = p.scratch / "publish"
    shutil.rmtree(work, ignore_errors=True); work.mkdir(parents=True)
    b = gitops.bundle(p, work / "main.bundle", refs=("refs/heads/main",))
    bare = work / "clone.git"
    gitops.clone_bundle_bare(b, bare)
    tree = work / "tree"; tree.mkdir()
    with tarfile.open(fileobj=io.BytesIO(gitops.archive_from_bare(bare, "main"))) as tf:
        tf.extractall(tree, filter="data")
    findings = scan(tree)
    if findings:
        print(f"Refusing to publish {slug}: {len(findings)} secret finding(s): {findings[:5]}")
        return False
    if not ask(f"Push main of {p.arm} ({pass_}) to {slug}? [y/N] "):
        return False
    gitops.push_from_bare(bare, f"https://github.com/{slug}.git")
    return True
```

`runner github-plan <pass>` prints the seven `gh repo create` lines, then for each GitHub-tracker arm the PAT
instructions (OD16): *"Create a fine-grained token: resource owner troupe-run; repository access: only
`<slug>`; permissions: Issues read/write, Metadata read; **expiration: 45 days**. Store it with `security
add-generic-password -s bakeoff-gh-<pass>-<arm> -a "$USER" -w`."* `runner github-verify <pass>` prints the
JSON and exits non-zero unless `repo.verify_ok` holds (every repo exists, is private, has issues enabled, is
empty, and **no `troupe-run` repo is public**, review G7).

```python
# tests/runner/test_repo_docker.py
import tarfile
import pytest
from runner import gitops, repo
from runner.paths import Paths

pytestmark = pytest.mark.docker

@pytest.fixture
def P(tmp_state):
    p = Paths("dry9", "dryrun")
    repo.init(p, "dryrun", "dry9")
    return p

def test_new_repo_has_one_empty_commit_on_main_with_the_neutral_identity(P):
    assert gitops.git(P, "rev-list", "--count", "HEAD").stdout.strip() == b"1"
    assert gitops.ls_files(P, "HEAD") == []
    s = gitops.state(P)
    assert s.branch == "main" and s.main == s.head and not s.dirty
    assert gitops.log(P, "HEAD~0", "HEAD") == [] and "Developer" in gitops.show(P, "HEAD")

def test_restore_returns_home_and_work_exactly_to_the_snapshot(P):
    repo.snapshot(P, 3)
    (P.repo / "junk.py").write_text("x"); (P.config_dir / "leak").write_text("y")
    repo.restore(P, 3)
    assert not (P.repo / "junk.py").exists() and not (P.config_dir / "leak").exists()

def test_export_contains_only_committed_files_of_main(P):
    (P.repo / "a.py").write_text("1")
    gitops.commit_all(P, gitops.IDENTITY, "a")
    (P.repo / "untracked.py").write_text("2")
    repo.export(P, gitops.main_sha(P), P.scratch / "x")
    assert sorted(f.name for f in (P.scratch / "x").iterdir()) == ["a.py"]

def test_work_on_a_branch_is_not_in_main(P):                                  # review F-1
    gitops.git(P, "checkout", "-q", "-b", "feature", write=True)
    (P.repo / "b.py").write_text("1")
    gitops.commit_all(P, gitops.IDENTITY, "b")
    s = gitops.state(P)
    assert s.branch == "feature" and not s.head_in_main and s.main != s.head

def test_a_hostile_hook_and_fsmonitor_never_run_on_the_host(P, tmp_path):     # review S-1
    marker = tmp_path / "pwned"
    (P.repo / ".git" / "hooks" / "pre-commit").write_text(f"#!/bin/sh\ntouch {marker}\n")
    (P.repo / ".git" / "hooks" / "pre-commit").chmod(0o755)
    gitops.git(P, "config", "core.fsmonitor", f"touch {marker}; false", write=True)
    gitops.state(P); gitops.porcelain(P)
    (P.repo / "c.py").write_text("1"); gitops.commit_all(P, gitops.IDENTITY, "c")
    assert not marker.exists()          # the host path does not exist inside the container

def test_restore_refuses_a_symlink_that_points_outside(P, tmp_path):          # review S-1 extension
    (P.repo / "evil").symlink_to("/etc/passwd")
    repo.snapshot(P, 4)
    with pytest.raises(tarfile.AbsoluteLinkError):
        repo.restore(P, 4)
    assert (P.repo / ".git").exists()                   # a refused restore leaves the current state intact
```

### Task 9: Setup flow, installed-command listing, manifest, model overrides (machine part)

Old Task 9 is split in three (review Q-12): this task is the machinery; Tasks 10–11 are the seven checklists
and verify scripts, written in full.

**Files:**
- Create: `runner/commands.py`, `runner/checklist.py`, `runner/config/project-principles.md`, `checklists/README.md`,
  `checklists/verify/lib.sh`, `tests/runner/test_commands.py`, `tests/runner/test_checklist.py`,
  `tests/runner/test_commands_docker.py`
- Modify: `runner/cli.py` (`setup <pass> <arm>`, `checklist <pass> <arm>`, `shell <pass> <arm>`; the owner prompts
  come from `owner.Owner`, Task 14 — until Task 14 lands, these subcommands are exercised only by tests with a
  stub owner)

**Interfaces:**
- `commands.listing(p: Paths, arm) -> Listing(names: set[str], models: list[dict])` — runs `LIST_PY` **inside the
  arm container** (network none, mounts read-only), so the host never walks arm-controlled trees; it lists
  project commands/skills/agents, plugins under `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR/plugins` (route A) and `/opt/plugins` (route
  B), and every `model:` in agent/skill/command frontmatter (review F-6).
- `commands.installed(p, arm) -> set[str]` (names without the leading `/`); `commands.leading(text) -> str | None`;
  `commands.invocation(name, arguments, installed) -> str | None` — the method's own invocation form of a named
  command **or skill** (`"bmad-create-architecture"` → `"/bmad-create-architecture"`, `"/opsx:apply x"` →
  `"/opsx:apply x"`), or `None` if nothing with that name is installed (review F-2).
- `checklist.Check(id, ok, detail)`; `checklist.green(checks, owner_items) -> bool`;
  `checklist.owner_items(md_text) -> list[tuple[str, str]]`; `checklist.HOST_CHECKS: dict[str, set[str]]`;
  `checklist.verify(p, arm, mode) -> list[Check]`; `checklist.host_checks(p, arm, pass_, smoke_stream) -> list[Check]`;
  `checklist.manifest(p, arm) -> dict[str, str]`; `checklist.manifest_diff(a, b) -> list[str]`;
  `checklist.gh_hosts_yml(pat) -> str`; `checklist.run_setup(pass_, arm, cred, owner) -> SetupResult`;
  `checklist.run_shell_only(pass_, arm, offline=False) -> list[Check]` (no model turns; for `network` tests and probe P9);
  `checklist.is_green(p) -> bool` (**the gate: `run-arm` refuses step 1 unless true**, design §6).
- **Verify-script protocol:** `checklists/verify/<arm>.sh <setup|step>` runs in the arm image, `--network none`,
  home and work mounted **read-only**, prints one JSON object per line `{"id","ok","detail"}`. `step` mode
  re-checks only pins (tool and plugin versions), so drift is caught at every step start (design §2).

**Setup flow (`runner setup <pass> <arm>`, owner present):**
1. `repo.init`.
2. In an egress `Stage(phase="setup")` with `setup_common + arm.egress.setup`:
   a. **Plugins.** Route A (marketplace, OD9): `claude plugin marketplace add <marketplace>` (when given) and
      `claude plugin install <install>` into the arm's home. Route B (plugin-dir): the same two commands run
      once, in pass 1 only, into a **scratch** config dir (`state/<pass>/<arm>/marketplace-check/`) as the
      recorded ergonomics check (exit codes, output, seconds → `00-setup/marketplace-check.json`); sessions load
      the vendored `/opt/plugins/<name>` via `--plugin-dir`. No model turn either way.
   b. Each `setup.shell` command runs as `bash -lc "<cmd>"` in the arm image, cwd = the repo, with
      `/setup/project-principles.md` mounted read-only. Output → `00-setup/shell/NN.log`.
   c. Each `setup.agent` prompt (`{principles}` replaced by the file's text) runs as a `role="setup"` turn in one
      resumed session. After each turn the owner sees the final text and either types a reply (sent as the
      next turn) or presses Enter to move on (setup is owner-attended; the owner's seconds are recorded as setup
      effort, design §8).
   c′. Each `setup.post_shell` command, as in b (steps that need what the agent turns created).
   d. The `smoke` prompt, if any, runs in a fresh session with cwd `/work/smoke` (not the repo); its stream must
      contain a `Skill` tool_use naming `expect_skill`; its transcripts are moved out of the arm's home into
      `00-setup/smoke/` and `/work/smoke` is deleted.
3. GitHub-tracker arms (real passes and the canary): the arm's PAT is read from the keychain item
   `bakeoff-gh-<pass>-<arm>` and written to `home/.config/gh/hosts.yml` (mode 600). If
   `runner/config/mcp/<arm>.json` exists (OD24 B with MCP only), it is copied to `home/.bk-mcp.json` with the PAT
   substituted for `${GITHUB_PAT}`.
4. If `gitops.state` is dirty: `gitops.commit_all(IDENTITY, "Set up <tool> <version>")`.
5. The verify script (`setup` mode) + host checks; `manifest.json` (SHA-256 of every file under
   `manifest_paths`); in pass 2 the manifest must equal pass 1's (`setup.manifest-matches-pass1`; a difference
   shows the files and asks the owner — review G6, OD20).
6. Owner items (`OV<n>`), y/n + note.
7. Writes `00-setup/{checklist.json, owner-checklist.json, versions.json, installed-commands.json,
   model-overrides.json, manifest.json, egress.log, turns/, transcripts/, shell/}` and `repo.snapshot(p, 1)`.

```python
def test_manifest_diff_names_changed_added_and_removed_files():
    assert checklist.manifest_diff({"a": "1", "b": "2"}, {"a": "1", "b": "3", "c": "4"}) == ["b (changed)", "c (added)"]
    assert checklist.manifest_diff({"a": "1"}, {}) == ["a (removed)"]

    c = checklist.model_ids_check([{"file": "agents/r.md", "model": "haiku"},
                                   {"file": "agents/x.md", "model": "claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929"}], ["claude-opus-5-5"])

    assert y.startswith("github.com:\n") and "oauth_token: github_pat_X" in y and "git_protocol: https" in y

    p = Paths("pass1", "control"); d = p.setup_results; d.mkdir(parents=True)
    (d / "checklist.json").write_text(json.dumps([{"id": "a", "ok": True, "detail": ""}]))
    (d / "owner-checklist.json").write_text(json.dumps({"OV1": {"ok": True, "note": ""}}))
    assert checklist.is_green(p)
    (d / "owner-checklist.json").write_text(json.dumps({"OV1": {"ok": False, "note": "no"}}))
    assert not checklist.is_green(p)
```
