- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `runner/commands.py`**

```python
# runner/commands.py
from __future__ import annotations
import json, re
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from runner import config, docker, gitops
from runner.docker import Mount, RunSpec
from runner.paths import Paths

LIST_PY = r'''
import json, re, sys
from pathlib import Path
repo, cfg = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2])
roots = [Path(x) for x in sys.argv[3:]]
names, models = set(), []
def model_of(f, shown):
    try:
        t = f.read_text(errors="replace")
    except OSError:
        return
    if t.startswith("---"):
        end = t.find("\n---", 3)
        m = re.search(r"(?m)^model:\s*['\"]?([^'\"\n#]+)", t[: end if end > 0 else 0])
        if m:
            models.append({"file": shown, "model": m.group(1).strip()})
def scan(root, plugin, shown_base):
    for f in sorted((root / "commands").rglob("*.md")) if (root / "commands").is_dir() else []:
        if f.is_symlink(): continue
        n = ":".join(f.relative_to(root / "commands").with_suffix("").parts)
        names.add(f"{plugin}:{n}" if plugin else n); names.add(n) if plugin else None
        model_of(f, f"{shown_base}commands/{f.relative_to(root / 'commands')}")
    for f in sorted((root / "skills").glob("*/SKILL.md")) if (root / "skills").is_dir() else []:
        if f.is_symlink(): continue
        n = f.parent.name
        names.add(f"{plugin}:{n}" if plugin else n); names.add(n)
        model_of(f, f"{shown_base}skills/{n}/SKILL.md")
    for f in sorted((root / "agents").glob("*.md")) if (root / "agents").is_dir() else []:
        if not f.is_symlink(): model_of(f, f"{shown_base}agents/{f.name}")
scan(repo / ".claude", "", ".claude/")
for base in [cfg / "plugins", *roots]:
    if not base.is_dir(): continue
    for man in sorted(base.rglob(".claude-plugin/plugin.json")):
        root = man.parent.parent
        try:
            name = json.loads(man.read_text())["name"]
        except Exception:
            continue
        scan(root, name, f"{root}/")
print(json.dumps({"names": sorted(names), "models": models}))
'''

@dataclass
class Listing:
    names: set[str]
    models: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)

def parse_listing(text: str) -> Listing:
    o = json.loads(text)
    return Listing(set(o["names"]), list(o["models"]))

def listing(p: Paths, arm: config.Arm) -> Listing:
    s = RunSpec(name=f"bk-list-{p.pass_}-{p.arm}", image=gitops.image_for(arm.name), label=f"bakeoff.run={p.pass_}/{p.arm}",
                network="none", mounts=[Mount(p.work, "/work", True), Mount(p.home, "/home/actor", True)],
                env={"HOME": "/home/actor"}, workdir="/work",
                cmd=["python3", "-c", LIST_PY, p.container_repo, "/home/actor/.claude", "/opt/plugins"],
                stdin=False, limits=config.load_pins().limits, proxy=False)
    r = docker.run(s, timeout=120)
    if r.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f"command listing failed: {r.stderr.decode(errors='replace')[:300]}")
    return parse_listing(r.stdout.decode())

def installed(p: Paths, arm: config.Arm) -> set[str]:
    return listing(p, arm).names

_LEAD = re.compile(r"^\s*(/[A-Za-z0-9][\w.:-]*)")

def leading(text: str) -> str | None:
    m = _LEAD.match(text)
    return m.group(1) if m else None

def invocation(name: str, arguments: str, installed_names: set[str]) -> str | None:
    """The method's own invocation form for a named command or skill (review F-2). Skills and commands are both
    invoked as /<name> in Claude Code (probe P6 confirms for skills)."""
    n = name.strip().split()[0].lstrip("/") if name.strip() else ""
    if n not in installed_names:
        return None
    return f"/{n}" + (f" {arguments.strip()}" if arguments and arguments.strip() else "")
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `runner/checklist.py`**

```python
# runner/checklist.py
from __future__ import annotations
import json, re, shutil, subprocess, time
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
from pathlib import Path
from runner import commands, config, credentials, docker, egress, gitops, repo, session, transcripts
from runner.docker import Mount, RunSpec
from runner.paths import Paths, REPO_ROOT

CHECKS_DIR = REPO_ROOT / "checklists"
PRINCIPLES = config.CONFIG_DIR / "project-principles.md"
COMMAND_ARMS = {"openspec", "speckit", "speckitty", "ccsdd", "superpowers"}   # a slash command in entry/bug_entry/must_run
HOST_CHECKS: dict[str, set[str]] = {
    arm: ({f"{arm}.entry-commands"} if arm in COMMAND_ARMS else set())
         | {"common.no-foreign-model-ids", "setup.manifest-matches-pass1"}
         | ({"github.issues-access"} if arm != "speckitty" else set())
         | ({"superpowers.smoke"} if arm == "superpowers" else set())
    for arm in config.REAL_ARMS}

@dataclass
class Check:
    id: str
    ok: bool
    detail: str

@dataclass
class SetupResult:
    checks: list[Check]
    owner: dict[str, dict]
    green: bool
    owner_seconds: float

def green(checks: list[Check], owner_items_: dict[str, bool]) -> bool:
    return bool(checks) and all(c.ok for c in checks) and all(owner_items_.values())

def owner_items(md: str) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    return re.findall(r"^- \[ \] (OV\d+): (.+)$", md, re.M)

def manifest_diff(a: dict[str, str], b: dict[str, str]) -> list[str]:
    out = [f"{k} (changed)" for k in sorted(a) if k in b and a[k] != b[k]]
    out += [f"{k} (added)" for k in sorted(set(b) - set(a))]
    out += [f"{k} (removed)" for k in sorted(set(a) - set(b))]
    return out

def model_ids_check(models: list[dict], accepted: list[str]) -> Check:
    """Aliases (opus/sonnet/haiku/inherit) are remapped by OD2's env; a FULL non-pinned model ID is not."""
    aliases = {"opus", "sonnet", "haiku", "inherit", "default"}
    bad = [m for m in models if m["model"] not in aliases and m["model"] not in accepted]
    return Check("common.no-foreign-model-ids", not bad,
                 "; ".join(f"{m['file']}: {m['model']}" for m in bad) or f"{len(models)} model settings, all aliases")

def gh_hosts_yml(pat: str) -> str:
    return f"github.com:\n    oauth_token: {pat}\n    git_protocol: https\n"

def _arm_spec(p: Paths, arm: config.Arm, cmd: list[str], *, network: str = "none", proxy: bool = False,
              ro: bool = True, extra: list[Mount] | None = None, name: str = "verify") -> RunSpec:
    return RunSpec(name=f"bk-{name}-{p.pass_}-{p.arm}-{int(time.time()*1000) % 10**8}", image=gitops.image_for(arm.name),
                   label=f"bakeoff.run={p.pass_}/{p.arm}", network=network,
                   mounts=[Mount(p.home, "/home/actor", ro), Mount(p.work, "/work", ro), *(extra or [])],
                   env={"HOME": "/home/actor", "CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR": "/home/actor/.claude",
                        "PLUGIN_ROUTE": config.load_pins().plugin_route, **arm.env},
                   workdir=p.container_repo, cmd=cmd, stdin=False, limits=config.load_pins().limits, proxy=proxy)

def verify(p: Paths, arm: config.Arm, mode: str) -> list[Check]:
    s = _arm_spec(p, arm, ["bash", f"/checks/{arm.name}.sh", mode],
                  extra=[Mount(CHECKS_DIR / "verify", "/checks", True)])
    r = docker.run(s, timeout=300)
    out = [Check(**json.loads(l)) for l in r.stdout.decode().splitlines() if l.startswith("{")]
    if r.returncode not in (0,) and not out:
        out = [Check(f"{arm.name}.verify-script", False, r.stderr.decode(errors="replace")[:300])]
    return out

def manifest(p: Paths, arm: config.Arm) -> dict[str, str]:
    paths_ = [x for x in arm.manifest_paths]
    if config.load_pins().plugin_route == "marketplace" and arm.plugins:
        paths_.append("~/.claude/plugins/cache")
    script = r"""cd "$1"; shift; for x in "$@"; do case "$x" in "~/"*) x="$HOME/${x#\~/}";; esac
      [ -e "$x" ] && find "$x" -type f ! -path '*/__pycache__/*' ! -name '*.pyc' -print0; done | sort -z | xargs -0 -r sha256sum"""
    r = docker.run(_arm_spec(p, arm, ["bash", "-c", script, "manifest", p.container_repo, *paths_]), timeout=300)
    return {line[66:]: line[:64] for line in r.stdout.decode().splitlines() if len(line) > 66}

def _shell(p: Paths, arm: config.Arm, cmd: str, st: egress.Stage, log: Path) -> int:
    s = _arm_spec(p, arm, ["bash", "-lc", cmd], network=st.net, proxy=True, ro=False, name="setup",
                  extra=[Mount(PRINCIPLES, "/setup/project-principles.md", True)])
    r = docker.run(s, timeout=1800)
    log.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    log.write_bytes(b"$ " + cmd.encode() + b"\n" + r.stdout + b"\n--- stderr ---\n" + r.stderr + f"\nexit {r.returncode}\n".encode())
    return r.returncode

def _plugins(p: Paths, arm: config.Arm, pass_: str, st: egress.Stage, out: Path) -> dict:
    route, rec = config.load_pins().plugin_route, {}
    if not arm.plugins or (route == "plugin-dir" and pass_ != "pass1"):
        return rec
    target_home = p.home if route == "marketplace" else p.base / "marketplace-check"
    (target_home / ".claude").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for i, pl in enumerate(arm.plugins):
        cmds = ([f"claude plugin marketplace add {pl.marketplace}"] if pl.marketplace else []) + [f"claude plugin install {pl.install}"]
        for j, c in enumerate(cmds):
            s = RunSpec(name=f"bk-plugin-{p.pass_}-{p.arm}-{i}{j}", image=gitops.image_for(arm.name),
                        label=f"bakeoff.run={p.pass_}/{p.arm}", network=st.net,
                        mounts=[Mount(target_home, "/home/actor", False)],
                        env={"HOME": "/home/actor", "CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR": "/home/actor/.claude"}, workdir="/home/actor",
                        cmd=["bash", "-lc", c], stdin=False, limits=config.load_pins().limits, proxy=True)
            t0 = time.monotonic(); r = docker.run(s, timeout=600)
            rec[c] = {"exit": r.returncode, "seconds": round(time.monotonic() - t0, 1),
                      "output": (r.stdout + r.stderr).decode(errors="replace")[-2000:]}
    (out / ("plugins.json" if route == "marketplace" else "marketplace-check.json")).write_text(json.dumps(rec, indent=2))
    return rec

def _agent_turns(p, arm, pass_, cred, owner, st, out) -> float:
    pins, text, sid, n, owner_s = config.load_pins(), PRINCIPLES.read_text(), None, 0, 0.0
    queue = [a.replace("{principles}", text) for a in arm.setup_agent]
    while queue:
        n += 1
        env = credentials.session_env(pins, arm, role="arm", isolated=False, resume=sid or "")
        t = session.Turn(pass_, arm.name, 0, 1, n, gitops.image_for(arm.name), queue.pop(0), env,
                         [Mount(p.home, "/home/actor", False), Mount(p.work, "/work", False)], st.net, True,
                         p.container_repo, p.inbox("setup"), "setup", arm.dry, p.config_dir)
        r = session.run_turn(t, cred, out, pins.limits.turn_seconds)
        sid = r.session_id or sid
        reply, secs = owner.setup_reply(r.final_text)
        owner_s += secs
        if reply:
            queue.insert(0, reply)
    return owner_s

def _smoke(p, arm, pass_, cred, st, out) -> Check:
    if not arm.smoke:
        return Check(f"{arm.name}.smoke", True, "no smoke test")
    pins, smoke_dir = config.load_pins(), p.work / "smoke"
    smoke_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    before = transcripts.snapshot(p.config_dir)
    env = credentials.session_env(pins, arm, role="arm", isolated=False)
    t = session.Turn(pass_, arm.name, 0, 1, 99, gitops.image_for(arm.name), arm.smoke.prompt, env,
                     [Mount(p.home, "/home/actor", False), Mount(p.work, "/work", False)], st.net, True,
                     "/work/smoke", p.inbox("setup"), "setup", arm.dry, p.config_dir)
    r = session.run_turn(t, cred, out / "smoke", pins.limits.turn_seconds)
    moved = transcripts.collect(p.config_dir, before, out / "smoke" / "transcripts")
    for rel in set(transcripts.snapshot(p.config_dir)) - set(before):       # move, don't copy, out of the arm's home
        (p.config_dir / "projects" / rel).unlink()
    shutil.rmtree(smoke_dir)
    invoked = _stream_invoked_skill(r.stream_path, arm.smoke.expect_skill)
    return Check(f"{arm.name}.smoke", invoked, f"Skill {arm.smoke.expect_skill} invoked: {invoked}; {len(moved)} transcripts moved")

def _stream_invoked_skill(stream: Path | None, skill: str) -> bool:
    import gzip
    if not stream:
        return False
    with gzip.open(stream, "rt") as fh:
        for line in fh:
            if '"tool_use"' not in line:
                continue
            try:
                ev = json.loads(line)
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                continue
            for c in (ev.get("message") or {}).get("content", []):
                if c.get("type") == "tool_use" and c.get("name") == "Skill" and \
                        str((c.get("input") or {}).get("skill", "")).split(":")[-1] == skill:
                    return True
    return False

def _install_github_pat(p: Paths, arm: config.Arm, pass_: str) -> str | None:
    if arm.tracker != "github" or arm.dry:
        return None
    r = subprocess.run(["security", "find-generic-password", "-s", f"bakeoff-gh-{pass_}-{arm.name}", "-w"],
                       capture_output=True, text=True)
    if r.returncode != 0 or not r.stdout.strip():
        raise RuntimeError(f"no keychain item bakeoff-gh-{pass_}-{arm.name} (run `runner github-plan {pass_}`)")
    pat = r.stdout.strip()
    gh = p.home / ".config" / "gh"; gh.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (gh / "hosts.yml").write_text(gh_hosts_yml(pat)); (gh / "hosts.yml").chmod(0o600)
    mcp = config.CONFIG_DIR / "mcp" / f"{arm.name}.json"
    if mcp.exists():                                                     # OD24 B with MCP only
        (p.home / ".bk-mcp.json").write_text(mcp.read_text().replace("${GITHUB_PAT}", pat))
        (p.home / ".bk-mcp.json").chmod(0o600)
    return pat

def host_checks(p: Paths, arm: config.Arm, pass_: str, smoke: Check | None, st: egress.Stage | None) -> list[Check]:
    out: list[Check] = []
    lst = commands.listing(p, arm)
    (p.setup_results / "installed-commands.json").write_text(json.dumps(sorted(lst.names), indent=2))
    (p.setup_results / "model-overrides.json").write_text(json.dumps(
        {"declared": lst.models, "effective": config.load_pins().model,
         "note": "OD2: every alias resolves to the pinned model; the method's own choices are listed here"}, indent=2))
    out.append(model_ids_check(lst.models, config.load_pins().accepted_models))
    if arm.name in COMMAND_ARMS:
        needed = [c.split()[0].lstrip("/") for c in (arm.entry, arm.bug_entry, *arm.must_run) if c.startswith("/")]
        if arm.export_after:                                     # OD24 B: the trigger and the export command itself
            needed += [arm.export_after.lstrip("/"), "speckit-taskstoissues"]
        missing = sorted(set(needed) - lst.names)
        out.append(Check(f"{arm.name}.entry-commands", not missing, f"missing: {missing or 'none'}"))
    if smoke is not None:
        out.append(smoke)
    if arm.tracker == "github" and not arm.dry and st is not None:
        slug = repo.github_slug(pass_, arm.name)
        s = _arm_spec(p, arm, ["bash", "-lc", f"gh issue list --repo {slug} --limit 1 && gh label list --repo {slug} --limit 1"],
                      network=st.net, proxy=True, name="ghcheck")
        r = docker.run(s, timeout=120)
        out.append(Check("github.issues-access", r.returncode == 0, (r.stdout + r.stderr).decode(errors="replace")[-300:]))
    m = manifest(p, arm)
    (p.setup_results / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(m, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
    if pass_ == "pass2":
        first = json.loads((Paths("pass1", arm.name).setup_results / "manifest.json").read_text())
        diff = manifest_diff(first, m)
        out.append(Check("setup.manifest-matches-pass1", not diff, "; ".join(diff[:20]) or "identical"))
    else:
        out.append(Check("setup.manifest-matches-pass1", True, f"{pass_}: recorded ({len(m)} files)"))
    return out

def run_setup(pass_: str, arm_name: str, cred, owner) -> SetupResult:
    arm, p, pins = config.load_arm(arm_name), Paths(pass_, arm_name), config.load_pins()
    repo.init(p, arm_name, pass_)
    out = p.setup_results; out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with egress.Stage(pass_, arm_name, "setup", pins.egress_setup_common + arm.egress_setup, out / "egress.log") as st:
        _plugins(p, arm, pass_, st, out)
        for i, cmd in enumerate(arm.setup_shell, 1):
            if _shell(p, arm, cmd, st, out / "shell" / f"{i:02d}.log") != 0:
                raise RuntimeError(f"setup shell step {i} failed: {cmd} (see {out/'shell'})")
        owner_s = _agent_turns(p, arm, pass_, cred, owner, st, out)
        for i, cmd in enumerate(arm.setup_post_shell, len(arm.setup_shell) + 1):
            if _shell(p, arm, cmd, st, out / "shell" / f"{i:02d}.log") != 0:
                raise RuntimeError(f"setup post-shell step {i} failed: {cmd}")
        smoke = _smoke(p, arm, pass_, cred, st, out) if arm.smoke else None
        _install_github_pat(p, arm, pass_)
        if gitops.state(p).dirty:
            gitops.commit_all(p, gitops.IDENTITY, f"Set up {arm.tool} {arm.version}")
        checks = verify(p, arm, "setup") + host_checks(p, arm, pass_, smoke, st)
    md = (CHECKS_DIR / f"{arm_name}.md").read_text()
    answers = owner.items(owner_items(md))
    owner_s += sum(a[2] for a in answers.values())
    rec = {k: {"ok": v[0], "note": v[1]} for k, v in answers.items()}
    (out / "checklist.json").write_text(json.dumps([asdict(c) for c in checks], indent=2))
    (out / "owner-checklist.json").write_text(json.dumps(rec, indent=2))
    from runner import images
    (out / "versions.json").write_text(json.dumps({"image": images.lock()["arms"].get(arm_name, {}),
                                                   "plugins": [asdict(x) for x in arm.plugins],
                                                   "plugin_route": pins.plugin_route,
                                                   "owner_setup_seconds": owner_s}, indent=2))
    repo.snapshot(p, 1)
    ok = green(checks, {k: v["ok"] for k, v in rec.items()})
    return SetupResult(checks, rec, ok, owner_s)

def run_shell_only(pass_: str, arm_name: str, offline: bool = False) -> list[Check]:
    """Tests and probe P9: repo.init + plugins + shell + post_shell steps (no model turns), then the verify script.
    offline=True gives the setup stage an EMPTY allowlist, so any network need shows up as a refusal (review G6)."""
    arm, p, pins = config.load_arm(arm_name), Paths(pass_, arm_name), config.load_pins()
    repo.init(p, arm_name, pass_)
    out = p.setup_results; out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    hosts = [] if offline else pins.egress_setup_common + arm.egress_setup
    with egress.Stage(pass_, arm_name, "setup", hosts, out / "egress.log") as st:
        _plugins(p, arm, "pass1", st, out)
        for i, cmd in enumerate(arm.setup_shell + arm.setup_post_shell, 1):
            if _shell(p, arm, cmd, st, out / "shell" / f"{i:02d}.log") != 0:
                raise RuntimeError(f"setup shell step {i} failed: {cmd} (see {out/'shell'})")
    return verify(p, arm, "setup")

def is_green(p: Paths) -> bool:
    d = p.setup_results
    if not (d / "checklist.json").exists() or not (d / "owner-checklist.json").exists():
        return False
    checks = [Check(**c) for c in json.loads((d / "checklist.json").read_text())]
    owner_ = {k: v["ok"] for k, v in json.loads((d / "owner-checklist.json").read_text()).items()}
    return green(checks, owner_)
```
(`owner.items` returns `{id: (ok, note, seconds)}`; `owner.setup_reply(text) -> (reply_or_empty, seconds)`; both
defined in Task 14.)

CLI additions (`cli.py`): `setup <pass> <arm>` → `checklist.run_setup(pass_, arm, credentials.from_keychain(),
owner.Owner())` (dry arms: `credentials.dummy()` and the scripted owner, Task 24), printing each check;
`checklist <pass> <arm>` → re-runs `verify(mode="setup")` and prints; `shell <pass> <arm>` → `os.execvp("docker",
[...run_argv of an interactive spec with "-t", setup egress, HISTFILE=/home/actor/.bash_history])`, with the
history copied to `00-setup/shell-history.txt` by `setup` when it next runs `checklist`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `checklists/verify/lib.sh` and `checklists/README.md`**

```bash
# checklists/verify/lib.sh — sourced by every verify script. Prints JSON lines; never modifies anything.
check() {  # check ID STATUS(0=ok) DETAIL
  local ok=false; [ "$2" = 0 ] && ok=true
  jq -cn --arg id "$1" --argjson ok "$ok" --arg d "$3" '{id:$id, ok:$ok, detail:$d}'
}
ok_if() { if "$@" >/dev/null 2>&1; then echo 0; else echo 1; fi; }
common() {  # common ARM EXPECTED_PLUGINS(space-separated, sorted; "" for none)
  local v; v=$(claude --version 2>&1)
  check common.claude-version "$( [ "$v" = "2.1.281 (Claude Code)" ]; echo $?)" "$v"
  check common.isolation-env "$( [ "${CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_AUTO_MEMORY:-}" = 1 ] && [ "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR" = /home/actor/.claude ]; echo $?)" ""
  local n; n=$(git -c safe.directory='*' config user.name)
  check common.git-identity "$( [ "$n" = "Developer" ]; echo $?)" "$n"
  local installed; installed=$( { jq -r '.plugins // {} | keys[]' "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR/plugins/installed_plugins.json" 2>/dev/null | sed 's/@.*//';
                                   ls /opt/plugins 2>/dev/null; } | sort -u | tr '\n' ' ' | sed 's/ $//')
  check common.no-foreign-plugins "$( [ "$installed" = "$2" ]; echo $?)" "installed: ${installed:-none}"
}
```

```markdown
<!-- checklists/README.md -->
# Owner one-time steps

1. In your own terminal (never in an agent session): `claude setup-token`, then
   `security add-generic-password -s bakeoff-claude-oauth -a "$USER" -w` and paste the token.
2. Confirm that the subscription terms allow this use (spike B open question 1).
3. For each pass: run the commands `uv run python -m runner github-plan <pass>` prints (private repos), create one
   fine-grained PAT per GitHub-tracker arm as it describes (**expiry 45 days**), store each under
   `bakeoff-gh-<pass>-<arm>`, then `uv run python -m runner github-verify <pass>` must pass.
4. Enable the results secret-scan hook once: `git config core.hooksPath .githooks` (Task 21).
5. Keep the machine otherwise idle during runs.
6. At the end of pass 1: revoke every pass-1 PAT (GitHub → Settings → Developer settings → Fine-grained tokens).
```

```markdown
# Setup checklist — control (plain Claude Code 2.1.281, no methodology)

## Source
design §2 (control arm).

## Pins
Claude Code 2.1.281 (bakeoff-base:cc2.1.281); model claude-opus-5-5.

## Setup (what the runner runs)
Nothing beyond `repo.init` (one empty commit) and the GitHub PAT for the baseline tracker.

## Machine-verified
- `common.claude-version` — `claude --version` is 2.1.281
- `common.isolation-env` — CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_AUTO_MEMORY=1 and CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=/home/actor/.claude
- `common.git-identity` — repo identity is the neutral `Developer`
- `common.no-foreign-plugins` — no plugin installed or vendored
- `control.no-method` — no `.claude/skills`, `.claude/commands`, `CLAUDE.md` or `AGENTS.md` in the repo
- `common.no-foreign-model-ids` — (host) no installed file names a full non-pinned model ID
- `github.issues-access` — (host) the arm's PAT can list issues and labels of its repo
- `setup.manifest-matches-pass1` — (host) pass 2's setup files equal pass 1's

## Owner-verified
- [ ] OV1: The repo contains only the empty initial commit.
- [ ] OV2: Telemetry: plain Claude Code with CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC=1; nothing else to disable (probe P9 evidence).

## Known traps
- None specific. The control must receive exactly the same harness text as every method arm.
```

```markdown
# Setup checklist — superpowers (obra/superpowers 6.4.2)

## Source
research/CANDIDATES.md §1.1; https://github.com/obra/superpowers#claude-code

## Pins
superpowers 6.4.2 at the tag's verified commit (arms/superpowers.toml `sha`); route per OD9
(`pins.toml [plugins] route`); Claude Code 2.1.281; model claude-opus-5-5.

## Setup (what the runner runs)
1. Route B (plugin-dir, default): the plugin is vendored at `/opt/plugins/superpowers` in the arm image and loaded
   with `--plugin-dir`; in pass 1 the documented `claude plugin install superpowers@claude-plugins-official` also
   runs once into a scratch config as a recorded ergonomics check. Route A: the documented install into the arm's home.
2. No init, no constitution (§1.1 item 2).
3. Smoke test: a fresh session in `/work/smoke` receives "Let's make a react todo list" and must invoke `brainstorming`.

## Machine-verified
- `common.claude-version` — `claude --version` is 2.1.281
- `common.isolation-env` — CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_AUTO_MEMORY=1 and CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=/home/actor/.claude
- `common.git-identity` — repo identity is the neutral `Developer`
- `common.no-foreign-plugins` — only `superpowers`
- `superpowers.plugin-version` — the loaded plugin's `plugin.json` version is 6.4.2
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the four verify scripts**

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# checklists/verify/control.sh MODE — arm image, cwd = repo, network none, mounts read-only
set -u; . /checks/lib.sh; MODE=${1:-setup}
common control ""
[ "$MODE" = step ] && exit 0
check control.no-method "$(ok_if bash -c '! [ -e .claude/skills ] && ! [ -e .claude/commands ] && ! [ -e CLAUDE.md ] && ! [ -e AGENTS.md ]')" "$(ls -A)"
```

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# checklists/verify/superpowers.sh MODE
set -u; . /checks/lib.sh; MODE=${1:-setup}
common superpowers "superpowers"
if [ "${PLUGIN_ROUTE:-plugin-dir}" = plugin-dir ]; then root=/opt/plugins/superpowers
else root=$(dirname "$(dirname "$(find "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR/plugins" -path '*superpowers*/.claude-plugin/plugin.json' | head -1)")"); fi
v=$(jq -r .version "$root/.claude-plugin/plugin.json" 2>/dev/null)
check superpowers.plugin-version "$( [ "$v" = 6.4.2 ]; echo $?)" "$root: $v"
[ "$MODE" = step ] && exit 0
missing=""; for s in brainstorming writing-plans subagent-driven-development test-driven-development finishing-a-development-branch; do
  [ -f "$root/skills/$s/SKILL.md" ] || missing="$missing $s"; done
check superpowers.skills "$( [ -z "$missing" ]; echo $?)" "missing:${missing:- none}"
```

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# checklists/verify/openspec.sh MODE
set -u; . /checks/lib.sh; MODE=${1:-setup}
common openspec ""
v=$(openspec --version 2>&1 | grep -oE '[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+' | head -1)
check openspec.version "$( [ "$v" = 1.13.2 ]; echo $?)" "$v"
[ "$MODE" = step ] && exit 0
n=$(ls -d .claude/skills/openspec-*/SKILL.md 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
check openspec.skills "$( [ "$n" -ge 1 ]; echo $?)" "$n skills"
missing=""; for c in propose apply archive update sync explore; do [ -f ".claude/commands/opsx/$c.md" ] || missing="$missing $c"; done
check openspec.commands "$( [ -z "$missing" ]; echo $?)" "missing:${missing:- none}"
check openspec.tree "$(ok_if test -d openspec)" ""
t=$(openspec config get telemetry.enabled 2>&1 | tail -1)
check openspec.telemetry-off "$( [ "$t" = false ] && [ "${OPENSPEC_TELEMETRY:-}" = 0 ]; echo $?)" "config: $t; env: ${OPENSPEC_TELEMETRY:-unset}"
check openspec.context "$(ok_if bash -c "grep -q '^context:' openspec/config.yaml && grep -q eventsourcing openspec/config.yaml")" ""
```

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# checklists/verify/bmad.sh MODE
set -u; . /checks/lib.sh; MODE=${1:-setup}
common bmad "bmad-core-tools bmad-method"
bad=""
for pl in bmad-method bmad-core-tools; do
  if [ "${PLUGIN_ROUTE:-plugin-dir}" = plugin-dir ]; then f=/opt/plugins/$pl/.claude-plugin/plugin.json
  else f=$(find "$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR/plugins" -path "*$pl*/.claude-plugin/plugin.json" | head -1); fi
  v=$(jq -r .version "$f" 2>/dev/null); case "$v" in 6.12.*) ;; *) bad="$bad $pl=$v";; esac
done
check bmad.plugin-version "$( [ -z "$bad" ]; echo $?)" "bad:${bad:- none}"
[ "$MODE" = step ] && exit 0
check bmad.setup-done "$(ok_if bash -c 'test -d _bmad && test -d _bmad-output')" ""
check bmad.ticketing-gh "$(ok_if grep -qE '"?gh"?' _bmad/custom/ticketing-store-config.toml)" ""
unlocked=""; for f in _bmad/scripts/*.py; do [ -e "$f" ] || continue
  if grep -q '^# /// script' "$f" && [ ! -f "$f.lock" ]; then unlocked="$unlocked $f"; fi; done
check bmad.scripts-locked "$( [ -z "$unlocked" ]; echo $?)" "unlocked:${unlocked:- none}"
```

Replace the id-equality test in `tests/runner/test_checklist.py` with an honestly named one (review Q-11), and add
the docker test that actually runs the scripts:

```python
# tests/runner/test_checklist.py (additions)
import re
from pathlib import Path
import pytest
from runner import checklist, config

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "checklists"
DONE_ARMS = ["bmad", "control", "openspec", "superpowers"]        # Task 11 extends this to all seven

def md_ids(arm):
    return set(re.findall(r"^- `([a-z0-9.-]+)`", (ROOT / f"{arm}.md").read_text(), re.M))

@pytest.mark.parametrize("arm", DONE_ARMS)
def test_every_machine_id_in_the_md_is_named_in_the_script_or_host_checks_and_vice_versa(arm):
    script = (ROOT / "verify" / f"{arm}.sh").read_text() + (ROOT / "verify" / "lib.sh").read_text()
    named = set(re.findall(r"check ([a-z0-9.-]+) ", script)) | checklist.HOST_CHECKS[arm]
    assert md_ids(arm) == named

@pytest.mark.parametrize("arm", DONE_ARMS)
def test_every_checklist_has_owner_items_and_traps_sections(arm):
    text = (ROOT / f"{arm}.md").read_text()
    assert "## Owner-verified" in text and "## Known traps" in text and checklist.owner_items(text)
```

```python
# tests/runner/test_verify_docker.py — the scripts really print what the md lists (review Q-11, Q-13)
import pytest
from runner import checklist, config, repo
from runner.paths import Paths
from tests.runner.test_checklist import md_ids, DONE_ARMS

pytestmark = pytest.mark.docker

@pytest.mark.parametrize("arm", DONE_ARMS)
def test_the_script_prints_exactly_the_non_host_ids_of_the_md(arm, tmp_state):
    p = Paths("dry9", arm); repo.init(p, arm, "dry9")
    printed = {c.id for c in checklist.verify(p, config.load_arm(arm), "setup")}
    assert printed == md_ids(arm) - checklist.HOST_CHECKS[arm]

@pytest.mark.parametrize("arm", ["superpowers", "bmad"])
def test_vendored_plugins_pass_their_version_checks_offline(arm, tmp_state):     # OD9 B
    p = Paths("dry9", arm); repo.init(p, arm, "dry9")
    got = {c.id: c.ok for c in checklist.verify(p, config.load_arm(arm), "step")}
    assert got[f"{arm}.plugin-version"] and got["common.claude-version"]
```

```python
import pytest

pytestmark = [pytest.mark.docker, pytest.mark.network]

def test_openspec_shell_setup_passes_its_machine_checks(tmp_state):
    got = {c.id: c for c in checklist.run_shell_only("dry9", "openspec")}
    for cid in ["openspec.version", "openspec.skills", "openspec.commands", "openspec.tree",
                "openspec.telemetry-off", "openspec.context"]:
```

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# checklists/verify/speckit.sh MODE
set -u; . /checks/lib.sh; MODE=${1:-setup}
common speckit ""
v=$(specify version 2>&1 | grep -oE '[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+' | head -1)
check speckit.version "$( [ "$v" = 1.0.12 ]; echo $?)" "$v"
[ "$MODE" = step ] && exit 0
missing=""; for s in specify clarify plan tasks analyze implement converge taskstoissues; do
  [ -f ".claude/skills/speckit-$s/SKILL.md" ] || missing="$missing $s"; done
check speckit.skills "$( [ -z "$missing" ]; echo $?)" "missing:${missing:- none}"
missing=""; for s in assess fix test; do [ -f ".claude/skills/speckit-bug-$s/SKILL.md" ] || missing="$missing $s"; done
check speckit.bug-skills "$( [ -z "$missing" ]; echo $?)" "missing:${missing:- none}"
c=.specify/memory/constitution.md
check speckit.constitution "$( [ -s $c ] && ! grep -qE '\[[A-Z_]{3,}\]' $c; echo $?)" "$(wc -c < $c 2>/dev/null) bytes"
```

### Task 12: Step handoffs (brief + contract + delivery sentence), step-5 delivery, how each method is driven

**Files:**
- Create: `runner/handoff.py`, `runner/config/scenario.toml`, `tests/runner/test_handoff.py`, `tests/runner/test_drive_config.py`
- Modify: `runner/config/arms/*.toml` (`[drive]`)

**Interfaces:**
- `handoff.norm(name)`, `handoff.contract_sections(contract) -> dict[str, str]`, `handoff.named(brief) ->
  (kind, names)`, `handoff.in_force(step, contract) -> str` (for the PO), `handoff.issue_body(brief) -> str`.
- `handoff.compose(step, brief, contract) -> str` = brief verbatim + `---` + each named contract section verbatim +
  `---` + **the delivery sentence** (`scenario.toml [messages] delivery`) — identical for every arm, every step
  (review F-1; kept regardless of OD4).
- `handoff.step5(brief, contract) -> list[str]` — OD6: one message (A) or `[CR-A message, CR-B message]` (B),
  per `scenario.toml [step5] delivery`.
- `handoff.bug_message(ref, contract) -> str` (step 6: the issue reference, the Aliases section, the delivery sentence).
- `handoff.entry_message(arm, step, text) -> str` (`entry`, or `bug_entry` at step 6; `str.replace`, never `format`).
- `handoff.MESSAGES`, `handoff.STEP5` (the parsed `scenario.toml` tables).

```python
# tests/runner/test_handoff.py
from pathlib import Path
import pytest
from runner import config, handoff

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "scenario"
C = (ROOT / "contract.md").read_text()
BRIEFS = sorted((ROOT / "steps").glob("0*.md"))
DELIVERY = handoff.MESSAGES["delivery"]

@pytest.mark.parametrize("brief", BRIEFS, ids=lambda p: p.name)
def test_every_named_section_resolves_to_exactly_one_contract_heading(brief):
    kind, names = handoff.named(brief.read_text())
    assert names and all(handoff.norm(n) in handoff.contract_sections(C) for n in names)

def test_step1_hands_over_the_five_sections_in_force_in_order():
    msg = handoff.compose(1, BRIEFS[0].read_text(), C)
    assert [l for l in msg.splitlines() if l.startswith("## ")] == [
        "## General (from step 1)", "## Includes (from step 1)", "## `show PATH` (step 1 and 2)",
        "## `set PATH VALUE` (from step 1)", "## `history [PATH]` (from step 1)"]

@pytest.mark.parametrize("step", [1, 2, 3, 4, 7])
def test_every_handoff_ends_with_the_one_identical_delivery_sentence(step):          # review F-1
    msg = handoff.compose(step, BRIEFS[step - 1].read_text(), C)
    assert msg.rstrip().endswith(DELIVERY) and msg.count(DELIVERY) == 1

def test_step3_hands_over_get_and_show_only():
    msg = handoff.compose(3, BRIEFS[2].read_text(), C)
    assert [l for l in msg.splitlines() if l.startswith("## ")][0].startswith("## `get` and `show`")
    assert msg.startswith(BRIEFS[2].read_text().rstrip("\n"))

def test_step5_two_requests_split_cr_a_from_cr_b(monkeypatch):                      # OD6 B
    monkeypatch.setitem(handoff.STEP5, "delivery", "two-requests")
    a, b = handoff.step5(BRIEFS[4].read_text(), C)
    assert "## CR-A" in a and "## CR-B" not in a and "two change requests" not in a and "## Overrides" in a
    assert b.startswith(handoff.STEP5["cr_b_intro"]) and "## CR-B" in b and "## CR-A" not in b
    assert a.rstrip().endswith(DELIVERY) and b.rstrip().endswith(DELIVERY)

def test_step5_one_message_keeps_both_change_requests(monkeypatch):                 # OD6 A
    monkeypatch.setitem(handoff.STEP5, "delivery", "one-message")
    [m] = handoff.step5(BRIEFS[4].read_text(), C)
    assert "## CR-A" in m and "## CR-B" in m

def test_issue_body_drops_the_contract_line_and_the_message_carries_the_section():
    body = handoff.issue_body(BRIEFS[5].read_text())
    assert "Contract sections" not in body and body.startswith("Our troupe.yml")
    msg = handoff.bug_message("troupe-run/bakeoff-specs-control#1", C)
    assert "troupe-run/bakeoff-specs-control#1" in msg and "## Aliases (from step 6)" in msg and msg.rstrip().endswith(DELIVERY)

def test_entry_template_is_filled_literally_even_with_braces():
    assert handoff.entry_message(config.load_arm("openspec"), 1, "x {y} z") == "/opsx:propose x {y} z"

def test_in_force_at_step3_excludes_the_replaced_show_section():
    text = handoff.in_force(3, C)
    assert "## `get` and `show`" in text and "## `show PATH` (step 1 and 2)" not in text
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `runner/handoff.py`**

```python
# runner/handoff.py
from __future__ import annotations
import re, tomllib
from runner import config
from runner.config import Arm

_SCEN = tomllib.loads((config.CONFIG_DIR / "scenario.toml").read_text())
MESSAGES: dict[str, str] = _SCEN["messages"]
STEP5: dict = _SCEN["step5"]
STEP6: dict[str, str] = _SCEN["step6"]

_LAST = re.compile(r"^Contract sections (in force|added): (.+)\.\s*$", re.M)
_FROM = re.compile(r"\(from step (\d)")
_ONLY = re.compile(r"\(step 1 and 2\)")

def norm(name: str) -> str:
    return name.replace("`", "").split("(")[0].strip()

def contract_sections(text: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    parts = re.split(r"(?m)^(?=## )", text)
    return {norm(p.splitlines()[0][3:]): p.rstrip("\n") for p in parts if p.startswith("## ")}

def named(brief: str) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:
    m = list(_LAST.finditer(brief))[-1]
    return m.group(1), [n.strip() for n in m.group(2).split(";")]

def _sections(brief: str, contract: str) -> str:
    secs = contract_sections(contract)
    return "\n\n".join(secs[norm(n)] for n in named(brief)[1])

def _deliver(body: str) -> str:
    return f"{body.rstrip()}\n\n---\n\n{MESSAGES['delivery']}\n"

def compose(step: int, brief: str, contract: str) -> str:
    return _deliver(f"{brief.rstrip()}\n\n---\n\n{_sections(brief, contract)}")

def step5(brief: str, contract: str) -> list[str]:
    if STEP5["delivery"] == "one-message":
        return [compose(5, brief, contract)]
    parts = re.split(r"(?m)^(?=## )", brief)
    cr_a = next(p for p in parts if p.startswith("## CR-A")).rstrip()
    cr_b = next(p for p in parts if p.startswith("## CR-B")).rstrip()
    line = _LAST.search(brief).group(0).strip()
    cr_b = _LAST.sub("", cr_b).rstrip()
    first = _deliver(f"{STEP5['title_a']}\n\n{cr_a}\n\n{line}\n\n---\n\n{_sections(brief, contract)}")
    second = _deliver(f"{STEP5['cr_b_intro']}\n\n{cr_b}")
    return [first, second]

def issue_body(brief: str) -> str:
    return _LAST.sub("", brief).rstrip() + "\n"

def bug_message(ref: str, contract: str) -> str:
    return _deliver(f"A bug has been reported in our tracker: {ref}. Please handle it.\n\n"
                    f"Contract sections added: Aliases.\n\n---\n\n{contract_sections(contract)['Aliases']}")

def in_force(step: int, contract: str) -> str:
    keep = []
    for name, sec in contract_sections(contract).items():
        head = sec.splitlines()[0]
        m = _FROM.search(head)          # checked first: the step-3 heading also quotes "(step 1 and 2)"
        ok = int(m.group(1)) <= step if m else bool(_ONLY.search(head)) and step <= 2
        if ok:
            keep.append(sec)
    return "\n\n".join(keep) + "\n"

def entry_message(arm: Arm, step: int, text: str) -> str:
    return (arm.bug_entry if step == 6 else arm.entry).replace("{handoff}", text)
```
(`in_force` keeps `set PATH VALUE` at step 5+ even though Overrides "replaces rule 2" inside it: the contract marks
the replacement inside the section. In OD6 B the CR-B message carries no contract section: CR-A's message already
handed over Overrides, which covers both.)

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `[drive]` per arm (OD5, OD19, OD24; review F-2, F-5).** One rule for every arm:
  `entry` = what a user of that method types to start a request (CANDIDATES); `must_run` = the documented closing
  command(s) of the method's per-change flow, sent only if the step ends without them having run.

```toml
# control.toml
[drive]
entry = "{handoff}"
bug_entry = "{handoff}"
must_run = []
must_run_source = "no methodology: no closing command"
fresh_session_before = []
export_after = ""

# superpowers.toml — skills auto-trigger (CANDIDATES §1.1 "no slash-command stages to run by hand")
[drive]

bug_entry = "{handoff}"
must_run = ["/superpowers:finishing-a-development-branch"]
must_run_source = "CANDIDATES §1.1 Workflow stages: … → requesting-code-review → finishing-a-development-branch"
fresh_session_before = []
export_after = ""

# bmad.toml — "ask the bmad skill" (CANDIDATES §1.5 item 3)
[drive]
entry = "Use the bmad skill for this request:\n\n{handoff}"
bug_entry = "Use the bmad skill for this request:\n\n{handoff}"
must_run = []
must_run_source = "CANDIDATES §1.5 Workflow stages: the one-session path (bmad-build) documents no closing command; bmad-retrospective closes epics only"
fresh_session_before = []
export_after = ""

# openspec.toml
[drive]
entry = "/opsx:propose {handoff}"
bug_entry = "/opsx:propose {handoff}"
must_run = ["/opsx:archive"]
must_run_source = "CANDIDATES §1.3 Workflow stages: /opsx:propose → /opsx:apply → /opsx:archive (archive merges deltas into specs)"
fresh_session_before = []
export_after = ""

# speckit.toml
[drive]
entry = "/speckit-specify {handoff}"
bug_entry = "/speckit-bug-assess {handoff}"
must_run = ["/speckit-converge"]
must_run_source = "CANDIDATES §1.2 Workflow stages: repeat implement → converge until convergence reports Converged"
fresh_session_before = []
export_after = "/speckit-tasks"          # OD24 B: send /speckit-taskstoissues once after tasks ("" = OD24 A)

# speckitty.toml
[drive]
entry = "/spec-kitty.specify {handoff}"
bug_entry = "/spec-kitty.specify {handoff}"
must_run = ["/spec-kitty.accept", "/spec-kitty.merge"]
must_run_source = "CANDIDATES §1.4 Workflow stages: … implement → review per WP → accept → consolidate/merge"
fresh_session_before = []
export_after = ""

# ccsdd.toml
[drive]
entry = "/kiro-discovery {handoff}"
bug_entry = "/kiro-discovery {handoff}"
must_run = ["/kiro-validate-impl"]
must_run_source = "CANDIDATES §1.7a Workflow: … → /kiro-impl → /kiro-validate-impl (GO/NO-GO)"
fresh_session_before = []
export_after = ""
```
Every slash command here is checked against the installed listing at setup (`<arm>.entry-commands`, Task 9); a
missing one blocks step 1 until the toml is corrected (e.g. after probe P6/P9). `fresh_session_before` is empty
for every arm until a citation is found (OD19 recommendation).

- [ ] **Step 5 (branch — only if OD24 = B *and* probe P11 finds that `/speckit-taskstoissues` needs the GitHub MCP
  server):** add the pinned server to the speckit image and its config. Nothing here runs otherwise.

```dockerfile
# append to runner/images/arm/speckit/Dockerfile (asset name and checksum file confirmed by P11 from the v1.12.2 release page)
ARG GHMCP_VERSION=1.12.2
ARG TARGETARCH
USER root
RUN a=$( [ "$TARGETARCH" = arm64 ] && echo arm64 || echo x86_64 ) \
 && curl -fsSLo /tmp/m.tgz "https://github.com/github/github-mcp-server/releases/download/v${GHMCP_VERSION}/github-mcp-server_Linux_${a}.tar.gz" \
 && curl -fsSLo /tmp/sums "https://github.com/github/github-mcp-server/releases/download/v${GHMCP_VERSION}/github-mcp-server_${GHMCP_VERSION}_checksums.txt" \
 && (cd /tmp && grep " github-mcp-server_Linux_${a}.tar.gz$" sums | awk '{print $1"  m.tgz"}' | sha256sum -c -) \
 && tar -xzf /tmp/m.tgz -C /usr/local/bin github-mcp-server && rm /tmp/m.tgz /tmp/sums
USER actor
```
```json
// runner/config/mcp/speckit.json — ${GITHUB_PAT} is substituted at setup (Task 9); the file lands in the arm's home, mode 600
{"mcpServers": {"github": {"command": "/usr/local/bin/github-mcp-server", "args": ["stdio", "--toolsets", "issues"],
                           "env": {"GITHUB_PERSONAL_ACCESS_TOKEN": "${GITHUB_PAT}"}}}}
```
`drive` passes `mcp=True` to `session_env` when `home/.bk-mcp.json` exists (Task 15), which adds
`--mcp-config` next to `--strict-mcp-config`, so this arm gets exactly this one server.

### Task 13: Product-owner agent — protocol, session, escalation, brief growth

**Files:**
- Create: `runner/config/po-protocol.md`, `runner/po.py`, `runner/faults.py` (the `HarnessFault` exception only;
  Task 22 adds the ledger), `tests/runner/test_po.py`, `tests/runner/test_po_docker.py`

**Interfaces:**
- `po.Answer(question, reply, escalate, cited)`; `po.Decision(kind, reply, answers, approved, command, arguments,
  significant)`; `kind ∈ question | approval | next_command | continue | step_done`.
- `po.parse(text) -> Decision` (raises `po.ProtocolError`).
- `po.PoContext(pass_, arm, step, attempt, paths, image, dry, contract_in_force, step_brief, qa_so_far, out_dir)` —
  `image` and `dry` replace draft 1's module global (review Q-8).
- `po.decide(ctx, arm_message, cred, n, brief_path) -> PoResult(decision, turns: list[TurnResult], pin: PinReport,
  retries: int)`: one fresh PO session per decision; a second session on a parse failure (`ProtocolError` after
  two); a **non-ok session** (API error, no result) is retried in place with `limits.transient_backoff`, then
  raises `po.SessionError(outcome)` — never `ProtocolError` (review C-5); `auth_failed` raises at once. The PO's
  transcripts are collected into `po/a<k>-NNN/transcripts/` and pin-verified (review C-6).
- `po.append_escalation(brief_path, question, answer, pass_, arm, step)`; `po.commit_brief(brief_path, pass_, arm,
  step)` (real passes only; commits exactly that path, review S-8).
- **The PO session (OD21):** base image (the dry-run image for dry arms); `isolated=True` (`--setting-sources ""`);
  tools `Read,Glob,Grep`; egress Anthropic only; its own home `state/<pass>/po-home/<arm>`; mounts: the arm's whole
  `/work` **read-only** (so worktree specs/plans can be reviewed), and `/po` read-only with `protocol.md`,
  `po-brief.md`, `contract.md` (sections in force), `step.md`, `qa.md`, `message.md`. Its own inbox
  (`Paths.inbox("po")`). The PO message is: "Read /po/protocol.md and follow it."

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the PO protocol** (harness text; identical for all arms; owner approves)

```markdown
<!-- runner/config/po-protocol.md -->
# Product-owner protocol

You are the product owner described in /po/po-brief.md. **This protocol replaces the brief's reply format:**
where the brief says to reply `ESCALATE: <question>`, you instead mark that question `"escalate": true` in the
JSON below. Everything else in the brief applies as written.

The brief is your only source of product answers, together with the contract sections in /po/contract.md and the
current request in /po/step.md. /po/qa.md holds what has already been asked and answered in this step; stay
consistent with it. The engineer's latest message is /po/message.md. The engineer's work is under /work (your
working directory is their repository); you may read it (Read, Glob, Grep) to review specs, plans or designs you
are asked to approve. You never write or edit anything.

Classify the engineer's latest message as exactly one kind, and reply with **one JSON object and nothing else**:

{"kind": "...", "answers": [], "reply": null, "approved": null, "command": null, "arguments": "",
 "significant": false}

- "question" — the engineer asks for information or product decisions. Put **one entry per question** in
  "answers": {"question": "<the question, one sentence>", "reply": "<answer>", "escalate": false,
  "cited": "<brief or contract section>"}. If neither the brief nor the contract answers a question, set that
  entry's "escalate": true and "reply": null. Answer every question you can; escalate only the ones you cannot.
- "approval" — the engineer asks you to approve a spec, plan, design, proposal, task list or similar. Read what
  they point to. Set "approved" true or false. "reply" is your verdict; if not approved, name the contract or brief
  section it contradicts and why.
- "next_command" — the message ends by telling the user to run one of the method's own commands or skills next.
  "command" is exactly the name the engineer gave (with or without a leading "/"), "arguments" any arguments they
  gave. If several are offered, take the one the engineer recommends first.
- "continue" — the engineer stopped only to ask whether to proceed, or reported progress mid-work without asking
  anything answerable from the brief. "reply" is exactly "Please continue."
- "step_done" — the engineer reports that the requested work is complete and asks nothing.

Set "significant": true when an answer decides behaviour the contract does not state literally, when you do not
approve, or when you escalate. Never mention later steps. Never write code.
```

```python
# tests/runner/test_po.py
import json, pytest
from runner import po

def J(**o):
    base = {"kind": "question", "answers": [{"question": "q?", "reply": "x", "escalate": False, "cited": "contract §General"}],
            "reply": None, "approved": None, "command": None, "arguments": "", "significant": False}
    base.update(o); return json.dumps(base)

def test_plain_json_parses():
    d = po.parse(J())
    assert d.kind == "question" and d.answers[0].reply == "x"

def test_json_inside_a_code_fence_parses():
    assert po.parse("```json\n" + J(kind="continue", answers=[], reply="Please continue.") + "\n```").kind == "continue"

def test_mixed_questions_answer_some_and_escalate_others():                    # review F-12
    d = po.parse(J(answers=[{"question": "a?", "reply": "yes", "escalate": False, "cited": "brief"},
                            {"question": "b?", "reply": None, "escalate": True, "cited": None}]))
    assert [a.escalate for a in d.answers] == [False, True] and d.escalated == ["b?"]

def test_next_command_may_name_a_skill_without_a_slash():                       # review F-2
    d = po.parse(J(kind="next_command", answers=[], command="bmad-create-architecture", arguments=""))
    assert d.command == "bmad-create-architecture"

@pytest.mark.parametrize("bad", [
    "not json", J(kind="chat"), J(answers=[]),
    J(answers=[{"question": "q", "reply": "an answer", "escalate": True, "cited": None}]),
    J(answers=[{"question": "q", "reply": None, "escalate": False, "cited": None}]),
    J(kind="approval", answers=[], reply="ok", approved=None),
    J(kind="next_command", answers=[], command=None),
    J(kind="continue", answers=[], reply="ok go"),
    J(kind="step_done", answers=[], reply="x"),
])
def test_protocol_violations_are_rejected(bad):
    with pytest.raises(po.ProtocolError):
        po.parse(bad)

def test_escalated_answer_is_appended_under_the_escalated_answers_heading(tmp_path):
    b = tmp_path / "po-brief.md"
    b.write_text("# Product-owner brief\n\n## Escalated answers\n(Appended during the bake-off: question, answer, date, arm that first raised it.)\n")
    po.append_escalation(b, "Should floats keep trailing zeros?", "Print as written in the file.", "pass1", "openspec", 1)
    tail = b.read_text().splitlines()[-1]
    assert tail.startswith("- **Q:** Should floats keep trailing zeros? **A:** Print as written in the file.")
    assert "pass1" in tail and "first raised by openspec at step 1" in tail

def test_protocol_says_it_replaces_the_briefs_escalate_format():               # review Q-3
    text = po.PROTOCOL.read_text()
    assert "replaces the brief's reply format" in text and "ESCALATE" in text
```

```python
from runner import config, credentials, po, repo
from runner.paths import Paths

pytestmark = pytest.mark.docker

def ctx(tmp_state):

                        "# Step 1", [], tmp_state / "out")

def test_decide_returns_the_decision_and_keeps_every_input(tmp_state, tmp_path):
    b = tmp_path / "brief.md"; b.write_text("# brief\n## Escalated answers\n")
    good = json.dumps({"kind": "continue", "answers": [], "reply": "Please continue.", "approved": None,
                       "command": None, "arguments": "", "significant": False})
    r = po.decide(ctx(tmp_state), f"working\nFAKE:reply={good}", credentials.dummy(), 1, b)
    assert r.decision.kind == "continue" and r.pin.ok
    for f in ["protocol.md", "po-brief.md", "contract.md", "step.md", "qa.md", "message.md", "decision.json", "model-pin.json"]:
        assert (tmp_state / "out" / "po" / "a1-001" / f).exists()

def test_unusable_reply_twice_is_a_protocol_error(tmp_state, tmp_path):
    b = tmp_path / "brief.md"; b.write_text("# brief\n## Escalated answers\n")
    with pytest.raises(po.ProtocolError):
        po.decide(ctx(tmp_state), "FAKE:reply=nonsense", credentials.dummy(), 2, b)

    import dataclasses

        config.load_pins(), limits=dataclasses.replace(config.load_pins().limits, transient_backoff=[0])))
    b = tmp_path / "brief.md"; b.write_text("# brief\n## Escalated answers\n")
    with pytest.raises(po.SessionError):
        po.decide(ctx(tmp_state), "FAKE:api_error=API Error: 529 overloaded", credentials.dummy(), 3, b)
```

```python
# runner/po.py
from __future__ import annotations
import json, re, shutil, time
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from runner import config, credentials, egress, gitops, session, transcripts
from runner.docker import Mount
from runner.paths import Paths, REAL_PASSES
from runner.transcripts import PinReport

KINDS = {"question", "approval", "next_command", "continue", "step_done"}
PROTOCOL = config.CONFIG_DIR / "po-protocol.md"
CONTINUE = "Please continue."
TRANSIENT = ("no_result", "is_error")

class ProtocolError(ValueError):
    pass

class SessionError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, outcome: str, detail: str = ""):
        super().__init__(f"PO session {outcome}: {detail[:200]}")
        self.outcome, self.detail = outcome, detail

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Answer:
    question: str
    reply: str | None
    escalate: bool
    cited: str | None

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Decision:
    kind: str
    reply: str | None
    answers: list[Answer]
    approved: bool | None
    command: str | None
    arguments: str
    significant: bool

    @property
    def escalated(self) -> list[str]:
        return [a.question for a in self.answers if a.escalate]

@dataclass
class PoResult:
    decision: Decision
    turns: list = field(default_factory=list)
    pin: PinReport | None = None
    retries: int = 0

def _extract(text: str) -> dict:
    m = re.search(r"\{.*\}", text, re.S)
    if not m:
        raise ProtocolError("no JSON object in PO reply")
    try:
        return json.loads(m.group(0))
    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
        raise ProtocolError(f"PO reply is not valid JSON: {e}") from e

def parse(text: str) -> Decision:
    o = _extract(text)
    try:
        answers = [Answer(a["question"], a.get("reply"), bool(a.get("escalate")), a.get("cited"))
                   for a in (o.get("answers") or [])]
        d = Decision(o["kind"], o.get("reply"), answers, o.get("approved"), o.get("command"),
                     o.get("arguments") or "", bool(o.get("significant")))
    except (KeyError, TypeError) as e:
        raise ProtocolError(f"malformed: {e}") from e
    if d.kind not in KINDS:
        raise ProtocolError(f"unknown kind {d.kind!r}")
    if d.kind == "question":
        if not d.answers:
            raise ProtocolError("a question needs at least one answer entry")
        for a in d.answers:
            if a.escalate and a.reply is not None:
                raise ProtocolError("an escalated entry has reply null")
            if not a.escalate and not a.reply:
                raise ProtocolError("a non-escalated entry needs a reply")
    if d.kind == "approval" and (d.approved is None or not d.reply):
        raise ProtocolError("approval needs approved true/false and a verdict")
    if d.kind == "next_command" and not (d.command and d.command.strip()):
        raise ProtocolError("next_command needs a command")
    if d.kind == "continue" and d.reply != CONTINUE:
        raise ProtocolError("continue reply must be exactly 'Please continue.'")
    if d.kind == "step_done" and d.reply is not None:
        raise ProtocolError("step_done has reply null")
    return d

@dataclass
class PoContext:
    pass_: str
    arm: str
    step: int
    attempt: int
    paths: Paths
    image: str
    dry: bool
    contract_in_force: str
    step_brief: str
    qa_so_far: list[dict]
    out_dir: Path

def _qa_md(qa: list[dict]) -> str:
    return "\n\n".join(f"Q{i}: {q['arm_message']}\nA{i} ({q['answered_by']}): {q['reply']}"
                       for i, q in enumerate(qa, 1)) or "(nothing yet)"

def decide(ctx: PoContext, arm_message: str, cred, n: int, brief_path: Path) -> PoResult:
    pins, arm = config.load_pins(), config.load_arm(ctx.arm)
    d = ctx.out_dir / "po" / f"a{ctx.attempt}-{n:03d}"; d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)   # attempts never collide
    shutil.copy(PROTOCOL, d / "protocol.md"); shutil.copy(brief_path, d / "po-brief.md")
    (d / "contract.md").write_text(ctx.contract_in_force); (d / "step.md").write_text(ctx.step_brief)
    (d / "qa.md").write_text(_qa_md(ctx.qa_so_far)); (d / "message.md").write_text(arm_message)
    home = ctx.paths.po_home; (home / ".claude").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    before = transcripts.snapshot(home / ".claude")
    res, last_err, parse_tries, k = PoResult(decision=None), None, 0, 0     # type: ignore[arg-type]
    backoff = list(pins.limits.transient_backoff)
    while True:
        k += 1
        msg = "Read /po/protocol.md and follow it."
        if last_err:
            msg += f"\n\nYour previous reply could not be used ({last_err}). Reply with one JSON object only."
        with egress.Stage(ctx.pass_, ctx.arm, "po", pins.egress_po, ctx.out_dir / "egress-po.log") as st:
            t = session.Turn(ctx.pass_, ctx.arm, ctx.step, ctx.attempt, n * 100 + k, ctx.image, msg,
                             credentials.session_env(pins, arm, role="po", isolated=True, tools="Read,Glob,Grep"),
                             [Mount(home, "/home/actor", False), Mount(ctx.paths.work, "/work", True), Mount(d, "/po", True)],
                             st.net, True, ctx.paths.container_repo, ctx.paths.inbox("po"), "po", ctx.dry, home / ".claude")
            r = session.run_turn(t, cred, d, pins.limits.turn_seconds)
        res.turns.append(r)
        if r.outcome == "auth_failed" or r.outcome == "timeout":
            raise SessionError(r.outcome, r.final_text)
        if r.outcome in TRANSIENT:
            if not backoff:
                raise SessionError(r.outcome, r.final_text)
            res.retries += 1
            time.sleep(backoff.pop(0))
            continue
        try:
            dec = parse(r.final_text)
        except ProtocolError as e:
            parse_tries += 1
            if parse_tries >= 2:
                raise
            last_err = str(e)
            continue
        res.decision = dec
        (d / "decision.json").write_text(json.dumps(asdict(dec), indent=2))
        files = transcripts.collect(home / ".claude", before, d / "transcripts")
        res.pin = transcripts.verify_pin(files, [x.stream_path for x in res.turns if x.stream_path], pins)
        (d / "model-pin.json").write_text(res.pin.to_json())
        return res

def append_escalation(brief: Path, question: str, answer: str, pass_: str, arm: str, step: int) -> None:
    today = datetime.now(timezone.utc).date().isoformat()
    line = f"- **Q:** {question} **A:** {answer} _({today}; {pass_}; first raised by {arm} at step {step})_\n"
    text = brief.read_text()
    if "## Escalated answers" not in text:
        raise ValueError("brief has no '## Escalated answers' section")
    brief.write_text(text.rstrip("\n") + "\n" + line)

def commit_brief(brief: Path, pass_: str, arm: str, step: int) -> None:
    """Real passes only: the brief is harness state and its history is evidence. Commits exactly this path."""
    if pass_ in REAL_PASSES:
        gitops.commit_harness_file(brief, f"PO brief: escalated answer ({pass_}, {arm} step {step})")
```

### Task 14: The owner in the loop — terminal prompts and the scripted owner

**Files:**
- Create: `runner/owner.py`, `tests/runner/test_owner.py`

**Interfaces** (both classes; every method returns the owner's seconds so owner time is evidence, design §8):
- `escalation(question, context) -> (answer, seconds)`
- `gate(arm_message, decision, repo_changes) -> (reply, action "accept"|"override", seconds)`
- `confirm(text) -> bool`
- `items(list[(id, text)]) -> {id: (ok, note, seconds)}`
- `setup_reply(text) -> (reply_or_empty, seconds)`
- `step_review(summary_md) -> ("continue"|"pause", seconds)`
- `reclassify(what, detail) -> "arm"|"harness"` (OD14 B: the first runaway breach per arm)
- `Owner(input_fn=input, out=print, clock=time.monotonic)` — terminal; `ScriptedOwner(path).bind(arm)` — dry
  arms only (raises `PermissionError` for a real arm).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write failing tests** (the terminal path is tested with scripted stdin — review §3 dry-run table)

```python
# tests/runner/test_owner.py
import pytest
from runner import config, owner
from runner.po import Decision

def D(): return Decision("approval", "Approved: consistent with the contract.", [], True, None, "", False)

def term(lines):
    it, shown = iter(lines), []
    ticks = iter(range(0, 1000, 5))
    return owner.Owner(input_fn=lambda prompt="": next(it), out=shown.append, clock=lambda: next(ticks)), shown

def test_enter_accepts_the_po_verdict_and_is_timed():
    o, shown = term([""])
    reply, action, secs = o.gate("Please approve the plan.", D(), " src/a.py | 3 +++")
    assert (reply, action, secs) == ("Approved: consistent with the contract.", "accept", 5)
    assert any("Approved: consistent" in s for s in shown) and any("src/a.py" in s for s in shown)

def test_e_lets_the_owner_type_an_override_ending_with_a_dot_line():
    o, _ = term(["e", "Not approved: step 3 changes show.", "Please revise.", "."])
    reply, action, _ = o.gate("Please approve.", D(), "")
    assert action == "override" and reply == "Not approved: step 3 changes show.\nPlease revise."

def test_escalation_answer_is_multi_line():
    o, _ = term(["Print floats", "exactly as written.", "."])
    assert o.escalation("Floats?", "ctx")[0] == "Print floats\nexactly as written."

def test_items_record_yes_no_and_a_note():
    o, _ = term(["y", "", "n", "constitution has extras"])
    got = o.items([("OV1", "a"), ("OV2", "b")])
    assert got["OV1"][:2] == (True, "") and got["OV2"][:2] == (False, "constitution has extras")

def test_confirm_defaults_to_no():
    o, _ = term([""])
    assert o.confirm("Push?") is False

def test_scripted_owner_is_refused_for_real_arms(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "s.toml").write_text("[escalations]\ndefault = 'x'\n")
    with pytest.raises(PermissionError):
        owner.ScriptedOwner(tmp_path / "s.toml").bind(config.load_arm("control"))

def test_scripted_owner_answers_accepts_and_pauses_where_scripted(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "s.toml").write_text("[escalations]\ndefault = 'Keep it simple.'\n[gates]\ndefault = 'accept'\n"
                                     "[review]\ndefault = 'continue'\npause_after = [2]\n")
    o = owner.ScriptedOwner(tmp_path / "s.toml").bind(config.load_arm("dryrun"))
    assert o.escalation("q?", "")[0] == "Keep it simple." == o.escalation_answer
    assert o.gate("please approve", D(), "")[:2] == ("Approved: consistent with the contract.", "accept")
    assert o.step_review("# Step 1 summary")[0] == "continue"
    assert o.step_review("# Step 2 summary")[0] == "pause"
    assert o.step_review("# Step 2 summary")[0] == "continue"          # a pause fires once
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement `runner/owner.py`**

```python
# runner/owner.py
from __future__ import annotations
import re, time, tomllib
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Callable

class Owner:
    """The owner at the terminal. Every prompt is timed."""
    def __init__(self, input_fn: Callable[..., str] = input, out: Callable[[str], None] = print,
                 clock: Callable[[], float] = time.monotonic):
        self._in, self._out, self._clock = input_fn, out, clock

    def _lines(self, prompt: str) -> str:
        self._out(prompt + " (end with a line holding only '.')")
        lines = []
        while (l := self._in("")) != ".":
            lines.append(l)
        return "\n".join(lines).strip()

    def escalation(self, question: str, context: str) -> tuple[str, float]:
        t0 = self._clock()
        self._out("=" * 72 + "\nESCALATION — the PO brief does not answer this:\n" + question)
        self._out("--- the arm's message (last 40 lines) ---\n" + "\n".join(context.splitlines()[-40:]))
        answer = self._lines("Your answer (it is added to the PO brief)")
        return answer, self._clock() - t0

    def gate(self, arm_message: str, decision, repo_changes: str) -> tuple[str, str, float]:
        t0 = self._clock()
        self._out("=" * 72 + "\nOWNER GATE — the arm asks for approval.")
        self._out("--- the arm's message (last 60 lines) ---\n" + "\n".join(arm_message.splitlines()[-60:]))
        self._out(f"--- PO draft verdict (approved={decision.approved}) ---\n{decision.reply}")
        self._out("--- repo changes since the step began ---\n" + (repo_changes or "(none)"))
        while True:
            k = self._in("[Enter] accept · e = override · d = show changes again: ").strip().lower()
            if k == "":
                return decision.reply, "accept", self._clock() - t0
            if k == "e":
                return self._lines("Your reply to the arm"), "override", self._clock() - t0
            if k == "d":
                self._out(repo_changes or "(none)")

    def confirm(self, text: str) -> bool:
        return self._in(text + " [y/N] ").strip().lower() in ("y", "yes")

    def items(self, items: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> dict[str, tuple[bool, str, float]]:
        out = {}
        for iid, text in items:
            t0 = self._clock()
            ok = self._in(f"{iid}: {text}\n  ok? [y/n] ").strip().lower() in ("y", "yes")
            note = self._in("  note (optional): ").strip()
            out[iid] = (ok, note, self._clock() - t0)
        return out

    def setup_reply(self, text: str) -> tuple[str, float]:
        t0 = self._clock()
        self._out("--- setup session says (last 40 lines) ---\n" + "\n".join(text.splitlines()[-40:]))
        k = self._in("[Enter] = move on · r = reply: ").strip().lower()
        reply = self._lines("Your reply") if k == "r" else ""
        return reply, self._clock() - t0

    def step_review(self, summary_md: str) -> tuple[str, float]:
        t0 = self._clock()
        self._out(summary_md)
        k = self._in("[Enter] continue to the next step · p = pause here: ").strip().lower()
        return ("pause" if k == "p" else "continue"), self._clock() - t0

    def reclassify(self, what: str, detail: str) -> str:
        self._out(f"First runaway breach for this arm: {what}\n{detail}")
        k = self._in("Count it as the arm's outcome (a) or as a harness fault (h)? [a/h] ").strip().lower()
        return "harness" if k == "h" else "arm"

class ScriptedOwner:
    """Dry runs only: answers from a TOML script, never from a person."""
    def __init__(self, script: Path):
        self.s = tomllib.loads(Path(script).read_text())
        self._paused: set[int] = set()

    def bind(self, arm) -> "ScriptedOwner":
        if not arm.dry:
            raise PermissionError("the scripted owner may only answer for dry-run arms")
        return self

    @property
    def escalation_answer(self) -> str:
        return self.s["escalations"]["default"]

    def escalation(self, question, context):
        return self.escalation_answer, 0.0

    def gate(self, arm_message, decision, repo_changes):
        g = self.s.get("gates", {}).get("default", "accept")
        return (decision.reply, "accept", 0.0) if g == "accept" else (g.split(":", 1)[1], "override", 0.0)

    def confirm(self, text):
        return bool(self.s.get("confirm", {}).get("default", True))

    def items(self, items):
        v = bool(self.s.get("items", {}).get("default", True))
        return {i: (v, "scripted", 0.0) for i, _ in items}

    def setup_reply(self, text):
        return self.s.get("setup", {}).get("default", ""), 0.0

    def step_review(self, summary_md):
        m = re.search(r"Step (\d+)", summary_md)
        step = int(m.group(1)) if m else 0
        if step in self.s.get("review", {}).get("pause_after", []) and step not in self._paused:
            self._paused.add(step)
            return "pause", 0.0
        return self.s.get("review", {}).get("default", "continue"), 0.0

    def reclassify(self, what, detail):
        return self.s.get("reclassify", {}).get("default", "arm")
```

### Task 15: The turn loop for one attempt at a step

**Files:**
- Create: `runner/drive.py`, `tests/runner/test_drive.py`
- Modify: `runner/faults.py` (add `classify_pin`), `tests/runner/conftest.py` (add `make_test_ctx`, review Q-9)

**Interfaces:**
- `drive.StepCtx(pass_, arm, step, paths, pins, out, contract_in_force, step_brief, brief_path, cred, owner,
  sha_before, later_messages=[], sleep=time.sleep, shas={}, snapshot=None, on_restore=None)` — `later_messages`
  carries OD6 B's CR-B message; `sleep` is injectable so tests never wait; `shas`, `snapshot` and `on_restore` are
  set by `orchestrate` (Task 23).
- `drive.run_attempt(ctx, attempt, message, resume=None) -> AttemptResult(outcome "done"|"stalled"|"timeout",
  session_id, turns, po_results, exchanges, interventions, breach None|"exchanges"|"turn_seconds", retries)`.
- `faults.classify_pin(report, who) -> HarnessFault | None` (`model-pin:no-evidence`, `model-pin:<model>`,
  `pin-claude-version:<v>`; `who` goes into the detail).
- Writes `out/questions.jsonl` (one line per exchange: `attempt, n, at, arm_message, kind, reply, answered_by
  (po|owner|po+owner|runner), escalated (list of questions), significant, cited, approved, owner_action,
  owner_seconds, po_cost_usd, po_retries`), `out/owner-gates.jsonl` (approvals only).

**Loop rules (OD19: one session per attempt, resumed each turn; B's cited fresh-session exceptions):**
1. If the message's leading command is in `arm.fresh_session_before`, start a new session (logged).
2. Send the message. `timeout` → attempt ends `timeout` (`breach="turn_seconds"`). `auth_failed` → harness fault
   `infra-auth` at once.
3. **Transient errors are retried in place (review C-4):** `no_result`, or `is_error` whose text matches
   overloaded/5xx/rate-limit/timeout, is retried in the same session with `messages.resume_after_error`, after
   `limits.transient_backoff` seconds (60, 300, 900). Only when those are exhausted, or for a non-transient
   `is_error`, is a harness fault raised (`infra-<outcome>:<first line, digits removed>`).
4. Any `credential_files` after a turn → harness fault `credential-persisted` (review S-3).
5. More than `limits.exchanges_per_attempt` exchanges → attempt ends `stalled` (`breach="exchanges"`, OD14).
6. OD6 B: at the end of turn `[step5] cr_b_after_turn` (and at a `step_done` before that), send CR-B via the entry
   command instead of consulting the PO; the preempted turn is logged as kind `preempted`.
7. Ask the PO. `ProtocolError` → `po-protocol:unparseable`; `SessionError` → `infra-po-<outcome>` (review C-5); a
   PO pin violation → `classify_pin(…, "po")` (review C-6).
8. By kind: **question** → every answered entry from the PO, every escalated entry from the owner (appended to the
   brief and committed); one combined reply (review F-12). **approval** → owner gate (OD7). **next_command** →
   `commands.invocation(command, arguments, installed)`; if nothing with that name is installed, "Please
   continue." and `next_command-not-installed:<name>` (review F-2). **continue** → "Please continue."
   **step_done** → first any pending closer (OD24 B export once `export_after` ran; then each `must_run` command
   neither sent nor invoked); else the one-time commit nudge (OD4 A, only when `messages.commit_on_main` is
   non-empty and the work is not on `main`: dirty, or `HEAD` not in `refs/heads/main`, or `main` unchanged since
   the step began); else a pending CR-B; else the attempt ends `done`.

```python
# tests/runner/conftest.py (additions)
import dataclasses, shutil
from runner import config, drive, credentials
from runner.paths import Paths, REPO_ROOT

class StubOwner:
    escalation_answer = "Print floats exactly as written in the file."
    def __init__(self): self.gates, self.escalations = [], []
    def escalation(self, q, ctx): self.escalations.append(q); return self.escalation_answer, 1.0
    def gate(self, msg, dec, diff): self.gates.append(msg); return dec.reply, "accept", 2.0
    def confirm(self, text): return True
    def reclassify(self, what, detail): return "arm"
    def step_review(self, md): return "continue", 0.0

def make_test_ctx(tmp_path, arm="openspec", step=1, **limits):
    pins = config.load_pins()
    if limits:
        pins = dataclasses.replace(pins, limits=dataclasses.replace(pins.limits, **limits))
    brief = tmp_path / "po-brief.md"; shutil.copy(REPO_ROOT / "scenario" / "po-brief.md", brief)
    out = tmp_path / "out"; out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return drive.StepCtx("dry9", config.load_arm(arm), step, Paths("dry9", arm), pins, out, "## General\n",
                         "# Step 1", brief, credentials.dummy(), StubOwner(), "sha0", [], lambda s: None)
```
