```python
# tests/runner/test_drive.py
import contextlib, json
import pytest
from runner import drive, faults, handoff, po
from runner.po import Decision, Answer, PoResult
from runner.session import TurnResult
from runner.transcripts import PinReport
from tests.runner.conftest import make_test_ctx

OKPIN = PinReport(True, 1, 1, 0, 0, ["2.1.281"], False, [])

def TR(text="", outcome="ok", sid="s1", cred_files=()):
    return TurnResult(sid, outcome, text, {}, {} if outcome == "ok" else None, 0.0, {}, {}, 1.0, [], None,
                      None, [], list(cred_files))

def D(kind, reply=None, answers=(), approved=None, command=None, arguments=""):
    return Decision(kind, reply, list(answers), approved, command, arguments, False)

@pytest.fixture
def rig(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    sent, script, state = [], {"arm": [], "po": []}, {"dirty": False, "head_in_main": True, "main": "sha1"}
    monkeypatch.setattr(drive.egress, "Stage", lambda *a, **k: contextlib.nullcontext(type("S", (), {"net": "n"})()))
    def fake_turn(t, cred, out, timeout):
        sent.append((t.message, t.env.get("BK_RESUME", ""))); return script["arm"].pop(0)
    monkeypatch.setattr(drive.session, "run_turn", fake_turn)
    monkeypatch.setattr(drive.po, "decide", lambda ctx, msg, cred, n, brief: PoResult(script["po"].pop(0), [TR()], OKPIN))
    monkeypatch.setattr(drive.commands, "installed", lambda p, arm: {"opsx:apply", "opsx:archive", "bmad-create-architecture"})
    monkeypatch.setattr(drive.gitops, "state", lambda p: type("R", (), {**state, "branch": "main", "head": "sha1"})())
    monkeypatch.setattr(drive.gitops, "diffstat", lambda p, a, b: "")
    monkeypatch.setattr(drive.gitops, "worktree_diffstat", lambda p: "")
    monkeypatch.setattr(drive, "_invoked", lambda cmd, turns: False)
    return sent, script, state, make_test_ctx(tmp_path, arm="openspec")

def msgs(sent): return [m for m, _ in sent]

def test_follows_the_methods_next_command_then_runs_must_run_then_ends(rig):
    sent, script, _, ctx = rig
    script["arm"] = [TR("proposal ready, run /opsx:apply"), TR("done"), TR("archived")]
    script["po"] = [D("next_command", command="/opsx:apply"), D("step_done"), D("step_done")]
    r = drive.run_attempt(ctx, 1, "/opsx:propose brief")
    assert msgs(sent) == ["/opsx:propose brief", "/opsx:apply", "/opsx:archive"] and r.outcome == "done"

def test_a_named_skill_is_sent_in_its_invocation_form(rig):                      # review F-2
    sent, script, _, ctx = rig
    ctx.arm = drive.config.load_arm("bmad")
    script["arm"] = [TR("Next, run the bmad-create-architecture skill."), TR("done")]
    script["po"] = [D("next_command", command="bmad-create-architecture"), D("step_done")]
    drive.run_attempt(ctx, 1, "go")
    assert msgs(sent)[1] == "/bmad-create-architecture"

def test_uninstalled_next_command_becomes_please_continue(rig):
    sent, script, _, ctx = rig
    ctx.arm = drive.config.load_arm("control")
    script["arm"] = [TR("run /opsx:frobnicate"), TR("ok")]
    script["po"] = [D("next_command", command="/opsx:frobnicate"), D("step_done")]
    r = drive.run_attempt(ctx, 1, "go")
    assert msgs(sent)[1] == "Please continue." and r.interventions == ["next_command-not-installed:/opsx:frobnicate"]

def test_mixed_questions_only_escalate_the_uncovered_one(rig):                   # review F-12
    sent, script, _, ctx = rig
    ctx.arm = drive.config.load_arm("control")
    script["arm"] = [TR("Two questions: a? b?"), TR("done")]
    script["po"] = [D("question", answers=[Answer("a?", "Yes, per General.", False, "General"),
                                            Answer("b?", None, True, None)]), D("step_done")]
    drive.run_attempt(ctx, 1, "go")
    assert ctx.owner.escalations == ["b?"]
    assert "Yes, per General." in msgs(sent)[1] and ctx.owner.escalation_answer in msgs(sent)[1]
    assert "b?" in ctx.brief_path.read_text()
    q = json.loads((ctx.out / "questions.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()[0])
    assert q["escalated"] == ["b?"] and q["answered_by"] == "owner+po"

def test_transient_api_error_is_retried_in_place_then_succeeds(rig):            # review C-4
    sent, script, _, ctx = rig
    ctx.arm = drive.config.load_arm("control")
    script["arm"] = [TR("API Error: 529 overloaded", outcome="is_error"), TR("done")]
    script["po"] = [D("step_done")]
    r = drive.run_attempt(ctx, 1, "go")
    assert r.outcome == "done" and r.retries == 1
    assert sent[1] == ("Please continue.", "s1")                               # same session, resumed

def test_transient_errors_become_a_harness_fault_only_when_retries_run_out(rig):
    sent, script, _, ctx = rig
    script["arm"] = [TR("API Error: 529 overloaded", outcome="is_error")] * 4
    with pytest.raises(faults.HarnessFault) as e:
        drive.run_attempt(ctx, 1, "go")
    assert e.value.kind == "infra-is_error" and "overloaded" in e.value.signature and len(sent) == 4

def test_a_non_transient_api_error_faults_at_once(rig):
    sent, script, _, ctx = rig
    script["arm"] = [TR("API Error: 400 invalid_request_error", outcome="is_error")]
    with pytest.raises(faults.HarnessFault):
        drive.run_attempt(ctx, 1, "go")
    assert len(sent) == 1

def test_a_persisted_credential_is_a_harness_fault(rig):                         # review S-3
    sent, script, _, ctx = rig
    script["arm"] = [TR("ok", cred_files=[".credentials.json"])]
    with pytest.raises(faults.HarnessFault) as e:
        drive.run_attempt(ctx, 1, "go")
    assert e.value.kind == "credential-persisted"

def test_po_session_failure_is_infra_not_protocol(rig, monkeypatch):            # review C-5
    sent, script, _, ctx = rig
    script["arm"] = [TR("hi")]
    def boom(*a, **k): raise po.SessionError("is_error", "API Error: 529")
    monkeypatch.setattr(drive.po, "decide", boom)
    with pytest.raises(faults.HarnessFault) as e:
        drive.run_attempt(ctx, 1, "go")
    assert e.value.kind == "infra-po-is_error"

def test_exchange_cap_ends_the_attempt_as_stalled(rig):
    sent, script, _, ctx = rig
    ctx.pins = drive.dataclasses.replace(ctx.pins, limits=drive.dataclasses.replace(ctx.pins.limits, exchanges_per_attempt=2))
    script["arm"] = [TR("?")] * 3
    script["po"] = [D("continue", "Please continue.")] * 3
    r = drive.run_attempt(ctx, 1, "go")
    assert r.outcome == "stalled" and r.breach == "exchanges"

def test_commit_nudge_is_sent_once_when_work_is_not_on_main(rig):               # OD4 A
    sent, script, state, ctx = rig
    ctx.arm = drive.config.load_arm("control")
    state["head_in_main"] = False
    script["arm"] = [TR("done"), TR("still done")]
    script["po"] = [D("step_done"), D("step_done")]
    r = drive.run_attempt(ctx, 1, "go")
    assert msgs(sent)[1] == handoff.MESSAGES["commit_on_main"] and r.outcome == "done"

def test_no_nudge_when_od4_b_is_chosen(rig, monkeypatch):                        # OD4 B
    sent, script, state, ctx = rig
    ctx.arm = drive.config.load_arm("control")
    monkeypatch.setitem(handoff.MESSAGES, "commit_on_main", "")
    state["dirty"] = True
    script["arm"] = [TR("done")]; script["po"] = [D("step_done")]
    assert drive.run_attempt(ctx, 1, "go").outcome == "done" and len(sent) == 1

def test_cr_b_preempts_the_end_of_the_second_turn(rig):                         # OD6 B
    sent, script, _, ctx = rig
    ctx.arm = drive.config.load_arm("control"); ctx.later_messages = ["CR-B text"]
    script["arm"] = [TR("Plan ready. Approve?"), TR("Question: x?"), TR("both done")]
    script["po"] = [D("approval", "Approved.", approved=True), D("step_done")]
    drive.run_attempt(ctx, 1, "CR-A text")
    assert msgs(sent) == ["CR-A text", "Approved.", "CR-B text"]
    kinds = [json.loads(l)["kind"] for l in (ctx.out / "questions.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()]
    assert kinds == ["approval", "preempted", "step_done"]

def test_cr_b_still_arrives_if_the_arm_reports_done_in_its_first_turn(rig):
    sent, script, _, ctx = rig
    ctx.arm = drive.config.load_arm("control"); ctx.later_messages = ["CR-B text"]
    script["arm"] = [TR("all done"), TR("CR-B done")]
    script["po"] = [D("step_done"), D("step_done")]
    drive.run_attempt(ctx, 1, "CR-A text")
    assert msgs(sent) == ["CR-A text", "CR-B text"]

def test_fresh_session_before_a_cited_command(rig):                              # OD19 B
    sent, script, _, ctx = rig
    ctx.arm = drive.dataclasses.replace(drive.config.load_arm("openspec"),
                                        fresh_session_before=[{"command": "/opsx:apply", "source": "cited"}])
    script["arm"] = [TR("run /opsx:apply"), TR("done"), TR("archived")]
    script["po"] = [D("next_command", command="/opsx:apply"), D("step_done"), D("step_done")]
    r = drive.run_attempt(ctx, 1, "/opsx:propose x")
    assert sent[1] == ("/opsx:apply", "") and "fresh-session:/opsx:apply" in r.interventions

def test_speckit_export_is_sent_once_after_tasks_ran(rig, monkeypatch):         # OD24 B
    sent, script, _, ctx = rig
    ctx.arm = drive.config.load_arm("speckit")
    script["arm"] = [TR("run /speckit-tasks"), TR("tasks done"), TR("exported"), TR("converged")]
    script["po"] = [D("next_command", command="/speckit-tasks"), D("step_done"), D("step_done"), D("step_done")]
    monkeypatch.setattr(drive.commands, "installed", lambda p, arm: {"speckit-tasks", "speckit-taskstoissues", "speckit-converge"})
    drive.run_attempt(ctx, 1, "/speckit-specify x")
    assert msgs(sent) == ["/speckit-specify x", "/speckit-tasks", "/speckit-taskstoissues", "/speckit-converge"]
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```python
# runner/faults.py (second part; Task 22 adds the ledger)
def classify_pin(report, who: str = "arm") -> "HarnessFault | None":
    if report is None:
        return None
    if report.no_evidence:
        return HarnessFault("model-pin", "no-evidence", f"{who}: no transcript evidence")
    for v in report.violations:
        if v["where"] == "claude_code_version":
            return HarnessFault("pin-claude-version", str(v["model"]), f"{who}: {v['file']}:{v['line']}")
    for v in report.violations:
        return HarnessFault("model-pin", str(v["model"]), f"{who}: {v['where']} {v['file']}:{v['line']}")
    return None
```

```python
# runner/drive.py
from __future__ import annotations
import dataclasses, gzip, json, re, time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Callable, Literal
from runner import commands, config, credentials, egress, faults, gitops, handoff, po, session
from runner.config import Arm, Pins
from runner.docker import Mount
from runner.faults import HarnessFault
from runner.paths import Paths

MESSAGES = handoff.MESSAGES
EXPORT_COMMAND = "/speckit-taskstoissues"            # OD24: the only export command; used when arm.export_after is set
TRANSIENT_RE = re.compile(r"overloaded|\b5\d\d\b|rate.?limit|timed? ?out|ECONNRESET|socket hang up", re.I)

@dataclass
class StepCtx:
    pass_: str
    arm: Arm
    step: int
    paths: Paths
    pins: Pins
    out: Path
    contract_in_force: str
    step_brief: str
    brief_path: Path
    cred: object
    owner: object
    sha_before: str
    later_messages: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    sleep: Callable[[float], None] = time.sleep
    shas: dict = field(default_factory=dict)          # po_brief_at_handoff, contract, step_brief (evidence)
    snapshot: int | str | None = None                 # the snapshot a harness re-run restores (None = the step)
    on_restore: Callable[[], str | None] | None = None # after a harness restore: returns a new first message (step 6)

@dataclass
class AttemptResult:
    outcome: Literal["done", "stalled", "timeout"]
    session_id: str | None
    turns: list = field(default_factory=list)
    po_results: list = field(default_factory=list)
    exchanges: int = 0
    interventions: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    breach: str | None = None
    retries: int = 0

def _now() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")

def _sig(text: str) -> str:
    first = text.splitlines()[0] if text else ""
    return re.sub(r"\d", "", first)[:80]

def _transient(r) -> str | None:
    if r.outcome == "no_result":
        return "no_result"
    if r.outcome == "is_error" and TRANSIENT_RE.search(r.final_text or ""):
        return _sig(r.final_text)
    return None

def _invoked(cmd: str, turns: list) -> bool:
    name = cmd.split()[0].lstrip("/")
    for t in turns:
        if not t.stream_path:
            continue
        with gzip.open(t.stream_path, "rt", errors="replace") as fh:
            for line in fh:
                if '"tool_use"' not in line or name.split(":")[-1] not in line:
                    continue
                try:
                    ev = json.loads(line)
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    continue
                for c in (ev.get("message") or {}).get("content", []):
                    if c.get("type") == "tool_use" and c.get("name") == "Skill" and \
                            str((c.get("input") or {}).get("skill", "")).split(":")[-1] == name.split(":")[-1]:
                        return True
    return False

def _log(qa: list, path: Path, entry: dict) -> None:
    qa.append(entry)
    with open(path, "a") as fh:
        fh.write(json.dumps(entry) + "\n")

def _turn(ctx: StepCtx, st, attempt: int, message: str, resume: str | None) -> session.TurnResult:
    p, pins, arm = ctx.paths, ctx.pins, ctx.arm
    n = len(list((ctx.out / "turns").glob(f"arm-a{attempt}-*.prompt.md"))) + 1
    image = pins.images["dryrun"] if arm.dry else arm.image
    env = credentials.session_env(pins, arm, role="arm", isolated=False, resume=resume or "",
                                  mcp=(p.home / ".bk-mcp.json").exists())
    t = session.Turn(ctx.pass_, arm.name, ctx.step, attempt, n, image, message, env,
                     [Mount(p.home, "/home/actor", False), Mount(p.work, "/work", False)], st.net, True,
                     p.container_repo, p.inbox("arm"), "arm", arm.dry, p.config_dir)
    return session.run_turn(t, ctx.cred, ctx.out, pins.limits.turn_seconds)

def _closers(ctx: StepCtx, sent: list[str], turns: list) -> list[str]:
    arm, out = ctx.arm, []
    ran = lambda c: c in sent or _invoked(c, turns)
    if arm.export_after and ran(arm.export_after) and not ran(EXPORT_COMMAND):
        out.append(EXPORT_COMMAND)
    out += [c for c in arm.must_run if not ran(c)]
    return out

def _not_on_main(ctx: StepCtx) -> bool:
    s = gitops.state(ctx.paths)
    return s.dirty or not s.head_in_main or s.main == ctx.sha_before

def run_attempt(ctx: StepCtx, attempt: int, message: str, resume: str | None = None) -> AttemptResult:
    pins, arm, p = ctx.pins, ctx.arm, ctx.paths
    res = AttemptResult("stalled", resume)
    qa_path = ctx.out / "questions.jsonl"; qa_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    qa = [json.loads(l) for l in qa_path.read_text().splitlines()] if qa_path.exists() else []
    sent: list[str] = []
    later = list(ctx.later_messages)
    nudged, turns_ended = False, 0
    cr_b_after = int(handoff.STEP5.get("cr_b_after_turn", 2))
    po_image = pins.images["dryrun"] if arm.dry else pins.images["base"]
    with egress.Stage(ctx.pass_, arm.name, "run", pins.egress_run_common + arm.egress_run, ctx.out / "egress.log") as st:
        while True:
            lead = commands.leading(message)
            if lead and any(lead == f["command"] for f in arm.fresh_session_before):
                res.session_id = None
                res.interventions.append(f"fresh-session:{lead}")
            r = _turn(ctx, st, attempt, message, res.session_id)
            backoff = list(pins.limits.transient_backoff)
            while (why := _transient(r)) and backoff:                   # review C-4: retry in place
                res.turns.append(r); res.retries += 1
                res.interventions.append(f"transient-retry:{why}")
                ctx.sleep(backoff.pop(0))
                sid = r.session_id or res.session_id
                r = _turn(ctx, st, attempt, MESSAGES["resume_after_error"] if sid else message, sid)
            res.turns.append(r)
            if r.outcome == "timeout":
                res.outcome, res.breach = "timeout", "turn_seconds"
                return res
            if r.outcome == "auth_failed":
                raise HarnessFault("infra-auth", "authentication_failed", f"turn {len(res.turns)}")
            if r.outcome in ("is_error", "no_result"):
                raise HarnessFault(f"infra-{r.outcome}", _sig(r.final_text) or r.outcome, (r.final_text or "")[:500])
            if r.credential_files:
                raise HarnessFault("credential-persisted", r.credential_files[0], ", ".join(r.credential_files))
            res.session_id = r.session_id or res.session_id
            res.exchanges += 1; turns_ended += 1
            if res.exchanges > pins.limits.exchanges_per_attempt:
                res.outcome, res.breach = "stalled", "exchanges"
                return res
            base = {"attempt": attempt, "n": len(res.turns), "at": _now(), "arm_message": r.final_text,
                    "escalated": [], "significant": False, "cited": None, "approved": None,
                    "owner_action": None, "owner_seconds": 0.0, "po_cost_usd": 0.0, "po_retries": 0}
            if later and turns_ended >= cr_b_after:                     # OD6 B
                message = handoff.entry_message(arm, ctx.step, later.pop(0))
                _log(qa, qa_path, {**base, "kind": "preempted", "reply": message, "answered_by": "runner"})
                res.interventions.append("step5-cr-b")
                continue
            try:
                pr = po.decide(po.PoContext(ctx.pass_, arm.name, ctx.step, attempt, p, po_image, arm.dry,
                                            ctx.contract_in_force, ctx.step_brief, qa, ctx.out),
                               r.final_text, ctx.cred, len(res.turns), ctx.brief_path)
            except po.ProtocolError as e:
                raise HarnessFault("po-protocol", "unparseable", str(e)) from e
            except po.SessionError as e:
                raise HarnessFault(f"infra-po-{e.outcome}", _sig(e.detail) or e.outcome, e.detail[:500]) from e
            res.po_results.append(pr)
            if (f := faults.classify_pin(pr.pin, "po")):
                raise f
            dec = pr.decision
            entry = {**base, "kind": dec.kind, "significant": dec.significant, "approved": dec.approved,
                     "po_cost_usd": sum(t.cost_usd for t in pr.turns), "po_retries": pr.retries, "answered_by": "po"}
            if dec.kind == "question":
                parts, by = [], set()
                for a in dec.answers:
                    if a.escalate:
                        ans, secs = ctx.owner.escalation(a.question, r.final_text)
                        po.append_escalation(ctx.brief_path, a.question, ans, ctx.pass_, arm.name, ctx.step)
                        po.commit_brief(ctx.brief_path, ctx.pass_, arm.name, ctx.step)
                        entry["owner_seconds"] += secs; entry["escalated"].append(a.question); by.add("owner")
                        parts.append((a.question, ans))
                    else:
                        by.add("po"); parts.append((a.question, a.reply))
                reply = parts[0][1] if len(parts) == 1 else "\n\n".join(f"On “{q}”: {ans}" for q, ans in parts)
                entry |= {"answered_by": "+".join(sorted(by)), "cited": "; ".join(a.cited or "" for a in dec.answers)}
            elif dec.kind == "approval":
                changes = gitops.diffstat(p, ctx.sha_before, "HEAD") + "\n" + gitops.worktree_diffstat(p)
                reply, action, secs = ctx.owner.gate(r.final_text, dec, changes)
                entry |= {"answered_by": "po" if action == "accept" else "owner", "owner_action": action,
                          "owner_seconds": secs}
                with open(ctx.out / "owner-gates.jsonl", "a") as fh:
                    fh.write(json.dumps({**entry, "po_verdict": dec.reply, "final_reply": reply}) + "\n")
            elif dec.kind == "next_command":
                inv = commands.invocation(dec.command or "", dec.arguments, commands.installed(p, arm))
                if inv:
                    reply = inv; sent.append(inv.split()[0])
                else:
                    reply = MESSAGES["continue"]
                    res.interventions.append(f"next_command-not-installed:{dec.command}")
            elif dec.kind == "continue":
                reply = MESSAGES["continue"]
            else:                                                       # step_done
                closers = _closers(ctx, sent, res.turns)
                if closers:
                    reply = closers[0]; sent.append(closers[0]); entry["answered_by"] = "runner"
                elif MESSAGES["commit_on_main"] and not nudged and _not_on_main(ctx):
                    reply = MESSAGES["commit_on_main"]; nudged = True; entry["answered_by"] = "runner"
                    res.interventions.append("commit_on_main")
                elif later:
                    reply = handoff.entry_message(arm, ctx.step, later.pop(0)); entry["answered_by"] = "runner"
                    res.interventions.append("step5-cr-b")
                else:
                    _log(qa, qa_path, {**entry, "reply": None})
                    res.outcome = "done"
                    return res
            _log(qa, qa_path, {**entry, "reply": reply})
            message = reply
```

### Task 16: The unannounced step-2 hand edit (OD1)

**What it is.** Before the step-2 brief is handed over, a "teammate" commits a small feature straight to `main`,
outside the method's workflow: **`TROUPE_THEATRE`, when set and non-empty, becomes the default for `--theatre`;
an explicit `--theatre` still wins.** It is contract-compatible (hidden tests never set `TROUPE_THEATRE`), so it
cannot change a hidden-test result; it touches the CLI surface every method's specs describe and leaves the
method's artefacts silently out of date — the drift design §4 wants detected. It is written by a scripted
plain-Claude session (same pinned model, `--setting-sources ""`, a fresh throwaway home, its own inbox — so the
arm can never read the prompt, review C-12), because arms' code differs. The runner verifies it and commits it
**in the arm's container** (so e.g. beads' hooks run where `bd` exists, review S-1) as `Sam Teammate`.

**Files:**
- Create: `runner/config/teammate-prompt.md`, `runner/handedit.py`, `tests/runner/test_handedit.py`,
  `tests/runner/test_handedit_docker.py`

**Interfaces:**
- `handedit.apply(ctx: drive.StepCtx, step1_tests: dict[str, str]) -> HandEdit(sha, files, numstat, probe,
  tries, side_effects, turns, pin)`; raises `HarnessFault("hand-edit", "session"|"scope"|"probe"|
  "changed-hidden-results")` after two failed tries (each try restores `02-pre-edit` first).
- `handedit.scope_plan(porcelain) -> ScopePlan(py, remove, restore, violations)` (review C-11);
  `handedit.clean_side_effects(p) -> ScopePlan`; `handedit.PROBE_SH`; `handedit.mentions(files, needle) -> int`
  (assistant text and tool inputs only, review C-10); `handedit.facts_from(...)`;
  `handedit.detect(ctx, edit, end_sha) -> dict` → `02/hand-edit/detection.json`.
- Consumes: `hidden.prepare`, `hidden.run_script`, `hidden.run` (Task 18); `gitops.*` (Task 8).
- **Order at step 2 (in `orchestrate`, Task 23):** `apply` = `snapshot("02-pre-edit")` → teammate turn → transcripts
  pin-checked → scope plan (side effects cleaned, recorded) → commit in the container → probe → step-1 hidden
  recheck (same outcomes as step 1's record) → `snapshot(2)`. The edit is part of step 2's starting state, so a
  harness re-run of step 2 keeps it.

```markdown
Do not create or edit any Markdown, YAML, TOML, JSON or other documentation, specification or configuration
```

```python
# tests/runner/test_handedit.py
import gzip, json
from runner import handedit

def test_python_changes_are_in_scope_and_side_effects_are_cleaned_not_failed():   # review C-11
    plan = handedit.scope_plan(" M src/troupe/cli.py\n?? tests/test_theatre_env.py\n?? src/troupe/__pycache__/cli.cpython-312.pyc\n"
                               "?? .pytest_cache/v/cache/nodeids\n M uv.lock\n")
    assert plan.py == ["src/troupe/cli.py", "tests/test_theatre_env.py"]
    assert plan.remove == [".pytest_cache/v/cache/nodeids", "src/troupe/__pycache__/cli.cpython-312.pyc"]
    assert plan.restore == ["uv.lock"] and plan.violations == []

def test_a_tracked_non_python_edit_or_a_deletion_is_a_violation():
    assert handedit.scope_plan(" M src/troupe/cli.py\n M docs/spec.md\n").violations == ["docs/spec.md (modified)"]
    assert handedit.scope_plan(" D src/troupe/cli.py\n").violations == ["src/troupe/cli.py (deleted)"]

def test_no_python_change_at_all_is_a_violation():
    assert handedit.scope_plan("").violations == ["no Python file changed"]

def test_probe_checks_env_default_and_explicit_flag_precedence():
    s = handedit.PROBE_SH
    assert "TROUPE_THEATRE=/tmp/t uv run troupe config show a" in s
    assert "TROUPE_THEATRE=/tmp/t uv run troupe config show --theatre /tmp/u a" in s

def test_mentions_count_assistant_text_and_tool_inputs_but_not_tool_results(tmp_path):   # review C-10
    f = tmp_path / "s.jsonl.gz"
    with gzip.open(f, "wt") as fh:
        fh.write(json.dumps({"type": "assistant", "message": {"content": [
            {"type": "text", "text": "I see TROUPE_THEATRE is supported."},
            {"type": "tool_use", "name": "Grep", "input": {"pattern": "TROUPE_THEATRE"}}]}}) + "\n")
        fh.write(json.dumps({"type": "user", "message": {"content": [
            {"type": "tool_result", "content": "cli.py: os.environ.get('TROUPE_THEATRE')"}]}}) + "\n")
    assert handedit.mentions([f], "TROUPE_THEATRE") == 2

def test_detection_facts_are_recorded_not_scored():
    facts = handedit.facts_from(probe_ok=True, non_py_mentions=["docs/spec.md"], session_mentions=2, po_mentions=0,
                                teammate_test_kept=True)
    assert facts == {"feature_kept": True, "documented_in": ["docs/spec.md"], "mentioned_in_session": True,
                     "session_mentions": 2, "raised_with_po": False, "teammate_test_kept": True}
```

```python
# tests/runner/test_handedit_docker.py — side-effect cleaning really happens in the container
import pytest
from runner import gitops, handedit, repo
from runner.paths import Paths

pytestmark = pytest.mark.docker

def test_side_effects_are_removed_and_the_lockfile_restored(tmp_state):
    p = Paths("dry9", "dryrun"); repo.init(p, "dryrun", "dry9")
    (p.repo / "uv.lock").write_text("v1\n"); (p.repo / "cli.py").write_text("x = 1\n")
    gitops.commit_all(p, gitops.IDENTITY, "base")
    (p.repo / "cli.py").write_text("x = 2\n"); (p.repo / "uv.lock").write_text("v2\n")
    (p.repo / "__pycache__").mkdir(); (p.repo / "__pycache__" / "cli.cpython-312.pyc").write_bytes(b"\0")
    plan = handedit.clean_side_effects(p)
    assert plan.violations == [] and plan.restore == ["uv.lock"]
    assert (p.repo / "uv.lock").read_text() == "v1\n" and not (p.repo / "__pycache__" / "cli.cpython-312.pyc").exists()
    assert gitops.porcelain(p).strip() == "M cli.py"
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `runner/handedit.py`**

```python
# runner/handedit.py
from __future__ import annotations
import gzip, json, re
from dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict
from pathlib import Path
from runner import config, credentials, egress, faults, gitops, hidden, repo, session, transcripts
from runner.docker import Mount
from runner.faults import HarnessFault
from runner.paths import Paths

MESSAGE = "Let TROUPE_THEATRE set the default theatre"
PROMPT = (config.CONFIG_DIR / "teammate-prompt.md").read_text().split("-->", 1)[-1].strip() + "\n"
NEEDLE = "TROUPE_THEATRE"

PROBE_SH = r"""set -u
mkdir -p /tmp/t/.troupe /tmp/u/.troupe
printf 'a: 1\n' > /tmp/t/.troupe/troupe.yml; printf 'a: 2\n' > /tmp/u/.troupe/troupe.yml
a=$(TROUPE_THEATRE=/tmp/t uv run troupe config show a 2>/dev/null); ra=$?
b=$(TROUPE_THEATRE=/tmp/t uv run troupe config show --theatre /tmp/u a 2>/dev/null); rb=$?
c=$(env -u TROUPE_THEATRE uv run troupe config show --theatre /tmp/t a 2>/dev/null); rc=$?
jq -cn --arg a "$a" --arg b "$b" --arg c "$c" --argjson ra $ra --argjson rb $rb --argjson rc $rc \
  '{env_default:($ra==0 and $a=="1"), flag_wins:($rb==0 and $b=="2"), unchanged_without_env:($rc==0 and $c=="1")}'
"""

@dataclass
class ScopePlan:
    py: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    remove: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    restore: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    violations: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

def scope_plan(porcelain: str) -> ScopePlan:
    plan = ScopePlan()
    for line in sorted(l for l in porcelain.splitlines() if l.strip()):
        code, path = line[:2], line[3:]
        if "D" in code:
            plan.violations.append(f"{path} (deleted)")
        elif path.endswith(".py"):
            plan.py.append(path)
        elif code == "??":
            plan.remove.append(path)                    # __pycache__, .pytest_cache, … : side effects, cleaned
        elif path == "uv.lock" or path.endswith("/uv.lock"):
            plan.restore.append(path)                   # `uv run` may rewrite the lock: a side effect, restored
        else:
            plan.violations.append(f"{path} (modified)")
    if not plan.py and not plan.violations:
        plan.violations.append("no Python file changed")
    plan.py.sort(); plan.remove.sort()
    return plan

def clean_side_effects(p: Paths) -> ScopePlan:
    plan = scope_plan(gitops.porcelain(p))
    if not plan.violations:
        gitops.remove_untracked(p, plan.remove)
        gitops.checkout_paths(p, plan.restore)
    return plan

def mentions(files: list[Path], needle: str) -> int:
    n = 0
    for f in files:
        with (gzip.open(f, "rt", errors="replace") if f.suffix == ".gz" else open(f, errors="replace")) as fh:
            for line in fh:
                if needle not in line:
                    continue
                try:
                    ev = json.loads(line)
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    continue
                if ev.get("type") != "assistant":
                    continue
                for c in (ev.get("message") or {}).get("content", []):
                    if c.get("type") == "text":
                        n += c.get("text", "").count(needle)
                    elif c.get("type") == "tool_use":
                        n += json.dumps(c.get("input")).count(needle)
    return n

def facts_from(*, probe_ok, non_py_mentions, session_mentions, po_mentions, teammate_test_kept) -> dict:
    return {"feature_kept": probe_ok, "documented_in": sorted(non_py_mentions),
            "mentioned_in_session": session_mentions > 0, "session_mentions": session_mentions,
            "raised_with_po": po_mentions > 0, "teammate_test_kept": teammate_test_kept}

@dataclass
class HandEdit:
    sha: str
    files: list[str]
    numstat: list
    probe: dict
    tries: int
    side_effects: dict
    turns: list = field(default_factory=list)
    pin: dict = field(default_factory=dict)

def _is_test(path: str) -> bool:
    name = path.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
    return name.startswith("test_") or name.endswith("_test.py") or "/tests/" in f"/{path}"

def _try(ctx, k: int, step1_tests: dict[str, str], out: Path) -> HandEdit:
    p, pins, arm = ctx.paths, ctx.pins, ctx.arm
    home = p.teammate_home(k); (home / ".claude").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    before = transcripts.snapshot(home / ".claude")
    env = credentials.session_env(pins, arm, role="teammate", isolated=True) | {"PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1"}
    image = pins.images["dryrun"] if arm.dry else pins.images["base"]
    with egress.Stage(ctx.pass_, arm.name, "teammate", pins.egress_run_common, out / "egress.log") as st:
        t = session.Turn(ctx.pass_, arm.name, 2, k, 1, image, PROMPT, env,
                         [Mount(home, "/home/actor", False), Mount(p.work, "/work", False)], st.net, True,
                         p.container_repo, p.inbox("teammate"), "teammate", arm.dry, home / ".claude")
        r = session.run_turn(t, ctx.cred, out, pins.limits.turn_seconds)
    files = transcripts.collect(home / ".claude", before, out / "transcripts" / f"t{k}")
    pin = transcripts.verify_pin(files, [r.stream_path] if r.stream_path else [], pins)
    if (f := faults.classify_pin(pin, "teammate")):
        raise f
    if r.outcome != "ok":
        raise HarnessFault("hand-edit", "session", r.outcome)
    plan = clean_side_effects(p)
    if plan.violations:
        raise HarnessFault("hand-edit", "scope", "; ".join(plan.violations))
    sha = gitops.commit_all(p, gitops.TEAMMATE, MESSAGE)
    prep = hidden.prepare(p, sha, "02-hand-edit", out)
    probe = json.loads(hidden.run_script(p, prep.hidden_copy, PROBE_SH).strip().splitlines()[-1])
    (out / "probe.json").write_text(json.dumps(probe, indent=2))
    if not all(probe.values()):
        raise HarnessFault("hand-edit", "probe", json.dumps(probe))
    recheck = hidden.run(p, prep.hidden_copy, 1, out / "recheck")
    (out / "recheck.json").write_text(json.dumps({"step1": step1_tests, "after_edit": recheck.tests}, indent=2))
    if recheck.tests != step1_tests:
        raise HarnessFault("hand-edit", "changed-hidden-results",
                           json.dumps({t: (step1_tests.get(t), o) for t, o in recheck.tests.items() if step1_tests.get(t) != o})[:500])
    ns = gitops.numstat(p, f"{sha}~1", sha)
    (out / "diff.patch").write_text(gitops.show(p, sha))
    edit = HandEdit(sha, plan.py, ns, probe, k, {"removed": plan.remove, "restored": plan.restore}, [r], asdict(pin))
    (out / "edit.json").write_text(json.dumps({"sha": sha, "files": plan.py, "tries": k,          # review F-11
                                               "lines_added": sum(a for a, _, _ in ns), "lines_removed": sum(d for _, d, _ in ns),
                                               "numstat": ns, "side_effects": edit.side_effects}, indent=2))
    return edit

def apply(ctx, step1_tests: dict[str, str]) -> HandEdit:
    p = ctx.paths
    out = ctx.out / "hand-edit"; out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    repo.snapshot(p, "02-pre-edit")
    last: HarnessFault | None = None
    for k in (1, 2):
        try:
            edit = _try(ctx, k, step1_tests, out)
            repo.snapshot(p, 2)
            return edit
        except HarnessFault as f:
            last = f
            (out / f"try-{k}-failed.json").write_text(json.dumps({"kind": f.kind, "signature": f.signature, "detail": f.detail}))
            repo.restore(p, "02-pre-edit")
    assert last is not None
    raise last

def detect(ctx, edit: HandEdit, end_sha: str) -> dict:
    p, out = ctx.paths, ctx.out / "hand-edit"
    prep = hidden.prepare(p, end_sha, "02-detect", out)
    probe_end = json.loads(hidden.run_script(p, prep.hidden_copy, PROBE_SH).strip().splitlines()[-1])
    arm_files = sorted((ctx.out / "turns").glob("arm-*.stream.jsonl.gz")) + \
                sorted(f for f in (ctx.out / "transcripts").rglob("*.jsonl.gz"))
    qa = [json.loads(l) for l in (ctx.out / "questions.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()] \
        if (ctx.out / "questions.jsonl").exists() else []
    kept_files = set(gitops.ls_files(p, end_sha))
    teammate_tests = [f for f in edit.files if _is_test(f)]
    facts = facts_from(probe_ok=all(probe_end.values()),
                       non_py_mentions=gitops.grep_files(p, end_sha, NEEDLE, ":!*.py"),
                       session_mentions=mentions(arm_files, NEEDLE),
                       po_mentions=sum(q.get("arm_message", "").count(NEEDLE) for q in qa),
                       teammate_test_kept=bool(teammate_tests) and all(f in kept_files for f in teammate_tests))
    (out / "detection.json").write_text(json.dumps(facts, indent=2))
    return facts
```
Evidence in `02/hand-edit/`: `turns/` (teammate prompt, stream, stderr), `transcripts/t<k>/` (pin-checked), `edit.json`,
`diff.patch`, `probe.json`, `recheck.json`, `detection.json`, and any `try-<k>-failed.json`. The teammate's cost
is harness cost, never arm cost (Task 20).

```bash

git commit -m "Runner: step-2 hand edit — isolated teammate, side effects cleaned, container commit, probe, honest detection facts"
```

### Task 17: Step 6's bug in each arm's tracker; tracker snapshots; round-trip facts

**Files:**
- Create: `runner/tracker.py`, `tests/runner/test_tracker.py`, `tests/runner/test_tracker_docker.py`

**Interfaces:**
- `tracker.IssueRef(backend, ref, url, id)`; `tracker.create_bug(ctx, owner) -> IssueRef`;
  `tracker.supersede(ctx, old: IssueRef, owner)` (a harness re-run of step 6 closes the old issue with "Superseded
  by a harness re-run", owner-confirmed, and the evidence names both); `tracker.snapshot(ctx) -> dict` (written as
  `tracker.json` after **every** step: design §2 records tracker quality separately);
  `tracker.round_trip_facts(ctx, issue, start_sha, end_sha, snap) -> dict` (step 6).
- Pure helpers: `gh_create_argv(slug, body_file)`, `gh_list_argv(slug)`, `gh_close_argv(slug, number)`,
  `parse_gh_url(text)`, `parse_bd(json_text)`, `commit_records(log)`, `commits_referencing(log, issue_id)`,
  `issue_links_commit(issue_json, shas)`.
- **Backends** (by `arm.tracker`, design §2):
  - `github` (openspec, superpowers, ccsdd, control as the baseline; speckit, whose export targets GitHub Issues;
    bmad, whose ticketing uses the `gh` store): created **from the host with the owner's own `gh` login** after
    `owner.confirm`. (This is `gh` against the GitHub API, never git on an arm repo, so review S-1 does not apply.)
    The arm reads it with its Issues-only PAT.
  - `beads` (speckitty): created **in the arm container** (arm image, network none, home and work mounted):
    `bd create --title <title> --type bug --description "$(cat /setup/issue.md)" --json`. Flags confirmed by P9.
  - `dry`: a file `state/<pass>/<arm>/tracker/issue-1.md`; ref `dry#1`.
- The step-6 message is `handoff.bug_message(ref, contract)` in the arm's `bug_entry`; the report itself lives only
  in the tracker (the round trip design §4 probes).
- Round-trip facts (facts only; Plan 3 scores): issue state and comment count; commits in `start..end`
  referencing the issue (`#N` or the beads id); whether the issue links any of those commits; the non-`.py` files
  changed in step 6 ("does the fix leave a spec trace", design §4).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write failing tests**

```python
# tests/runner/test_tracker.py
from runner import tracker

def test_github_issue_argv_is_a_bug_with_the_fixed_title_and_a_body_file(tmp_path):
    a = tracker.gh_create_argv("troupe-run/bakeoff-specs-openspec-pass1", tmp_path / "body.md")
    assert a[:4] == ["gh", "issue", "create", "--repo"]
    assert a[a.index("--title") + 1] == "Setting one agent's model changed another agent's model"
    assert a[a.index("--type") + 1] == "Bug" and "--body-file" in a

def test_issue_url_becomes_a_ref():
    ref = tracker.parse_gh_url("https://github.com/troupe-run/bakeoff-specs-openspec-pass1/issues/1\n")
    assert ref.ref == "troupe-run/bakeoff-specs-openspec-pass1#1" and ref.id == "1"

def test_beads_create_json_becomes_a_ref():
    assert tracker.parse_bd('{"id": "bd-a3f8", "title": "x"}').ref == "beads bd-a3f8"

def test_commit_references_are_found_by_number_or_beads_id():
    log = "abc\x1fFix alias write-through (#1)\n\x1e\ndef\x1ftidy\nadd #12 later\n\x1e\nfed\x1fClose bd-a3f8\n\x1e"
    assert tracker.commits_referencing(log, "1") == ["abc"]            # "#12" is not "#1"
    assert tracker.commits_referencing(log, "bd-a3f8") == ["fed"]

def test_issue_links_a_commit_when_body_or_comments_name_its_sha():
    issue = {"body": "x", "comments": [{"body": "Fixed in abc1234def"}]}
    assert tracker.issue_links_commit(issue, ["abc1234def5678"]) is True
    assert tracker.issue_links_commit(issue, ["0000000"]) is False

def test_round_trip_facts_are_raw_facts(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setattr(tracker.gitops, "commits_between", lambda p, a, b: "abc1234\x1fFix alias (#1)\n\x1e")
    monkeypatch.setattr(tracker.gitops, "numstat", lambda p, a, b: [(3, 1, "src/cli.py"), (5, 0, "specs/aliases.md")])
    ctx = type("C", (), {"paths": None, "arm": type("A", (), {"tracker": "github"})()})()
    issue = tracker.IssueRef("github", "o/r#1", "https://x", "1")
    snap = {"issues": [{"number": 1, "state": "CLOSED", "comments": [{"body": "done in abc1234"}], "body": ""}]}
    f = tracker.round_trip_facts(ctx, issue, "s0", "s1", snap)
    assert f == {"issue": "o/r#1", "state": "CLOSED", "comments": 1, "commits_referencing": ["abc1234"],
                 "issue_links_commit": True, "non_py_files_changed": ["specs/aliases.md"]}
```

```python
import pytest

def test_dry_backend_writes_the_issue_file(tmp_state, tmp_path):
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement `runner/tracker.py`**

```python
# runner/tracker.py
from __future__ import annotations
import json, re, subprocess
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
from pathlib import Path
from runner import config, docker, gitops, handoff, repo
from runner.docker import Mount, RunSpec
from runner.paths import REPO_ROOT

BRIEF = REPO_ROOT / "scenario" / "steps" / "06-bug-alias.md"

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class IssueRef:
    backend: str
    ref: str
    url: str | None
    id: str

def gh_create_argv(slug: str, body_file: Path) -> list[str]:
    return ["gh", "issue", "create", "--repo", slug, "--title", handoff.STEP6["title"],
            "--body-file", str(body_file), "--type", "Bug"]

def gh_list_argv(slug: str) -> list[str]:
    return ["gh", "issue", "list", "--repo", slug, "--state", "all", "--limit", "200", "--json",
            "number,title,state,labels,createdAt,closedAt,comments,body,url"]

def gh_close_argv(slug: str, number: str) -> list[str]:
    return ["gh", "issue", "close", number, "--repo", slug, "--comment", "Superseded by a harness re-run"]

def parse_gh_url(text: str) -> IssueRef:
    url = text.strip().splitlines()[-1]
    m = re.search(r"github\.com/([^/]+/[^/]+)/issues/(\d+)", url)
    return IssueRef("github", f"{m.group(1)}#{m.group(2)}", url, m.group(2))

def parse_bd(text: str) -> IssueRef:
    o = json.loads(text)
    o = o[0] if isinstance(o, list) else o
    return IssueRef("beads", f"beads {o['id']}", None, o["id"])

def commit_records(log: str) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    """(sha, message) pairs from gitops.commits_between's \x1e/\x1f-separated records."""
    out = []
    for rec in log.split("\x1e"):
        rec = rec.strip("\n")
        if "\x1f" in rec:
            sha, _, msg = rec.partition("\x1f")
            out.append((sha.strip(), msg))
    return out

def commits_referencing(log: str, issue_id: str) -> list[str]:
    pat = re.compile(rf"(?<![\w-])#{re.escape(issue_id)}\b") if issue_id.isdigit() else re.compile(re.escape(issue_id))
    return [sha for sha, msg in commit_records(log) if pat.search(msg)]

def issue_links_commit(issue: dict, shas: list[str]) -> bool:
    text = (issue.get("body") or "") + "\n" + "\n".join(c.get("body", "") for c in issue.get("comments") or [])
    return any(s[:7] in text for s in shas)

def _bd(ctx, *cmd: str, extra: list[Mount] | None = None) -> str:
    p, arm = ctx.paths, ctx.arm
    s = RunSpec(name=f"bk-bd-{p.pass_}-{p.arm}", image=gitops.image_for(arm.name), label=f"bakeoff.run={p.pass_}/{p.arm}",
                network="none", mounts=[Mount(p.home, "/home/actor", False), Mount(p.work, "/work", False), *(extra or [])],
                env={"HOME": "/home/actor"}, workdir=p.container_repo, cmd=list(cmd), stdin=False,
                limits=config.load_pins().limits, proxy=False)
    r = docker.run(s, timeout=120)
    if r.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f"bd failed: {r.stderr.decode(errors='replace')[:300]}")
    return r.stdout.decode()

def create_bug(ctx, owner) -> IssueRef:
    body = ctx.out / "issue-body.md"; body.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    body.write_text(handoff.issue_body(BRIEF.read_text()))
    backend = ctx.arm.tracker
    if backend == "dry":
        d = ctx.paths.base / "tracker"; d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (d / "issue-1.md").write_text(f"# {handoff.STEP6['title']}\n\n{body.read_text()}")
        return IssueRef("dry", "dry#1", None, "1")
    if backend == "beads":
        out = _bd(ctx, "bash", "-c", 'bd create --title "$1" --type bug --description "$(cat /setup/issue.md)" --json',
                  "bd", handoff.STEP6["title"], extra=[Mount(body, "/setup/issue.md", True)])
        return parse_bd(out)
    slug = repo.github_slug(ctx.pass_, ctx.arm.name)
    if not owner.confirm(f"Create the step-6 issue in {slug}?"):
        raise RuntimeError("owner declined the step-6 issue; step 6 cannot start")
    r = subprocess.run(gh_create_argv(slug, body), capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
    return parse_gh_url(r.stdout)

def supersede(ctx, old: IssueRef, owner) -> None:
    if old.backend == "github" and owner.confirm(f"Close {old.ref} as superseded by a harness re-run?"):
        subprocess.run(gh_close_argv(old.ref.split("#")[0], old.id), check=True)
    elif old.backend == "beads":
        _bd(ctx, "bd", "close", old.id, "--reason", "Superseded by a harness re-run")

def snapshot(ctx) -> dict:
    backend = ctx.arm.tracker
    if backend == "dry":
        d = ctx.paths.base / "tracker"
        issues = [{"ref": f"dry#{f.stem.split('-')[1]}", "text": f.read_text()} for f in sorted(d.glob("issue-*.md"))] if d.exists() else []
    elif backend == "beads":
        issues = json.loads(_bd(ctx, "bd", "list", "--json", "--all") or "[]")
    else:
        r = subprocess.run(gh_list_argv(repo.github_slug(ctx.pass_, ctx.arm.name)), capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
        issues = json.loads(r.stdout)
    snap = {"backend": backend, "issues": issues}
    (ctx.out / "tracker.json").write_text(json.dumps(snap, indent=2))
    return snap

def round_trip_facts(ctx, issue: IssueRef, start_sha: str, end_sha: str, snap: dict) -> dict:
    log = gitops.commits_between(ctx.paths, start_sha, end_sha)
    refs = commits_referencing(log, issue.id)
    shas = [sha for sha, _ in commit_records(log)]
    match = next((i for i in snap["issues"] if str(i.get("number", i.get("id", ""))) == issue.id
                  or i.get("ref") == issue.ref), {})
    return {"issue": issue.ref, "state": match.get("state", match.get("status")),
            "comments": len(match.get("comments") or []), "commits_referencing": refs,
            "issue_links_commit": issue_links_commit(match, shas),
            "non_py_files_changed": sorted(f for _, _, f in gitops.numstat(ctx.paths, start_sha, end_sha) if not f.endswith(".py"))}
```

```bash
git commit -m "Runner: step-6 bug in each arm's tracker (GitHub from the host, beads in the container, dry), snapshots, round-trip facts"
```

### Task 18: Hidden tests against an arm, inside a tester container

**Files:**
- Create: `runner/hidden.py`, `runner/preflight.py` (`idle()`; Task 23 adds the rest),
  `tests/runner/test_hidden_parse.py`, `tests/runner/test_hidden_docker.py`

**Interfaces:**
- `hidden.prepare(p, sha, label, out_dir, with_quality=False) -> Prepared(hidden_copy, quality_copy, prep)`:
  exports the committed tree (`repo.export`, i.e. `git archive` in a container) into
  `state/<pass>/<arm>/scratch/<label>/a/bakeoff-specs-<arm>/` (copy **A**, hidden tests only) and, with
  `with_quality`, a second export into `…/b/…` (copy **B**, quality tools; mutmut is installed **only here**, review
  C-1). A prep container (tester image, **PyPI-only** egress) runs `uv sync --frozen` (or `uv sync` when there is no
  `uv.lock`, recorded as `lockfile: false`) in each copy; a failed sync is retried once; a second failure is
  `install_failed`: the arm's result, every test failing, not a fault (the owner may declare a registry outage a
  harness fault with `faults declare`). Writes `hidden/prep-<label>.json`.
- `hidden.prepare_copy(p, dir, out_dir, label) -> Prepared` (the same, for an already-exported directory: the reference).
- `hidden.run(p, copy, step, out_dir) -> Summary` — collection of the tests first valid at this step, the full run,
  individual re-runs of failures, and **flake classification against the reference** (review C-2).
- `hidden.run_script(p, copy, script) -> str` (network none; used by the hand-edit probe).
- `hidden.Summary(step, total, passed, failed, flaky, flaky_arm, tests, new_at_step, new_passed,
  new_discriminating_passed, exit_code, install_failed)` → `hidden/summary.json`; `hidden/flake.json`.
- `preflight.idle() -> (ok, detail)`: no other `bakeoff.run` container, host 1-minute load < `load_factor × cpu`,
  **and the Docker VM's** load (`/proc/loadavg` read in a throwaway container) < `load_factor × nproc` (review §3 NOTE 8).
- **The run container:** tester image; `--network none`; copy's parent at `/work` (so the repo is at
  `/work/bakeoff-specs-<arm>`, the path the arm used); the harness's `tests/` and `pyproject.toml` read-only at
  `/harness/…` (only in this container); a fresh empty `/home/actor`; `/out` for results; env `UV_OFFLINE=1`,
  `UV_NO_SYNC=1`, `PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1`, `UV_CACHE_DIR=/tmp/uv-cache`, and nothing from the host (no `USER`,
  no `LOGNAME`, so `getpass` falls through to the passwd entry on both sides — review §3 NOTE 1).
- **Flakes (review C-2):** each failed test is re-run twice alone in a fresh container → `failed` (all three failed)
  or flaky. A flaky test is then run 3× alone **against the reference** (`/opt/reference` in the tester image, with
  `TROUPE_REF_STEP=N`): flaky on the reference too → **harness** flake (`flaky`, reported to Task 22 as
  `flaky-test:<nodeid>`); stable on the reference → **arm** flake (`flaky_arm`, counted as failed for the arm, recorded).
- **`new_discriminating_passed`** counts tests first valid at this step that pass, **excluding**
  `harness/negative_allowlist.txt` (those pin deliberately unchanged behaviour and pass on a do-nothing arm).

**Every PLAN-2 NOTE, resolved by construction:**

| Plan 1 note (ledger) | Resolution here |
|---|---|
| `getpass.getuser()` in the test vs the CLI's login name — same user | pytest and the CLI are one process tree in one container, uid 1000; no `USER`/`LOGNAME` in env (docker test asserts it), so both read the passwd entry |
| `served` fixture reachable from the arm (`BAKEOFF_HTTP_HOST`) | one network namespace; `127.0.0.1` works; loopback exists under `--network none` |
| `remote.py` bind vs advertise | not needed: both `127.0.0.1` in one namespace |
| theatre tmp dirs at the same path inside and outside | there is no outside |
| env vars don't cross `docker exec` | no `docker exec`; `driver.Cli` passes env to a local subprocess |
| `docker exec` doesn't proxy signals; `watch` leaks | `Watching` signals the local `uv run` leader (Plan 1 selftest: one SIGTERM); `--init` reaps; container removed |
| any `--cmd-prefix` wrapper must forward SIGTERM | no `--cmd-prefix` is used |
| run pytest inside the arm container, tests read-only only there | a tester container on the same base image runs the arm's committed code; `tests/` only there (OD23) |
| run on an otherwise idle machine | `preflight.idle()` (host **and** Docker VM) plus the runner lock; sequential by construction |

```python
# tests/runner/test_hidden_parse.py
from runner import hidden

JUNIT = '''<testsuites><testsuite>
<testcase classname="tests.hidden.test_step2_sync" name="test_a"/>
<testcase classname="tests.hidden.test_step2_sync" name="test_b"><failure message="x"/></testcase>
<testcase classname="tests.hidden.test_step1_history" name="test_history_does_not_reconcile"/>
</testsuite></testsuites>'''

def test_junit_outcomes_are_parsed():
    assert hidden.parse_junit(JUNIT) == {"tests.hidden.test_step2_sync::test_a": "passed",
                                         "tests.hidden.test_step2_sync::test_b": "failed",
                                         "tests.hidden.test_step1_history::test_history_does_not_reconcile": "passed"}

def test_collect_only_node_ids_become_junit_ids():
    out = "tests/hidden/test_step2_sync.py::test_a\ntests/hidden/test_step2_sync.py::test_b[x]\n\n2 tests collected\n"
    assert hidden.parse_collected(out) == ["tests.hidden.test_step2_sync::test_a", "tests.hidden.test_step2_sync::test_b[x]"]

def test_allowlisted_new_tests_do_not_count_as_progress():
    tests = {"m::test_a": "failed", "m::test_history_does_not_reconcile": "passed"}
    s = hidden.summarise(2, tests, ["m::test_a", "m::test_history_does_not_reconcile"], flaky=[], flaky_arm=[], exit_code=1)
    assert s.new_passed == 1 and s.new_discriminating_passed == 0

def test_arm_flakes_count_as_failed_and_harness_flakes_as_flaky():         # review C-2
    tests = {"m::a": "failed", "m::b": "failed", "m::c": "passed"}
    s = hidden.summarise(2, tests, ["m::a", "m::b", "m::c"], flaky=["m::a"], flaky_arm=["m::b"], exit_code=1)
    assert (s.passed, s.failed, s.flaky, s.flaky_arm) == (1, 1, 1, 1)
    assert s.tests["m::b"] == "failed" and s.tests["m::a"] == "flaky"

def test_flake_verdict_needs_three_failures_to_be_a_plain_failure():
    assert hidden.flake_verdict(["failed", "failed", "failed"]) == "failed"
    assert hidden.flake_verdict(["failed", "passed", "failed"]) == "flaky"

def test_node_id_for_a_rerun_is_the_file_path_form():
    assert hidden.to_nodeid("tests.hidden.test_step2_sync::test_b[x]") == "tests/hidden/test_step2_sync.py::test_b[x]"
```

```python
EXPECTED = {1: 101, 2: 109, 3: 121, 4: 135, 5: 146, 6: 151, 7: 161}   # results/instrument-validation.md

@pytest.fixture(scope="module")

    return p, hidden.prepare_copy(p, dst, tmp_path_factory.mktemp("prep"), "ref").hidden_copy

@pytest.mark.parametrize("step", range(1, 8))

def test_the_tester_env_has_no_login_name_variables(ref):                    # review §3 NOTE 1 lock-in

    env = hidden.run_script(p, copy, "env")

```
(`prepare_copy`'s reference copy is gated by `TROUPE_REF_STEP`, which the hidden conftest defaults from `--step`, so
the same copy behaves as each step; an arm ignores that variable. `test_show_nested_path_and_list_index` is a real, unparametrised step-1 test.)

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `runner/hidden.py` and `preflight.idle()`**

```python
# runner/hidden.py
from __future__ import annotations
import json, os, re, shutil, time, xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict, field
from pathlib import Path
from runner import config, docker, egress, repo
from runner.docker import Mount, RunSpec
from runner.paths import Paths, REPO_ROOT

ALLOW = {l.split("#")[0].strip() for l in (REPO_ROOT / "harness/negative_allowlist.txt").read_text().splitlines()
         if l.split("#")[0].strip()}
ENV = {"UV_OFFLINE": "1", "UV_NO_SYNC": "1", "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1", "UV_CACHE_DIR": "/tmp/uv-cache",
       "HOME": "/home/actor", "UV_PYTHON_DOWNLOADS": "never"}
PYTEST = ["/opt/harness/bin/python", "-m", "pytest"]

def parse_junit(xml: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    out = {}
    for tc in ET.fromstring(xml).iter("testcase"):
        o = "failed" if tc.find("failure") is not None or tc.find("error") is not None else \
            "skipped" if tc.find("skipped") is not None else "passed"
        out[f"{tc.get('classname')}::{tc.get('name')}"] = o
    return out

def parse_collected(text: str) -> list[str]:
    ids = []
    for line in text.splitlines():
        if "::" in line and line.startswith("tests/"):
            path, _, name = line.strip().partition("::")
            ids.append(path.removesuffix(".py").replace("/", ".") + "::" + name)
    return ids

def to_nodeid(junit_id: str) -> str:
    mod, _, name = junit_id.partition("::")
    return mod.replace(".", "/") + ".py::" + name

def flake_verdict(outcomes: list[str]) -> str:
    return "failed" if all(o == "failed" for o in outcomes) else "flaky"

@dataclass
class Summary:
    step: int
    total: int
    passed: int
    failed: int
    flaky: int
    flaky_arm: int
    tests: dict[str, str]
    new_at_step: list[str]
    new_passed: int
    new_discriminating_passed: int
    exit_code: int
    install_failed: bool = False
    def write(self, out_dir: Path) -> None:
        (out_dir / "hidden").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (out_dir / "hidden" / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(asdict(self), indent=2, sort_keys=True))

def summarise(step, tests, new_at_step, flaky, flaky_arm, exit_code, install_failed=False) -> Summary:
    final = {t: ("flaky" if t in flaky else "failed" if t in flaky_arm else o) for t, o in tests.items()}
    new = [t for t in new_at_step if t in final]
    return Summary(step, len(final), sum(o == "passed" for o in final.values()),
                   sum(o == "failed" for o in final.values()), sum(o == "flaky" for o in final.values()),
                   len([t for t in flaky_arm if t in final]), final, new, sum(final[t] == "passed" for t in new),
                   sum(final[t] == "passed" and t.split("::")[-1].split("[")[0] not in ALLOW for t in new),
                   exit_code, install_failed)

@dataclass
class Prepared:
    hidden_copy: Path
    quality_copy: Path | None
    prep: dict = field(default_factory=dict)

def _spec(p: Paths, copy: Path, cmd: list[str], *, network: str = "none", proxy: bool = False, out: Path | None = None,
          env: dict | None = None, workdir: str | None = None, name: str = "tester") -> RunSpec:
    home = p.scratch / "tester-home"; shutil.rmtree(home, ignore_errors=True); home.mkdir(parents=True)
    mounts = [Mount(copy.parent, "/work", False), Mount(home, "/home/actor", False),
              Mount(REPO_ROOT / "tests", "/harness/tests", True), Mount(REPO_ROOT / "pyproject.toml", "/harness/pyproject.toml", True)]
    if out is not None:
        out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True); mounts.append(Mount(out, "/out", False))
    return RunSpec(name=f"bk-{name}-{p.pass_}-{p.arm}-{int(time.time()*1000) % 10**8}",
                   image=config.load_pins().images["tester"], label=f"bakeoff.run={p.pass_}/{p.arm}",
                   network=network, mounts=mounts, env={**ENV, **(env or {})},
                   workdir=workdir or f"/work/{copy.name}", cmd=cmd, stdin=False,
                   limits=config.load_pins().limits, proxy=proxy)

def _sync(p: Paths, copy: Path, st, with_mutmut: bool) -> dict:
    lock = (copy / "uv.lock").exists()
    cmd = "uv sync --frozen" if lock else "uv sync"
    if with_mutmut:
        cmd += " && uv pip install --python .venv/bin/python --require-hashes -r /opt/mutmut-requirements.txt"
    rec = {"lockfile": lock, "tries": []}
    for _ in (1, 2):
        r = docker.run(_spec(p, copy, ["bash", "-lc", cmd], network=st.net, proxy=True,
                             env={"UV_OFFLINE": "0", "UV_NO_SYNC": "0"}, name="prep"), timeout=1800)
        rec["tries"].append({"exit": r.returncode, "log": (r.stdout + r.stderr).decode(errors="replace")[-4000:]})
        if r.returncode == 0:
            rec["ok"] = True
            return rec
    rec["ok"] = False
    return rec

def prepare_copy(p: Paths, copy: Path, out_dir: Path, label: str, quality_copy: Path | None = None) -> Prepared:
    pins = config.load_pins()
    with egress.Stage(p.pass_, p.arm, "tester", pins.egress_tester_prep, out_dir / "egress-tester.log") as st:
        rec = {"hidden": _sync(p, copy, st, False)}
        if quality_copy is not None:
            rec["quality"] = _sync(p, quality_copy, st, True)
    (out_dir / "hidden").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (out_dir / "hidden" / f"prep-{label}.json").write_text(json.dumps(rec, indent=2))
    return Prepared(copy, quality_copy, rec)

def prepare(p: Paths, sha: str, label: str, out_dir: Path, with_quality: bool = False) -> Prepared:
    base = p.scratch / label; shutil.rmtree(base, ignore_errors=True)
    a = base / "a" / f"bakeoff-specs-{p.arm}"; repo.export(p, sha, a)
    b = None
    if with_quality:
        b = base / "b" / f"bakeoff-specs-{p.arm}"; repo.export(p, sha, b)
    return prepare_copy(p, a, out_dir, label, b)

def _pytest(p: Paths, copy: Path, args: list[str], out: Path, impl: str | None = None,
            env: dict | None = None, timeout: int = 1800):
    cmd = [*PYTEST, *args, "--impl", impl or f"/work/{copy.name}", "-q", "-p", "no:cacheprovider", "--rootdir", "/harness"]
    return docker.run(_spec(p, copy, cmd, out=out, env=env, workdir="/harness"), timeout=timeout)

def run_script(p: Paths, copy: Path, script: str) -> str:
    return docker.run(_spec(p, copy, ["bash", "-c", script]), timeout=600).stdout.decode(errors="replace")

def classify_flaky(p: Paths, step: int, nodeid: str, copy: Path | None = None) -> str:
    """Run the test 3x alone against the reference at this step: any failure there = harness flake."""
    scratch = p.scratch / "ref-flake"; scratch.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    dummy = copy or (scratch / "bakeoff-specs-ref"); dummy.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    for _ in range(3):
        r = _pytest(p, dummy, [f"tests/{nodeid.removeprefix('tests/')}", "--step", str(step)], scratch / "out",
                    impl="/opt/reference", env={"TROUPE_REF_STEP": str(step)}, timeout=600)
        if r.returncode != 0:
            return "harness"
    return "arm"

def run(p: Paths, copy: Path, step: int, out_dir: Path, install_failed: bool = False) -> Summary:
    hd = out_dir / "hidden"; hd.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    col = _pytest(p, copy, ["tests/hidden", "--step", str(step), "--first-step-only", str(step), "--collect-only"], hd)
    new_at_step = parse_collected(col.stdout.decode(errors="replace"))
    r = _pytest(p, copy, ["tests/hidden", "--step", str(step), "--junitxml=/out/junit.xml"], hd)
    (hd / "pytest.log").write_bytes(r.stdout + b"\n--- stderr ---\n" + r.stderr)
    tests = parse_junit((hd / "junit.xml").read_text()) if (hd / "junit.xml").exists() else {}
    flaky, flaky_arm, reruns = [], [], {}
    for t, o in tests.items():
        if o != "failed" or install_failed:
            continue
        outcomes = ["failed"]
        for _ in range(2):
            rr = _pytest(p, copy, [to_nodeid(t), "--step", str(step)], hd / "rerun", timeout=600)
            outcomes.append("passed" if rr.returncode == 0 else "failed")
        reruns[t] = outcomes
        if flake_verdict(outcomes) == "flaky":
            (flaky if classify_flaky(p, step, to_nodeid(t)) == "harness" else flaky_arm).append(t)
    (hd / "flake.json").write_text(json.dumps({"reruns": reruns, "harness_flaky": flaky, "arm_flaky": flaky_arm}, indent=2))
    s = summarise(step, tests, new_at_step, flaky, flaky_arm, r.returncode, install_failed)
    s.write(out_dir)
    return s
```

```python
# runner/preflight.py (first part)
from __future__ import annotations
import os, subprocess
from runner import config, docker

def _vm_load() -> tuple[float, int]:
    img = config.load_pins().images["base"]
    r = subprocess.run(["docker", "run", "--rm", "--network", "none", img, "sh", "-c", "cat /proc/loadavg; nproc"],
                       capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
    load, cpus = r.stdout.split("\n")[0].split()[0], r.stdout.split("\n")[1]
    return float(load), int(cpus)

def idle() -> tuple[bool, str]:
    others = docker.running("bakeoff.run")
    f = config.load_pins().limits.load_factor
    host, hcpu = os.getloadavg()[0], os.cpu_count() or 1
    vm, vcpu = _vm_load()
    ok = not others and host < f * hcpu and vm < f * vcpu
    return ok, (f"containers={len(others)} host_load1={host:.2f}/{f*hcpu:.1f} "
                f"vm_load1={vm:.2f} vm_cpus={vcpu} vm_limit={f*vcpu:.1f}")
```
(`install_failed` is passed by `stepflow` when `prep["hidden"]["ok"]` is false: the run still happens — every test
fails on an uninstallable project — and no failure is re-run.)

### Task 19: Code quality — `mypy --strict`, lint, mutation score (OD15)

**Files:**
- Create: `runner/quality.py`, `tests/runner/test_quality.py`, `tests/runner/test_quality_docker.py`

**Interfaces:**
- `quality.run_all(p, prepared: hidden.Prepared, out_dir) -> dict` → `quality/{mypy.txt, ruff.json, ruff-arm.json
  (only if the arm configures ruff), mutation.json, mutmut.log, summary.json}`; `summary.json` = `mypy_errors,
  ruff_findings, ruff_arm_findings, mutation {killed, survived, timeout, suspicious, no_tests, skipped, total,
  score, partial, baseline_failed, sampled, sample_size, sampling}, targets`. All in the tester container,
  `--network none`: mypy and ruff on copy **A**, mutmut on copy **B** (review C-1).
- `quality.targets(copy) -> list[str]` (`src` if present; else the top-level package of the `troupe` entry point;
  else `.` — recorded).
- **mypy:** `/opt/harness/bin/mypy --strict --no-incremental --python-executable .venv/bin/python <targets>`.
- **Lint:** `/opt/harness/bin/ruff check --isolated --exit-zero --output-format json .` for every arm; plus the arm's
  own config when it has one.
- **Mutation:** `[tool.mutmut] paths_to_mutate = <targets>` is written into copy B's `pyproject.toml` (replacing any
  arm setting, for comparability). The arm's own tests run first; if they fail, `baseline_failed` (the arm's result).
  **OD15 A** (`mutation_sample = 0`): `timeout <mutation_seconds> mutmut run --max-children 4`, then `mutmut results
  --all true`. **OD15 B** (`mutation_sample = N`): list every mutant without running any (`mutmut run
  "__bk_list_only__"` then `mutmut results --all true`), choose the N whose `sha256(name)` sort first (deterministic,
  arm-agnostic), run exactly those (`mutmut run <names…>`, still under the time cap), read their statuses. If the
  listing yields nothing (the mechanism is unavailable in the pinned mutmut), fall back to A and record
  `sampling: "unavailable"`. `score = killed / (total − no_tests − skipped)`.

```python
# tests/runner/test_quality.py
from runner import quality

def test_mypy_tail_is_parsed():
    assert quality.mypy_errors("x\nFound 12 errors in 3 files (checked 9 source files)\n") == 12
    assert quality.mypy_errors("Success: no issues found in 9 source files\n") == 0

def test_targets_prefer_src_then_entry_point_package(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "pyproject.toml").write_text('[project.scripts]\ntroupe = "troupe_cfg.cli:main"\n')
    (tmp_path / "troupe_cfg").mkdir()
    assert quality.targets(tmp_path) == ["troupe_cfg"]
    (tmp_path / "src").mkdir()
    assert quality.targets(tmp_path) == ["src"]

def test_mutation_score_excludes_untested_and_skipped():
    s = quality.mutation_summary({"killed": 60, "survived": 20, "timeout": 5, "suspicious": 0, "no_tests": 10,
                                  "skipped": 5, "total": 100}, partial=False, baseline_failed=False)
    assert s["score"] == 60 / 85

def test_ruff_findings_count_is_the_json_array_length():
    assert quality.ruff_count('[{"code": "F401"}, {"code": "E501"}]') == 2

def test_mutmut_results_are_parsed_into_counts():
    text = "    troupe.cli.x__mutmut_1: killed\n    troupe.cli.x__mutmut_2: survived\n    troupe.cli.y__mutmut_1: no tests\n"
    names, counts = quality.parse_results(text)
    assert names == ["troupe.cli.x__mutmut_1", "troupe.cli.x__mutmut_2", "troupe.cli.y__mutmut_1"]
    assert counts == {"killed": 1, "survived": 1, "timeout": 0, "suspicious": 0, "no_tests": 1, "skipped": 0,
                      "not_checked": 0, "total": 3}

def test_the_sample_is_deterministic_and_does_not_depend_on_order():         # OD15 B
    names = [f"m.f__mutmut_{i}" for i in range(500)]
    a = quality.sample(names, 200); b = quality.sample(list(reversed(names)), 200)
    assert a == b and len(a) == 200

def test_mutmut_table_replaces_any_arm_setting(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "pyproject.toml").write_text('[project]\nname = "x"\n\n[tool.mutmut]\npaths_to_mutate = ["other"]\nfoo = 1\n\n[tool.ruff]\nx = 1\n')
    quality.set_mutmut_paths(tmp_path, ["src"])
    t = (tmp_path / "pyproject.toml").read_text()
    assert 'paths_to_mutate = ["src"]' in t and "other" not in t and "foo" not in t and "[tool.ruff]" in t
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement `runner/quality.py`**

```python
# runner/quality.py
from __future__ import annotations
import hashlib, json, re, tomllib
from pathlib import Path
from runner import config, docker, hidden
from runner.paths import Paths

STATUS = {"killed": "killed", "survived": "survived", "timeout": "timeout", "suspicious": "suspicious",
          "no tests": "no_tests", "skipped": "skipped", "not checked": "not_checked", "segfault": "killed"}

def mypy_errors(text: str) -> int:
    m = re.search(r"Found (\d+) errors?", text)
    return int(m.group(1)) if m else 0

def ruff_count(text: str) -> int:
    return len(json.loads(text or "[]"))

def targets(copy: Path) -> list[str]:
    if (copy / "src").is_dir():
        return ["src"]
    try:
        scripts = tomllib.loads((copy / "pyproject.toml").read_text()).get("project", {}).get("scripts", {})
        mod = scripts.get("troupe", "").split(":")[0].split(".")[0]
        if mod and (copy / mod).is_dir():
            return [mod]
    except (OSError, tomllib.TOMLDecodeError):
        pass
    return ["."]

def mutation_summary(c: dict, *, partial: bool, baseline_failed: bool, **extra) -> dict:
    denom = c["total"] - c["no_tests"] - c["skipped"]
    return {**c, "score": (c["killed"] / denom) if denom > 0 else None, "partial": partial,
            "baseline_failed": baseline_failed, **extra}

def parse_results(text: str) -> tuple[list[str], dict]:
    names, counts = [], {v: 0 for v in ["killed", "survived", "timeout", "suspicious", "no_tests", "skipped", "not_checked"]}
    for m in re.finditer(r"(?m)^\s*(\S+__mutmut_\d+): ([a-z ]+?)\s*$", text):
        names.append(m.group(1)); counts[STATUS.get(m.group(2), "not_checked")] += 1
    counts["total"] = len(names)
    return names, counts

def sample(names: list[str], n: int) -> list[str]:
    return sorted(sorted(set(names), key=lambda x: hashlib.sha256(x.encode()).hexdigest())[:n])

def set_mutmut_paths(copy: Path, paths_: list[str]) -> None:
    f = copy / "pyproject.toml"
    text = f.read_text() if f.exists() else ""
    text = re.sub(r"(?ms)^\[tool\.mutmut\]\n.*?(?=^\[|\Z)", "", text).rstrip("\n")
    f.write_text(text + f"\n\n[tool.mutmut]\npaths_to_mutate = {json.dumps(paths_)}\n")

def _run(p: Paths, copy: Path, script: str, timeout: int) -> tuple[int, str]:
    r = docker.run(hidden._spec(p, copy, ["bash", "-c", script], name="quality"), timeout=timeout)
    return r.returncode, (r.stdout + r.stderr).decode(errors="replace")

def list_mutants(p: Paths, copy: Path, tg: list[str]) -> list[str]:
    set_mutmut_paths(copy, tg)
    _, out = _run(p, copy, ".venv/bin/mutmut run '__bk_list_only__' >/dev/null 2>&1; .venv/bin/mutmut results --all true", 1800)
    return parse_results(out)[0]

def _mutation(p: Paths, copy: Path, tg: list[str], out_dir: Path) -> dict:
    lim = config.load_pins().limits
    rc, base = _run(p, copy, ".venv/bin/python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider -x", 1800)
    log = ["$ baseline: pytest\n" + base[-4000:]]
    if rc == 5:                                            # the arm has no tests of its own
        return mutation_summary({k: 0 for k in ["killed", "survived", "timeout", "suspicious", "no_tests", "skipped", "total"]},
                                partial=False, baseline_failed=False, sampled=False, sample_size=0, sampling="no tests")
    if rc != 0:
        return mutation_summary({k: 0 for k in ["killed", "survived", "timeout", "suspicious", "no_tests", "skipped", "total"]},
                                partial=False, baseline_failed=True, sampled=False, sample_size=0, sampling="n/a")
    set_mutmut_paths(copy, tg)
    sampling, chosen = "all", []
    if lim.mutation_sample > 0:
        names = list_mutants(p, copy, tg)
        if names:
            chosen, sampling = sample(names, lim.mutation_sample), "seeded-sha256"
        else:
            sampling = "unavailable"
    run = ".venv/bin/mutmut run --max-children 4" + ("".join(f" '{n}'" for n in chosen) if chosen else "")
    rc, out = _run(p, copy, f"timeout {lim.mutation_seconds} {run}; echo __rc=$?; .venv/bin/mutmut results --all true",
                   lim.mutation_seconds + 900)
    log.append(f"$ {run}\n" + out[-20000:])
    (out_dir / "quality" / "mutmut.log").write_text("\n".join(log))
    names, counts = parse_results(out)
    if chosen:
        wanted = set(chosen)
        _, counts = parse_results("\n".join(l for l in out.splitlines() if l.strip().split(":")[0] in wanted))
    partial = "__rc=124" in out or counts.get("not_checked", 0) > 0
    return mutation_summary({k: counts[k] for k in ["killed", "survived", "timeout", "suspicious", "no_tests", "skipped", "total"]},
                            partial=partial, baseline_failed=False, sampled=bool(chosen),
                            sample_size=len(chosen), sampling=sampling)

def run_all(p: Paths, prep: "hidden.Prepared", out_dir: Path) -> dict:
    qd = out_dir / "quality"; qd.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    a, b = prep.hidden_copy, prep.quality_copy
    tg = targets(a)
    _, mypy_out = _run(p, a, "/opt/harness/bin/mypy --strict --no-incremental --python-executable .venv/bin/python "
                       + " ".join(tg), 1800)
    (qd / "mypy.txt").write_text(mypy_out)
    _, ruff_out = _run(p, a, "/opt/harness/bin/ruff check --isolated --exit-zero --output-format json . 2>/dev/null", 600)
    (qd / "ruff.json").write_text(ruff_out)
    has_cfg = any((a / f).exists() for f in ("ruff.toml", ".ruff.toml")) or \
        "[tool.ruff" in ((a / "pyproject.toml").read_text() if (a / "pyproject.toml").exists() else "")
    ruff_arm = None
    if has_cfg:
        _, ra = _run(p, a, "/opt/harness/bin/ruff check --exit-zero --output-format json . 2>/dev/null", 600)
        (qd / "ruff-arm.json").write_text(ra); ruff_arm = ruff_count(ra)
    mut = _mutation(p, b, tg, out_dir) if b is not None and prep.prep.get("quality", {}).get("ok") else \
        mutation_summary({k: 0 for k in ["killed", "survived", "timeout", "suspicious", "no_tests", "skipped", "total"]},
                         partial=False, baseline_failed=True, sampled=False, sample_size=0, sampling="install failed")
    (qd / "mutation.json").write_text(json.dumps(mut, indent=2))
    s = {"mypy_errors": mypy_errors(mypy_out), "ruff_findings": ruff_count(ruff_out), "ruff_arm_findings": ruff_arm,
         "mutation": mut, "targets": tg}
    (qd / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(s, indent=2))
    return s
```

### Task 20: Evidence per step (design §7) and the owner's step summary

**Files:**
- Create: `runner/evidence.py`, `tests/runner/test_evidence.py`, `tests/runner/test_evidence_docker.py`
- Modify: `tests/runner/conftest.py` (add `finalise_inputs`, review Q-9)

**Interfaces:**
- `evidence.finalise(ctx, *, attempts, extra_turns, hidden, quality, pin, sha_before, started, ended, fault_entries,
  shas, tests_seconds, outcome) -> Path` (writes everything below; returns `summary.md`). The parameter is
  `fault_entries`, not `faults` (review Q-10). `shas = {"po_brief_at_handoff", "contract", "step_brief"}`.
- `evidence.cost(turn_lists: dict[str, list[TurnResult]]) -> dict`; `evidence.research_attempts(turns, egress_log)
  -> dict` (review F-8); `evidence.possible_missed_approvals(qa) -> list[dict]` (review C-8).

| Design §7 asks for | File(s) | Source |
|---|---|---|
| full session transcripts | `transcripts/a<k>/**.jsonl.gz` (arm incl. subagents), `turns/*.{prompt.md,stream.jsonl.gz,stderr.gz}`, `po/a<k>-NNN/` (inputs, turns, transcripts, decision, pin), `hand-edit/` (step 2) | Tasks 6, 7, 13, 16 |
| cost with its unit and source | `cost.json`: unit USD, source "Claude Code 2.1.281 stream-json result.total_cost_usd — client-side estimate (OAuth subscription; not a bill)", `arm`, `po`, `harness` (teammate) apart, each with usd, turns, usage, modelUsage | OD18 |
| wall-clock time | `run.json`: `started_at`, `ended_at`, `wall_seconds`, `arm_session_seconds`, `po_seconds`, `owner_wait_seconds`, `tests_seconds` | runner clocks |
| every question, who answered, whether escalated | `questions.jsonl`, `owner-gates.jsonl` | Task 15 |
| hidden-test results | `hidden/{junit.xml, summary.json, pytest.log, flake.json, prep-*.json}` | Task 18 |
| mutation score, `mypy --strict`, lint | `quality/*` | Task 19 |
| the arm repo's git SHA at the end of the step | `git.json`: `sha_before`, `sha_after` (= `refs/heads/main`, review F-1), `head`, `branch`, `head_in_main`, `dirty_at_end`, `log`, `diffstat` | `gitops` |
| (design §11) model pin verified | `model-pin.json` (arm) + `po/a<k>-NNN/model-pin.json` | Tasks 7, 13 |
| (design §6) recorded pins and isolation | `run.json`: `claude_version`, `model`, `plugin_route`, image IDs (arm, tester, proxy), SHA-256 of `po-brief.md` at handoff and at end, of `contract.md` and of the step brief; `model_overrides` (review F-6) | pins, image lock, setup |
| (design §4 step 2 / step 6) | `hand-edit/*`, `tracker.json` | Tasks 16, 17 |
| egress | `egress.log` (+ `egress-po.log`, `egress-tester.log`); refusals in `summary.md` | Task 4 |
| (OD10) research attempts | `run.json.research_attempts` (WebSearch/WebFetch permission denials + refused hosts) | review F-8 |

`summary.md` (the owner's step review, OD7): outcome and attempts; hidden result (passed/total, new passed, new
discriminating passed, arm flakes); escalations with answers; significant PO answers; owner gates; **possible missed
approvals**; interventions; egress refusals; research attempts; cost; wall-clock; model-pin status; quality numbers;
model overrides (if any).
