```python
# tests/runner/test_evidence.py
import json
from runner import evidence
from runner.session import TurnResult
from tests.runner.conftest import finalise_inputs

def TR(usd, model="claude-opus-5-5", denials=()):
    return TurnResult("s", "ok", "", {}, {}, usd, {"input_tokens": 10, "output_tokens": 2},
                      {model: {"inputTokens": 10}}, 1.0, [], None, None, list(denials), [])

def test_cost_states_unit_and_source_and_keeps_arm_po_and_harness_apart():
    c = evidence.cost({"arm": [TR(1.25), TR(0.75)], "po": [TR(0.1)], "harness": [TR(0.2)]})
    assert c["unit"] == "USD" and "client-side estimate" in c["source"]
    assert c["arm"]["usd"] == 2.0 and c["arm"]["turns"] == 2 and c["po"]["usd"] == 0.1 and c["harness"]["usd"] == 0.2
    assert c["arm"]["usage"]["input_tokens"] == 20

def test_research_attempts_count_denied_web_tools_and_refused_hosts():         # review F-8
    log = 'CONNECT docs.python.org:443 HTTP/1.1\nProxying refused on filtered domain "docs.python.org"\n'
    r = evidence.research_attempts([TR(0, denials=[{"tool_name": "WebSearch"}, {"tool_name": "Bash"}])], log)
    assert r == {"denied_web_tools": 1, "refused_hosts": ["docs.python.org"]}

def test_possible_missed_approvals_are_listed():                                # review C-8
    qa = [{"kind": "continue", "arm_message": "Shall I proceed with this plan?", "n": 3},
          {"kind": "continue", "arm_message": "Working on tests.", "n": 4},
          {"kind": "approval", "arm_message": "Please approve.", "n": 5}]
    assert [q["n"] for q in evidence.possible_missed_approvals(qa)] == [3]

def test_finalise_writes_every_file_design_7_lists(tmp_path, monkeypatch):     # strengthened (review Q-11)
    ctx, kw = finalise_inputs(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    summary = evidence.finalise(ctx, **kw)
    d = summary.parent
    for rel in ["run.json", "cost.json", "git.json", "model-pin.json", "summary.md",   # written by finalise itself
                "questions.jsonl", "hidden/summary.json", "quality/summary.json"]:     # written earlier, checked present
        assert (d / rel).exists(), rel
    run = json.loads((d / "run.json").read_text())
    for k in ["started_at", "ended_at", "wall_seconds", "arm_session_seconds", "po_seconds", "owner_wait_seconds",
              "tests_seconds", "claude_version", "model", "plugin_route", "images", "po_brief_sha256_at_handoff",
              "po_brief_sha256_at_end", "contract_sha256", "step_brief_sha256", "research_attempts", "model_overrides",
              "outcome", "attempts", "interventions"]:
        assert k in run, k
    git = json.loads((d / "git.json").read_text())
    assert git["sha_after"] == "main-sha" and git["head_in_main"] is True
    assert "Possible missed approvals" in summary.read_text()
```

```python
# tests/runner/conftest.py (addition) — real inputs for finalise; only gitops (containers) is faked here
import json as _json
from runner import evidence, gitops, hidden
from runner.transcripts import PinReport

def finalise_inputs(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    ctx = make_test_ctx(tmp_path, arm="control", step=3)
    (ctx.out / "questions.jsonl").write_text(_json.dumps({"kind": "continue", "arm_message": "Shall I proceed?", "n": 1,
                                                          "owner_seconds": 0.0, "escalated": [], "significant": False}) + "\n")
    s = hidden.summarise(3, {"m::a": "passed"}, ["m::a"], [], [], 0); s.write(ctx.out)
    (ctx.out / "quality").mkdir(); (ctx.out / "quality" / "summary.json").write_text("{}")
    monkeypatch.setattr(gitops, "state", lambda p: gitops.RepoState("main", False, "main-sha", "main-sha", True))
    monkeypatch.setattr(gitops, "log", lambda p, a, b: [{"sha": "main-sha", "author": "Developer", "subject": "x"}])
    monkeypatch.setattr(gitops, "diffstat", lambda p, a, b: " a.py | 1 +")
    from runner.drive import AttemptResult
    kw = dict(attempts=[AttemptResult("done", "s1")], extra_turns={}, hidden=s, quality={"mutation": {}},
              pin=PinReport(True, 1, 1, 0, 0, ["2.1.281"], False, []), sha_before="sha0", started=1.7e9, ended=1.7e9 + 10,
              fault_entries=[], shas={"po_brief_at_handoff": "b", "contract": "c", "step_brief": "s"},
              tests_seconds=3.0, outcome="done")
    return ctx, kw
```

```python
# tests/runner/test_evidence_docker.py — finalise with real containerised git on a dry repo
import pytest
from runner import evidence, gitops, repo
from runner.transcripts import PinReport
from runner.drive import AttemptResult
from runner import hidden
from tests.runner.conftest import make_test_ctx

pytestmark = pytest.mark.docker

def test_git_json_names_main_and_the_step_log(tmp_state, tmp_path):
    ctx = make_test_ctx(tmp_path, arm="dryrun", step=1)
    before = repo.init(ctx.paths, "dryrun", "dry9")
    (ctx.paths.repo / "a.py").write_text("1"); gitops.commit_all(ctx.paths, gitops.IDENTITY, "step 1")
    s = hidden.summarise(1, {}, [], [], [], 0); s.write(ctx.out)
    evidence.finalise(ctx, attempts=[AttemptResult("done", "s")], extra_turns={}, hidden=s, quality={},
                      pin=PinReport(True, 1, 0, 0, 0, [], False, []), sha_before=before, started=1.7e9, ended=1.7e9 + 1,
                      fault_entries=[], shas={"po_brief_at_handoff": "", "contract": "", "step_brief": ""},
                      tests_seconds=0, outcome="done")
    import json
    g = json.loads((ctx.out / "git.json").read_text())
    assert g["sha_after"] == gitops.main_sha(ctx.paths) and [e["subject"] for e in g["log"]] == ["step 1"]
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement `runner/evidence.py`**

```python
# runner/evidence.py
from __future__ import annotations
import hashlib, json, re
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from runner import config, egress, gitops, images, handoff
from runner.paths import Paths

SOURCE = ("Claude Code 2.1.281 stream-json result.total_cost_usd — client-side estimate "
          "(OAuth subscription; not a bill)")
APPROVAL_WORDS = re.compile(r"\b(approve|approval|sign[- ]?off|proceed|go ahead|LGTM|confirm)\b", re.I)

def sha256_file(p: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() if p.exists() else ""

def _iso(t: float) -> str:
    return datetime.fromtimestamp(t, timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")

def cost(turn_lists: dict[str, list]) -> dict:
    out = {"unit": "USD", "source": SOURCE}
    for who, turns in turn_lists.items():
        usage: dict[str, int] = {}; mu: dict[str, dict] = {}
        for t in turns:
            for k, v in (t.usage or {}).items():
                if isinstance(v, (int, float)):
                    usage[k] = usage.get(k, 0) + v
            for m, v in (t.model_usage or {}).items():
                agg = mu.setdefault(m, {})
                for k, x in v.items():
                    if isinstance(x, (int, float)):
                        agg[k] = agg.get(k, 0) + x
        out[who] = {"usd": round(sum(t.cost_usd for t in turns), 6), "turns": len(turns), "usage": usage, "modelUsage": mu}
    return out

def research_attempts(turns: list, egress_log: str) -> dict:
    denied = sum(1 for t in turns for d in (t.permission_denials or []) if d.get("tool_name") in ("WebSearch", "WebFetch"))
    refused = sorted({h for h, v in egress.decisions(egress_log) if v == "refused"})
    return {"denied_web_tools": denied, "refused_hosts": refused}

def possible_missed_approvals(qa: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    return [q for q in qa if q.get("kind") == "continue" and APPROVAL_WORDS.search(q.get("arm_message") or "")]

def _qa(out: Path) -> list[dict]:
    f = out / "questions.jsonl"
    return [json.loads(l) for l in f.read_text().splitlines()] if f.exists() else []

def _gates(out: Path) -> list[dict]:
    f = out / "owner-gates.jsonl"
    return [json.loads(l) for l in f.read_text().splitlines()] if f.exists() else []

def finalise(ctx, *, attempts, extra_turns, hidden, quality, pin, sha_before, started, ended, fault_entries,
             shas, tests_seconds, outcome) -> Path:
    out, p, pins = ctx.out, ctx.paths, ctx.pins
    arm_turns = [t for a in attempts for t in a.turns]
    po_turns = [t for a in attempts for r in a.po_results for t in r.turns]
    harness_turns = [t for ts in extra_turns.values() for t in ts]
    qa, gates = _qa(out), _gates(out)
    egress_log = (out / "egress.log").read_text() if (out / "egress.log").exists() else ""
    st = gitops.state(p)
    git = {"sha_before": sha_before, "sha_after": st.main, "head": st.head, "branch": st.branch,
           "head_in_main": st.head_in_main, "dirty_at_end": st.dirty,
           "log": gitops.log(p, sha_before, st.main or st.head), "diffstat": gitops.diffstat(p, sha_before, st.main or st.head)}
    (out / "git.json").write_text(json.dumps(git, indent=2))
    c = cost({"arm": arm_turns, "po": po_turns, "harness": harness_turns})
    (out / "cost.json").write_text(json.dumps(c, indent=2))
    (out / "model-pin.json").write_text(pin.to_json())
    lock = images.lock() if images.LOCK.exists() else {"arms": {}, "images": {}}
    overrides_f = p.setup_results / "model-overrides.json"
    run = {"pass": ctx.pass_, "arm": ctx.arm.name, "step": ctx.step, "outcome": outcome, "attempts": len(attempts),
           "started_at": _iso(started), "ended_at": _iso(ended),               # epoch seconds (time.time())
           "wall_seconds": round(ended - started, 1),
           "arm_session_seconds": round(sum(t.wall_seconds for t in arm_turns), 1),
           "po_seconds": round(sum(t.wall_seconds for t in po_turns), 1),
           "owner_wait_seconds": round(sum(q.get("owner_seconds", 0.0) for q in qa), 1),
           "tests_seconds": round(tests_seconds, 1),
           "claude_version": pins.claude_version, "model": pins.model, "plugin_route": pins.plugin_route,
           "images": {"arm": lock["arms"].get(ctx.arm.name, {}).get("id"),
                      "tester": lock["images"].get(pins.images["tester"], {}).get("id"),
                      "proxy": lock["images"].get(pins.images["proxy"], {}).get("id")},
           "po_brief_sha256_at_handoff": shas["po_brief_at_handoff"], "po_brief_sha256_at_end": sha256_file(ctx.brief_path),
           "contract_sha256": shas["contract"], "step_brief_sha256": shas["step_brief"],
           "interventions": [i for a in attempts for i in a.interventions],
           "breaches": [a.breach for a in attempts if a.breach],
           "research_attempts": research_attempts(arm_turns, egress_log),
           "model_overrides": json.loads(overrides_f.read_text())["declared"] if overrides_f.exists() else [],
           "pass2_addendum": ctx.pass_ == "pass2" and shas["po_brief_at_handoff"] != sha256_file(ctx.brief_path),
           "harness_faults": fault_entries}
    (out / "run.json").write_text(json.dumps(run, indent=2))
    missed = possible_missed_approvals(qa)
    esc = [q for q in qa if q.get("escalated")]
    sig = [q for q in qa if q.get("significant")]
    mut = (quality.get("mutation") or {})
    lines = [f"# Step {ctx.step} summary — {ctx.arm.name} ({ctx.pass_})", "",
             f"**Outcome:** {outcome} after {len(attempts)} attempt(s); interventions: {run['interventions'] or 'none'}",
             f"**Hidden tests:** {hidden.passed}/{hidden.total} passed; new at this step {hidden.new_passed}/"
             f"{len(hidden.new_at_step)} (discriminating: {hidden.new_discriminating_passed}); arm flakes: {hidden.flaky_arm}",
             f"**Model pin:** {'ok' if pin.ok else 'VIOLATION'} ({pin.assistant_messages} transcript assistant messages)",
             f"**Cost (estimate):** arm ${c['arm']['usd']:.2f} · PO ${c['po']['usd']:.2f} · harness ${c['harness']['usd']:.2f}",
             f"**Time:** {run['wall_seconds']} s wall, {run['owner_wait_seconds']} s waiting on the owner",
             f"**Quality:** mypy {quality.get('mypy_errors')} errors · ruff {quality.get('ruff_findings')} · "
             f"mutation score {mut.get('score')} ({mut.get('sampling', '')}{', partial' if mut.get('partial') else ''})",
             f"**Research attempts:** {run['research_attempts']}", "", "## Escalations"]
    lines += [f"- {', '.join(q['escalated'])} → {q['reply']}" for q in esc] or ["- none"]
    lines += ["", "## Significant PO answers"] + ([f"- {q['arm_message'][:120]!r} → {str(q['reply'])[:200]}" for q in sig] or ["- none"])
    lines += ["", "## Owner gates"] + ([f"- {g['owner_action']}: {str(g['final_reply'])[:200]}" for g in gates] or ["- none"])
    lines += ["", "## Possible missed approvals (PO said `continue`, the arm used approval wording)"]
    lines += [f"- turn {q['n']}: {q['arm_message'][:200]!r}" for q in missed] or ["- none"]
    lines += ["", "## Egress refusals"] + ([f"- {h}" for h in run["research_attempts"]["refused_hosts"]] or ["- none"])
    if run["model_overrides"]:
        lines += ["", "## Method's own model choices (overridden, OD2)"] + [f"- {m['file']}: {m['model']}" for m in run["model_overrides"]]
    (out / "summary.md").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
    return out / "summary.md"
```

### Task 21: Secret scan of evidence and publish trees (review S-2)

Transcripts, streams and egress logs are committed and eventually published. An arm that runs `gh auth token`,
`cat ~/.config/gh/hosts.yml` or `env` puts its PAT into a transcript. So: every results commit, every step end and
every publish is scanned, **decompressing `.gz`**, for token patterns and for the actual keychain values; a
finding fails closed and is reported by path and kind only (never the matched text).

**Files:**
- Create: `runner/secrets.py`, `.githooks/pre-commit`, `tests/runner/test_secrets.py`
- Modify: `runner/cli.py` (`scan [--staged] [PATH…]`), `runner/gitops.py` (`staged_files()` — harness repo only)

**Interfaces:**
- `secrets.PATTERNS` (GitHub PATs `github_pat_…`, `ghp_/gho_/ghs_/ghu_…`, Anthropic `sk-ant-…`, private-key blocks);
  `secrets.Finding(path, kind, line)`; `secrets.scan_tree(root, literals=()) -> list[Finding]` (no symlink
  following; `.gz` decompressed); `secrets.scan_files(paths, literals=())`; `secrets.literals() -> list[str]` (the
  keychain Claude token and the dry-run dummy token; PATs are caught by pattern).
- Called by: the pre-commit hook (`runner scan --staged`), `stepflow` at every step end (a finding is the harness
  fault `secret-in-evidence:<kind>`, Task 22), `repo.publish` (refuses on any finding), `runner dry-run --report`.

```python
import gzip, os

PAT = "github_pat_" + "A" * 22 + "_" + "b" * 59

def test_a_pat_in_a_plain_file_is_found_by_path_and_kind_only(tmp_path):

    [f] = secrets.scan_tree(tmp_path)

def test_a_token_inside_a_gz_is_found(tmp_path):

        fh.write("ok\nsk-ant-oat01-" + "x" * 40 + "\n")

    (tmp_path / "tree").mkdir(); os.symlink(tmp_path / "outside" / "k", tmp_path / "tree" / "k")
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement**

```python
# runner/secrets.py
from __future__ import annotations
import gzip, os, re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

PATTERNS = {
    "github-pat": re.compile(rb"github_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{40,}"),
    "github-token": re.compile(rb"\bgh[pousr]_[A-Za-z0-9]{30,}"),
    "anthropic-token": re.compile(rb"sk-ant-[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{30,}"),
    "private-key": re.compile(rb"-----BEGIN (?:RSA |OPENSSH |EC )?PRIVATE KEY-----"),
}

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Finding:
    path: str
    kind: str
    line: int

def _bytes(p: Path) -> bytes:
    data = p.read_bytes()
    if p.suffix == ".gz":
        try:
            return gzip.decompress(data)
        except (OSError, EOFError):
            return data
    return data

def _scan_bytes(path: str, data: bytes, literals: list[bytes]) -> list[Finding]:
    out = []
    for i, line in enumerate(data.splitlines(), 1):
        for kind, rx in PATTERNS.items():
            if rx.search(line):
                out.append(Finding(path, kind, i))
        for lit in literals:
            if lit and lit in line:
                out.append(Finding(path, "literal", i))
    return out

def scan_files(paths: list[Path], literals: list[str] | tuple = ()) -> list[Finding]:
    lits = [l.encode() for l in literals if l]
    out: list[Finding] = []
    for p in paths:
        if p.is_symlink() or not p.is_file():
            continue
        out += _scan_bytes(str(p), _bytes(p), lits)
    return out

def scan_tree(root: Path, literals: list[str] | tuple = ()) -> list[Finding]:
    files = []
    for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(root, followlinks=False):
        dirnames[:] = [d for d in dirnames if not (Path(dirpath) / d).is_symlink() and d != ".git"]
        files += [Path(dirpath) / f for f in filenames]
    return scan_files(sorted(files), literals)

def literals() -> list[str]:
    from runner import credentials
    out = ["dry-run-dummy-token"]
    try:
        out.append(credentials.from_keychain().raw_for_scanner())
    except RuntimeError:
        pass
    return out
```

```bash
#!/bin/sh
# .githooks/pre-commit — enable once with: git config core.hooksPath .githooks   (checklists/README.md step 4)
exec uv run --quiet python -m runner scan --staged
```

`gitops.staged_files()` (harness repo): `git -C <REPO_ROOT> diff --cached --name-only -z` → paths. `runner scan
--staged` scans those files; `runner scan PATH…` scans trees; both print `path:line kind` per finding and exit 1 on
any finding.

### Task 22: Stop rules — harness faults vs arm faults, attempts, halt (design §9)

**Files:**
- Modify: `runner/faults.py` (ledger, namespaces, declare), `runner/cli.py` (`faults list [--pass P]`, `faults declare
  <pass> <arm> <step> <slug> --scope rerun-step|retest`, `faults resume <pass> --note "<text>"`)
- Create: `runner/stepflow.py`, `tests/runner/test_faults.py`, `tests/runner/test_stepflow.py`
- Modify: `tests/runner/conftest.py` (the `fake_step` fixture)

**Classification (Review Focus 4):**

| Signal | Class | `kind:signature` | What happens |
|---|---|---|---|
| a transcript/stream model outside `accepted_models` (arm, PO, teammate) | harness | `model-pin:<model>` | restore the step snapshot, re-run the step |
| zero arm transcript messages for an attempt | harness | `model-pin:no-evidence` | re-run the step |
| a stream `claude_code_version` other than the pin | harness | `pin-claude-version:<v>` | re-run the step |
| `auth_failed`; `is_error`/`no_result` **after in-place retries** | harness | `infra-<outcome>:<msg>` | re-run the step |
| PO session failure after retries | harness | `infra-po-<outcome>:<msg>` | re-run the step |
| a docker or proxy error raised by the runner | harness | `infra-container:<msg>` | re-run the step |
| PO reply unusable twice | harness | `po-protocol:unparseable` | re-run the step |
| hand edit fails twice | harness | `hand-edit:<which>` | re-run step 2 from `02-pre-edit` |
| a credential file in the arm's home after a turn | harness | `credential-persisted:<file>` | re-run the step (and the owner investigates) |
| a hidden test flaky **on the reference too** | harness | `flaky-test:<nodeid>` | the owner fixes the test; **re-test only** (same SHA) |
| a hidden test flaky on the arm, **stable on the reference** | arm | — | counted as failed; recorded in `flake.json` (review C-2) |
| first runaway breach per arm, owner says "harness" (OD14 B) | harness | `runaway:<breach>` | re-run the step |
| owner-declared (wrong brief, allowlist too narrow, runner bug) | harness | `manual:<slug>` | `rerun-step` or `retest` |
| attempt ends `timeout`/`stalled` (not reclassified) | arm | — | counts as an attempt; tests run on committed `main` |
| `new_discriminating_passed == 0` after an attempt | arm (no progress) | — | next attempt gets `messages.no_progress` in the same session; after **3**, the arm **stops at this step** (OD3) |
| `install_failed` | arm | — | recorded; counts as no progress |

- **Halt rule:** harness faults go to a ledger — `results/faults.jsonl` for real passes, `results/<pass>/faults.jsonl`
  for dry/probe/canary passes (so they can never halt the real bake-off). The **second** occurrence of the same
  `(kind, signature)` since the last owner resolution writes `HALTED` and every command except `faults …` refuses
  to run until `faults resume --note` records a resolution (design §9). The first occurrence pauses for the owner
  (`Re-run now?`), because design §9 says harness faults are *fixed* and then re-run.
- **After a harness fault (review C-3):** the step snapshot is restored; `attempt` numbering continues (files never
  collide) but the **arm-attempt count and history reset to zero**, and every attempt since the snapshot — including
  earlier no-progress ones — is parked under `attempts/<k>-harness-<kind>/`, so the evidence holds one clean timeline
  and the arm gets its full three attempts.
- A no-progress attempt keeps its turns and questions at the top level (the session continues); only its hidden
  results and an `attempt.json` move to `attempts/<k>-no-progress/`.
- Arm stop: `results/<pass>/<arm>/arm-result.json` = `{"stopped_at": N, "reason": "no progress in 3 attempts",
  "last_hidden": {...}}`; the remaining steps are not run for that arm in that pass (design §9).

**Interfaces:**
- `faults.record(f, pass_, arm, step, attempt) -> "rerun"|"halt"`; `faults.halted(pass_) -> str | None`;
  `faults.resume(pass_, note)`; `faults.for_step(pass_, arm, step) -> list[dict]`;
  `faults.declare(pass_, arm, step, slug, scope)`; `faults.ledger_paths(pass_) -> (faults_file, halted_file)`.
- `stepflow.run_step(ctx, first_message, extra=None) -> StepOutcome(status "done"|"stopped", attempts, hidden,
  quality)`; `stepflow.Halt`, `stepflow.Paused`; `stepflow.park(out, k, label)`.

```python
# tests/runner/test_faults.py
import pytest
from runner import faults
from runner.faults import HarnessFault

@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def ledger(tmp_state):
    return tmp_state

def test_first_occurrence_reruns_second_identical_one_halts():
    f = HarnessFault("model-pin", "claude-haiku-4-5")
    assert faults.record(f, "pass1", "superpowers", 3, 1) == "rerun"
    assert faults.record(f, "pass1", "openspec", 1, 1) == "halt"        # same fault, any arm
    assert faults.halted("pass1") and faults.halted("pass2")          # real passes share one ledger

def test_dry_passes_have_their_own_ledger():
    f = HarnessFault("model-pin", "claude-haiku-4-5")
    faults.record(f, "dry3", "dryrun-pinfault", 1, 1); faults.record(f, "dry3", "dryrun-pinfault", 1, 2)
    assert faults.halted("dry3") and not faults.halted("pass1")

def test_different_signatures_do_not_add_up():
    assert faults.record(HarnessFault("infra-api", "overloaded"), "pass1", "bmad", 2, 1) == "rerun"
    assert faults.record(HarnessFault("infra-api", "rate limited"), "pass1", "bmad", 2, 1) == "rerun"

def test_owner_resolution_resets_the_count():
    f = HarnessFault("flaky-test", "tests.hidden.test_step2_sync::test_x")
    faults.record(f, "pass1", "a", 2, 1); faults.record(f, "pass1", "a", 2, 1)
    faults.resume("pass1", "fixed the readiness wait")
    assert not faults.halted("pass1") and faults.record(f, "pass1", "a", 2, 1) == "rerun"

def test_for_step_returns_only_that_steps_entries():
    faults.record(HarnessFault("x", "1"), "pass1", "a", 2, 1); faults.record(HarnessFault("x", "2"), "pass1", "a", 3, 1)
    assert [e["signature"] for e in faults.for_step("pass1", "a", 2)] == ["1"]

def test_declare_records_a_manual_fault_with_its_scope():
    faults.declare("pass1", "a", 4, "brief-typo", "retest")
    [e] = faults.for_step("pass1", "a", 4)
    assert e["kind"] == "manual" and e["signature"] == "brief-typo" and e["scope"] == "retest"
```

```python
# tests/runner/test_stepflow.py — attempts and the stop rule, with drive/hidden/quality/evidence faked (conftest.fake_step)
from runner import stepflow

def test_three_no_progress_attempts_stop_the_arm(fake_step):
    fake_step.progress = [0, 0, 0]
    out = stepflow.run_step(fake_step.ctx, "go")
    assert out.status == "stopped" and out.attempts == 3
    assert fake_step.messages == ["go", fake_step.no_progress, fake_step.no_progress]

def test_partial_progress_is_final(fake_step):
    fake_step.progress = [2]
    out = stepflow.run_step(fake_step.ctx, "go")
    assert out.status == "done" and out.attempts == 1

def test_harness_fault_restores_the_snapshot_and_does_not_count(fake_step):
    fake_step.raise_on = {1: ("model-pin", "claude-haiku-4-5")}
    fake_step.progress = [3]
    out = stepflow.run_step(fake_step.ctx, "go")
    assert out.status == "done" and out.attempts == 1 and fake_step.restored == [fake_step.ctx.step]

def test_a_harness_fault_after_no_progress_resets_the_count_and_parks_both(fake_step):     # review C-3
    fake_step.progress = [0, 0, 0, 0]
    fake_step.raise_on = {2: ("infra-container", "boom")}
    out = stepflow.run_step(fake_step.ctx, "go")
    assert out.status == "stopped" and out.attempts == 3                 # three arm attempts after the restore
    assert fake_step.messages == ["go", fake_step.no_progress, "go", fake_step.no_progress, fake_step.no_progress]
    assert (1, "harness-infra-container") in fake_step.parked and (2, "harness-infra-container") in fake_step.parked

def test_pin_violation_found_after_a_clean_attempt_is_still_a_harness_fault(fake_step):
    fake_step.pin_violation_on = {1: "claude-sonnet-5"}
    fake_step.progress = [3, 3]
    out = stepflow.run_step(fake_step.ctx, "go")
    assert fake_step.restored == [fake_step.ctx.step] and out.status == "done"

def test_a_harness_flake_is_retested_on_the_same_commit(fake_step):
    fake_step.progress = [1]
    fake_step.harness_flaky_first = ["tests.hidden.test_step2_sync::test_x"]
    stepflow.run_step(fake_step.ctx, "go")
    assert fake_step.hidden_runs == 2 and fake_step.prepares == 1

def test_first_runaway_breach_may_be_reclassified_by_the_owner(fake_step):  # OD14 B
    fake_step.breach_on = {1: "exchanges"}
    fake_step.ctx.owner.reclassify = lambda what, detail: "harness"
    fake_step.progress = [2]
    stepflow.run_step(fake_step.ctx, "go")
    assert fake_step.restored == [fake_step.ctx.step]
```

```python
# tests/runner/conftest.py (addition) — the fake_step fixture used by test_stepflow.py
import pytest as _pytest
from runner import stepflow, handoff
from runner.drive import AttemptResult
from runner.transcripts import PinReport
from runner.faults import HarnessFault

class FakeStep:
    def __init__(self, ctx):
        self.ctx, self.messages, self.restored, self.parked = ctx, [], [], []
        self.progress, self.raise_on, self.pin_violation_on, self.breach_on = [], {}, {}, {}
        self.harness_flaky_first, self.hidden_runs, self.prepares, self.calls = [], 0, 0, 0
        self.no_progress = handoff.MESSAGES["no_progress"]

@_pytest.fixture
def fake_step(tmp_path, monkeypatch, tmp_state):
    ctx = make_test_ctx(tmp_path, arm="control", step=2)
    fs = FakeStep(ctx)
    def run_attempt(c, attempt, message, resume=None):
        fs.messages.append(message); fs.calls += 1
        if fs.calls in fs.raise_on:
            raise HarnessFault(*fs.raise_on[fs.calls])
        return AttemptResult("done", "s1", breach=fs.breach_on.get(fs.calls))
    def verify_pin(files, streams, pins, **k):
        bad = fs.pin_violation_on.get(fs.calls)
        return PinReport(not bad, 1, 1, 0, 0, ["2.1.281"], False,
                         [{"file": "f", "line": 1, "where": "assistant", "model": bad}] if bad else [])
    def hidden_run(p, copy, step, out, install_failed=False):
        fs.hidden_runs += 1
        flaky = fs.harness_flaky_first if fs.hidden_runs == 1 else []
        n = fs.progress[min(fs.calls, len(fs.progress)) - 1] if fs.progress else 0
        return stepflow.hidden.summarise(step, {"m::t": "passed" if n else "failed", **{t: "failed" for t in flaky}},
                                         ["m::t"], flaky, [], 0)
    monkeypatch.setattr(stepflow.drive, "run_attempt", run_attempt)
    monkeypatch.setattr(stepflow.transcripts, "verify_pin", verify_pin)
    monkeypatch.setattr(stepflow.transcripts, "snapshot", lambda d: {})
    monkeypatch.setattr(stepflow.transcripts, "collect", lambda d, b, dest: [])
    monkeypatch.setattr(stepflow.gitops, "main_sha", lambda p: "main-sha")
    monkeypatch.setattr(stepflow.hidden, "prepare", lambda *a, **k: (setattr(fs, "prepares", fs.prepares + 1) or
                        stepflow.hidden.Prepared(tmp_path, tmp_path, {"hidden": {"ok": True}, "quality": {"ok": True}})))
    monkeypatch.setattr(stepflow.hidden, "run", hidden_run)
    monkeypatch.setattr(stepflow.quality, "run_all", lambda *a, **k: {"mutation": {}})
    monkeypatch.setattr(stepflow.evidence, "finalise", lambda *a, **k: ctx.out / "summary.md")
    monkeypatch.setattr(stepflow.repo, "restore", lambda p, s: fs.restored.append(s))
    monkeypatch.setattr(stepflow, "park", lambda out, k, label: fs.parked.append((k, label)))
    monkeypatch.setattr(stepflow, "wait_idle", lambda ctx: None)
    return fs
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement the ledger**

```python
# runner/faults.py (ledger part — keep the file's imports together at its top when appending)
import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal
from runner import paths

def _now() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds")

def ledger_paths(pass_: str) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
    if pass_ in paths.REAL_PASSES:
        return paths.FAULTS, paths.HALTED
    return paths.RESULTS / pass_ / "faults.jsonl", paths.RESULTS / pass_ / "HALTED"

def _entries(pass_: str) -> list[dict]:
    f, _ = ledger_paths(pass_)
    return [json.loads(l) for l in f.read_text().splitlines()] if f.exists() else []

def _append(pass_: str, entry: dict) -> None:
    f, _ = ledger_paths(pass_)
    f.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(f, "a") as fh:
        fh.write(json.dumps(entry) + "\n")

def record(f: "HarnessFault", pass_: str, arm: str, step: int, attempt: int) -> Literal["rerun", "halt"]:
    key, since = (f.kind, f.signature), []
    for e in _entries(pass_):
        if e.get("resolution"):
            since = []
        elif (e.get("kind"), e.get("signature")) == key:
            since.append(e)
    entry = {"at": _now(), "pass": pass_, "arm": arm, "step": step, "attempt": attempt, "kind": f.kind,
             "signature": f.signature, "detail": f.detail, "action": "halt" if since else "rerun"}
    _append(pass_, entry)
    if since:
        _, halted_file = ledger_paths(pass_)
        halted_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        halted_file.write_text(json.dumps({"reason": "same harness fault twice (design §9)", "first": since[-1],
                                           "second": entry}, indent=2))
        return "halt"
    return "rerun"

def halted(pass_: str) -> str | None:
    _, h = ledger_paths(pass_)
    return h.read_text() if h.exists() else None

def resume(pass_: str, note: str) -> None:
    """A resolution resets every signature's count: the owner reviewed the whole ledger when resuming."""
    _append(pass_, {"resolution": True, "note": note, "at": _now()})
    ledger_paths(pass_)[1].unlink(missing_ok=True)

def for_step(pass_: str, arm: str, step: int) -> list[dict]:
    return [e for e in _entries(pass_) if e.get("arm") == arm and e.get("step") == step and not e.get("resolution")]

def declare(pass_: str, arm: str, step: int, slug: str, scope: str) -> None:
    if scope not in ("rerun-step", "retest"):
        raise ValueError("scope is rerun-step or retest")
    _append(pass_, {"at": _now(), "pass": pass_, "arm": arm, "step": step, "attempt": None, "kind": "manual",
                    "signature": slug, "detail": "owner-declared", "action": scope, "scope": scope})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `runner/stepflow.py`**

```python
# runner/stepflow.py
from __future__ import annotations
import json, shutil, time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from runner import drive, evidence, faults, gitops, handoff, hidden, preflight, quality, repo, transcripts
from runner.faults import HarnessFault

class Halt(Exception):
    pass

class Paused(Exception):
    pass

@dataclass
class StepOutcome:
    status: str                  # "done" | "stopped"
    attempts: int
    hidden: hidden.Summary
    quality: dict = field(default_factory=dict)

def park(out: Path, k: int, label: str) -> None:
    """Move attempt k's evidence under attempts/<k>-<label>/. Hidden results always; for a harness label also its
    turns, transcripts, PO files and its questions / owner-gates lines, so a faulted attempt leaves nothing on top."""
    dest = out / "attempts" / f"{k}-{label}"; dest.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    earlier = out / "attempts" / f"{k}-no-progress"
    if label.startswith("harness-") and earlier.exists():      # a no-progress attempt later discarded by a fault
        shutil.move(str(earlier), str(dest / "no-progress"))
    if (out / "hidden").exists():
        shutil.move(str(out / "hidden"), str(dest / "hidden"))
    if label.startswith("harness-"):
        for f in list((out / "turns").glob(f"*-a{k}-*")) if (out / "turns").exists() else []:
            (dest / "turns").mkdir(exist_ok=True); shutil.move(str(f), str(dest / "turns" / f.name))
        if (out / "transcripts" / f"a{k}").exists():
            shutil.move(str(out / "transcripts" / f"a{k}"), str(dest / "transcripts"))
        for d in list((out / "po").glob(f"a{k}-*")) if (out / "po").exists() else []:
            (dest / "po").mkdir(exist_ok=True); shutil.move(str(d), str(dest / "po" / d.name))
        for name in ("questions.jsonl", "owner-gates.jsonl"):
            f = out / name
            if f.exists():
                keep, moved = [], []
                for l in f.read_text().splitlines():
                    (moved if json.loads(l).get("attempt") == k else keep).append(l)
                f.write_text("".join(x + "\n" for x in keep))
                if moved:
                    (dest / name).write_text("".join(x + "\n" for x in moved))
    (dest / "attempt.json").write_text(json.dumps({"attempt": k, "label": label}))

def wait_idle(ctx) -> None:
    for _ in range(20):
        ok, detail = preflight.idle()
        if ok:
            return
        time.sleep(30)
    if not ctx.owner.confirm(f"The machine is not idle ({detail}). Run the hidden tests anyway?"):
        raise Paused()

def _breach_seen(ctx) -> bool:
    f = ctx.paths.results / "breaches.json"
    return f.exists() and bool(json.loads(f.read_text()))

def _note_breach(ctx, what: str, verdict: str) -> None:
    f = ctx.paths.results / "breaches.json"; f.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    prior = json.loads(f.read_text()) if f.exists() else []
    f.write_text(json.dumps(prior + [{"step": ctx.step, "breach": what, "owner": verdict}], indent=2))

def _handle_fault(ctx, f: HarnessFault, parked: list[int]) -> None:
    for k in parked:
        park(ctx.out, k, f"harness-{f.kind}")
    if faults.record(f, ctx.pass_, ctx.arm.name, ctx.step, parked[-1] if parked else 0) == "halt":
        raise Halt(faults.halted(ctx.pass_))
    if not ctx.owner.confirm(f"Harness fault {f.kind}: {f.signature}. Fix it, then re-run step {ctx.step} now?"):
        raise Paused()
    repo.restore(ctx.paths, ctx.snapshot if ctx.snapshot is not None else ctx.step)

def run_step(ctx, first_message: str, extra: dict | None = None) -> StepOutcome:
    extra, t0 = extra or {}, time.time()
    attempt, arm_attempts, history, since_restore = 0, 0, [], []
    message, resume = first_message, None
    lim = ctx.pins.limits
    while True:
        attempt += 1
        before = transcripts.snapshot(ctx.paths.config_dir)
        try:
            ar = drive.run_attempt(ctx, attempt, message, resume)
            files = transcripts.collect(ctx.paths.config_dir, before, ctx.out / "transcripts" / f"a{attempt}")
            pin = transcripts.verify_pin(files, [t.stream_path for t in ar.turns if t.stream_path], ctx.pins)
            if (f := faults.classify_pin(pin, "arm")):
                raise f
            if ar.breach and lim.owner_reclassifies_first_breach and not _breach_seen(ctx):     # OD14 B
                verdict = ctx.owner.reclassify(ar.breach, f"step {ctx.step}, attempt {attempt}")
                _note_breach(ctx, ar.breach, verdict)
                if verdict == "harness":
                    raise HarnessFault("runaway", ar.breach, f"owner reclassified the first breach (step {ctx.step})")
        except HarnessFault as f:
            _handle_fault(ctx, f, since_restore + [attempt])
            since_restore, arm_attempts, history = [], 0, []                                    # review C-3
            if ctx.on_restore is not None:          # step 6: the old issue is superseded, a new one created
                first_message = ctx.on_restore() or first_message
            message, resume = first_message, None
            continue
        arm_attempts += 1
        since_restore.append(attempt)
        wait_idle(ctx)
        t_tests = time.time()
        prep = hidden.prepare(ctx.paths, gitops.main_sha(ctx.paths), f"{ctx.step:02d}-a{attempt}", ctx.out,
                              with_quality=True)
        summary = hidden.run(ctx.paths, prep.hidden_copy, ctx.step, ctx.out, not prep.prep["hidden"].get("ok", False))
        while summary.flaky:                                   # harness flakes only (review C-2): fix the test, re-test
            flaky = [t for t, o in summary.tests.items() if o == "flaky"]
            if "halt" in [faults.record(HarnessFault("flaky-test", t), ctx.pass_, ctx.arm.name, ctx.step, attempt)
                          for t in flaky]:
                raise Halt(faults.halted(ctx.pass_))
            if not ctx.owner.confirm(f"Hidden test(s) flaky on the reference too: {flaky}. Fix, then re-test this commit?"):
                raise Paused()
            summary = hidden.run(ctx.paths, prep.hidden_copy, ctx.step, ctx.out)         # same SHA, no new session
        tests_s = time.time() - t_tests
        progressed = summary.new_discriminating_passed > 0
        if progressed or arm_attempts == lim.attempts_no_progress:
            q = quality.run_all(ctx.paths, prep, ctx.out)
            outcome = "done" if progressed else "stopped"
            evidence.finalise(ctx, attempts=history + [ar], extra_turns=extra, hidden=summary, quality=q, pin=pin,
                              sha_before=ctx.sha_before, started=t0, ended=time.time(),
                              fault_entries=faults.for_step(ctx.pass_, ctx.arm.name, ctx.step),
                              shas=ctx.shas, tests_seconds=tests_s, outcome=outcome)
            return StepOutcome(outcome, arm_attempts, summary, q)
        park(ctx.out, attempt, "no-progress")
        history.append(ar)
        message, resume = handoff.MESSAGES["no_progress"], ar.session_id
```

CLI: `faults list [--pass P]` prints the ledger; `faults declare …` → `faults.declare` and, for `rerun-step`, sets
`progress.json` `{"rerun": step}` (Task 23 honours it); `faults resume <pass> --note` → `faults.resume`.

```bash
git add runner/faults.py runner/stepflow.py runner/cli.py tests/runner/test_faults.py tests/runner/test_stepflow.py tests/runner/conftest.py
git commit -m "Runner: namespaced fault ledger and halt rule; stepflow with C-3 reset, flake-vs-arm split, OD14 reclassification"
```

### Task 23: Orchestration — order, run an arm, run a pass, reset for pass 2, pass-1 close-out

**Files:**
- Create: `runner/orchestrate.py`, `tests/runner/test_orchestrate.py`
- Modify: `runner/preflight.py` (`check_images`, `lock`), `runner/cli.py` (`order`, `run-arm`, `run-pass`,
  `reset-pass2`, `status`), `tests/runner/conftest.py` (`tmp_results`, `fake_arm_env`)

**Interfaces:**
- `orchestrate.NotReady(Exception)`; `orchestrate.order(pass_, seed=None) -> list[str]` (pass 1: `random.Random(seed)`
  shuffle of `all_arms()`, control swapped out of first place (OD13); pass 2: pass 1's order, **reversed** or the
  same per `pins.pass2_order`; canary: `["control"]`; written to `results/<pass>/order.json` with the seed and rule).
- `orchestrate.run_arm(pass_, arm, cred, owner, limits_override=None, stop_after=None) -> dict` (resumable from
  `progress.json`; `limits_override` only for dry arms; `stop_after` only for the canary);
  `orchestrate.run_pass(pass_, cred, owner)`; `orchestrate.reset_pass2(owner)`; `orchestrate.close_out_pass1() -> Path`;
  `orchestrate.status() -> str`.
- `preflight.check_images(arm, p)` (raises `NotReady` if an image ID differs from `images.lock.json` or from the
  arm's `00-setup/versions.json`, design §2); `preflight.lock()` (context manager: `fcntl.flock` on `state/runner.lock`).

**`run_arm` sequence** (the only place that knows step order):
1. Refuse if `faults.halted(pass_)`, if `checklist.is_green` is false (design §6), or if `check_images` fails. Take the
   lock; `docker.sweep()`.
2. A pending `faults declare … --scope rerun-step` (in `progress.json`) moves that step's results to
   `NN-superseded-<n>/`, restores its snapshot and runs it again; `--scope retest` re-runs tests, quality and evidence on
   the step's recorded `sha_after`.
3. For each step from `progress.json` onwards:
   a. `checklist.verify(mode="step")`; any failure → harness fault `setup-drift:<id>` (recorded; the owner fixes it).
   b. Step 2: `handedit.apply` (it takes the step-2 snapshot; the step's `sha_before` is the teammate commit). Other
      steps: `repo.snapshot(p, step)`.
   c. Messages: step 6 → `tracker.create_bug` then `handoff.bug_message`; step 5 → `handoff.step5` (OD6); others →
      `handoff.compose`. Saved as `NN/handoff.md` (+ SHA-256 of the brief, the contract and `po-brief.md`).
   d. `stepflow.run_step(ctx, entry_message(first), extra={"harness": teammate turns})`. Step 6 sets `ctx.on_restore`
      so a harness re-run supersedes the old issue and creates a new one (GitHub issues cannot be rolled back).
   e. Step 2: `handedit.detect`. Step 6: `tracker.round_trip_facts` → `tracker-round-trip.json`. Every step:
      `tracker.snapshot`.
   f. `secrets.scan_tree(NN/)`: any finding → harness fault `secret-in-evidence:<kind>` and a stop until the owner
      scrubs it (review S-2).
   g. `progress.json` updated; a `stopped` outcome writes `arm-result.json` and ends the arm (design §9).
   h. `owner.step_review(summary.md)` → continue or pause (a pause exits cleanly; `run-arm` resumes).
4. At the end: `repo.bundle` → `results/<pass>/<arm>/repo.bundle`; `arm-result.json`; `repo.publish` to the
   **private** repo (OD8), which secret-scans a bundle clone first (review S-1, S-2).

**`reset_pass2` (OD20):** refuses unless every arm has `results/pass1/<arm>/arm-result.json`; writes
`results/pass1/close-out.md` (below) and asks the owner to confirm each item; copies `scenario/po-brief.md` to
`results/pass2/po-brief-at-start.md` with its SHA-256; checks `state/pass2/` does not exist; prints `github-plan
pass2` and tells the owner to run `setup pass2 <arm>` for each arm. Nothing from `state/pass1/` is ever mounted in pass 2.
**Pass-1 close-out (review S-2):** every pass-1 PAT revoked; `runner scan results/pass1` clean; bundles present.

```python
import json as _j

from runner import orchestrate

        return handedit.HandEdit("edit-sha", ["cli.py"], [], {}, 1, {}, [], {})
    monkeypatch.setattr(handedit, "apply", apply)
    monkeypatch.setattr(handedit, "detect", lambda ctx, e, sha: env.calls.append(("handedit.detect", ctx.step)))
    def create_bug(ctx, owner):
        env.issues += 1; env.calls.append(("tracker.create_bug", ctx.step))
        return tracker.IssueRef("github", f"troupe-run/x#{env.issues}", None, str(env.issues))
    monkeypatch.setattr(tracker, "create_bug", create_bug)
    monkeypatch.setattr(tracker, "supersede", lambda ctx, old, owner: env.calls.append(("tracker.supersede", old.id)))
    monkeypatch.setattr(tracker, "snapshot", lambda ctx: {"issues": []})
    monkeypatch.setattr(tracker, "round_trip_facts", lambda *a: {})
    def run_step(ctx, first, extra=None):
        env.calls.append(("stepflow.run_step", ctx.step)); env.first_messages[ctx.step] = first
        ctx.out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (ctx.out / "summary.md").write_text(f"# Step {ctx.step} summary\n")
        (ctx.out / "hidden").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        (ctx.out / "hidden" / "summary.json").write_text(_j.dumps({"tests": {}}))
        if ctx.step == 6 and ctx.on_restore:
            env.restore_message = ctx.on_restore()
        status = "stopped" if env.stop_at == ctx.step else "done"
        return stepflow.StepOutcome(status, 1, hidden.summarise(ctx.step, {}, [], [], [], 0), {})
    monkeypatch.setattr(stepflow, "run_step", run_step)
    env.arm_result = lambda: tmp_state / "results" / "pass1" / "openspec" / "arm-result.json"
    return env
```

```python
# tests/runner/test_orchestrate.py
import dataclasses, json, pytest
from runner import config, orchestrate

def test_order_is_reproducible_from_the_seed_and_control_never_goes_first(tmp_results):
    for seed in range(50):
        o = orchestrate.order("pass1", seed)
        assert o == orchestrate.order("pass1", seed) and o[0] != "control" and sorted(o) == sorted(config.all_arms())
    assert json.loads((tmp_results / "pass1" / "order.json").read_text())["seed"] == 49

def test_pass2_reverses_pass1_by_default(tmp_results):                          # OD13 B
    o1 = orchestrate.order("pass1", 7)
    assert orchestrate.order("pass2") == list(reversed(o1))

def test_pass2_can_keep_pass1_order(tmp_results, monkeypatch):                   # OD13 A
    o1 = orchestrate.order("pass1", 7)
    monkeypatch.setattr(orchestrate.config, "load_pins",
                        lambda: dataclasses.replace(config.load_pins(), pass2_order="same"))
    assert orchestrate.order("pass2") == o1

def test_step1_is_refused_until_the_checklist_is_green(fake_arm_env):
    fake_arm_env.green = False
    with pytest.raises(orchestrate.NotReady, match="checklist"):
        orchestrate.run_arm("pass1", "openspec", fake_arm_env.cred, fake_arm_env.owner)

def test_hand_edit_happens_before_step2_and_the_bug_goes_to_the_tracker_at_step6(fake_arm_env):
    orchestrate.run_arm("pass1", "openspec", fake_arm_env.cred, fake_arm_env.owner)
    calls = fake_arm_env.calls
    assert calls.index(("handedit.apply", 2)) < calls.index(("stepflow.run_step", 2))
    assert calls.index(("tracker.create_bug", 6)) < calls.index(("stepflow.run_step", 6))
    assert fake_arm_env.first_messages[6].startswith("/opsx:propose A bug has been reported in our tracker:")
    assert ("repo.publish", 0) in calls

def test_a_step6_harness_rerun_supersedes_the_issue_and_names_the_new_one(fake_arm_env):
    orchestrate.run_arm("pass1", "openspec", fake_arm_env.cred, fake_arm_env.owner)
    assert ("tracker.supersede", "1") in fake_arm_env.calls and "troupe-run/x#2" in fake_arm_env.restore_message

def test_step5_hands_cr_b_to_the_step_as_a_later_message(fake_arm_env, monkeypatch):   # OD6 B
    seen = {}
    real = orchestrate.stepflow.run_step
    def spy(ctx, first, extra=None):
        seen[ctx.step] = list(ctx.later_messages); return real(ctx, first, extra)
    monkeypatch.setattr(orchestrate.stepflow, "run_step", spy)
    orchestrate.run_arm("pass1", "openspec", fake_arm_env.cred, fake_arm_env.owner)
    assert len(seen[5]) == 1 and "## CR-B" in seen[5][0] and seen[4] == []

def test_a_stopped_arm_runs_no_further_steps(fake_arm_env):
    fake_arm_env.stop_at = 3
    orchestrate.run_arm("pass1", "openspec", fake_arm_env.cred, fake_arm_env.owner)
    assert [s for (c, s) in fake_arm_env.calls if c == "stepflow.run_step"] == [1, 2, 3]
    assert json.loads(fake_arm_env.arm_result().read_text())["stopped_at"] == 3

def test_run_arm_resumes_after_a_pause(fake_arm_env):
    fake_arm_env.pause_after = 2
    orchestrate.run_arm("pass1", "openspec", fake_arm_env.cred, fake_arm_env.owner)
    fake_arm_env.pause_after = None
    orchestrate.run_arm("pass1", "openspec", fake_arm_env.cred, fake_arm_env.owner)
    assert [s for (c, s) in fake_arm_env.calls if c == "stepflow.run_step"] == [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7]

def test_pass2_reset_refuses_while_pass1_is_incomplete(fake_arm_env):
    with pytest.raises(orchestrate.NotReady, match="pass1"):
        orchestrate.reset_pass2(fake_arm_env.owner)
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement `preflight.check_images`/`lock` and `runner/orchestrate.py`**

```python
# runner/preflight.py (additions)
import contextlib, fcntl, json
from runner import images, paths

class NotReady(RuntimeError):
    pass

def check_images(arm_name: str, p) -> None:
    lock = images.lock()
    a = lock["arms"][arm_name]
    tags = {a["image"]: a["id"], **{t: v["id"] for t, v in lock["images"].items()}}
    for tag, want in tags.items():
        if images.current_id(tag) != want:
            raise NotReady(f"image {tag} differs from images.lock.json (design §2: no mid-bake-off upgrade)")
    setup = json.loads((p.setup_results / "versions.json").read_text())["image"].get("id")
    if setup and setup != a["id"]:
        raise NotReady(f"{arm_name}: image differs from the one recorded at setup")

@contextlib.contextmanager
def lock():
    paths.LOCK.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(paths.LOCK, "w") as fh:
        try:
            fcntl.flock(fh, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        except BlockingIOError:
            raise NotReady("another runner holds state/runner.lock (one thing at a time)")
        yield
```

```python
# runner/orchestrate.py
from __future__ import annotations
import dataclasses, hashlib, json, random, shutil
from pathlib import Path
from runner import (checklist, config, docker, drive, egress, faults, gitops, handedit, handoff, paths, preflight,
                    repo, secrets, stepflow, tracker)
from runner.faults import HarnessFault
from runner.paths import Paths, REPO_ROOT, brief_path

NotReady = preflight.NotReady
CONTRACT = REPO_ROOT / "scenario" / "contract.md"
BRIEFS = sorted((REPO_ROOT / "scenario" / "steps").glob("0*.md"))

def _sha(p: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

def order(pass_: str, seed: int | None = None) -> list[str]:
    out_f = paths.RESULTS / pass_ / "order.json"; out_f.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if pass_ == "pass1":
        arms = config.all_arms(); random.Random(seed).shuffle(arms)
        if arms[0] == "control":
            arms[0], arms[1] = arms[1], arms[0]
        rec = {"seed": seed, "rule": "shuffle; control not first (OD13)", "order": arms}
    elif pass_ == "pass2":
        first = json.loads((paths.RESULTS / "pass1" / "order.json").read_text())["order"]
        rule = config.load_pins().pass2_order
        arms = list(reversed(first)) if rule == "reverse" else list(first)
        rec = {"seed": None, "rule": f"pass 1 order, {rule} (OD13)", "order": arms}
    else:
        arms = ["control"] if pass_ == "canary" else config.all_arms()
        rec = {"seed": None, "rule": pass_, "order": arms}
    out_f.write_text(json.dumps(rec, indent=2))
    return arms

def _progress(p: Paths) -> dict:
    return json.loads(p.progress.read_text()) if p.progress.exists() else {"next_step": 1, "done": [], "rerun": None}

def _save(p: Paths, prog: dict) -> None:
    p.progress.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    p.progress.write_text(json.dumps(prog, indent=2))

def _fault(pass_, arm, step, f: HarnessFault) -> None:
    if faults.record(f, pass_, arm, step, 0) == "halt":
        raise stepflow.Halt(faults.halted(pass_))
    raise NotReady(f"harness fault {f.kind}: {f.signature} — fix it, then run `run-arm` again")

def _supersede_dir(p: Paths, step: int) -> None:
    d, k = p.step(step), 1
    while (p.results / f"{step:02d}-superseded-{k}").exists():
        k += 1
    if d.exists():
        shutil.move(str(d), str(p.results / f"{step:02d}-superseded-{k}"))

def run_arm(pass_: str, arm_name: str, cred, owner, limits_override: dict | None = None,
            stop_after: int | None = None) -> dict:
    """limits_override is used only by `dry_run` (short backoffs, a short turn limit for dry4); refused for real arms."""
    arm, p, pins = config.load_arm(arm_name), Paths(pass_, arm_name), config.load_pins()
    if limits_override:
        if not arm.dry:
            raise PermissionError("limits_override is for dry-run arms only")
        pins = dataclasses.replace(pins, limits=dataclasses.replace(pins.limits, **limits_override))
    if faults.halted(pass_):
        raise NotReady(f"bake-off halted: {faults.halted(pass_)}")
    if not checklist.is_green(p):
        raise NotReady(f"{arm_name}: setup checklist is not green (design §6) — run `runner setup {pass_} {arm_name}`")
    preflight.check_images(arm_name, p)
    contract = CONTRACT.read_text()
    with preflight.lock():
        docker.sweep()
        prog = _progress(p)
        if prog.get("rerun"):                                       # `faults declare … --scope rerun-step`
            step = prog["rerun"]; _supersede_dir(p, step); repo.restore(p, step)
            prog.update(next_step=step, rerun=None, done=[s for s in prog["done"] if s < step]); _save(p, prog)
        edit = None
        for step in range(prog["next_step"], (stop_after or 7) + 1):       # stop_after: the canary (Task 26)
            for c in checklist.verify(p, arm, "step"):
                if not c.ok:
                    _fault(pass_, arm_name, step, HarnessFault("setup-drift", c.id, c.detail))
            out = p.step(step); out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            brief_file = BRIEFS[step - 1]; brief_text = brief_file.read_text()
            ctx = drive.StepCtx(pass_, arm, step, p, pins, out, handoff.in_force(step, contract), brief_text,
                                brief_path(pass_), cred, owner, gitops.main_sha(p) or gitops.head(p))
            extra: dict = {}
            if step == 2:
                step1 = json.loads((p.step(1) / "hidden" / "summary.json").read_text())["tests"]
                try:
                    edit = handedit.apply(ctx, step1)
                except HarnessFault as f:
                    _fault(pass_, arm_name, 2, f)
                extra["harness"] = edit.turns; ctx.sha_before = edit.sha; ctx.snapshot = 2
            else:
                repo.snapshot(p, step)
            issue = None
            if step == 6:
                issue = tracker.create_bug(ctx, owner)
                messages = [handoff.bug_message(issue.ref, contract)]
                def on_restore(ctx=ctx):
                    nonlocal issue
                    tracker.supersede(ctx, issue, owner)
                    issue = tracker.create_bug(ctx, owner)
                    return handoff.entry_message(arm, 6, handoff.bug_message(issue.ref, contract))
                ctx.on_restore = on_restore
            elif step == 5:
                messages = handoff.step5(brief_text, contract)
            else:
                messages = [handoff.compose(step, brief_text, contract)]
            ctx.later_messages = messages[1:]
            ctx.shas = {"po_brief_at_handoff": _sha(ctx.brief_path), "contract": _sha(CONTRACT), "step_brief": _sha(brief_file)}
            (out / "handoff.md").write_text("\n\n===== next message =====\n\n".join(messages))
            try:
                outcome = stepflow.run_step(ctx, handoff.entry_message(arm, step, messages[0]), extra)
            except stepflow.Paused:
                _save(p, {**prog, "next_step": step}); return {"paused_at": step}
            main = gitops.main_sha(p)
            if step == 2 and edit is not None:
                handedit.detect(ctx, edit, main)
            snap = tracker.snapshot(ctx)
            if step == 6 and issue is not None:
                (out / "tracker-round-trip.json").write_text(json.dumps(
                    tracker.round_trip_facts(ctx, issue, ctx.sha_before, main, snap), indent=2))
            prog["done"].append(step); prog["next_step"] = step + 1; _save(p, prog)
            if (found := secrets.scan_tree(out, secrets.literals())):
                _fault(pass_, arm_name, step, HarnessFault("secret-in-evidence", found[0].kind, found[0].path))
            if outcome.status == "stopped":
                p.results.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                (p.results / "arm-result.json").write_text(json.dumps(
                    {"stopped_at": step, "reason": "no progress in 3 attempts", "last_hidden": outcome.hidden.__dict__}, indent=2))
                return {"stopped_at": step}
            decision, _ = owner.step_review((out / "summary.md").read_text())
            if decision == "pause" and step < 7:
                return {"paused_after": step}
        if stop_after:
            return {"stopped_after": stop_after}
        repo.bundle(p, p.results / "repo.bundle")
        (p.results / "arm-result.json").write_text(json.dumps({"completed": True, "last_step": 7}, indent=2))
        if repo.github_slug(pass_, arm_name):
            repo.publish(p, pass_, owner.confirm, lambda d: secrets.scan_tree(d, secrets.literals()))
        return {"completed": True}

def run_pass(pass_: str, cred, owner) -> None:
    for arm_name in json.loads((paths.RESULTS / pass_ / "order.json").read_text())["order"]:
        p = Paths(pass_, arm_name)
        if (p.results / "arm-result.json").exists():
            continue
        if not p.base.exists():
            if not owner.confirm(f"Set up {arm_name} for {pass_} now (owner present)?"):
                return
            checklist.run_setup(pass_, arm_name, cred, owner)
        r = run_arm(pass_, arm_name, cred, owner)
        if "paused_at" in r or "paused_after" in r:
            return

CLOSE_OUT = """# Pass 1 close-out (review S-2)

- [ ] Every pass-1 fine-grained PAT is revoked (GitHub → Settings → Developer settings → Fine-grained tokens).
- [ ] `uv run python -m runner scan results/pass1` reports no findings.
- [ ] Every arm has `results/pass1/<arm>/repo.bundle` and `arm-result.json`.
- [ ] `scenario/po-brief.md` is committed; its SHA-256 is recorded in `results/pass2/po-brief-at-start.md`'s header.
"""

def close_out_pass1() -> Path:
    f = paths.RESULTS / "pass1" / "close-out.md"; f.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    f.write_text(CLOSE_OUT)
    return f

def reset_pass2(owner) -> None:
    missing = [a for a in config.all_arms() if not (Paths("pass1", a).results / "arm-result.json").exists()]
    if missing:
        raise NotReady(f"pass1 is incomplete: {missing}")
    f = close_out_pass1()
    for line in [l for l in f.read_text().splitlines() if l.startswith("- [ ]")]:
        if not owner.confirm(line[6:]):
            raise NotReady("pass-1 close-out not confirmed")
    if (paths.STATE / "pass2").exists():
        raise NotReady("state/pass2 exists; pass 2 starts from nothing (OD20)")
    src = brief_path("pass1"); dst = paths.RESULTS / "pass2" / "po-brief-at-start.md"
    dst.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    dst.write_text(f"<!-- sha256 {_sha(src)} -->\n" + src.read_text())
    print("\n".join(repo.github_plan("pass2")))
    print("Then, for each arm in `runner order pass2`: runner setup pass2 <arm>")

def status() -> str:
    rows = []
    for pass_dir in sorted(paths.RESULTS.glob("*/")):
        for arm_dir in sorted(pass_dir.glob("*/")):
            p = Paths(pass_dir.name, arm_dir.name)
            prog = _progress(p) if p.progress.exists() else {}
            res = json.loads((arm_dir / "arm-result.json").read_text()) if (arm_dir / "arm-result.json").exists() else {}
            rows.append(f"{pass_dir.name:8} {arm_dir.name:12} next={prog.get('next_step', '-')} {res or ''}")
    return "\n".join(rows)
```

CLI (`cli.py`): `order <pass> [--seed N]`, `run-arm <pass> <arm>`, `run-pass <pass>` (both with
`credentials.from_keychain()` and `owner.Owner()`; `stepflow.Halt` → print the HALTED record, exit 2; `NotReady` →
print, exit 1), `reset-pass2`, `status`. Every command except `faults …` refuses while `faults.halted(pass)`.

```bash
git commit -m "Runner: arm order (OD13), resumable run-arm/run-pass, step-6 issue supersede, pass-1 close-out, pass-2 reset"
```

### Task 24: Dry run — the whole runner, end to end, against the reference implementation as a fake arm

No model is called and GitHub is not touched: the arm, the PO and the teammate are the deterministic
`fake_claude.py` in the dry-run image, and the credential is the dummy. Everything else is real: containers, egress
stages, containerised git, snapshots, the turn loop, escalation and brief growth (on a copy of the brief), owner
gates (scripted), the hand edit, the dry tracker, hidden tests in the tester container, quality tools, evidence,
model-pin checks, fault classification, the stop rules, the secret scan. Four dry passes:

| Pass | Arm config | Proves |
|---|---|---|
| `dry1` | `dryrun` | happy path through all 7 steps: escalation → owner → brief copy grows; approval → owner gate; `next_command` + `must_run` (step 3); the hand edit (step 2) with probe and detection; step 5 as two requests (OD6 B); the step-6 issue; hidden counts **exactly** 101/109/121/135/146/151/161 with 0 failed; quality numbers present; evidence complete; no token anywhere |
| `dry2` | `dryrun-broken` | the arm stop rule: at step 2 the arm pins the reference to step 1, no discriminating step-2 test passes → `no_progress` sent twice → stops at step 2 after 3 attempts |
| `dry3` | `dryrun-pinfault` | the model pin and the halt rule: a subagent transcript on `claude-haiku-4-5` → fault → restore and re-run → the same fault → `results/dry3/HALTED` |
| `dry4` | `dryrun-edge` | the paths draft 1 only unit-tested (review §3 table): a transient `529` retried in place (C-4); a turn **timeout** at step 2 (attempt ends `timeout`, the scripted owner keeps it an arm outcome, OD14 B prompt exercised); a **pause** after step 1 and a **resume** |

**Files:**
- Modify: `runner/images/dryrun/Dockerfile` (adds `COPY reference /opt/reference`), `runner/images/dryrun/fake_claude.py`
  (the roles below), `runner/config/arms/dryrun.toml`, `runner/orchestrate.py` (`dry_run()`, `dry_report()`),
  `runner/cli.py` (`dry-run [--report]`)
- Create: `runner/config/arms/{dryrun-broken,dryrun-pinfault,dryrun-edge}.toml`, `runner/config/dryrun-owner.toml`,
  `runner/config/dryrun-owner-dry4.toml`, `checklists/{dryrun,dryrun-broken,dryrun-pinfault,dryrun-edge}.md`,
  `checklists/verify/{dryrun,dryrun-broken,dryrun-pinfault,dryrun-edge}.sh` (created only here, review Q-7),
  `tests/runner/test_dryrun_e2e.py`, `results/dryrun-validation.md` (generated, committed)

**Fake claude roles** (state in `$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR/fake-state.json`, keyed by session):
- **PO** (the prompt names `/po/protocol.md`): reads `/po/message.md` (a `FAKE:reply=` directive there still wins, for
  Task 13's tests) and returns a valid protocol-v2 object: "print floats exactly as written" → `question` with that
  entry escalated; "Do you approve" → `approval`, approved, reply "Approved: consistent with the contract.";
  a message ending "run /dry-next" → `next_command` `/dry-next`; "is complete" → `step_done`; else `continue`.
- **Teammate** (the prompt starts "You are a developer on this project making a quick change"): edits
  `src/troupe_ref/cli.py` with `sed 's/sp.add_argument("--theatre", default=".")/sp.add_argument("--theatre",
  default=__import__("os").environ.get("TROUPE_THEATRE") or ".")/'` and replies with that file name.
- **Arm** (anything else). Step from the message: "# Step N" heading; "# Step 5 — a change request" or "A second
  change request" = 5; "A bug has been reported" = 6 (a resumed session keeps the step it started with). Behaviour
  follows the **n-th successful turn of the session** (an errored turn does not count), so a retried or nudged turn
  never skips the commit:
  - turn 1: step 1 → "Before I start: should `config show` print floats exactly as written in the file?"; other
    steps → "Plan: implement step N as briefed. Do you approve?"
  - turn 2: step 1 → copy `/opt/reference/{pyproject.toml,uv.lock,src,tests}` into the repo, `git add -A && git commit
    -m "Step 1"`; other steps → `git commit --allow-empty -m "Step N"`. `dryrun-broken` at step 2 instead commits
    `src/troupe_ref/step.py` rewritten to `STEP = min(int(os.environ.get("TROUPE_REF_STEP", "7")), 1)`. Reply: step 3
    → "Done with the core; next, run /dry-next"; otherwise "Step N is complete."
  - turn 3 and later: on `/dry-next` "Step 3 is complete."; on `/dry-close` "Closed. Step N is complete."; on
    anything else ("Please continue.", the no-progress message, a CR-B message) "Step N is complete."
  - `dryrun-pinfault`: `env = { FAKE_SUBAGENT_MODEL = "claude-haiku-4-5" }` → a subagent transcript on that model.
  - `dryrun-edge`: the first turn of its step-1 session emits `API Error: 529 overloaded` (an `is_error` result) and
    does not count as a turn; the first turn of its step-2 session writes its transcript lines and then sleeps 120 s
    (so the pin check has evidence, as a real timed-out turn would; the dry run gives dry4
    `turn_seconds = 20`). Because the reference copied at step 1 is complete and the hidden conftest gates it by
    `--step`, step 2 still makes progress on attempt 1 after the timeout.
- `arms/dryrun.toml` (and the three variants, identical except `name`, `repo_name`, `env`):
  `dry = true`, `tracker = "dry"`, `entry = "{handoff}"`, `must_run = ["/dry-close"]`, `must_run_source = "runner
  self-test"`, `[setup] shell = ["mkdir -p .claude/commands && printf 'Dry next\\n' > .claude/commands/dry-next.md &&
  printf 'Dry close\\n' > .claude/commands/dry-close.md"]`. `/dry-close` is sent once per step (the fake never
  invokes it itself).
- `dryrun-owner.toml`: `[escalations] default = "Print floats exactly as written in the file."`, `[gates] default =
  "accept"`, `[items] default = true`, `[review] default = "continue"`, `[confirm] default = true`, `[reclassify]
  default = "arm"`. `dryrun-owner-dry4.toml`: the same plus `[review] pause_after = [1]`.
- `checklists/dryrun.md`: `## Machine-verified` lists the four `common.*` ids, `common.no-foreign-model-ids`,
  `setup.manifest-matches-pass1`; `## Owner-verified` `- [ ] OV1: dry run (scripted owner).`; `## Known traps` none.
  The three variant md files are identical copies. `checklists/verify/dryrun.sh`: `set -u; . /checks/lib.sh; common
  dryrun ""`; each variant script is `exec bash /checks/dryrun.sh "$@"`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the end-to-end acceptance test** (docker; runs the four dry passes once per module)

```python
# tests/runner/test_dryrun_e2e.py
import gzip, json, subprocess
import pytest
from runner import secrets
from runner.paths import RESULTS, REPO_ROOT, STATE

pytestmark = pytest.mark.docker
EXPECTED = {1: 101, 2: 109, 3: 121, 4: 135, 5: 146, 6: 151, 7: 161}

@pytest.fixture(scope="module", autouse=True)
def dry_runs():
    subprocess.run(["uv", "run", "python", "-m", "runner", "dry-run", "--report"], cwd=REPO_ROOT, check=True, timeout=10800)

def J(*p): return json.loads(RESULTS.joinpath(*p).read_text())
def lines(*p): return [json.loads(l) for l in RESULTS.joinpath(*p).read_text().splitlines()]

@pytest.mark.parametrize("step", range(1, 8))
def test_dry1_hidden_results_match_the_validated_reference_counts(step):
    s = J("dry1", "dryrun", f"{step:02d}", "hidden", "summary.json")
    assert (s["total"], s["passed"], s["failed"], s["flaky"], s["flaky_arm"]) == (EXPECTED[step], EXPECTED[step], 0, 0, 0)

@pytest.mark.parametrize("step", range(1, 8))
def test_dry1_every_step_has_the_design_7_evidence_and_a_pin_report_without_violations(step):   # renamed (Q-11)
    d = RESULTS / "dry1" / "dryrun" / f"{step:02d}"
    for rel in ["run.json", "cost.json", "questions.jsonl", "git.json", "summary.md", "quality/summary.json", "handoff.md"]:
        assert (d / rel).exists(), rel
    pin = J("dry1", "dryrun", f"{step:02d}", "model-pin.json")
    assert pin["ok"] and pin["assistant_messages"] > 0 and not pin["violations"]
    assert J("dry1", "dryrun", f"{step:02d}", "git.json")["sha_after"]           # refs/heads/main recorded (F-1)

def test_dry1_escalation_reached_the_owner_and_grew_the_copy_not_the_real_brief():
    q = lines("dry1", "dryrun", "01", "questions.jsonl")
    assert any(e["escalated"] and "owner" in e["answered_by"] for e in q)
    assert "print floats exactly as written" in (STATE / "dry1" / "po-brief.md").read_text().lower()
    assert subprocess.run(["git", "diff", "--quiet", "scenario/po-brief.md"], cwd=REPO_ROOT).returncode == 0

def test_dry1_hand_edit_was_verified_and_detection_facts_recorded():
    assert all(J("dry1", "dryrun", "02", "hand-edit", "probe.json").values())
    assert J("dry1", "dryrun", "02", "hand-edit", "detection.json")["feature_kept"] is True
    assert J("dry1", "dryrun", "02", "hand-edit", "edit.json")["files"] == ["src/troupe_ref/cli.py"]

def test_dry1_step3_followed_the_methods_next_command_and_ran_must_run():
    q = lines("dry1", "dryrun", "03", "questions.jsonl")
    assert [e["reply"] for e in q if e["kind"] in ("next_command", "step_done") and e["reply"]] == ["/dry-next", "/dry-close"]

def test_dry1_step5_was_two_requests():                                          # OD6 B
    kinds = [e["kind"] for e in lines("dry1", "dryrun", "05", "questions.jsonl")]
    assert "preempted" in kinds and "A second change request" in (RESULTS / "dry1/dryrun/05/handoff.md").read_text()

def test_dry1_step6_bug_went_to_the_tracker():
    assert J("dry1", "dryrun", "06", "tracker.json")["issues"][0]["ref"] == "dry#1"

def test_dry1_mutation_score_was_measured_on_copy_b():
    assert J("dry1", "dryrun", "07", "quality", "summary.json")["mutation"]["total"] > 0

def test_no_credential_in_any_evidence_state_or_container_spec():               # strengthened (review Q-11)
    for root in [RESULTS / f"dry{i}" for i in range(1, 5)] + [STATE / f"dry{i}" for i in range(1, 5)]:
        assert secrets.scan_tree(root, ["dry-run-dummy-token"]) == [], root      # .gz files are decompressed
    rep = json.loads((RESULTS / "dryrun-specs.json").read_text())
    assert rep["specs"] > 100 and rep["specs_containing_token"] == 0              # every RunSpec the dry run built
    assert subprocess.run(["docker", "ps", "-aq", "--filter", "label=bakeoff.run"], capture_output=True,
                          text=True).stdout.strip() == ""

def test_dry2_arm_stops_at_step2_after_three_no_progress_attempts():
    assert J("dry2", "dryrun-broken", "arm-result.json")["stopped_at"] == 2
    parked = sorted(p.name for p in (RESULTS / "dry2/dryrun-broken/02/attempts").iterdir())
    assert parked == ["1-no-progress", "2-no-progress"]
    assert not (RESULTS / "dry2/dryrun-broken/03").exists()

def test_dry3_same_pin_fault_twice_halts():
    assert (RESULTS / "dry3" / "HALTED").exists()
    assert [e["signature"] for e in lines("dry3", "faults.jsonl")] == ["claude-haiku-4-5", "claude-haiku-4-5"]
    assert not (RESULTS / "HALTED").exists()                                     # the real ledger is untouched

def test_dry4_transient_error_was_retried_in_place_without_a_harness_fault():   # review C-4
    run = J("dry4", "dryrun-edge", "01", "run.json")
    assert any(i.startswith("transient-retry:") for i in run["interventions"])
    ledger = RESULTS / "dry4" / "faults.jsonl"
    assert not ledger.exists() or not any(e.get("kind", "").startswith("infra-") for e in lines("dry4", "faults.jsonl"))

def test_dry4_timeout_ended_an_attempt_as_an_arm_outcome_and_the_step_still_completed():
    run = J("dry4", "dryrun-edge", "02", "run.json")
    assert "turn_seconds" in run["breaches"] and run["attempts"] == 1 and run["outcome"] == "done"
    assert J("dry4", "dryrun-edge", "breaches.json")[0]["owner"] == "arm"

def test_dry4_paused_after_step1_and_resumed_to_the_end():
    assert J("dry4", "dryrun-edge", "arm-result.json")["completed"] is True
    assert J("dry4", "dryrun-edge", "resume.json")["paused_after"] == 1
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement** the fake roles, the arm configs, owner scripts, checklists, and in `orchestrate.py`:

```python
# runner/orchestrate.py (additions)
from runner import credentials, docker as _docker, owner as owner_mod

DRY = [("dry1", "dryrun", "dryrun-owner.toml", {"transient_backoff": [1, 1, 1]}),
       ("dry2", "dryrun-broken", "dryrun-owner.toml", {"transient_backoff": [1, 1, 1]}),
       ("dry3", "dryrun-pinfault", "dryrun-owner.toml", {"transient_backoff": [1, 1, 1]}),
       ("dry4", "dryrun-edge", "dryrun-owner-dry4.toml", {"transient_backoff": [1, 1, 1], "turn_seconds": 20})]

def dry_run() -> dict:
    cred, res = credentials.dummy(), {}
    with _docker.recording() as specs:
        for pass_, arm_name, script, limits in DRY:
            shutil.rmtree(paths.STATE / pass_, ignore_errors=True); shutil.rmtree(paths.RESULTS / pass_, ignore_errors=True)
            (paths.STATE / pass_).mkdir(parents=True)
            shutil.copy(REPO_ROOT / "scenario" / "po-brief.md", brief_path(pass_))
            o = owner_mod.ScriptedOwner(config.CONFIG_DIR / script).bind(config.load_arm(arm_name))
            checklist.run_setup(pass_, arm_name, cred, o)
            try:
                r = run_arm(pass_, arm_name, cred, o, limits)
                if "paused_after" in r or "paused_at" in r:                 # dry4: pause, then resume
                    (Paths(pass_, arm_name).results / "resume.json").write_text(json.dumps(r))
                    r = run_arm(pass_, arm_name, cred, o, limits)
                res[pass_] = r
            except stepflow.Halt as h:
                res[pass_] = {"halted": str(h)[:500]}
    token = "dry-run-dummy-token"
    bad = sum(1 for s in specs if token in json.dumps(s.env) or any(token in x for x in s.cmd))
    (paths.RESULTS / "dryrun-specs.json").write_text(json.dumps({"specs": len(specs), "specs_containing_token": bad}))
    return res

def dry_report(res: dict) -> Path:
    findings = [f for i in range(1, 5) for root in (paths.RESULTS / f"dry{i}", paths.STATE / f"dry{i}")
                for f in secrets.scan_tree(root, ["dry-run-dummy-token"])]
    left = _docker.running("bakeoff.run")
    spec = json.loads((paths.RESULTS / "dryrun-specs.json").read_text())
    ok = (not findings and not left and spec["specs_containing_token"] == 0 and "halted" in res.get("dry3", {})
          and res.get("dry2", {}).get("stopped_at") == 2 and res.get("dry1", {}).get("completed")
          and res.get("dry4", {}).get("completed"))
    lines = ["# Dry-run validation", "", f"- results: {json.dumps(res)[:2000]}",
             f"- secret findings (evidence + state, .gz decompressed): {len(findings)}",
             f"- container specs built: {spec['specs']}; containing the token: {spec['specs_containing_token']}",
             f"- leftover containers: {len(left)}", "", f"**Result: {'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}**", ""]
    out = paths.RESULTS / "dryrun-validation.md"; out.write_text("\n".join(lines))
    return out
```
`runner dry-run [--report]` → `dry_run()` (and `dry_report()`); exits 1 unless the report says PASS.

### Task 25: Live probe (owner-run; spends a little) — confirm the headless assumptions

The first real Claude use; runs only on the owner's command (`uv run python -m runner probe`, which asks for
confirmation, then reads the keychain credential). Namespace `probe`; results in `results/probe/`. P9 and P11 need no
model and can be run alone with `runner probe --no-model`.

**Files:**
- Create: `runner/probe.py`, `tests/runner/test_probe.py`, `results/probe/probe.md` (generated), `results/probe/P*.json`
- Modify: `runner/cli.py` (`probe [--no-model]`)

| # | Probe | Pass condition / what it decides |
|---|---|---|
| P1 | `claude -p "Reply with the word ok"` in the base image | `init.model` and the transcript's `message.model` recorded; `pins.accepted_models` set to exactly that; **the transcript is at `$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR/projects/<cwd with / → ->/<session>.jsonl`** (review C-6: the fake's layout is confirmed, not assumed) |
| P2 | second turn with `--resume <id>`: "What word did you reply?" | answer "ok" → OD19 (resume across containers with a persistent home) works |
| P3 | "Use the Task tool with model haiku to start a subagent that replies sub-ok" | a transcript under `…/<session>/subagents/` exists and its model is the pin → OD2's alias remap works |
| P3b | a project agent `.claude/agents/cheap.md` with frontmatter `model: claude-haiku-4-5` (a **full ID**), invoked through the Task tool | records whether a full ID escapes the remap (expected: it does) → confirms that `common.no-foreign-model-ids` must catch it at setup (review F-6) |
| P4 | "Use the AskUserQuestion tool to ask me red or blue." with it disallowed | what 2.1.281 does (denial, then a text question?) → confirms OD11 |
| P5 | `claude plugin marketplace add/install` for superpowers and both BMAD plugins into a scratch config (setup egress); **and** a session with `--plugin-dir /opt/plugins/<name>` in each arm image | install works headless (route A) and the vendored plugin loads (route B: its skills appear in `init`); BMAD `subdir`s confirmed or corrected |
| P6 | a project command `/hello` and a project skill `.claude/skills/greet/SKILL.md` invoked as `/greet` in `-p` mode | slash commands **and skills-as-slash** work in `-p` — the basis of OD5 and review F-2's invocation form |
| P7 | `--disallowedTools WebSearch,WebFetch,AskUserQuestion` and `--tools Read,Glob,Grep`; plus a repo `CLAUDE.md` saying "Always answer PINEAPPLE" with `--setting-sources ""` | `init.tools` confirms both lists; the isolated session does **not** say PINEAPPLE (the behaviour draft 1's `test_isolated_sessions_…` only claimed, review Q-11) |
| P8 | proxy log over P1–P7, split by stage | run-stage hosts ⊆ `run_common`; any extra host is listed for the owner |
| P9 | no model: `bd create --help`, `spec-kitty --help`, `spec-kitty tracker --help`, `openspec config --help`; each tool's help and install tree grepped for `telemetry`/`analytics`/`posthog`/`sentry`; `run_shell_only` for every arm **online and offline**, and `commands.listing` | exact flags and command names (corrects `[setup]`, `[drive]`, checklist ids where the draft guessed — e.g. G1's binding syntax); telemetry per tool (review G8); whether `specify init` / `cc-sdd` / `spec-kitty init` fetch templates at setup (offline run fails or refuses a host → pinned only by the manifest comparison, review G6) |
| P10 | after P1/P2: every file under the probe's `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` searched for the token bytes; any `.credentials.json` | nothing found → the fd hand-off leaves no token on disk (review S-3); found → stop, the owner decides before any arm runs |
| P11 | no model: `.claude/skills/speckit-taskstoissues/SKILL.md` after `specify init` | records whether it uses `gh` or the GitHub MCP server (`mcp__github`) → decides OD24's branch (Task 12 Step 5) |

```python
# tests/runner/test_probe.py
from runner import probe

def test_transcript_path_verdict_names_the_expected_layout(tmp_path):
    cfg = tmp_path / ".claude"
    (cfg / "projects" / "-work-probe").mkdir(parents=True)
    (cfg / "projects" / "-work-probe" / "s1.jsonl").write_text("{}\n")
    assert probe.transcript_layout(cfg, "/work/probe", "s1") == {"expected": "projects/-work-probe/s1.jsonl", "found": True}

def test_token_search_reports_files_by_name_only(tmp_path):

    assert probe.files_containing(tmp_path, b"SECRET") == ["a.json"]

def test_od24_route_is_read_from_the_skill_text():
```
