- [ ] **Step 2: Implement `runner/probe.py`**

```python
# runner/probe.py — owner-run live probe (Task 25). Every probe writes results/probe/P<n>.json.
from __future__ import annotations
import gzip, json, os, re, shutil
from pathlib import Path
from runner import checklist, commands, config, credentials, docker, egress, gitops, repo, session, transcripts
from runner.docker import Mount, RunSpec
from runner.paths import Paths, RESULTS

OUT = RESULTS / "probe"

def transcript_layout(cfg: Path, cwd: str, sid: str) -> dict:
    rel = f"projects/{cwd.replace('/', '-')}/{sid}.jsonl"
    return {"expected": rel, "found": (cfg / rel).exists()}

def files_containing(root: Path, needle: bytes) -> list[str]:
    hits = []
    for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(root, followlinks=False):
        for f in filenames:
            p = Path(dirpath) / f
            if not p.is_symlink() and needle in p.read_bytes():
                hits.append(str(p.relative_to(root)))
    return sorted(hits)

def export_route(skill_text: str) -> str:
    if "mcp__github" in skill_text or "github-mcp" in skill_text.lower():
        return "mcp"
    if re.search(r"\bgh issue create\b", skill_text):
        return "gh"
    return "unknown"

def _save(name: str, data: dict) -> dict:
    OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (OUT / f"{name}.json").write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2, default=str))
    return data

def _turn(p: Paths, cred, n: int, msg: str, st, *, resume: str = "", tools: str = "", isolated: bool = False,
          image: str | None = None, workdir: str | None = None, extra_env: dict | None = None) -> session.TurnResult:
    pins, arm = config.load_pins(), config.load_arm("control")
    env = credentials.session_env(pins, arm, role="arm", isolated=isolated, tools=tools, resume=resume) | (extra_env or {})
    t = session.Turn("probe", "control", 0, 1, n, image or pins.images["base"], msg, env,
                     [Mount(p.home, "/home/actor", False), Mount(p.work, "/work", False)], st.net, True,
                     workdir or p.container_repo, p.inbox("probe"), "probe", False, p.config_dir)
    return session.run_turn(t, cred, OUT, 1800)

def _stream_events(r: session.TurnResult) -> list[dict]:
    out = []
    with gzip.open(r.stream_path, "rt") as fh:
        for l in fh:
            try:
                out.append(json.loads(l))
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                pass
    return out

def with_model(cred) -> dict:
    pins, res = config.load_pins(), {}
    p = Paths("probe", "control")
    shutil.rmtree(p.base, ignore_errors=True); repo.init(p, "control", "probe")
    token = cred.raw_for_scanner().encode()
    with egress.Stage("probe", "control", "run", pins.egress_run_common, OUT / "egress-run.log") as st:
        r1 = _turn(p, cred, 1, "Reply with the word ok", st)
        files = transcripts.safe_files(p.config_dir / "projects")
        res["P1"] = _save("P1", {"init_model": r1.init.get("model"), "outcome": r1.outcome,
                                 "transcript_models": sorted({(json.loads(l).get("message") or {}).get("model")
                                                             for f in files for l in f.read_text().splitlines()
                                                             if '"assistant"' in l}),
                                 "layout": transcript_layout(p.config_dir, p.container_repo, r1.session_id or "")})
        r2 = _turn(p, cred, 2, "What word did you reply?", st, resume=r1.session_id or "")
        res["P2"] = _save("P2", {"answer": r2.final_text, "ok": "ok" in r2.final_text.lower()})
        before = transcripts.snapshot(p.config_dir)
        r3 = _turn(p, cred, 3, "Use the Task tool with model haiku to start a subagent that replies sub-ok; tell me its reply.", st)
        sub = [f for f in transcripts.collect(p.config_dir, before, OUT / "P3") if "subagents" in str(f)]
        res["P3"] = _save("P3", {"subagent_files": [str(f) for f in sub],
                                 "pin": transcripts.verify_pin(sub, [], pins).__dict__ if sub else None})
        (p.repo / ".claude" / "agents").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (p.repo / ".claude" / "agents" / "cheap.md").write_text(
            "---\nname: cheap\ndescription: replies cheap-ok\nmodel: claude-haiku-4-5\n---\nReply with cheap-ok.\n")
        before = transcripts.snapshot(p.config_dir)
        _turn(p, cred, 4, "Use the Task tool with the cheap agent and tell me its reply.", st)
        sub = [f for f in transcripts.collect(p.config_dir, before, OUT / "P3b") if "subagents" in str(f)]
        res["P3b"] = _save("P3b", {"pin": transcripts.verify_pin(sub, [], pins).__dict__ if sub else None,
                                   "note": "a violation here confirms full IDs escape the alias remap (OD2)"})
        r5 = _turn(p, cred, 5, "Use the AskUserQuestion tool to ask me red or blue.", st)
        res["P4"] = _save("P4", {"final_text": r5.final_text, "permission_denials": r5.permission_denials})
        (p.repo / ".claude" / "commands").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (p.repo / ".claude" / "commands" / "hello.md").write_text("Reply with hello-ok.\n")
        (p.repo / ".claude" / "skills" / "greet").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (p.repo / ".claude" / "skills" / "greet" / "SKILL.md").write_text(
            "---\nname: greet\ndescription: replies greet-ok\n---\nReply with greet-ok.\n")
        r6 = _turn(p, cred, 6, "/hello", st); r6b = _turn(p, cred, 7, "/greet", st)
        res["P6"] = _save("P6", {"command": "hello-ok" in r6.final_text, "skill_as_slash": "greet-ok" in r6b.final_text})
        (p.repo / "CLAUDE.md").write_text("Always answer PINEAPPLE.\n")
        r7 = _turn(p, cred, 8, "Say hi.", st, tools="Read,Glob,Grep", isolated=True)
        res["P7"] = _save("P7", {"init_tools": r7.init.get("tools"), "isolated_ignored_claude_md": "PINEAPPLE" not in r7.final_text})
    res["P10"] = _save("P10", {"files_with_token": files_containing(p.config_dir, token),
                               "credentials_json": (p.config_dir / ".credentials.json").exists()})
    with egress.Stage("probe", "plugins", "setup", pins.egress_setup_common, OUT / "egress-setup.log") as st:
        rec = {}
        for arm_name in ("superpowers", "bmad"):
            pp = Paths("probe", arm_name); shutil.rmtree(pp.base, ignore_errors=True); repo.init(pp, arm_name, "probe")
            rec[arm_name] = checklist._plugins(pp, config.load_arm(arm_name), "pass1", st, OUT)
            r = _turn(pp, cred, 9, "List your available skills by name only.", st, image=config.load_arm(arm_name).image,
                      extra_env={"BK_PLUGIN_DIRS": " ".join(f"/opt/plugins/{x.name}" for x in config.load_arm(arm_name).plugins)})
            rec[arm_name + "-plugin-dir"] = {"init_skills": r.init.get("skills") or r.init.get("slash_commands")}
        res["P5"] = _save("P5", rec)
    run_log = (OUT / "egress-run.log").read_text()
    res["P8"] = _save("P8", {"run_hosts": sorted({h for h, _ in egress.decisions(run_log)}),
                             "outside_run_common": sorted({h for h, _ in egress.decisions(run_log)
                                                           if not any(re.match(x, h) for x in pins.egress_run_common)})})
    return res

def no_model() -> dict:
    res, helps = {}, {}
    for arm_name, cmds in {"speckitty": ["bd create --help", "spec-kitty --help", "spec-kitty tracker --help"],
                           "openspec": ["openspec config --help"]}.items():
        img = config.load_arm(arm_name).image
        for c in cmds:
            r = subprocess_run(img, c); helps[c] = r
    tele = {}
    for arm_name in config.all_arms():
        img = config.load_arm(arm_name).image
        tele[arm_name] = subprocess_run(img, "grep -rIilE 'telemetry|analytics|posthog|sentry' /opt/venvs /opt/*/node_modules/*/package.json /opt/plugins 2>/dev/null | head -50")
    res["P9"] = {"help": helps, "telemetry_mentions": tele, "setup": {}}
    for arm_name in config.all_arms():
        entry = {}
        for offline in (False, True):
            ns = "probe-offline" if offline else "probe"
            shutil.rmtree(Paths(ns, arm_name).base, ignore_errors=True)
            try:
                checks = checklist.run_shell_only(ns, arm_name, offline=offline)
                entry["offline" if offline else "online"] = {c.id: c.ok for c in checks}
            except RuntimeError as e:
                entry["offline" if offline else "online"] = {"error": str(e)[:500]}
        res["P9"]["setup"][arm_name] = entry
    _save("P9", res["P9"])
    sk = Paths("probe", "speckit").repo / ".claude" / "skills" / "speckit-taskstoissues" / "SKILL.md"
    res["P11"] = _save("P11", {"route": export_route(sk.read_text()) if sk.exists() else "skill missing"})
    return res

def subprocess_run(image: str, cmd: str) -> str:
    s = RunSpec(name=f"bk-probe-{abs(hash(cmd)) % 10**8}", image=image, label="bakeoff.run=probe/help", network="none",
                mounts=[], env={}, workdir="/tmp", cmd=["bash", "-lc", cmd], stdin=False,
                limits=config.load_pins().limits, proxy=False)
    r = docker.run(s, timeout=120)
    return (r.stdout + r.stderr).decode(errors="replace")[-6000:]

def report(res: dict) -> Path:
    lines = ["# Live probe", ""] + [f"## {k}\n```json\n{json.dumps(v, indent=2, default=str)[:4000]}\n```" for k, v in sorted(res.items())]
    lines += ["", "## Corrections to apply (owner)", "",
              "- `pins.toml accepted_models` = P1's transcript model string(s).",
              "- If P3 shows a non-pinned subagent model: OD2 needs the owner's decision before superpowers runs.",
              "- P3b: expected violation — confirms the setup check; no action if `common.no-foreign-model-ids` exists.",
              "- If P6.skill_as_slash is false: `commands.invocation` must send skills as `Use the <name> skill.` instead.",
              "- If P7.isolated_ignored_claude_md is false: stop — the PO and teammate are not isolated.",
              "- P9: correct `[setup]` / checklist ids to the real flags; record telemetry per tool in each checklist OV.",
              "- P9 offline failures: note which setups fetch at setup time (pinned by the manifest comparison only).",
              "- P10: any file listed → stop; the owner decides before any arm runs.",
              "- P11: `mcp` → apply Task 12 Step 5 if OD24 = B; `gh` → nothing to add."]
    (OUT / "probe.md").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
    return OUT / "probe.md"
```
The offline runs use the separate namespace `probe-offline` (so both runs keep their evidence). `runner probe` → `owner.Owner().confirm("Run the live probe (spends a
little quota)?")`, then `with_model(credentials.from_keychain())` and `no_model()`, then `report()`; `--no-model` runs
only `no_model()`.

- [ ] **Step 3 (owner):** `uv run python -m runner probe`. Review `results/probe/probe.md` and apply its corrections to
  `pins.toml`, `arms/*.toml` and `checklists/`, each as its own commit. Re-run `uv run pytest tests/runner -q` and
  `uv run python -m runner dry-run --report`; both must still pass.

- [ ] **Step 4: Commit**

```bash
git add runner/probe.py runner/checklist.py runner/cli.py tests/runner/test_probe.py results/probe
git commit -m "Runner: live probe — model string, transcript path, resume, subagent pin (alias and full ID), tools, plugins, token on disk, setup flags"
```

### Task 26: Canary — one real arm, steps 1–2, before go/no-go (owner-approved: OD25; spends real quota)

The review's §3 table lists what the dry run cannot exercise: the real CLI with every flag together, the real
transcript layout across many turns, a real PO, the terminal owner prompts, the real GitHub tracker path and token
persistence under real use. The probe checks each assumption alone; the canary checks them **together**, on the
cheapest arm, before anything is scored. **Nothing in this task runs without the owner's explicit approval of OD25.**

Namespace `canary` (its own ledger, brief copy and results), arm `control`, steps 1 and 2 (step 2 includes the hand
edit), owner present, real credential, real limits. Escalation answers go into the canary's **copy** of the brief; the
owner may copy any of them into the real brief deliberately before pass 1.

**Files:**
- Modify: `runner/orchestrate.py` (`canary(cred, owner)`, `canary_check() -> list[tuple[str, bool, str]]`),
  `runner/cli.py` (`canary [--check]`)
- Create: `tests/runner/test_canary_live.py` (marked `live`), `results/canary/canary.md` (generated)

- [ ] **Step 1: Owner preparation** — create the private repo `troupe-run/bakeoff-specs-control-canary` and a PAT for
  it stored as `bakeoff-gh-canary-control` (the `github-plan` text applies with `canary` in place of the pass).

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement**

```python
# runner/orchestrate.py (additions)
def canary(cred, owner) -> dict:
    if not owner.confirm("Run the canary (control arm, steps 1-2, real model and PO; spends quota — OD25)?"):
        return {"skipped": True}
    shutil.rmtree(paths.STATE / "canary", ignore_errors=True)
    (paths.STATE / "canary").mkdir(parents=True)
    shutil.copy(REPO_ROOT / "scenario" / "po-brief.md", brief_path("canary"))
    order("canary")
    checklist.run_setup("canary", "control", cred, owner)
    r = run_arm("canary", "control", cred, owner, stop_after=2)
    ctx_like = type("C", (), {"pass_": "canary", "arm": config.load_arm("control"), "paths": Paths("canary", "control"),
                              "out": Paths("canary", "control").results / "tracker-check"})()
    ctx_like.out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    issue = tracker.create_bug(ctx_like, owner)                      # the real GitHub write path, owner-confirmed
    p = Paths("canary", "control")
    s = checklist._arm_spec(p, config.load_arm("control"), ["bash", "-lc",
                            f"gh issue view {issue.id} --repo {repo.github_slug('canary', 'control')} --json title"],
                            network="none", name="ghread")
    with egress.Stage("canary", "control", "run", config.load_pins().egress_run_common + ["^api\\.github\\.com$"],
                      ctx_like.out / "egress.log") as st:
        s = dataclasses.replace(s, network=st.net, proxy=True)
        read = docker.run(s, timeout=120)
    tracker.supersede(ctx_like, issue, owner)                        # closes it again
    (ctx_like.out / "result.json").write_text(json.dumps({"issue": issue.ref, "arm_pat_read_ok": read.returncode == 0}))
    return r

def canary_check() -> list[tuple[str, bool, str]]:
    base = paths.RESULTS / "canary" / "control"
    rows = []
    for step in (1, 2):
        d = base / f"{step:02d}"
        pin = json.loads((d / "model-pin.json").read_text())
        rows.append((f"step {step}: arm pin verified from real transcripts", pin["ok"] and pin["assistant_messages"] > 0,
                     f"{pin['assistant_messages']} messages, versions {pin['claude_code_versions']}"))
        po_pins = [json.loads(f.read_text()) for f in (d / "po").glob("*/model-pin.json")]
        rows.append((f"step {step}: every PO decision pin-verified", bool(po_pins) and all(x["ok"] for x in po_pins),
                     f"{len(po_pins)} PO decisions"))
        run = json.loads((d / "run.json").read_text())
        rows.append((f"step {step}: no harness fault", not run["harness_faults"], str(run["harness_faults"])[:200]))
        rows.append((f"step {step}: evidence scan clean", not secrets.scan_tree(d, secrets.literals()), ""))
        hs = json.loads((d / "hidden" / "summary.json").read_text())
        rows.append((f"step {step}: hidden tests ran in the tester container", hs["total"] > 0, f"{hs['passed']}/{hs['total']}"))
    det = base / "02" / "hand-edit" / "detection.json"
    rows.append(("hand edit applied and detection recorded", det.exists(), det.read_text()[:200] if det.exists() else ""))
    tc = json.loads((base / "tracker-check" / "result.json").read_text())
    rows.append(("GitHub issue created by the owner and read with the arm's PAT", tc["arm_pat_read_ok"], tc["issue"]))
    creds = list((paths.STATE / "canary" / "control" / "home" / ".claude").glob(".credentials.json"))
    rows.append(("no credential file in the arm's home", not creds, ""))
    lines = ["# Canary check", ""] + [f"- [{'x' if ok else ' '}] {what} — {detail}" for what, ok, detail in rows]
    lines += ["", f"**Result: {'PASS' if all(ok for _, ok, _ in rows) else 'FAIL'}**", ""]
    (paths.RESULTS / "canary" / "canary.md").write_text("\n".join(lines))
    return rows
```

```python
# tests/runner/test_canary_live.py — spends real quota; only with --run-live, only after OD25 is approved
import pytest
from runner import orchestrate

pytestmark = [pytest.mark.live, pytest.mark.docker, pytest.mark.network]

def test_the_canary_checks_all_pass():
    rows = orchestrate.canary_check()
    assert all(ok for _, ok, _ in rows), [r for r in rows if not r[1]]
```

  summaries. Any FAIL row is fixed (and the dry run re-run) before Task 27.

### Task 27: Go/no-go before any real arm (owner gate)

Not code: the checklist the owner signs before `run-pass pass1`, kept as `results/pass1/go-no-go.md`.

- [ ] Every owner decision OD1–OD25 answered; every **SPEC CHANGE** OD (OD2, OD3, OD4, OD5, OD6, OD8, OD10, OD14,
      OD17, OD20, OD23, and OD24 if A) explicitly approved and recorded in `docs/design.md`; parameters set to the answers.
- [ ] Harness texts approved: `po-protocol.md`, `teammate-prompt.md`, `scenario.toml` (delivery sentence, no-progress
      nudge, commit nudge, step-5 texts), `project-principles.md`.
- [ ] `uv run pytest tests -q`, `… -m docker -q`, `… -m "docker and network" -q` green; `results/dryrun-validation.md` PASS.
- [ ] `results/probe/probe.md` reviewed; its corrections applied and committed; P7 isolation and P10 token checks passed.
- [ ] Canary: `results/canary/canary.md` PASS (or the owner records why it was skipped).
- [ ] Final `runner images build --rebuild`; `images.lock.json` committed; `state/images/*.tar` saved (no rebuild until the
      bake-off ends, review S-5).
- [ ] Keychain item `bakeoff-claude-oauth` present; subscription terms confirmed for this use.
- [ ] `runner github-plan pass1` commands run; one PAT per GitHub-tracker arm stored, **expiry ≤ 45 days**;
      `runner github-verify pass1` passes — every repo private and empty, and **no `troupe-run` repo is public**
      (review G7; includes this harness repo, so no arm can read the hidden tests or spike A's findings).
- [ ] The results secret-scan hook is enabled (`git config core.hooksPath .githooks`).
- [ ] The machine is otherwise idle; `runner order pass1 --seed <n>` recorded.
- [ ] Then, for each arm in order: `runner setup pass1 <arm>` (owner present), then `runner run-arm pass1 <arm>`.

**Placeholder scan (draft 2).** Every task has concrete code, commands and tests. What remains unverified is
unverifiable without a live run, is named, and has a task that settles it: the transcript model string and layout
(P1); `claude plugin …` headless and the BMAD plugin `subdir`s (P5); Spec Kitty 3.2.7's `tracker` syntax and beads'
`bd create` flags (P9 — setup fails loudly if wrong); skills as `/name` in `-p` (P6); `--tools`/`--disallowedTools`
syntax and `--setting-sources ""` isolation (P7); `AskUserQuestion` in `-p` (P4); token on disk (P10); Spec Kit's
export route (P11); mutmut 3 listing mutants without running them (Task 19's docker test; OD15 falls back to A). The
`sha` and digest values in `pins.toml`/`arms/*.toml` are written by `runner images pin` (Tasks 2, 8), never by hand.

**Consistency checks done:** `session.Turn` has the same field order at every construction site (Tasks 6, 9, 13, 15,
16, 25). File stems include role and attempt (`<role>-a<k>-NNN`), PO directories include the attempt
(`po/a<k>-NNN`), so nothing overwrites anything. `po.decide` returns `PoResult` everywhere it is consumed (Tasks 13,
15, 22). The fault ledger is namespaced (real passes share `results/faults.jsonl`; dry/probe/canary have their own).
Every git command on an arm repo goes through `gitops` (Task 8 test). `refs/heads/main` is what is tested and
recorded (Tasks 8, 20, 22). The expected hidden counts (101…161) are those in `results/instrument-validation.md`. The
no-progress rule uses `new_discriminating_passed` (Plan 1's allowlist means a do-nothing arm passes some "new" tests).

**Spec gaps and changes (each is an owner decision above):** the step-2 hand edit (OD1); the model pin vs methods'
own cheaper models (OD2); "attempt", "no progress" and the nudge (OD3); what is tested and how arms are told (OD4);
who types a command-driven method's next stage (OD5); step 5's concurrency (OD6); owner-visible approvals and pass-2
escalations (OD7, OD12); repo naming/visibility across passes (OD8); install route (OD9); web access (OD10); runaway
limits (OD14); results path (OD17); what pass 2 resets (OD20); principles slots (OD22); hidden tests in a tester
container vs "from outside" (OD23); Spec Kit's export (OD24); the canary (OD25).
